((tolkāppiyam, eḻuttatikāram, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam))

((ciṟappuppāyiram))

{{S PREF}}

TE0n


vaṭa vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ kumari
y-āy iṭai+
tamiḻ kūṟu nal= ulakattu
vaḻakku* um^ ceyyuḷ um āy iru mutaliṉ
eḻuttu* um^ col= um poruḷ um* nāṭi+
cem^ tamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntu-nūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa v-eṇṇi+
pulam^ tokuttōṉ ē pōkku* aṟu paṉuval*
^nilam^taru-tiruviṉ+ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu
aṟaṅ karai nāvil* ^nāṉ maṟai muṟṟiya
v-ataṅkōṭṭu* ācāṟku* aril* ^tapa+ terintu
mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu muṟai kāṭṭi
malku nīr varaippiṉ aintiram* niṟainta
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyar tōṟṟi+
pal pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimaiyōṉ ē.

{{S NAC}} eṉpatu pāyiram.

e+ nūl uraippiṉum a+ nūṟku+ pāyiram uraittu uraikka eṉpatu ilakkaṇam. eṉṉai?

{{S CIT}}
`āyiram* mukattāṉ akaṉṟatu* āyiṉum
pāyiram illatu paṉuval aṉṟu* ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ.

pāyiram eṉṟatu puṟam*v-uraiyai. nūl kēṭkiṉṟāṉ puṟam*v-urai kēṭkiṉ+ $koḻu+ ceṉṟavaḻi+ tuṉ=ūci iṉitu cellum-āṟu pōla a+ nūl iṉitu viḷaṅkutaliṉ+ puṟam*v-urai kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. eṉṉai?

{{S CIT}}
`paru+ poruṭṭu* ākiya pāyiram^ kēṭṭārkku
nuṇ poruṭṭu ākiya nūl iṉitu viḷaṅkum.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ.

a+ pāyiram^ tāṉ talai y-amainta yāṉaikku viṉai y-amainta pākaṉ pōla v-um aḷappu* ariya ākāyattiṟku viḷakkam ākiya tiṅkaḷ um ñāyiṟu* um pōla v-um nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyā+ ciṟappiṟṟu* āy iruttaliṉ, atu kēḷākkāl+ kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurīi+ pōla v-uṅ kuṟicci pukka māṉ pōla v-um māṇākkaṉ iṭar+paṭum eṉka.

a+ pāyiram potu v-um^ ciṟappu* um eṉa iru-vakaittu.

avaṟṟuḷ+ potu+pāyiram ellā nūl%mukattu* um uraikka+paṭum. atu tāṉ nāṉku vakaittu.

{{S CIT}}
`īvōṉ ^taṉmai y-ītal iyaṟkai
koḷvōṉ ^taṉmai kōṭaṉ marapu* eṉa
v-īr-iraṇṭu eṉpa potuviṉ ^tokai y-ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṉum itaṉāṉ aṟika.

īvōr kaṟkappaṭuvōr um^ kaṟkappaṭātōr um eṉa iru-vakaiyar. avaruḷ+ kaṟkappaṭuvōr nāṉku tiṟattār.

{{S CIT}}
`malai nilam pū-v-ē tulā+kōl eṉṟiṉṉar
$ulaivil uṇarvuṭaiyōr.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉuḷ,

{{S CIT}}
`malai y-ē,
aḷakkal ākā+ perumai y-um arumai y-um
$aruṅkakalam uṭaimai y-um ēṟaṟku* arumai y-um
porunta+ kūṟupa poccāppu* iṉṟi.'

`nilattiṉ iyalpu* ē niṉaikkum^ kālai+
poṟai y-uṭaimaiyoṭu $ceypāṅku* amaintapiṉ
viḷaital vaṇmai y-um pōy+ cārntōrai
y-iṭutal um eṭuttal um $iṉṉaṇam āka
v-iyaiya+ kūṟupa v-iyalpu* uṇarntōr ē.'

`pūviṉatu* iyalpu ē porunta+ kūṟiṉ
maṅkalam ātal um* nāṟṟam uṭaimai y-um^
kālattiṉ malartal um vaṇṭiṟku ñekiḻtal um
kaṇṭōr uvattal um viḻaiya+ paṭutal um
uvamattu* iyalpiṉ uṇara+ kāṭṭupa.'

`tulā+kōl iyalpē tūkkum^ kālai
mikiṉum^ kuṟaiyiṉum* nillātu* ākal um
aiyam tīrttal um* naṭuvunilaimaiyōṭu*
eyta+ kūṟupa v-iyalpu* uṇarntōr ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉa nāṉku* um^ kaṇṭukoḷka.

iṉi+ kaṟkappaṭātōr um nāṉku tiṟattār.

{{S CIT}}
`kaḻal+ pey kuṭam ē maṭal+paṉai muṭa+teṅku
kuṇṭikai+ paruttiyōṭu* ivai y-eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉuḷ+ kaḻal+pey-kuṭam āvatu koḷvōṉ uṇarvu ciṟitu* āyiṉum^ tāṉ kaṟṟatu* ellām oruṅku* uraittal. maṭal+paṉai y-eṉpatu piṟarāṟ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyatu* āki iṉitu* ākiya payaṉkaḷai+ koṇṭiruttal. muṭa+teṅku* eṉpatu oruvar nīr vārkka+ piṟarkku+ payaṉ-paṭuvatu pōla oruvar vaḻi-paṭa+ piṟarkku uraittal. kuṇṭikai+ parutti y-eṉpatu coriyiṉum vīḻātu ciṟitu-ciṟitāka vāṅka+ $koṭukkumatu pōla+ koḷvōṉ uṇarvu peritu* āyiṉum^ ciṟitu-ciṟitāka+ kūṟutal.

iṉi,

{{S CIT}}
`ītal iyalpu* ē y-iyalp-uṟa+ kiḷappiṉ+
poḻippu* ē y-akalam* nuṭpam eccam eṉa+
paḻippu* il pal=urai payiṉṟa nāviṉaṉ
pukaḻnta matiyiṉ+ poruntum $ōraiyiṉ+
tikaḻnta v-aṟiviṉaṉ teyvam vāḻtti+
koḷvōṉ uṇar-vakai y-aṟintavaṉ koḷvara+
koṭuttal% marapu* eṉa+ kūṟiṉar pulavar.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

iṉi+ koḷvōr um^ kaṟpikkappaṭuvōr um^ kaṟpikkappaṭātōr um eṉa iru-vakaiyar. avaruḷ+ kaṟpikkappaṭuvōr aṟu-vakaiyar.

avar tām,

{{S CIT}}
`taṉ makaṉ ācāṉ makaṉ ē $maṉmakaṉ
poruḷ naṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻi-paṭuvōṉ ē
y-$uraikōḷāḷaṉōṭu* ivar eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} ivar taṉmai,

{{S CIT}}
`aṉṉam^ kiḷi y-ē $naṉṉiṟa $neyyari
yāṉai $yāṉē $ṟeṉṟivai pōla+
kūṟi+ koḷpa kuṇam* māṇṭōr ē.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

iṉi+ kaṟpikkappaṭātōr eṇvakaiyar.

{{S CIT}}
`maṭi māṉi poccāppaṉ kāmukaṉ kaḷvaṉ
aṭunōy+ piṇiyāḷaṉ āṟā+ ciṉattaṉ
taṭumāṟu neñcattavaṉ uḷḷiṭṭu* eṇmar
neṭunūlai+ kaṟkalātār.'

{{S NAC}} eṉa ivar.

ivar taṉmai,

{{S CIT}}
`$kuraṅkeṟi $viḷaṅkā $yerumai $yāṭē
$tōṇi y-eṉṟāṅku* ivai y-eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

ivaruḷ+ kaḷam^ kaṭiyappaṭṭār:

{{S CIT}}
`moḻivatu* uṇarātār muṉṉiruntu kāyvār
paṭiṟu pala v-uraippār pal-kāl nakuvār
tiritaru neñcattār tīyavai y-ōrppār
kaṭiyappaṭṭār avaiyiṉ kaṇ.'

{{S NAC}} iṉi+ kōṭaṉ-marapu:

{{S CIT}}
`kōṭaṉ marapu kūṟum^ kālai+
poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭaṉ muṉiyāṉ
muṉ=um piṉ=um iraviṉ-um pakaliṉ-um
akalāṉ āki y-aṉpoṭu keḻīi+
kuṇattoṭu paḻaki+ kuṟippiṉ vaḻi-niṉṟu*
ācaṟa v-uṇarntōṉ vā v-eṉa vantāṅku*
iru v-eṉa v-iruntu* ēṭu* aviḻ eṉa v-aviḻttu+
col=eṉa+ colli+ cel=eṉa+ ceṉṟu
parukuvaṉ aṉṉa v-ārvattaṉ āki+
cittira+ pāvaiyiṉ $attaka v-aṭaṅki+
cevi vāy āka neñcu kaḷaṉ āka+
kēṭṭavai kēṭṭavai vallaṉ āki+
pōṟṟi+ kōṭal ataṉatu paṇpu* ē.'

`ettiṟam ācāṉ uvakkum attiṟam
aṟattiṉ+ tiriyā+ $paṭarcci vaḻipāṭu* ē'

`cevvaṉ terikiṟpāṉ mey+nōkki+ kāṇkiṟpāṉ
pal=urai y-um^ kēṭpāṉ mika+peritu* um^ kātalāṉ
teyvattai+ pōla matippāṉ tiripu* illāṉ
iv=āṟu māṇpu* um uṭaiyāṟku* uraippa v-ē
cevvitiṉ* nūlai+ terintu.'

`vaḻakkiṉ ilakkaṇam iḻukku* iṉṟu* aṟital
pāṭam pōṟṟal kēṭṭavai niṉaittal
ācāṉ+ cārntavai y-amaivara+ kēṭṭal
a+māṇpu* uṭaiyōr tammoṭu payiṟal
viṉātal viṉāyavai viṭuttal eṉṟiṉṉavai
kaṭaṉ ā+ koḷiṉ ē maṭa naṉi y-ikakkum.'

`aṉaiyaṉ allōṉ kēṭkuvaṉ āyiṉ
viṉaiyiṉ uḻappoṭu payaṉ talai+paṭāaṉ.'

`aṉaiyaṉ allōṉ a+marapu* illōṉ
kēṭkuvaṉ āyiṉ+ koḷvōṉ* nallaṉ.'

{{S NAC}} ivaṟṟāṉ uṇarka.

i+māṇākkaṉ muṟṟa uṇarntāṉ ām āṟu,

{{S CIT}}
`oru kuṟi kēṭpōṉ iru-kāl+ kēṭpiṉ+
peruka nūliṟ piḻaipāṭu* ilaṉ ē'

`mu+kāṟ kēṭpiṉ muṟai y-aṟintu* uraikkum.'

`ācāṉ uraittatu* amaivara+ koḷiṉum^
$kāṟkūṟu* allatu paṟṟalaṉ ākum.'

`av viṉaiyāḷaroṭu payil vakai y-orupāl+
cevvitiṉ uraippa v-av=iru pāl-um
aiyaṟu pulamai māṇpu naṉi y-uṭaittu* ē.'

`piṟarkku* urai y-iṭattē nūl+kalappu* ākum^
tiṟa+paṭa v-uṇarum^ teḷiviṉōrkku* ē.'

{{S NAC}} ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika.

potu+pāyiram muṟṟiṟṟu.

iṉi+ ciṟappuppāyiram āvatu taṉṉāl uraikkappaṭum nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. atu patiṉoru-vakai y-ām.

`ākkiyōṉ peyar-ē vaḻi y-ē y-ellai
nūṟ-peyar yāppu* ē nutaliya poruḷ ē
kēṭpōr payaṉōṭu* āy eṇ poruḷ um
vāyppa+ kāṭṭal pāyirattu iyalpu* ē.'

`kālam^ kaḷaṉ ē kāraṇam eṉṟu* i+
mū-vakai y-ēṟṟi moḻinar um uḷar-ē.'

i+patiṉoṉṟu* um i+pāyirattu* uḷ=ē peṟappaṭṭaṉa.

iṉi+ ciṟappu+pāyirattu* ilakkaṇam^ ceppum-āṟu,

`pāyirattu* ilakkaṇam pakarum^ kālai
nūl* nutal poruḷai+ taṉ=akattu* aṭakki
y-āciriyamāṉ um veṇpāvāṉ um*
$maruviya vakaiyāṉ* nuvaṟal vēṇṭum.'

itaṉāṉ aṟika.

nūlceytāṉ pāyiram^ ceytāṉ āyiṉ+ taṉṉai+ pukaḻntāṉ ām.

`tōṉṟā tōṟṟi+ tuṟai pala muṭippiṉum^
tāṉ taṟ pukaḻtal* takuti y-aṉṟu* ē.'

eṉpa v-ākaliṉ.

pāyirañ-ceyvār taṉ āciriyar-um^ taṉṉoṭu oruṅku-kaṟṟa oru-cālai māṇākkar-um^ taṉ māṇākkar-um-eṉa ivar. avar-uḷ i+nūṟku+ pāyiram^ ceytār tamakku oru-cālai māṇākkar-ākiya paṉampāraṉār.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṭa-vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ-kumari āy-iṭai --- vaṭakkiṉkaṇ vēṅkaṭam-um^ teṟkiṉkaṇ+ kumari y-um ākiya av= iraṇṭu* ellaikku* uḷ=iruntu, tamiḻ kūṟum nal ulakattu vaḻakku* um^ ceyyuḷ um āy iru mutaliṉ --- tamiḻai+ collum nal=āciriyaratu vaḻakku*um^ ceyyuḷ-um-ākiya av=iraṇṭai y-um aṭi y-āka+ koḷḷukaiyiṉāl-ē, cen-tamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntu-nūl kaṇṭu - avar kūṟum^ centamiḻ iyalpu* āka+ poruntiya centamiḻ-nāṭṭiṟku iyainta vaḻakkōṭu* ē muṉṉai y-ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ iyainta paṭiyai muṟṟa+kaṇṭu, muṟai+paṭa eṇṇi --- av= ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ellām^ cil vāḻ-nāḷ+ pal-piṇi+ ciṟṟu* aṟiviṉōrkku aṟiyal-ākāmaiyiṉ yāṉ ittuṇai varai y-aṟuttu uṇarttuval eṉṟu a+ nūlkaḷiṉ+ kiṭanta v-āṟu* aṉṟi atikāram* muṟaiyāṉ muṟaimai+paṭa+ ceytalai eṇṇi, eḻuttu* um^ col=um poruḷ-um nāṭi --- avvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uḷ eḻuttiṉai y-um^ colliṉai y-um poruḷiṉai y-um ārāyntu, pōkku aṟu paṉuval --- pattu-vakai+ kuṟṟam-um^ tīrntu muppattiraṇṭu vakai uttiyoṭu puṇarnta i+nūl uḷ=ē, mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu-muṟai kāṭṭi+ pulan-tokuttōṉ-ē --- a+mū-vakai y-ilakkaṇam-um mayaṅkā muṟaimaiyāṉ+ ceykiṉṟamaiyiṉ eḻuttu* ilakkaṇattai muṉṉar+ kāṭṭi+ piṉṉar ēṉai y-ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai y-um^ tokuttu+ kūṟiṉāṉ, nilan-taru tiruviṉ+ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu --- māṟṟāratu nilattai+ koḷḷum pōr+ tiruviṉai y-uṭaiya pāṇṭiyaṉ mā-kīrtti avaiyiṉkaṇ=ē, aṟaṅ-karai-nāviṉ nāṉ-maṟai muṟṟiya ataṅkōṭṭu* ācāṟku aril tapa+terintu --- aṟam-ē kūṟum nāviṉai y-uṭaiya nāṉku vētattiṉai y-um muṟṟa aṟinta ataṅkōṭu* eṉkiṟa ūriṉ āciriyaṉukku+ kuṟṟam-aṟa ārāyntu-kūṟi, malku-nīr-varaippiṉ aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyar tōṟṟi --- kaṭal-cūḻnta ulakiṉ-kaṇ=ē aintira-viyākaraṇattai niṟaiya aṟinta paḻaiya kāppiya+ kuṭiyiṉ-uḷḷōṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyarai māyāmal niṟutti, pal-pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimaiyōṉ ē --- pala pukaḻkaḷai y-um iv=ulakiṉ-kaṇ=ē māyāmal niṟuttiya tava vēṭattai y-uṭaiyōṉ; eṉṟavāṟu.

iruntu tamiḻai+ collum eṉa+ koḷḷukaiyiṉāl ē poruntiya nāṭu eṉa+ kaṇṭu eṇṇi ārāyntu i+ nūl-uḷ=ē tokuttāṉ, avaṉ yār eṉiṉ avaiyiṉ-kaṇ=ē kūṟi ulakiṉ kaṇ=ē taṉ peyarai niṟutti+ pukaḻai niṟuttiya paṭimaiyōṉ eṉka.

i+pāyiram-um^ ceyyuḷ-ātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam $māṭṭuṟuppu nikaḻa+ kūṟiṉār. itaṟku iṅṅaṉam^ kaṇ=aḻittal uraiyāciriyar karuttu* eṉpatu avar ururaiyāṉ uṇarka.

iṉi maṅkala-marapiṉ+ kāriyañ-ceyvār vaṭakku* um^ kiḻakku* um nōkki y-um^ cintittu* um naṟ-karumaṅkaḷ ceyvār ātaliṉ maṅkalam-ākiya vaṭa-ticaiyai muṟkūṟiṉār, i+nūl niṉṟu nilavutal vēṇṭi. teṉpulattārkku vēṇṭuvaṉa ceyvār teṟku* um mēṟku* um nōkki y-um^ karumaṅkaḷ ceyvār ātaliṉ teṉ ticaiyai+ piṟkūṟiṉār. nilaṅ-kaṭanta neṭu-muṭi y-aṇṇalai nōkki ulakam^ tavañ-ceytu vīṭu-peṟṟa malai y-ātalāṉ-um ellārāṉ-um aṟiya+paṭutalāṉ-um vēṅkaṭattai ellai y-āka+ kūṟiṉār. kumari y-um^ tīrttam ākaliṉ ellai y-āka+ kūṟiṉār. iv= iraṇṭiṉai y-um^ kālai y-ē ōtuvārkku nal-viṉai y-uṇṭām eṉṟu karuti ivaṟṟai y-ē kūṟiṉār. ivai y-iraṇṭu* um $akappāṭṭu* ellai y-āyiṉa. eṉṉai? kumari y-āṟṟiṉ teṟku nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭu kaṭalkoṇṭatu* ākaliṉ. kiḻakku* um mēṟku* um^ kaṭal ellai y-āka muṭitaliṉ vēṟu* ellai kūṟār-āyiṉār. vēṅkaṭam-um^ kumari y-um yāṇṭaiya eṉṟāl vaṭa-vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ-kumari y-eṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ ataṉai viḷaṅka+ kūṟiṉār.

ulakam-eṉṟatu pala-poruḷ-oru-col=ākaliṉ īṇṭu uyarntōrai y-uṇarttiṟṟu, ulakam avarai y-ē kaṇ=āka v-uṭaimaiyiṉ. eṉṉai?

`vaḻakku* eṉa+ paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟu* ē
nikaḻcci avar-kaṭṭu* ākalāṉa.' ((marapiyal//92))

eṉa marapu* iyal-uḷ+ kūṟutaliṉ. av=uyarntōr-āvār akattiyaṉār-um mārkkaṇṭēyar-um^ talai+caṅkattārum mutalāyiṉōr. ulakattu ulakattiṉ-uṭaiya eṉa virikka.

vaḻakku* āvatu cila-coṟ piṟanta a+ kālattu iḵtu aṟattai uṇarttiṟṟu, itu poruḷai uṇarttiṟṟu, iḵtu iṉpattai uṇarttiṟṟu, itu vīṭṭai uṇarttiṟṟu eṉṟu uṇarvippatu.

ceyyuḷ āvatu `pāṭṭurainūlē' ((ceyyu//79)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya ēḻu nilam um aṟam* mutaliya mūṉṟu poruḷ um payappa nikaḻvatu. mutaliṉ eṉṟatu $mutalukaiyiṉālē eṉṟavāṟu.

eḻuttu* eṉṟatu yātaṉai y-eṉiṉ, kaṭpulaṉ ākā uru v-um^ kaṭpulaṉ ākiya vaṭivu* um uṭaittu* āka vēṟu-vēṟu vakuttu+koṇṭu taṉṉai y-ē uṇartti y-um^ coṟku iyaintu* um niṟkum ōcaiyai y-ām. kaṭal-oli caṅk-oli mutaliya ōcaikaḷ poruḷ uṇarttāmaiyāṉ um $muṟku $vīḷai $ilatai mutaliyaṉa poruḷuṇarttiṉa v-ēṉum eḻuttu* ākāmaiyāṉ um avai īṇṭu+ koḷḷār āyiṉar. īṇṭu uru v-eṉṟatu maṉaṉ uṇarvu* āy niṟkum^ karuttu+ poruḷai. atu ceṟippa+ cēṟalāṉ um^ ceṟippa varutalāṉ um iṭaiyeṟiyappaṭutalāṉ um iṉpam* tuṉpattai y-ākkalāṉ um uru v-um uru v-um^ kūṭi+ piṟattalāṉ um unti mutal āka+ tōṉṟi eṇ vakai nilattu* um piṟantu kaṭpulaṉ ām^ taṉmai y-iṉṟi+ cevikkaṇ+ ceṉṟu uṟum ūṟu* uṭaimaiyāṉ um vicumpiṟ piṟantu iyaṅkuvatu* ōr taṉmai y-uṭaimaiyāṉ um^ kāṟṟiṉ kuṇam āvatu* ōr uru v-ām. vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai kōṭalāṉ um uru v-ē y-āyiṟṟu. itaṉai+ kāṟṟiṉ kuṇam ē y-eṉṟal iv= āciriyar karuttu. itaṉai vicumpiṉ kuṇam eṉpār um uḷar. iv= uru `uru v-uru v-āki' ((eḻu//17)) eṉa v-um `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uru v-ākummē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉa v-um^ kāṭci+ poruṭku* um^ ciṟupāṉmai varum. vaṭivu* āvatu kaṇ+pulaṉ āki y-ē niṟkum. atu vaṭṭam^ caturam mutaliya muppattiraṇṭaṉ uḷ oṉṟai uṇarttum. maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇ= uṇarvu* illōr um uṇartaṟku eḻuttukkaṭku vēṟu vēṟu vaṭivam^ kāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṟ kaṇ+pulaṉ ākiya vari-vaṭivu* um uṭaiya v-āyiṉa. itaṟku viti `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uru v-ākum= ē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉṉum^ cūttiram mutaliyaṉa v-ām. ivaṟṟāṟ perumpāṉmai meykku* ē vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. `ekara okarattu iyaṟkai y-um aṟṟu* ē' ((eḻu//16)) eṉa uyirkku* um^ ciṟupāṉmai vaṭivu kūṟiṉār.

iṉi+ taṉṉai uṇarttum ōcai y-āvatu taṉ piṟappai y-um māttiraiyai y-um ē aṟivittu+ taṉṉai+ peṟa nikaḻum ōcai.

coṟku iyaiyum ōcai y-āvatu {ōr eḻuttu* oru moḻi} mutaliya v-āy varum ōcai.

akattiyaṉār ataṅkōṭṭāciriyarai nōkki `nīr tolkāppiyaṉ ceyta nūlai+ kēḷaṟka' v-eṉṟu kūṟutalāṉ um, tolkāppiyaṉār um pal-kāl-um^ ceṉṟu `yāṉ ceyta nūlai nīr kēṭṭal vēṇṭum' eṉṟu kūṟutalāṉ um, iv= iruvar-um vekuḷāmal i+ nūṟku+ kuṟṟam^ kūṟi viṭuval eṉa+ karuti avar kūṟiya kaṭāviṟku* ellām viṭai kūṟutaliṉ `aril* ^tapa+ terintu' eṉṟār.

avar kēḷaṉmiṉ eṉṟaṟku+ kāraṇam eṉṉai y-eṉiṉ, tēvar ellārum^ kūṭi yām^ cēra v-iruttaliṉ mēru+ tāḻntu teṉ ^ticai uyarntatu, itaṟku akattiyaṉār ē āṇṭiruttaṟ kuriyar eṉṟu avarai vēṇṭi+koḷḷa, avar um^ teṉ ^ticaikkaṇ+ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅkai-yāru* uḻai+ ceṉṟu kāviriyārai vāṅki+koṇṭu, piṉṉar yamatakkiṉiyār uḻai+ ceṉṟu avar makaṉār tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārai vāṅki+koṇṭu, pulattiyaṉār uḻai+ceṉṟu avar uṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār ulōpā muttiraiyārai avar koṭuppa nīr ēṟṟu irīi+ peyarntu, tuvarāpati+ pōntu nilam^ kaṭanta neṭu muṭi y-aṇṇal vaḻi+kaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmarai y-um patiṉeṇ-kōṭi vēḷir uḷ= iṭṭārai y-um aruvāḷarai y-um^ koṇṭu-pōntu, kāṭu keṭuttu nāṭu* ākki+ potiyiṉ-kaṇ iruntu, irāvaṇaṉai+ kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatarai āṇṭu iyaṅkāmai vilakki+ tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyār ākiya tolkāppiyaṉārai nōkki, `nī ceṉṟu kumariyārai+ koṇṭu varuka' v-eṉa+ kūṟa avar um em perumāṭṭiyai eṅṅaṉam^ koṇṭu varuval eṉṟārkku `muṉṉāka+ piṉṉāka nāṟ kōl nīḷam akala niṉṟu koṇṭu varuka v-eṉa avar-um aṅṅaṉam^ koṇṭu varu-vaḻi, vaiyai nīr kaṭuki+ kumariyārai īrttu+ koṇṭu pōka+, tolkāppiyaṉār kaṭṭaḷai y-iṟantu ceṉṟu ōr $vetirṅ-kōlai muṟittu nīṭṭa, atu paṟṟi y-ēṟiṉār; atu kuṟṟam eṉṟu akattiyaṉār kumariyārai y-um^ tolkāppiyaṉārai y-um^ `cuvarkkam pukāppir' eṉa+ capittār; `yāṅkaḷ oru kuṟṟam um^ ceyyātu* irukka eṅkaḷai+ capittamaiyāṉ emperumāṉ um^ cuvarkkam pukāppir' eṉa avar akattiyaṉārai+ capittār. ataṉāṉ avar vekuṇṭār ātaliṉ avaṉ ceyta nūlai+ kēḷaṟka v-eṉṟār eṉka.

nāṉku kūṟu* um āy maṟainta poruḷ um uṭaimaiyāṉ `nāṉ maṟai' y-eṉṟār. avai taittiriyam um pauṭikam um^ $talavakāram um^ cāma-vētam um ām. iṉi irukkum yacu v-um cāmam um atarvaṇam um eṉpār um uḷar. atu poruntātu; ivar i+ nūl ceyta-piṉṉar vēta-viyātar cil% ^nāḷ+ pal piṇi+ ciṟṟu* aṟiviṉōr uṇartaṟku nāṉku kūṟu āka ivaṟṟai+ ceytār ātaliṉ.

vaṭa vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ kumari y-eṉa v-ē ellai y-um, eḻuttu* um^ col= um poruḷ um nāṭi y-eṉa v-ē nutaliya poruḷ um payaṉ um yāppu um, muntu-nūl kaṇṭu* eṉa v-ē vaḻi y-um, muṟai+paṭa v-eṇṇi y-eṉa v-ē kāraṇam-um, pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu eṉa v-ē kālam um^ kaḷaṉ um, aril* ^tapa+ terintu* eṉa v-ē kēṭṭōr um, taṉ peyar tōṟṟi y-eṉa v-ē ākkiyōṉ peyar um nūl+ peyar um peṟa+paṭṭaṉa.

((1. nūṉmarapu))

eḻuttu* eṉa+paṭupa
akara mutal*
ṉakara v-iṟuvāy muppaḵtu* eṉpa
cārntu varal% marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alam^ kaṭai y-ē.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

iv= atikāram eṉṉa peyarttu* ō v-eṉiṉ eḻuttu* ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamai kāraṇattāṉ eḻuttu* atikāram eṉṉum peyarttu. eḻuttai uṇarttiya atikāram eṉa virikka. atikāram --- muṟaimai.

eḻuttu uṇarttum iṭattu eṉaittu vakaiyāṉ uṇarttiṉār ō v-eṉiṉ eṭṭu vakaiyāṉ um eṭṭu* iṟanta pala vakaiyāṉ um uṇarttiṉār eṉka.

eṭṭu vakaiya v-eṉpār kūṟum-āṟu: --- eḻuttu iṉaittu* eṉṟal um iṉṉa peyariṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa muṟaiyiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa aḷaviṉa v-eṉṟalum iṉṉa piṟappiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa puṇarcciyiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa taṉmaiyiṉa v-eṉṟal um ām. ivaṟṟuḷ taṉmai y-um vaṭivu* um namakku uṇarttal ākāmaiyiṉ āciriyar īṇṭu uraittilar. ēṉaiya itaṉuḷ+ peṟutum.

eḻuttu iṉaittu* eṉṟalai+ tokai vakai viriyāṉ uṇarka. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu tokai. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭu* um uṭampu patiṉeṭṭu* um^ cārpiṉ+ ^tōṟṟam mūṉṟu* um ataṉ vakai. aḷapeṭai y-ēḻu* um uyirmey y-irunūṟṟorupattāṟu* um avaṟṟōṭu* um^ kūṭṭi irunūṟṟaimpattāṟu* eṉal viri.

iṉi eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟaiy um^ tokai y-um i+ cūttirattāṉ+ peṟṟām. vakai `aukāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//8)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//9)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avai tām^, kuṟṟiyalikaram^ kuṟṟiyalukaram' ((eḻu//2)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām. viri `kuṉṟicai moḻi vayiṉ' ((eḻu//41)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām.

aḷavu `avaṟṟuḷ, a i u' ((eḻu//3)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `ā ī ū' ((eḻu//4)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē' ((eḻu//11)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avviyal* ^nilaiyum' ((eḻu//12)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām.

piṟappu piṟappiyal uḷ+ peṟṟām.

puṇarcci `uyir iṟu col% muṉ' ((eḻu//107)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avaṟṟu, ṇiṟutta colliṉ' ((eḻu//108)) eṉpataṉāṉ um piṟavāṟṟāṉ um peṟṟām.

iṉi eṭṭu* iṟanta pal vakaiya v-eṉpār kūṟum āṟu: --- eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟaivu um^ kūṭṭam um pirivu* um mayakkam um moḻi y-ākkam um nilai y-um iṉam um oṉṟu pala v-ātal um^ tirintataṉ ^tiripu atu v-eṉṟal um piṟitu* eṉṟal um atu v-um piṟitu* um eṉṟal um nilaiyiṟṟu* eṉṟal um nilaiyātu* eṉṟal um nilaiyiṟṟu* um nilaiyātu* um eṉṟal um iṉṉōraṉṉa pala v-um ām.

kūṭṭam `meyyōṭu* iyaiyiṉum' ((eḻu//10)) `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟṟām.

nilai `paṉṉīr uyir um' ((eḻu//59)) `uyirmey= allaṉa' ((eḻu//90)) `uyir au' ((eḻu//69)) `ña ṇa na ma ṉa' ((eḻu//78)) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟāṉ moḻikku mutal ām eḻuttu* um īṟu* ām eḻuttu* um peṟṟām.

iṉam `val= eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//19)) `mel= eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//20)) `iṭai y-eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//21)) `aukāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//8)) `ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//9)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟṟām. iv= uruvu* ākiya ōcaikku āciriyar vaṭivu kūṟāmai uṇarka. iṉi vari-vaṭivu kūṟuṅkāl meykku* ē perumpāṉmai y-um vaṭivu kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.

nilaiyātu* eṉṟal nilaimoḻiyatu īṟṟukkaṇ ^niṉṟum varumoḻiyatu mutaṟkaṇ ^niṉṟum puṇarcci tam= uḷ ilavātal. atu `maruviṉ ^tokuti' ((eḻu//111)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

nilaiyiṟṟu* um nilaiyātu* um eṉṟal `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar um' ((eḻu//226)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya akaram `irā v-eṉ kiḷavikku* akaram illai' ((eḻu//227)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

i+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ karuvi y-um^ ceykai y-um eṉa iru-vakaiya.

avaṟṟu* uḷ+ karuvi puṟa+puṟa+ karuvi y-um puṟa+karuvi y-um akappuṟa+ karuvi y-um aka+karuvi y-um eṉa nāl-vakaittu. nūṉ-marapu* um piṟappu*iyal um puṟa+puṟa+ karuvi. moḻi-marapu puṟa+karuvi. puṇar iyal aka+puṟa+karuvi. `ekara okaram peyarkku* īṟu* ākā' ((eḻu//272)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa aka+karuvi.

iṉi+ ceykai y-um puṟa+puṟa+ceykai y-um puṟa+ceykai y-um aka+puṟa+ ceykai y-um aka+ceykai y-um eṉa nāl-vakaittu. `ellā moḻikku* um uyir varu-vaḻi y-ē'(eḻu//140) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa puṟa+puṟa+ceykai. `la ṉa v-eṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar' ((eḻu//149)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa puṟa+ceykai. `ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi y-iṟuti' ((eḻu//163)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa aka+puṟa+ceykai. tokai marapu mutaliya ōttiṉ uḷ iṉṉa īṟu iṉṉa v-āṟu muṭiyum eṉa+ ceykai kūṟuvaṉa v-ellām aka+ceykai. iv vikaṟpam ellām^ tokai y-āka uṇarka.

iv= ōttu* eṉ ^nutaliṟṟu* ō v-eṉiṉ, atu v-um ataṉ peyar uraippa v-ē aṭaṅkum.

iv= ōttu* eṉṉa peyarttu* ō v-eṉiṉ i+ tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūṟku marapu* ām^ tuṇaikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai+ tokuttu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ nūṉ-marapu* eṉṉum peyarttu* āyiṟṟu.

nūl eṉṟatu nūl pōṟaliṉ oppiṉ āyatōr āku-peyar ām. av= oppu* āya v-āṟu* eṉṉai y-eṉiṉ, kuṟṟam^ kaḷaintu eḵkiya $paṉṉuṉai+ pañcikaḷai y-ellām^ kai val% makaṭūu+ tūymai y-um nuṇmai y-um uṭaiya v-āka ōr iḻai+ paṭuttiṉāl+pōla `viṉaiyiṉ* ^nīṅki viḷaṅkiya v-aṟivaṉāl' ((marapiyal//94)) ē vaḻu+kaḷaintu eḵkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai y-ellām mutal um muṭivu* um māṟukōḷ iṉṟu* āka v-um^, tokaiyiṉ um vakaiyiṉ um poruṇmai kāṭṭi y-um, urai y-um^ kāṇṭikai y-um uḷ niṉṟu akala v-um, īr aim^ kuṟṟam um iṉṟi īr aintu* aḻaku peṟa, muppattiraṇṭu tantira v-uttiyōṭu puṇara v-um,

`oru poruḷ* ^nutaliya cūttirattāṉ um
iṉa moḻi kiḷanta v-ōttiṉāṉ um
potu moḻi kiḷanta paṭalattāṉ um*
mūṉṟu* uṟuppu* aṭakkiya piṇṭattāṉ um' ((cey//168))

oru-neṟi+paṭa+ puṇarkka+paṭūum^ taṉmai y-uṭaimaiyāṉ eṉka. marapu, ilakkaṇam, muṟaimai, taṉmai eṉpaṉa oru-poruḷ+kiḷavi.

āyiṉ nūl eṉṟatu īṇṭu mūṉṟu* atikārattiṉai y-um aṉṟu* ē? iv=ōttu mūṉṟu* atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam āya v-āṟu* eṉṉai y-eṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟai y-um iv= atikārattiṟku* um^ ceyyuḷ iyaṟku* um oppa+ kūṟiyatu. īṇṭu+ kūṟiya muppattumūṉṟaṉai+ patiṉaintu* ākki āṇṭu+ tokai kōṭalil tokai vēṟu* ām. aḷavu ceyyuḷ iyaṟku* um iv= atikārattiṟku* um otta aḷavu* um ovvā aḷavu* um uḷavāka+ kūṟiyatu. kuṟiṟku* um neṭiṟku* um^ kūṟiya māttirai iraṇṭu* iṭattiṟku* um otta aḷavu. āṇṭu+ kūṟum^ ceyyuṭku aḷavu kōṭaṟku īṇṭaikku+ payaṉ tārāta aḷapeṭai kūṟiyatu ovvā aḷavu. aḵtu `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattōṭu āṇṭu māṭṭeṟiyum-āṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉṉum^ kuṟil um neṭil um mū-vakai y-iṉam um āytam um vaṇṇattiṟku* um iv= atikārattiṟku* um oppa+ kūṟiyaṉa. kuṟaivu* um iraṇṭaṟku* um okkum. kūṭṭam um pirivu* um mayakkam um iv= atikārattiṟku* ē uriyaṉa v-āka+ kūṟiyaṉa. `a+ mū v-āṟum' ((eḻu//22)) eṉṉum^ cūttira mutaliyaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭi+ col= ām āṟu kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ+ col= atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār āyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu* atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam^ kūṟutaliṉ iv= ōttu nūliṉatu ilakkaṇam^ kūṟiyatu* āyiṟṟu. nūl eṉṟatu tolkāppiyam eṉṉum piṇṭattai. iv= ōttilakkaṇaṅkaḷ* ^tām eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟai y-um^ tokai y-um aḷavu* um^ kuṟaivu* um^ kūṭṭam um iṉam um mayakkam um ām. ēṉaiya iv= atikārattu* uḷ ēṉai y-ōttukkaḷ uḷ uṇarttupa.

aṟṟu* ēl aḵtu* āka, i+ talai+cūttiram eṉ ^nutaliṟṟu* ō v-eṉiṉ eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟai y-um tokai y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuttu* eṉa+paṭupa --- eḻuttu* eṉṟu ciṟappittu+ colla+paṭuvaṉa, akaram* mutal ṉakaram* v-iṟuvāy muppaḵtu* eṉpa --- akaram mutal ṉakaram īṟu* āka+ kiṭanta muppatu* eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭai y-ē --- cārntu varutalai+ tamakku ilakkaṇam āka v-uṭaiya mūṉṟu* um allāta iṭattu; eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, a+ mūṉṟu* um^ kūṭiya vaḻi muppattu mūṉṟu* eṉpa. a-ā-i-ī-u-ū-e-ē-ai-o-ō-au-k-ṅ-c-ñ-ṭ-ṇ-t-n-p-m-y-r-l-v-ḻ-ḷ-ṟ-ṉ eṉa varum. eṉa+paṭuva v-eṉṟu ciṟappittu* uṇarttutalāṉ aḷapeṭai y-um uyirmey= um i+tuṇai+ ciṟappu* ila; ocai y-uṇarvārkku+ karuvi y-ākiya vari vaṭivu* um^ ciṟappu* ilā eḻuttu* āka+ koḷḷa+paṭum.

akaram mutal ātal āriyattiṟku* um okkum ēṉum īṇṭu+ tamiḻ eḻuttu* ē kūṟukiṉṟār eṉpatu uṇartaṟku ṉakara v-iṟuvāy eṉṟār.

paṭupa, paṭuva. paṭupa v-eṉpatu paṭuttal-ōcaiyāl toḻil+peyar āka+ kūṟa+paṭum. pakaram um vakaram um īṇṭu niṟṟaṟku+ tam= uḷ otta urimaiya v-ēṉum eḻuttu* eṉa+paṭupa v-eṉa+ $tūkkaṟṟu niṟkum^ col+cīr aṭikku+ paṭupa v-eṉpatu iṉ=ōcaittu* āy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu+ paṭupa v-eṉṟu* ē pāṭam ōtuka. iḵtu aṉ peṟāta akara v-īṟṟu+ pala v-aṟi col.

akara ṉakaram eṉa v-ē peyar um^ kūṟiṉār.

eḻuttukkaṭku* ellām akaram mutal ātaṟku+ kāraṇam `meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//46)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟupa. vīṭu pēṟṟiṟku uriya āṇmakaṉai uṇarttum^ ciṟappāṉ ṉakaram piṉ vaittār. iṉi eḻuttukkaṭku* um^ kiṭakkai muṟai y-āyiṉa v-āṟu kūṟutum.

kuṟṟu* eḻuttukkaḷai muṉ= āka+ kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉam otta neṭṭu* eḻuttukkaḷai avaṟṟiṉ piṉ= āka+ kūṟiṉār. oru māttirai kūṟi y-ē iraṇṭu māttirai kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ. aṉṟi iraṇṭai muṉ+ kūṟiṉ āl ō v-eṉiṉ, ākātu; oṉṟu niṉṟu ataṉōṭu piṉṉarum oṉṟu kūṭi y-ē iraṇṭu* āvatu* aṉṟi iraṇṭu* eṉpatu* oṉṟu iṉṟu* ātaliṉ. itaṉāṉ oṉṟu tāṉ pala kūṭi y-ē eṇ virintatu* eṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaram eṇṇum piṟappu* um poruḷ um ottaliṉ vaittār. ikarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaram vaittār, piṟappu ovvātu* ēṉum `a-i-u a+ mūṉṟu* um^ cuṭṭu' ((eḻu//31)) eṉa+ cuṭṭu+poruṭṭu* āy niṟkiṉṟa iṉam^ karuti. avai aim pāl+ kaṇ= um perum-pāṉmai varum-āṟu uṇarka. ekaram ataṉ piṉ vaittār, akara ikaraṅkaḷōṭu piṟappu oppumai paṟṟi. aikāra aukāraṅkaṭku iṉam ākiya kuṟṟu* eḻuttu iṉṟu* ēṉum piṟappu oppumai paṟṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāra aukāram vaittār. okaram no eṉa meyyōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu* allatu tāṉ āka ōr eḻuttu* oru moḻi y-ākā ciṟappu* iṉmai nōkki aikārattiṉ piṉ vaittār. a-i-u-e eṉṉum nāṉku* um a+koṟṟaṉ i+koṟṟaṉ u+koṟṟaṉ e+koṟṟaṉ eṉa meyyōṭu kūṭāmal tām iṭaiccol= āy niṉṟu* āyiṉum mēl varum peyarkaḷōṭu kūṭi+ cuṭṭu+ poruḷ um viṉā+ poruḷ um uṇarttum. okaram meyyōṭu kūṭi y-ē taṉ poruḷ uṇarttuvatu* allatu tāṉ āka+ poruḷ uṇarttātu* eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉṉum a-ā-u-ū-e-ē-o-ō-au eṉpaṉa tam= uḷ vaṭivu okkum. i-ī-ai tam= uḷ vaṭivu ovvā. iṉṉum ivai aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl neṭṭu* eḻuttōṭu kuṟṟu* eḻuttiṟku ōcai iyaiyum āṟṟāṉ um uṇarka. iṉi+ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu ākāra īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷ ātalāṉ um poruḷ okkum. puṇarcci oppumai uyirmayaṅkiyal uḷ+ peṟutum. i+ muṟai vaḻuvāmal mēl āḷum āṟu uṇarka.

iṉi+ kakāra ṅakāram um^ cakāra ñakāram um ṭakāra ṇakāram um^ takāra nakāram um pakāra makāram um^ tamakku+ piṟappu* um^ ceykai y-um ottaliṉ val= oṟṟu* iṭai y-ē mel= oṟṟu+ kalantu vaittār. mutal* ^nā v-um mutal aṇṇam um iṭai nā v-um iṭai y-aṇṇam um nuṉi nā v-um nuṉi y-aṇṇam um itaḻ iyaital um āki+ piṟakkiṉṟa iṭattiṉ muṟaimai nōkki av= eḻuttukkaḷai+ ka-ca-ṭa-ta-pa-ṅa-ña-ṇa-na-ma-ṉa v-eṉa i+ muṟai y-ē vaittār. piṟappu oppumaiyāṉ um ṉakāram ṟakāram āy+ tiritalāṉ um ṟakāram um ṉakāram um^ cēra vaittār. ivai tamiḻ eḻuttu* eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku+ piṉṉar vaittār. iṉi iṭai y-eḻuttukkaḷil yakāram muṉ vaittār. atuvum uyirkaḷ pōla miṭaṟṟu+ piṟanta vaḷi aṇṇaṅkaṇ= uṟṟu aṭaiya+ piṟattaliṉ. rakāram ataṉōṭu piṟappu ovvātu* ēṉum^ ceykai ottaliṉ ataṉ piṉ vaittār. lakāram um vakāram um^ tammiṉ+ piṟappu* um^ ceykai y-um ovvā v-ēṉum^ kal valitu col valitu eṉṟāl+pōla+ tammiṉ+ cērntu varum^ coṟkaḷ perum-pāṉmai y-eṉpatu paṟṟi lakāram um vakāram um^ cēra vaittār. ḻakāram um ḷakāram um oṉṟāṉ um iyaipu* ila v-ēṉum `iṭai y-eḻuttu* eṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa' ((eḻu//21)) eṉṟāl+ $canta v-iṉpattiṟku iyaipu* uṭaimai karuti+ cēra vaittār pōlum.

akaram uyir akaram um uyirmey= akaram um eṉa iraṇṭu. iḵtu ēṉai y-uyirkaṭku* um okkum. eṉa v-ē, ōr-uyir patiṉeṭṭu* āyiṟṟu.

iv= eḻuttu* eṉappaṭṭa ōcaiyai $aruvu* eṉpār aṟiyātār. ataṉai uruvu* eṉṟu* ē kōṭum. atu ceṟippa+ cēṟalāṉ um^, ceṟippa varutalāṉ um, iṭai y-eṟiya+paṭutalāṉ um^, cevikkaṇ+ ceṉṟu uṟutalāṉ um, iṉpa tuṉpattai ākkutalān um, uruvu* um uruvu* uṅ kūṭi+ piṟattalāṉ un, talai y-um miṭaṟu* um neñcu* um eṉṉum mūṉṟu* iṭattu* um nilai-peṟṟu+ pal= um itaḻ um nā v-um mūkku* um aṇṇam um uṟa+ piṟakkum eṉṟamaiyāṉ um uruvu* ē y-ām. aruvu* ē y-āyiṉ iv= iṭattiṉ+ kūṟiyaṉa iṉmai uṇarka. allatūum, vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai y-eṉṟu ōtiyamaiyāṉ um uṇarka. uṭampoṭu puṇarttal eṉṉum ilakkaṇattāṉ iv= ōcai uruvu* ātal nilaipeṟṟatu* eṉṟu uṇarka. ataṟku+ kāraṇam um muṉṉar+ kūṟiṉām.

iv= eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruviṟku vaṭivu kūṟār āyiṉār, atu muppattiraṇṭu vaṭiviṉ uḷ iṉṉa eḻuttiṟku iṉṉa vaṭivu* eṉa+ piṟarkku uṇarttutaṟku aritu* eṉpatu karuti. av vaṭivu ārāyum iṭattu+ $peṟṟapeṟṟa vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivu* ām, kuḻal akattiṉ+ kūṟiṉ+ kuḻal vaṭivum^ kuṭattu* akattiṉ+ kūṟiṉ+ kuṭa vaṭivu* um $veḷḷiṭaiyiṉ+ kūṟiṉ ellā+ ticai y-um $nīrttaraṅkamum pōla.

`ellā mey= um uruvu* uruvu* āki' ((eḻu//17)) eṉa v-um `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uruvu* ākum= ē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉa v-um `meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal' ((eḻu//15)) eṉa v-um^ ciṟupāṉmai vaṭivu* um^ kūṟuvar. atu vaṭṭam^ caturam mutaliya muppattiraṇṭaṉ uḷ oṉṟai uṇarttum. maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇ= uṇarvu illōr um uṇartaṟku eḻuttukkaṭku vēṟuvēṟu vaṭivam^ kāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṉ+ kaṭpulaṉ ākiya varivaṭivu* um uṭaiya v-āyiṉa. perumpāṉmai meykku* ē vaṭivu kūṟiṉār, uyirkku vaṭivu* iṉmaiyiṉ. `ekara v-okarattu* iyaṟkai y-um aṟṟu* ē' ((eḻu//16)) eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmai uyirkku* um vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. (1)

TE2n

avai tām^
kuṟṟu* iyal ikaram^ kuṟṟu* iyal ukaram
āytam eṉṟa
mu+ pāl+ puḷḷi y-um eḻuttu* ōr-aṉṉa.

itu mēl+ cārntu varum eṉṟa mūṉṟiṟku* um peyar um muṟai y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: --- avai tām --- mēl+ cārntu varum eṉa+paṭṭavai tām, kuṟṟiyal ikaram^ kuṟṟiyal ukaram āytam eṉṟa mu+pāl+puḷḷi y-um --- kuṟṟiyal ikaram um^ kuṟṟiyal ukaram um āytam um eṉṟa colla+paṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatu* ākiya puḷḷi vaṭivu* um ām; eḻuttu* ōr-aṉṉa --- avai y-um muṉ+kūṟiya muppatu* eḻuttōṭu oru taṉmai y-āy vaḻaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

muṉ+kūṟiya iraṇṭu* um ummai tokka niṉṟaṉa. ikara ukaram^ kuṟuki niṉṟaṉa, vikāram* vakaiyāṉ+ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutaliṉ. ivaṟṟai iṅṅaṉam^ kuṟiyiṭṭu* āḷutal ellārkkum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉam+kōl kuṟukiṉāl+ pirappam^ kōl ākātatu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkam um uyir ē y-ām. ivaṟṟai+ puṇarcci vēṟṟumai y-um poruḷ vēṟṟumai y-um paṟṟi vēṟu* ōr eḻuttu* āka vēṇṭiṉār.

ivaṟṟu* uḷ+ kuṟṟiyal ukaram nērpacai y-um nirai pacai y-um āka+ cīrkaḷai+ pala v-ākkum āṟu ceyyuḷiyal uḷ uṇarka.

āytam eṉṟa ōcai tāṉ aṭuppu+kūṭṭu+pōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu* eṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytam eṉṟa muppāl+ puḷḷi y-um eṉṟār. ataṉai ikkālattār naṭuvu vāṅkiyiṭṭu* eḻutupa. itaṟku vaṭivu kūṟiṉār, ēṉai y-oṟṟukkaḷ pōla uyir ēṟātu ōcai vikāram āy niṟpatu* oṉṟu* ākaliṉ. eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcaikaḷ pōla+ koḷḷiṉum^ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟaṟku eḻuttu* ē y-ām eṉṟār. itaṉai+ puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu* eṉa v-ē ēṉai y-eḻuttukkaḷ ellām vari-vaṭiviṉa v-ātal peṟṟām.

muṉ ^niṉṟa cūttirattāṉ+ cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭai y-ē eḻuttu* eṉappaṭupa muppaḵtu* eṉpa eṉa v-ē, cārntu varal% marapiṉ mūṉṟu* um ē ciṟantaṉa, ēṉaiya muppatu* um avvāṟu ciṟantila v-eṉa v-um poruḷ* ^tantu niṟṟaliṉ ataṉai vilakki+ ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu ivai y-um oppa vaḻaṅkum eṉṟaṟku eḻuttu* ōraṉṉa eṉṟār.

i+ peyarkaḷ ē peyar. i+ muṟai y-ē muṟai. tokai y-um mūṉṟu* ē. i+ mūṉṟu peyar um paṇpu+tokai.

`avaitām' `āytam eṉṟa' eṉpaṉa col+cīr-aṭi. (2)

TE3n

avaṟṟu* uḷ, a i u
e o eṉṉum a+pāl aintu um
ōr aḷapu icaikkum^ kuṟṟu* eḻuttu eṉpa.

itu muṉ+ kūṟiyavaṟṟu* uḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟu* uḷ --- muṉ+kūṟiya muppatu* eḻuttiṉ uḷ, a-i-u-e-o eṉṉum a+pāl aintu* um --- akara ikara ukara ekara okaram eṉṟu kūṟa+paṭum a+pakutikaḷ aintu* um, ōr aḷapu icaikkum^ kuṟṟu* eḻuttu* eṉpa --- orōvoṉṟu ōr aḷapu* āka olikkum^ kuṟṟu* eḻuttu eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉai y-uṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; eṉṟavāṟu.

i+ kāraṇa+peyar mēl āḷumāṟu āṇṭu uṇarka.

tamakku iṉam āyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ allatu kuṟumai neṭumai koḷḷappaṭāmaiyiṉ, aḷaviṉ+paṭṭu amaintaṉa v-ām^ kuṟṟu* eḻuttiṉ+ kuṟuki mey arai-māttirai peṟṟatu* ēṉum^ kuṟṟu* eḻuttu eṉa+ peyar peṟātu* āyiṟṟu, oru māttirai peṟṟa mey tamakku iṉam āka iṉmaiyiṉ. kuṟṟu* eḻuttu* eṉpatu paṇpu+tokai. iṉi icaippatu* um icai y-um vēṟu* āka uṇaraṟka. atu poruṭṭaṉmai.

`avaṟṟu* uḷ' `a-i-u' eṉpaṉa col+cīr-aṭi. (3)

TE4n

ā ī ū ē ai
ō au eṉṉum a+pāl ēḻu* um
īr aḷapu* icaikkum* neṭṭu* eḻuttu eṉpa.

itu v-um atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ā-ī-ū-ē-ai-ō-au eṉṉum a+pāl ēḻu* um --- ā-ī-ū-ē-ai-ō-au eṉṟu colla+paṭum a+kūṟṟu* ēḻu* um, īr aḷapu icaikkum neṭṭu* eḻuttu eṉpa --- orōvoṉṟu iraṇṭu māttirai y-āka olikkum neṭṭu* eḻuttu eṉṉum^ kuṟippiṉai y-uṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar; eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, aḷavu* um^ kāraṇa+kuṟi y-um iṅṅaṉam uṇartti mēl āḷupa. aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ kuṟiya eḻuttiṉ neṭiya v-ātaṟku+ kuṟṟu* eḻuttu* ākiya iṉam^ tamakku* iṉṟu* ēṉum māttirai y-oppumaiyāṉ neṭṭu* eḻuttu eṉṟār.
