TE5n

mū v-aḷapu* icaittal ōr eḻuttu* iṉṟu* ē.

iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; ōr eḻuttu mū v-aḷapu* āy um icaikkum^ kollō v-eṉṟu aiya+paṭutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōr eḻuttu mū v-aḷapu icaittal-iṉṟu --- ōr-eḻuttu* ē niṉṟu mūṉṟu māttirai y-āka icaittal-iṉṟu; eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, pala eḻuttu+ kūṭiya iṭattu mūṉṟu māttirai y-um nāṉku māttirai y-um icaikkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, perumpāṉmai mūṉṟu māttirai y-ē peṟum eṉṟār pulavar. pala eḻuttu* eṉa v-ē, nāṉku māttirai y-um peṟutal peṟṟām. (5)

TE6n

nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ av= aḷapu* uṭaiya
kūṭṭi y-eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu māttirai nīḷum āṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ --- vaḻakku* iṭattu* um^ ceyyuḷ iṭattu* um ōcai y-um poruḷ um peṟutal kāraṇam āka iraṇṭu māttirai peṟṟa eḻuttu a+māttiraiyiṉ mikku olittalai virumpuvar āyiṉ, av= aḷapu uṭaiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- tām^ karutiya māttiraiyai+ tarutaṟku uriya eḻuttukkaḷai+ kūṭṭi a+ māttiraikaḷai eḻuppuka v-eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar; eṉṟavāṟu.

kūṭṭi y-eḻuppum āṟu `kuṉṟu* icai moḻi' ((eḻu//41)) `ai-au v-eṉṉum' ((eḻu//42)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻu vakaittu* eṉa+ kūṟupa.

utāraṇam: āa, īi, ūu, ēe, aii, ōo, auu eṉa varum. ivai mūṉṟu māttirai peṟṟaṉa. ivaitām `neṭṭu* eḻuttu ēḻu* ē y-or eḻuttu oru moḻi' ((eḻu//43)) eṉṟa a+neṭṭu* eḻuttukkaḷ ē aḷapu* eṭuttaliṉ+ collāmal eytiṉa. iṉi `aḷapeṭai y-acainilai y-ākal um urittu* ē' ((ceyyuḷiyal//17)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷ-iyal+ cūttirattāṉ eḻuttu* ām^taṉmai y-um eytiṟṟu.

itu tāṉ iyaṟkai y-aḷapeṭai y-um^ ceyyuṭku+ pulavar ceytu-koṇṭa ceyaṟkai y-aḷapeṭai y-um āy+ col+ ^taṉmai eyti-niṉṟu alaku peṟum-āṟu* um^ kuṟṟiyal ikara+ kuṟṟiyal ukaraṅkaḷ pōla eḻuttu* ām^ taṉmai eyti alaku peṟātu niṟkum āṟu* um a+cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. eṉa v-ē, eḻuttu* ām^ taṉmai y-um uṭaimaiyiṉ aḷapeṭaiyōṭu kūṭi eḻuttu nāṟpatu eṉṟal um poruntiṟṟu. oṟṟu* aḷapeṭai ceyyuṭku* ē varutaliṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟār āyiṉār.

av= aḷapu* uṭaiya eṉa+ paṉmai y-āka+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivar um nāṉku māttirai y-um^ koṇṭār. eṉṉai? iv=āciriyarai `muntu nūl kaṇṭu' eṉṟār ākaliṉ. māpurāṇattu,

`ceyyuṭkaḷ ōcai citaiyuṅkāl īr aḷapu* um
aiya+pāṭu* iṉṟi $yaṇaiyumā $maitīroṟṟu*
iṉṟi y-um^ ceyyuḷ+ keṭiṉ oṟṟai y-uṇṭākku
kuṉṟu mēl oṟṟu* aḷapum^ koḷ.'

eṉṟa cūttirattāṉ avar koṇṭa nāṉku māttirai y-um iv= āciriyarkku nērtal vēṇṭutaliṉ. atu `ceṟāaay vāḻiya neñcu' ((kuṟaḷ//1200)) `tūuu+ tīm pukai tol vicumpu' `pēeerttu+kol' `ilāaarkku* illai+ tamar' `virāaay+ ceyyāmai naṉṟu' `marīii+piṉṉai+ pirivu' eṉa+ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭku* ellām nāṉku māttirai peṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. aṉṟi mūṉṟu māttirai peṟṟaṉa v-ēl āciriya+taḷai taṭṭu+ ceppal-ōcai keṭum āyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam aḷapu* eṭātu-niṉṟu āciriya+ taḷai taṭṭu niṟpaṉa kalikku uṟuppu* ākiya koccaka veṇpākkaḷ; ivai aṉṉa v-aṉṟu* eṉa uṇarka.

kōṭṭu nūṟu* um mañcaḷ um^ kūṭiya vaḻi+ piṟanta cev vaṇṇam pōla neṭil um^ kuṟil um^ kūṭiya kūṭṭattu+ piṟanta piṉṉar+ piḷavu-paṭā v-ōcaiyai aḷapeṭai y-eṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭiṉār. ivai kūṭṭi+ colliya kālattu* allatu pulappaṭā, eḷ= āṭṭiya vaḻi y-allatu eṇṇey pulappaṭā v-āṟu pōla v-eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi aḷapeṭai y-allāta ōcaikaḷ ellām icai y-ōcai y-ātaliṉ avaṟṟai `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa. (6)

TE7n

kaṇṇimai noṭi y-eṉa v-av= ē māttirai
nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟu* ē.

itu māttiraikku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kaṇṇimai noṭi eṉa av= ē māttirai --- kaṇṇimai y-eṉa v-um noṭi y-eṉa v-um av= iraṇṭu* ē eḻuttiṉ māttiraikku aḷavu, nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟē --- nuṇṇitāka nūl ilakkaṇattai uṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭa neṟi eṉṟavāṟu.

`eṉa' eṇṇiṉ+ pirintu iraṇṭu* iṭattu* um^ kūṭiṟṟu. kaṇṇimai noṭi eṉṉum pala poruḷ oru coṟkaḷ īṇṭu+ toḻil% mēl um ōcai mēl um muṟai y-ē niṉṟaṉa. āciriyar ellār um eḻuttiṟku ivai y-ē aḷavāka+ kūṟaliṉ ivar um^ kūṟiṉār. iyaṟkai makaṉ taṉ kuṟippiṉ aṉṟi iraṇṭu* imai y-um oru kāl+kūṭi nīṅkiṉa kāla+kaḻivu* um a eṉa+ piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟa+ kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um okkum. i+ kaṇ=imaiyiṉatu pākam meykku* um^ cārpiṉ+ ^tōṟṟattiṟku* um itaṉ pākam makara+ kuṟukkattiṟku* um^ koḷka. i+kaṇ=imai iraṭṭittu varutal neṭiṟku* um atu mūṉṟu* um nāṉku* um āy varutal aḷapeṭaikku* um^ koḷka. atu pōla v-ē noṭittal+ ^toḻiliṉ+ piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟa+kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um a eṉa+ piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟa+kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um okkum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟku* um^ kūṟiya v-āṟē koḷka.

iṉi av= aḷavai tāṉ niṟuttaḷ attal peytu* aḷattal cārtti y-aḷattal nīṭṭi y-aḷattal teṟittu* aḷattal $tēṅka mukantu* aḷattal eṇṇi y-aḷattal eṉa eḻu vakaittu. avaṟṟuḷ itu cārtti y-aḷattal ām.

kaṇ=imaikku* um noṭikku* um aḷavu ārāyiṉ varampu* iṉṟi ōṭum eṉṟu karuti `nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟu' eṉṟu muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉṉum utti kūṟiṉār. iḵtu āṇai kūṟutal um ām. eṉavē, eḻuttiṟku* ē aḷavu kūṟi māttiraikku aḷavu kūṟiṟṟu* ilar. noṭiyiṉ+ kaṇṇimai ciṟappu* uṭaittu, uḷḷattāṉ niṉaittu nikaḻāmaiyiṉ. (7)

TE8n

aukāram* v-iṟuvāy+
paṉṉīr eḻuttu* um uyir eṉa moḻipa.

itu kuṟilai y-um neṭilai y-um^ tokuttu vēṟu* ōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aukāra iṟuvāy+ paṉṉīr eḻuttu* um --- akaram mutal-āka aukāram īṟu* āka+ kiṭanta paṉṉiraṇṭu* eḻuttu* um, uyir eṉa moḻipa --- uyir eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉai y-uṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itu v-um āṭci y-um^ kāraṇam um nōkkiyatu*ōr kuṟi. mey patiṉeṭṭiṉai y-um iyakki+ tāṉ $aruvāy vaṭivu* iṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyir āyiṟṟu. ivai meykku uyir āy niṉṟu meykaḷai iyakkum ēl uyir eṉa vēṟu* ōr eḻuttu* iṉṟām piṟa v-eṉiṉ, meyyiṉ* ^niṟkum uyir um^ taṉi y-ē niṟkum uyir um vēṟu* eṉa uṇarka. eṉṉai? `akara mutala' ((kuṟaḷ//1)) eṉpuḻi akaram^ taṉi y-uyir um āy+ kakaram* v-oṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uyir um āy vēṟu niṟṟaliṉ. av= akaram^ taṉi y-ē niṟṟalāṉ um pala meykkaḷ% niṉṟu avva+ meykaṭku icainta ōcaikaḷai+ payantu* ē niṟṟalāṉ um vēṟupaṭṭatu* ākaliṉ oṉṟu* ē y-āy um pala v-ē y-āy-um niṟpatu* ōr taṉmaiyai y-uṭaittu* eṉṟu kōṭum; iṟaivaṉ oṉṟu* ē y-āy niṟkum^ taṉmai y-um pal= uyirkku* um^ tāṉ ē y-āy avaṟṟiṉ aḷavu* āy niṟkum^ taṉmai y-um pōla. atu a eṉṟa vaḻiyum ūra eṉa viḷi y-ēṟṟa-vaḻi y-um `akara mutala' eṉṟa vaḻi y-um mū v-iṉaṅkaḷil ēṟiṉa-vaḻi y-um ōcai vēṟupaṭṭa v-āṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam icaittu* uḻi y-um māttirai oṉṟu* ē y-ām. iḵtu ēṉai y-uyirkaṭku* um okkum.

aukāra v-iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇpu+tokai. ummai muṟṟu* ummai. `akara mutal' eṉa muṉ+kūṟi+ pōntamaiyiṉ īṇṭu īṟu* ē kūṟiṉār. (8)

TE9n

ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy+,
patiṉeṇ= eḻuttu mey= eṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu uyir allaṉavaṟṟai+ tokuttu ōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy+ patiṉeṇ eḻuttu* um --- kakāram mutal ṉakāram īṟu* āy+ kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttu* um, mey= eṉa moḻipa --- mey= eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉai y-uṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itu v-um āṭci y-um^ kāraṇam um nōkkiya kuṟi. eṉṉai? paṉṉīr uyirkku* um^ tāṉ iṭam^ koṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkum^ taṉmai peṟṟa uṭampu* āy niṟṟaliṉ.

ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇpu+tokai. ummai muṟṟu* ummai. muṉṉar ṉakāram* v-iṟuvāy eṉpuḻi muppatu* eḻuttiṟku* um īṟu* ām eṉṟār, īṇṭu+ patiṉeṭṭu* eḻuttiṟku* um īṟu* ām eṉṟār ātaliṉ+ kūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟu* aṉṟu. (9)

TE10n

meyyōṭu* iyaiyiṉum uyir iyal* ^tiriyā.

iḵtu uyir-meykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyir meyyōṭu iyaiyiṉum --- paṉṉīr uyir um patiṉeṭṭu meyyōṭu* um^ kūṭi niṉṟaṉa v-āyiṉum, iyal tiriyā --- tam aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um eṇṇum^ tirintu nillā eṉṟavāṟu.

itu `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpataṉai nōkki niṟṟaliṉ etiratu pōṟṟal ām. uyir um mey= um atikāra+paṭutaliṉ īṇṭu vaittār. a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um oṉṟu* eṉṉum eṇṇum^ kā eṉa niṉṟa iṭattu* um okkum eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. piṟa v-um aṉṉa. āyiṉ oṉṟu* arai māttirai y-um iraṇṭu* arai māttirai y-um uṭaiyaṉa oru māttirai y-um iraṇṭu māttirai y-um āya v-āṟu eṉṉai y-eṉiṉ, nīr taṉittu aḷantu* uḻi y-um nāḻi y-āy arai nāḻi y-uppiṉ+ kalantu* uḻi y-um^ kūṭi oṉṟarai nāḻi y-āy mikāta v-āṟu pōlvatu* ōr poruḷ+peṟṟi y-eṉṟu koḷvatu* allatu kāraṇam^ kūṟal ākāmai uṇarka. āciriyaṉ āṇai eṉpār um uḷar.

`viḷaṅkāy tiraṭṭiṉār illai+ kaḷaṅkaṉiyai+
kār eṉa+ ceytār um il' ((nālaṭiyār//103//107))

eṉpatu* ē kāṭṭiṉār uraiyāciriyar um. (10)

TE11n

meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē y-arai y-eṉa moḻipa.

itu taṉi-meykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē arai y-eṉa moḻipa --- meyyiṉatu māttiraiyiṉai orōvoṉṟu arai-māttirai y-uṭaiya v-eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

av= araimāttirai y-um^ taṉittu+ kūṟi+ kāṭṭal ākātu, nā+ ciṟitu puṭaipeyarum^ taṉmai y-āy niṟṟaliṉ. iṉi ataṉai+ cila moḻi mēl+ peytu kākkai kōṅku kavvai y-eṉa+ kāṭṭupa. mey= eṉpatu aḵṟiṇai y-iyal+ peyar ātaliṉ mey= eṉṉum oṟṟumai paṟṟi arai y-eṉṟār. (11)

TE12n

avviya ṉilaiyu mēṉai mūṉṟē.

itu cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟaṟkum aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai mūṉṟu --- cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟum, avviyal nilaiyum --- muṟkūṟiya arai māttiraiyākiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

kēṇmiyā nāku eḵku eṉa varum. (12)

TE13n

arai y-aḷavu kuṟukal% makaram uṭaittu* ē
y-icai y-iṭaṉ arukum^ teriyum^ kālai.

itu meykaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: icai iṭaṉ makaram araiyaḷavu kuṟukaluṭaittu --- vēṟōr eḻuttiṉatu ōcaiyiṉkaṇ makaravoṟṟu+ taṉ araimāttiraiyiṉ+ kuṟuki+ kālmāttirai peṟutalaiyuṭaittu, teriyuṅkālai arukum --- ārāyuṅkālattu+ tāṉ ciṟupāṉmaiyāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pōṉm varumvaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa orumoḻikkaṇṇum irumoḻikkaṇṇum^ koḷka. itu piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟa leṉṉum utti. (13)

TE14n

uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.

itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceykiṉṟatu.

makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṭpeṟupuḷḷi --- puṟattu+ peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟpeṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum --- makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē puṟattu+ peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭku+ kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcipporuḷai uṇartti niṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: kappi kappi (kammi) eṉavarum. iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal. (14)

TE15n

meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.

itu taṉimeykkum uyirmeykkum oppumaimēl vēṟṟumaiceytal kūṟukiṉṟatu. eṉṉai? uyirmeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkum^ taṉimeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu oṉṟāka eḻutiṉavaṟṟai oṉṟākkutaṟku+ piṉpu puḷḷi peṟukaveṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal --- patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷiṉ taṉmaiyāvatu puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟalām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē uyirmeykaṭku+ puḷḷiyiṉṟāyiṟṟu. k-ṅ...ṟ-ṉ eṉavarum. ivaṟṟai+ puḷḷiyiṭṭu+ kāṭṭavē puḷḷi peṟuvataṟku muṉṉar akaram uṭaṉiṉṟatōr meyvaṭivē peṟṟu niṉṟaṉavaṟṟai+ piṉṉar appuḷḷiyiṭṭu+ taṉimey yākkiṉāreṉpatūum peṟutum. itaṉāṉē kakaram ṅakaram mutaliyaṉa puḷḷipeṟuvataṟku muṉṉar iyalpāka akaram peṟṟē niṟkumeṉpatūum puḷḷi peṟuṅkālattu avvakaram nīṅkumeṉpatūum piṉṉar a+ puḷḷi nīṅki uyirēṟumiṭattu+ taṉkaṇ akaram nīṅkiyē pōka varukiṉṟatōr uyir yātāyiṉum oṉṟu ēṟi niṟkumeṉpatūum peṟṟām. `meyyiṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//46)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉum ituvē itaṟku+ karuttātal uṇarka. (15)

TE16n

ekara okarattu iyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ekara okarattu iyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē --- ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu nilaiyum meypōla+ puḷḷipeṟum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku+ puḷḷi yiṉṟāyiṟṟu.

e/-o/-eṉa varum.

iḵtu uyirmeykkum okkum.

makaram ārāyccippaṭṭatu kaṇṭu makarattiṟku vaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceytu, atikārattāṉ meyyiṉ taṉmai kūṟi, ataṉpiṉ māṭṭēṟṟaliṉ ekara okarattaiyum^ kūṟiṉār. (16)

TE17n

puḷḷi y-illā v-ellā mey=um
uruvu* uruvu* āki y-akaramōṭu* uyirttal um
ēṉai y-uyirōṭu* uruvu tirintu* uyirttal um
āy īr iyala v-uyirttal āṟu* ē.

itu meyyum uyirum^ kūṭi+ puṇarumāṟum āṇṭu avai tiriyātum^ tirintum niṟkumāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷi illā ellā meyyum --- uyirai+ peṟutaṟku+ puḷḷiyai+ pōkkiṉa ellā meykaḷum, uruuruvāki akaramōṭu uyirttalum --- puḷḷi peṟukiṉṟa kālattu iyalpākiya akara nīṅkiya vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivāki niṉṟu piṉṉar ēṟiya akarattōṭukūṭi olittalum, ēṉaiuyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum --- oḻinta patiṉōruyirum^ kūṭi avvaṭivu tirintu olittalum, āyīriyala uyirttalāṟē --- eṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya avai olikkum muṟaimai eṉṟavāṟu.

puḷḷiyillā meyyeṉavē muṉpeṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷiyai uyirēṟṟutaṟku+ pōkkiṉamai peṟutum. uruvuruvākiyeṉavē puḷḷi peṟutaṟkāka iyalpākiya akaram nīṅkiya vaṭivē piṉṉar akaram peṟutaṟkum vaṭivāmeṉpatu kūṟiṉār.

ka-ṅa-ya eṉa varum.

uruvu tirintu uyirttalāvatu mēlum^ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟum kōṭupeṟṟum puḷḷipeṟṟum puḷḷiyum^ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟum uyirttalām. ki kī mutaliyaṉa mēlvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke kē mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. kā ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyai ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyai vaḷaitteḻutiṉār. ko kō ṅo ṅō mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyum^ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam^ tirintu olippavē uyirmey paṉṉirupatiṉeṭṭu irunūṟṟorupattāṟāyiṟṟu. ākavē uyirmeykku vaṭivum oruvāṟṟāṉ+ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē mey taṉakku iyalpākiya akarattai nīṅki niṟpatōr taṉmaiyum piṟitōruyirai ēṟkum^ taṉmaiyum uṭaiyateṉpatūum, uyirmeykkaṇ+ pulappaṭātu iyalpākiya akaramāy niṟkum^ taṉmaiyum mey puḷḷipeṟ ṟaḻintavaḻi avaṟṟiṟku+ takka uyirāy+ pulappaṭṭuvarum^ taṉmaiyum uṭaiyateṉpatūum peṟṟām. uyirmey yeṉpatu ummai+ tokai. (17)

TE18n

meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē.

itu meyyum uyirum^ kūṭiyavaḻi avaṟṟiṉ ōcai niṟkum muṟaimai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu --- meyyiṉatu ōcai tōṉṟiya piṉṉatām, uyir tōṉṟu nilaiyē --- uyiriṉatu ōcai tōṉṟum nilai eṉṟavāṟu.

muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ mey muṉṉar niṟpa uyir piṉ vantu ēṟumeṉṟār. ammuṟaiyē ōcaiyum piṟakkumeṉṟār. itaṉāṉē māttirai koḷḷuṅkāl uppum nīrum pōla oṉṟēyāy niṟṟalum vēṟupaṭuttu+ kāṇuṅkāl viralum viralum^ cēraniṉṟāṟpōla vēṟāy niṟṟalum peṟṟām. nīr uppiṉ kuṇamēyāyavāṟupōla uyirum meyyiṉ kuṇamēyāy vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai eyti niṟṟal koḷka.

utāraṇam: ka-ṅa-ya eṉa+ kūṭṭamum pirivum mūvakaiyōcaiyum^ kāṇka. (18)

TE19n

valleḻuttu eṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa.

itu taṉimeykaḷuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa --- ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum^ taṉimeykaḷai, valleḻutteṉpa --- valleḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

iḵtu āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. oḻinta melleḻuttaiyum iṭaiyeḻuttaiyam nōkki+ tām valleṉṟicaittalāṉum valleṉa+ talaivaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṟṟu. (19)

TE20n

melleḻuttu eṉpa ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa --- ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum^ taṉi meykaḷai, melleḻutteṉpa --- melleḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. melleṉṟicaittalāṉum melleṉṟu mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum melleḻuttāyiṟṟu. (20)

TE21n

iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ya ra la va ḻa ḷa --- ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum^ taṉi meykaḷai, iṭaiyeḻutteṉpa --- iṭaiyeḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. iṭainikarttāy olittalāṉum iṭainikarttāya miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum iṭaiyeḻuttāyiṟṟu.

valliṉattu+ ka-ca-ta-pa eṉṉum nāṉkam melliṉattu ṅa-na-ma eṉṉum mūṉṟum iṭaiyiṉattu ya-va eṉṉum iraṇṭum moḻikku mutalātal nōkki immuṟaiyē vaittār. ippeyarāṉē eḻutteṉṉum ōcaikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. uyirkkum^ kuṟumai neṭumai kūṟaliṉ avaiyum uruvāyiṉa. itu cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattiṟkum okkum. (21)

TE22n

ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
mey+mayaṅku* uṭaṉilai teriyum^ kālai.

itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭum^ taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum uyirmey uyirmeyyōṭum^ taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ammūvāṟum --- aṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu kūṟāka+ pakutta patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ --- vaḻakkiṭattum^ ceyyuḷiṭattum eḻuttukkaḷai+ kūṭṭi moḻippaṭuttu vaḻaṅkutal uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅkuṭaṉilai --- taṉimey taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa piṟameyyōṭum^ taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyum, uṭaṉmayaṅkum nilai --- appatiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭum^ taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyumeṉa iraṇṭām, teriyum^ kālai --- avai mayaṅkum moḻiyām^ taṉmai ārāyuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, taṉittuniṉṟa eḻuttuṭaṉ muṉṉiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ tām^ kūṭumāṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. kaṭka eṉṟāl iṭainiṉṟa taṉimey muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉiṉ vēṟāya kakaravoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kākkai eṉṟāl iṭainiṉṟa kakaravoṟṟu muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. karu eṉa īreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ karutu eṉa mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum uyirmey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. tuṇaṅkai eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kal vil eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu.

teriyuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ uyir muṉṉar uyirmeymmayakkamum uyir muṉṉar+ taṉimeymmayakkamum^ koḷka. avai aḷai āmpal eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa.

meymmayakkaṅkaḷuḷ taṉimeymuṉṉar+ piṟamey niṉṟu mayaṅkutal palavātaliṉ+ pala cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟi+ taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantu mayaṅkutalai oru cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa. avai mayaṅkuṅkāl valliṉattil ṭakaramum ṟakaramum melliṉamāṟum iṭaiyiṉamāṟum piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkumeṉṟum valliṉattiṉ+ kacatapakkaḷ taṉ meyyōṭaṉṟi+ piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkāveṉṟum uyttuṇara+ kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.

mūvāṟu meṉṉum ummai muṟṟummai.

iccūttiram mutalāka `meynnilaicuṭṭiṉ' ((eḻu//30)) īṟāka mēṟkūṟum moḻimarapiṟku+ poruntiya karuvi kūṟukiṉṟateṉṟuṇarka; eḻuttukkaḷ tammiṉ+ kūṭi+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatātaliṉ. (22)

TE23n

ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
ka ca pa veṉṉu mūveḻuttu uriya.

itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkum mayakkam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- moḻiyiṭai niṉṟa ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum nāṉku puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca pa eṉṉum mūveḻuttu uriya --- ka ca pa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭka kaṭci naṭpu eṉavum^ kaṟka muyaṟci kaṟpu eṉavum^ celka valci celpa eṉavum^ koḷka. nīḷciṉai koḷpa eṉavum^ taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻi+ puṇarcciyākaliṉ īṇṭaikkākā. (21)

TE24n

avaṟṟuḷ
laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavum^ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar --- lakāra ḷakāramākiya puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavum^ tōṉṟum --- kacapakkaḷē yaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

kolyāṉai celvam veḷyāṟu kaḷvaṉ eṉa varum.

ivaṟṟuḷ+ kolyāṉai eṉa viṉaittokaiyum veḷyāṟu eṉa+ paṇputtokaiyum nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceyvataṟku iyaiyāmaiyiṉ `maruviṉpāttiya' eṉṟu kūṟuvarātaliṉ ivvāciriyar ivaṟṟai orumoḻiyāka+ koḷvareṉṟu uṇarka. ikkaruttāṉē mēlum viṉaittokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum orumoḻiyākakkoṇṭu utāraṇam^ kāṭṭutum. aṉṟi ivvāciriyar nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattu viṉaittokaikkaṇṇum paṇputtokaikkaṇṇumaṉṟi orumoḻikkaṇṇē mayaṅkuvaṉavum uḷavātaliṉ, avaṟṟai+ kaṇṭu ilakkaṇam^ kūṟiṉār. avai piṉṉar iṟantaṉaveṉṟu oḻittu utāraṇamillaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam^ kāṭṭāmal+ pōtalē naṉṟeṉṟu kūṟalumoṉṟu. (24)

TE25n

ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
tatta micaika ḷottaṉa nilaiyē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tatta micaikaḷ --- tamakkiṉamāy muṉṉiṉṟa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟakkaḷ, ottaṉa nilaiyē --- piṉṉiṟṟaṟku+ poruntiṉa mayaṅki niṟṟaṟkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṅkaṉ kañcaṉ kaṇṭaṉ kantaṉ kampaṉ maṉṟaṉ eṉa varum. teṅku piñcu vaṇṭu pantu kampu kaṉṟu eṉa+ kuṟṟukaramum^ kāṭṭupa. (25)

TE26n

avaṟṟuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar+
ka ca ña pa ma ya vav vēḻu muriya

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- mēṟkūṟiya melloṟṟu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar --- ṇakāraṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, kacañapamayava ēḻum uriya --- ṭaṟakkaḷē yaṉṟi+ kacañapamayava eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṇku veṇcāntu veṇñāṇ paṇpu veṇmai maṇyāṟu eṇvaṭṭu eṉavum, puṉku puṉcey meṉñāṇ aṉpu vaṉmai iṉyāḻ puṉvaraku eṉavum varum. eṇvaṭṭu viṉaittokai. eṇku puṉku peyar. (26)

TE27n

ña na ma va veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- ña na ma va eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟṟaṉṟē --- yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal poruṇmai peṟṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam āciriyar cūttirañceytaliṉ akkālattu oru moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟṟām. avai ikkālattu iṟantaṉa.

iṉi uraiyāciriyar uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉa irumoḻikkaṇ varuvaṉa utāraṇamāka+ kāṭṭiṉārāleṉiṉ, āciriyar orumoḻiyāmāṟu īṇṭu+ kūṟi, irumoḻi puṇarttaṟku+ puṇariyaleṉṟu vēṟōr iyalum^ kūṟi, ataṉkaṇ `meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum' ((eḻu//107)) eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kūṟiyavaḻi+ piṉṉum `ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti' ((eḻu//163)) eṉṟum piṟāṇṭum īṟukaṭōṟum eṭuttōti+ puṇarppar. ātaliṉ īṇṭu irumoḻippuṇarcci kāṭṭiṉ+ kūṟiyatukūṟa leṉṉum^ kuṟṟamām. ataṉāl avai kāṭṭutal poruntāmai uṇarka. (27)

TE28n

maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvum^ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ --- muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ makaramākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, vavvum^ tōṉṟum --- pakarayakaramēyaṉṟi vakaramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṟkum utāraṇam ikkālattu iṟantaṉa. aṉṟi, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kāṭṭiṉ `vakāra micaiyu makāram^ kuṟukum' ((eḻu//330)) eṉṟa viti vēṇṭāvām. (28)

TE29n

yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yaraḻaveṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- yaraḻa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar,mutalākeḻuttum --- moḻikku mutalāmeṉa mēṟkūṟum oṉpateḻuttukkaḷum, ummaiyāṉ moḻikku mutalākāta piṟa eḻuttukkaḷum, ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum --- ṅakāramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyka ārka āḻka, āytal ārtal āḻtal, āynar ārnar āḻnar, āypavai ārpavai āḻpavai, vāymai nērmai kīḻmai, eycilai vārcilai vāḻcēri, teyvam cērvatu vāḻvatu, pāyñekiḻi nērñekiḻi vāḻñeṇṭu, ceyyāṟu pōryāḷai vīḻyāṉai eṉa moḻikku mutalām oṉpatum vantu mayaṅkiṉa. ceyyāṟu eṉa yakarattiṉ muṉṉar yakaram vantatu taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantatām.

iṉi ummaiyāṟkoṇṭa moḻikku mutalākātavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum^ cila kāṭṭutum: ōyvu cōrvu vāḻvu, ōyvōr cōrvōr vāḻvōr, āyñar cērñar āḻñar eṉavarum. piṟa eḻuttukkaḷōṭu varuvaṉa uḷavēṉum vaḻakkum^ ceyyuḷum nōkki+ kūṟikkoḷka.

iṉi vēyṅṅaṉam vērṅṅaṉam vēḻṅṅaṉam eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta ṅakaram iṭaivanta coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟuṇarka, āciriyar ōtutaliṉ. itaṉai `ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉa+ pirittōtiṉār akkālattum aritāka vaḻaṅkaliṉ.

iṉi vēykaṭitu vērkaṭitu vīḻkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avai irumoḻiyāka nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu mēṟpuṇarkkiṉṟaṉa īṇṭaikkākā eṉa maṟukka. (29)

TE30n

meynnilai cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttum^
tammuṉ+ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalam^ kaṭaiyē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē taṉimey taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ --- poruṇilaimaiyai+ karutiṉ, ellā eḻuttum --- patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, tammuṉ tām varūum --- tammuṉṉē tām vantu mayaṅkum, raḻa alaṅkaṭaiyē --- rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷallāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kākkai eṅṅaṉam paccai maññai paṭṭai maṇṇai tattai venney appu ammai veyyar elli evvi kaḷḷi koṟṟi kaṉṉi eṉa varum.

meynnilai cuṭṭiṉeṉṟataṉāl taṉimey muṉṉar uyirmey varumeṉṟu koḷka. ellāmeṉṟatu rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ oḻintaṉavaṟṟai+ taḻuviṟṟu. (30)

TE31n

a i u am mūṉṟum^ cuṭṭu.

itu kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: a i u ammūṉṟum^ cuṭṭu --- a i u eṉṟu kūṟiya ammūṉṟum^ cuṭṭeṉṉum^ kuṟiyiḷaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi, cuṭṭi aṟiyappaṭum poruḷai uṇarttaliṉ. taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ ekaram viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉa varum. ivai peyarai+ cārntu tattam^ kuṟippiṉ+ poruḷceyta iṭaiccol. iccūttiram orutalaimoḻita leṉṉum utti. ituvum mēlaiccūttiramum eḻuttāntaṉmaiyaṉṟi moḻinilaimaippaṭṭu vēṟōr kuṟipeṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ moḻimarapiṉai+ cēravaittār. (31)

TE32n

ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā.

itu neṭṭeḻutteṉṟavaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ā ē ō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ammūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttaliṉ.

utāraṇam: uṇkā uṇkē uṇkō eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ākāram ikkālattu viṉāvāy varuta laritu. nīyē nīyō eṉpatu ikkālattu varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ē ō eṉpaṉa iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷḷum^ kūṟiṉār, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ tarum poruṭṭokai paṟṟi. itu moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉṉum uttikku iṉamām, yakara ākāramum viṉāvāy varutaliṉ. (32)

TE33n

aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um oṟṟu* icai nīṭal um
uḷa v-eṉa moḻipa v-icaiyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟaiya v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi icainūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟi, avvilakkaṇam innūṟkum^ koḷkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷapiṟantu uyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṟkūṟiya uyirum uyirmeyyum māttiraiyai iṟantolittalum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ araimāttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭolittalum yāḻ nūliṭattaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, icaiyoṭu civaṇiya uḷaveṉa moḻipa --- aṅṅaṉam aḷapiṟantum nīṇṭum icaittal ōcaiyōṭu poruntiya nālvakai+ ceyyuṭkaḷukkum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyai iṟantolikkumāṟu kaṇṭu, aḵtu iṟantolikkum iṭam^ kūṟiṉār, eḻuttum^ collum poruḷum^ kiṭakkum iṭam^ ceyyuḷiṭamātaliṉ. atu mikkolittalai+ ceyyuḷiyaliṉkaṇ `māttirai yeḻuttiya lacaivakai yeṉāa' ((cey//1)) eṉa irupattāṟu uṟuppiṟkum^ ciṟappuṟuppāka muṟkūṟip, piṉṉar

`māttirai y-aḷavu* um eḻuttu* iyal vakai y-um*
mēl+ kiḷantu* aṉṉa v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.' ((cey//2))

eṉa iccūttirattōṭu māṭṭeṟintu, piṉṉum

`eḻuttaḷa veñciṉum^ cīrnilai tāṉē

kuṉṟalu mikutalu milleṉa moḻipa.' ((cey//3))

eṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār. itu etiratupōṟṟaleṉṉam uttiyam^ kūṟiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: `varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay valippaṉyāṉ kēeḷiṉi' ((kali//11)) eṉpuḻi ḻakara ākāramum kakara ēkāramum māttirai iṟantolittavāṟu uṇarka. `piṭi yūṭṭi+ piṉṉuṇṇum^m^ kaḷiṟeṉavumuraittaṉarē' ((kali//11)) eṉpuḻi ṅakaravoṟṟu aḷaviṟantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻinta mūvakai+ ceyyuṭkum ivvāṟē tattamakkuriya pāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉai naṭātti aḷavu mikumāṟu kāṇka.

civaṇiya eṉpatu toḻiṟpeyar. icaiyoṭu civaṇiya eṉavē ceyyuḷātal peṟṟām. narampeṉṟatu ākupeyarāy yāḻiṉai uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟaiyeṉṟatu nūlai. moḻipa veṉṟum eṉmaṉār pulavareṉṟum irukāl+ kūṟiyavataṉāl, iṅṅaṉam poruḷ kūṟalē āciriyarkku+ karuttāyiṟṟu. eṉṉai? ceyyuḷiyaluḷ+ kūṟiya `māttiraiyaḷavum' eṉṉum^ cūttirattil `mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa' ((cey//2)) eṉṉum māṭṭēṟṟiṟku ivvōttiṉuḷ vēṟōr cūttiram iṉmaiyiṉ. ivvilakkaṇam^ kūṟākkāl+ ceyyuṭku+ pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻātu avai uraicceyyuṭpōla niṟṟaliṉ ivvilakkaṇam^ kūṟalē vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarka.

`cūttirattu+ poruḷ aṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
viṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellā
moṉṟa vuraippa turaiyeṉa+ paṭumē.' ((marapiyal//103))

eṉṉum marapiyal+ cūttirattāṉē ivvāṟē cūttiraṅkaḷai nalintu poruḷuraippaṉavellām^ koḷka. (33)

((nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((2. moḻimarapu))

TE34n

kuṟṟiyal ikara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
yā-v-eṉ ciṉai micai y-uraiyacai+ kiḷavikku
āvayiṉ varūu makaram ūrntu ē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. mēl eḻuttu uṇarttiyapiṉṉar avai tammuḷ+ toṭarumāṟum uṇartti avveḻuttāḷum moḻiyatu marapu uṇarttukiṉṟamaiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉa+ kāraṇa+ peyarttāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram muṉṉar+ cārntu varumeṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ+ kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uraiyacai+ kiḷavikku varūum --- tāṉ kūṟum poruḷai+ kōṭaṟku oruvaṉai etirmukamākkum^ colliṟku+ poruntavarum, āvayiṉ --- ammiyāveṉṉum iṭaiccollai+ collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉaimicai makaram ūrntu --- yāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉai yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉa varum. kēḷeṉṟatu uraiyacaikkiḷavi; ataṉai+ cārntu taṉakku iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyāveṉṉum iṭaiccol. avviṭaiccol mutalum ataṉiṉ+ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppumā meṉṟu karuti yāveṉciṉai eṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yāvum ikaram arai māttiraiyātaṟku+ cārpu. ivviṭaiccol taṉittuniṟṟal āṟṟāmaiyiṉ+ kēḷeṉpataṉōṭu cārntu oru collāyē niṉṟuḻi iṭainiṉṟa ikaram orumoḻiyiṭattu+ kuṟṟiyalikaramāy varutalāṉum āṇṭu uṇarttaṟku+ ciṟappiṉmaiyāṉum īṇṭu+ pōttantu kūṟiṉār. ūrnteṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaramum uyireṉpatu peṟṟām, uyirkkallatu ēṟutaliṉmaiyiṉ. (1)

TE35n

puṇariya ṉilaiyiṭai+ kuṟukalu murittē
yuṇara+ kūṟiṉ muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum.

itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puṇariyal nilaiyiṭai+ kuṟukalum urittē --- akkuṟṟiyalikaram puṇariyaluḷ orumoḻikkaṇṇaṉṟi irumoḻi tammiṉ+ puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilaimaikkaṇṇum^ kuṟukutalurittu, uṇarakkūṟiṉ muṉṉarttōṉṟum --- ataṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇara+ kūṟattoṭaṅkiṉ avai kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaluḷḷē kūṟappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

kuṟukalumeṉṉumiṭattu ummaiyai nilaiyiṭaiyumeṉa māṟikkūṭṭuka. `yakaramvaruvaḻi' ((eḻu//410)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu yakaram iṭam, ukaram cārnta valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu.

utāraṇam: nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu eṉavarum. itu moḻivāmeṉṉum utti (2)

TE36n

neṭṭeḻuttu imparum^ toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum^
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.

iḵtu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattukku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntē --- kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttukkaḷ āṟiṉaiyum ūrntu, neṭṭeḻuttimparum^ toṭarmoḻi īṟṟum --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum aivakai+ toṭarmoḻiyiṉ iṟutiyiṉum niṟṟal vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉ toṭarmoḻiyiṉatu īṟeṉa nilattatakalampōla oṉṟiyaṟkiḻamaippaṭṭu niṉṟatu, ammoḻiyiṉ+ ṟīrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram nillāmaiyiṉ. vallāṟu paṇputtokai. muṟṟummai tokkuniṉṟatu. atikāra muṟaimaiyeṉṉum uttiyāṉ niṟṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu varuvikka.

utāraṇam: nāku varaku teḷku eḵku kokku kuraṅku eṉavarum. ivvāṟuvakaiyum iṭam; valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu. eṉavē, moḻikku īṟātalum peṟṟām. perumuracu tirumuracu eṉpaṉa irumoḻikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. paracu iṅku ētu eṉṉum muṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiccitaivu. tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉa viṉaikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟimuṭiya araimāttiraiyāy niṟṟalum, muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉarvanta uyirēṟi muṭiyāmaiyum tammuḷ vēṟṟumai. (3)

TE37n

iṭaippaṭiṉ+ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.

itu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ araimāttiraiyiṉ+ kuṟukivaru meṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭaippaṭiṉ+ kuṟukum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē --- avvukaram orumoḻiyuḷaṉṟi+ puṇarmoḻiyiṭaippaṭiṉ taṉ araimāttiraiyiṉum^ kuṟukum iṭaṉum uṇṭu, kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉa --- ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭu+ peṟuvateṉiṉ, ataṉ puṇarccimuṟaimai aṟiyum^ kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaluḷ eṉṟavāṟu.

`valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi' ((eḻu//409)) eṉpataṉuḷ valleḻuttu+ toṭarmoḻiyum valleḻuttu varumvaḻiyum iṭam; īṟṟu valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu.

utāraṇam: cekkukkaṇai cukkukkōṭu eṉavarum. ivai araimāttiraiyiṉ+ kuṟukiyavāṟu ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttu uṇarka. iṭaṉumeṉavē itu ciṟupāṉmaiyāyiṟṟu. (4)

TE38n

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāyta+ puḷḷi
yuyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āytam orumoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āytappuḷḷi --- āytamākiya oṟṟu, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittu --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyirōṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟiṉ mēliṭattatāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

vallavāṟaṉ micaitteṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭu+ puḷḷi yeṉṟataṉāṉum `āyta+ toṭarmoḻi' ((eḻu//406)) eṉa mēṟkūṟutalāṉum uyireṉṟatu īṇṭu+ perumpāṉmaiyum^ kuṟṟukaramēyām. ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai yuyirkaḷaiyum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: eḵku kaḵcu kaḵṭu kaḵtu kaḵpu kaḵṟu aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉavarum. kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟai meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci eṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭu+ puḷḷiyeṉṟataṉāṉum āytamum meyyāyiṟṟu, aḵkāmai veḵkāmai aḵki veḵki aḵkam eṉa+ piṟavuyirkaḷōṭum vantatu. kaḵciyāteṉa+ tirintatuvum^ kuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu puṇarntatām. (5)

TE39n

īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micaimai tōṉṟum.

iḵtu avvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉum --- nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattum, icaimai tōṉṟum --- ataṉ araimāttiraiyē icaikkum^ taṉmai tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉavarum. ivvīṟu iyalumāṟu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ+ peṟutum. īṇṭum iṭam kuṟṟeḻuttiṉmēl varum valleḻuttu. (6)

TE40n

uruviṉ um icaiyiṉ um aruki+ tōṉṟu
moḻikkuṟippu ellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā
v-āytam aḵkā+ kālai y-āṉa.

iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ+ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki āytattiṟku eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uruviṉum icaiyiṉum aruki+ tōṉṟum^ kuṟippumoḻiyum --- niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōcaiyiṉkaṇṇum^ ciṟupāṉmai āytantōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittalaiyuṭaiya collum, ellāmoḻiyum --- avaiyoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā --- oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷpōla araimāttiraiyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai mikkum naṭantu, āytam aḵkā+ kālaiyāṉa --- āytam^ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, īṇṭu ārāycciyiṉṟēṉum^ ceyyuḷiyaliṉ+ kūṟum `oṟṟaḷapeṭuppiṉu maṟṟeṉamoḻipa` ((ceyyuḷiyal//18)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu+ `kaṇṇṭaṇṇeṉa+ kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum' eṉpuḻi+ caṇṇṇeṉpatu cīrnilai yeytiṉāṟpōlak `kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅkallatarattam' eṉa niṟattiṉkaṇṇum `cuḵḵṟeṉṉum^ taṇṭōṭṭu+ peṇṇai' eṉa icaiyiṉkaṇṇum vanta āytam orumāttiraipeṟṟu+ cīrnilai yeytuṅkāl, āṇṭu+ peṟukiṉṟa orumāttiraikku īṇṭu etiratupōṟṟi viti kūṟiṉār, āytam atikārappaṭṭamai kaṇṭu. `eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kaiyarā yiṉṉuyir --- veḵḵ kuvārkkillai vīṭu' eṉṟu ēṉaiyiṭattum vantaṉa. oṟṟaḷapeṭukkumāṟu ivvatikārattu+ kūṟiṟṟilar, aḵtu uyiraḷapeṭaipōla+ cīrnilaiyeytutalum acainilaiyāntaṉmaiyu muṭaiyavāy+ ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ. itaṉāṉē oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum oru māttirai peṟumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

eḻuttiṉeṉṟa iṉ uvamapporuḷ. iyalāveṉṟatu ceyyāveṉṉum viṉaiyeccam.

ivvāṟaṉṟi ikkuṟippuccoṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutappaṭāveṉṟu poruḷkūṟiṉ+ ceyyuḷiyalōṭu māṟupaṭṭu māṟukoḷa+kūṟal eṉṉum^ kuṟṟam^ taṅkum eṉṟu uṇarka. (7)

TE41n

kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikku
neṭṭeḻuttu impa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.

iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi+ ceyyuḷiyalainōkki `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ' ((eḻu//6)) eṉa muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭaiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu icai niṟaikkum --- aḷapeṭuttu+ kūṟākkāl+ kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya avvaḷapeṭai+ coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōcaiyai niṟaikkum, avai yāvaiyōveṉiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttē --- neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉāka+ tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉavarum. kuṉṟicai moḻi eṉṟataṟku icaikuṉṟumoḻi eṉṟumām. iṉamottalāvatu piṟappum puṇarcciyum ōcaiyum vaṭivum ottal. īṇṭu moḻiyeṉṟatu `aḷapeṭaiyacainilai' ((ceyyuḷiyal//17)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyal+ cūttirattu eṭṭu iyaṟcīriṉ pāṟpaṭukiṉṟa eṇvakai aḷapeṭaiccoṟkaḷaiyum. avai āa kaṭāa āaḻi paṭāakai āaṅku āavatu pukāarttu virāayatu eṉpaṉavām. kaṭṭaḷaikoḷḷā āciriyar ivaṟṟai+ taṉinilai mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟutinilai yeṉṟum aṭakkupa. iṉi moḻiyeṉṟataṟku+ taṉinilai ēḻaṉaiyumē koḷḷiṉ, oḻinta iyaṟcīrppāṟpaṭum aḷapeṭai kōṭaṟku iṭamiṉmai uṇarka. (8)

TE42n

aiau veṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
kikara vukara micainiṟai vākum.

iḵtu ottakuṟṟeḻuttu illātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

ituvum etiratu pōṟṟal.

itaṉ poruḷ: aiau eṉṉum āyīreḻuttiṟku --- tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟku, ikara ukaram icainiṟaivākum --- īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷai+ cārttikkūṟa, avai a+ kuṉṟicaimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōcaiyai niṟaippaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.

aii auu eṉa niraṉiṟaiyāka+ koḷka. ivaṟṟai muṟkūṟiya iyaṟcīreṭṭiṟkum ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu utāraṇam^ kāṭṭikkoḷka.

ittuṇaiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu. (9)

TE43n

neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē yoreḻuttu orumoḻi.

iḵtu ōreḻuttorumoḻi uṇarttutal nutaliyavaṟṟuḷ neṭṭeḻuttākum moḻiyākkam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē --- neṭṭeḻuttākiya uyirkaḷēḻum, ōreḻuttorumoḻi --- ōreḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟṟummai tokuttu īṟṟacaiyēkāram virittār.

utāraṇam: ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉavarum. aukāram uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārātu. ū eṉpatu tacai. iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum viti. kā tī pū cē tai kō kau eṉavarum. ivai tammaiyuṇaraniṉṟa vaḻi eḻuttām. iṭainiṉṟu poruḷuṇarttiyavaḻi+ collām. neṭṭeḻuttēṟiyamey neṭṭeḻuttāyum^ kuṟṟeḻuttēṟiyamey kuṟṟeḻuttāyum niṟṟalēyaṉṟi meykku neṭumaiyum^ kuṟumaiyum iṉmai uṇarka. (10)

TE44n

kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu moḻiniṟai pilavē.

itu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyākā. avaṟṟuḷ+ cila moḻiyākumeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum --- kuṟṟeḻuttākiya uyiraintum, moḻiniṟaipu ilavē --- tāmē niṟaintu niṉṟu moḻiyātal ila; cila meyyōṭu kūṭi niṟaintu niṉṟu moḻiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tu no eṉavarum. ivai uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārāmaiyāṉum, uyirkkaṇṇum, ēḷai akaramum ekaramum, akkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉa+ taṉittu niṉṟu uṇarttalāṟṟātu iṭaiccollāy+ peyarai+ cārntu niṉṟu cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum `niṟaipila' veṉṟār. muṟṟummai īṇṭu eccappaṭṭu niṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. (11)

TE45n

ōr eḻuttu oru moḻi y-īr eḻuttu oru moḻi
y-iraṇṭu* iṟantu* icaikkum^ toṭarmoḻi y-uḷa+paṭa
mūṉṟu* ē moḻi-nilai tōṉṟiya neṟi y-ē.

itu muṉṉar meymmayakkam uṭaṉilaimayakkam^ kūṟalāṉum īṇṭu `neṭṭeḻuttēḻē' ((eḻu//43)) eṉpataṉāṉum eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟiṉār, ammoḻikku iccūttirattāṉ+ peyarum muṟaiyum^ tokaiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟār.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi iraṇṭiṟantu icaikkum^ toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa --- ōreḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭaṉai iṟantu palavāṟṟāṉ icaikkum^ toṭarmoḻiyuṭaṉē kūṭa, moḻinilai mūṉṟē --- moḻikaḷiṉ nilaimai mūṉṟēyām. tōṉṟiya neṟiyē --- avai tōṉṟiya vaḻakkuneṟikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ā kā nā ōreḻuttorumoḻi, maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ īreḻuttorumoḻi, kuravu aravu mūveḻuttorumoḻi, kaṇaviri nāleḻuttorumoḻi, akattiyaṉār aiyeḻuttorumoḻi, tirucciṟṟampalam āṟeḻuttorumoḻi, perumpaṟṟappuliyūr ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi.

ōreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ toṭarmoḻiyu meṉṉātu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum ōtiṉār, cila pala eṉṉum^ tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki.

āciriyar oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉṟu koṇṭaṉarātaliṉ mā kā eṉa niṉṟa coṟkaḷ māl kāl eṉa oṟṟaṭuttuḻi oṟṟiṉāṉ vēṟu poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai īreḻuttorumoḻiyeṉṟum, nāku varaku eṉṉum^ kuṟṟukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ+ kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ colloṭukūṭi+ poruḷtantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai īreḻuttorumoḻi mūveḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟum^ kōṭumeṉpārkku, āciriyar poruḷai+ karutātu māttirai kuṟaintamaipaṟṟi `uyirileḻuttu meṇṇappaṭā' ((ceyyuḷiyal//44)) `kuṟilēneṭilē kuṟiliṇai' ((ceyyuḷiyal//3)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyal+ cūttiraṅkaḷāl ivaṟṟai eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā eṉṟu vilakkuvarātaliṉ, avaṟṟāl īṇṭu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum^ koḷḷiṉ, māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉṉum^ kuṟṟam^ taṅkumeṉṟu maṟukka.

iṉi `neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē yōreḻuttu orumoḻi' ((eḻu//43)) `kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu moḻiniṟai pilavē' ((eḻu//44)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ meykku+ kuṟumai neṭumai yiṉmaiyāṉ uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya neṭilum^ kuṟilumē moḻiyāmeṉṟu kūṟi, mīṭṭum ataṉaiyē iccūttirattāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟeṭuttu ataṉōṭē īreḻuttaiyum iraṇṭiṟantataṉaiyum^ kūṭṭi moḻiyāka+ kōṭaliṉ, oṟṟiṉai+ kūṭṭi eḻuttāka+ kōṭal āciriyarkku+ karuttaṉmaiyuṇarka. aṉṟiyum `moḻippaṭutticaippiṉum' ((eḻu//53)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉum uṇarka. `akara mutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa' ((eḻu//1)) eṉa oṟṟiṉaiyum eḻutteṉṟatu eḻuttiṉ taṉmai kūṟiṟṟu. īṇṭu moḻiyāntaṉmai kūṟiṟṟu. (12)

TE46n

meyyi ṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum.

itu taṉimeykaḷai akaram iyakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉiyakkam --- taṉimeykaḷiṉatu naṭappu, akaramoṭu civaṇum --- akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē oruvaṉ taṉimeykaḷai nāvāṉ+ karuttupporuḷākiya uruvāka iyakkum iyakkamum mūvakaiyāṉ+ kāṭci+ poruḷākiya vaṭivāka iyakkum iyakkamum akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `valleḻuttu eṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa' ((eḻu//19)) `kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā vaṇṇam' ((eḻu//89)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa nāvāl iyakkiyavāṟu kāṇka. eḻuti+ kāṭṭumiṭattu+ kakaram mutaliyaṉa uyirpeṟṟu niṉṟa vaṭivāka eḻuti+ piṉṉar+ taṉimeyyākkutaṟku+ puḷḷiyiṭṭu+ kāṭṭukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ vaṭivai iyakkumiṭattum akaram^ kalantu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.

iṅṅaṉam meykkaṇ akaram^ kalantu niṟkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṟpōla+ patiṉōruyirkkaṇṇum akaram^ kalantu niṟkumeṉpatu āciriyar kūṟārāyiṉār, annilaimai tamakkē pulappaṭuttalāṉum piṟarkku ivvāṟu uṇarttutal aritākalāṉumeṉṟu uṇarka. iṟaivaṉ iyaṅkutiṇaikkaṇṇum nilaittiṇaikkaṇṇum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyāy niṟkumāṟu ellārkkum oppa muṭintāṟpōla akaramum uyirkkaṇṇum^ taṉimeykkaṇṇum^ kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppamuṭintatu. `akaramutala' eṉṉum^ kuṟaḷāṉ akaramākiya mutalaiyuṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷellām, atupōla iṟaivaṉākiya mutalaiyuṭaittu ulakameṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār uvamai kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉuṅ, kaṇṇaṉ eḻuttukkaḷil akaramākiṉṟēṉ yāṉēyeṉa+ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum piṟanūlkaḷāṉum uṇarka.

itaṉāṉ uṇmaittaṉmaiyum^ ciṟitu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉ+ kūṟātu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār, `valleḻutteṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa'(eḻu//19) eṉṟa iṭattu+ tāṉ iṭainiṉṟu oṟṟeṉpatōr poruḷai uṇartti moḻiyāntaṉmai eytiniṟṟaliṉ. (13)

TE47n

tammiyal kiḷappi ṉellā veḻuttu
meynnilai mayakka māṉa millai.

itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy niṉṟakālattu ammeyyāṉ+ peyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellā eḻuttum --- paṉṉīruyirum, meynnilai tam iyal mayakkam^ kiḷappiṉ --- meyyiṉ taṉmaiyilē tammuṭaiya taṉmai mayaṅkiṟṟāka+ peyar kūṟiṉ, māṉamillai --- kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

meyyiṉ taṉmaiyāvatu vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai; tammiyalāvatu uyirttaṉmai; eṉṟatu valleḻuttu melleḻuttu iṭaiyeḻutteṉa uyirmeykkum peyariṭṭāḷutal kūṟiṟṟu. avai `valleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//19)) eṉavum, `melleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉiṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum ((eḻu//20)) eṉavum, `iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉpa yaralavaḻaḷa' ((eḻu//21)) eṉavum piṟāṇṭum āḷpa. eḻuttai vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimaiyeṉa vicēṭitta ciṟappāṉ ippeyar kūṟiṉār.

iḵtaṉṟi+ patiṉeṭṭumeyyiṉ taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu meymmayakkam^ kūṟiya vakaiyāṉaṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟi, `avaṟṟuḷ laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//24)) eṉpataṉai+ kāṭṭil aḵtu irumoḻikkaṇṇateṉa maṟukka. (14)

TE48n

yaraḻa veṉṉu mūṉṟu moṟṟa+
kacatapa ṅañanama vīroṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟa --- yaraḻa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷiyum oṟṟāyniṟpa, kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟākum --- kacatapakkaḷum ṅañanamakkaḷum vantu īroṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vēykka vāycci pāyttal vāyppu eṉavum, pīrkku nērcci vārttal ārppu eṉavum, vāḻkkai tāḻcci tāḻttal tāḻppu eṉavum, kāyṅkaṉi tēyñcatu kāyntaṉam kāympuṟam, eṉavum, nērṅkal nērñcilai nērntilai nērmpuṟam eṉavum varum. ḻakārattiṟku vāḻntaṉam ikkālattu nakaravoṟṟu varum. ēṉaiya mūṉṟum ikkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉiṉ uṇarka.

iṉi+ tāḻṅkulai tāḻñciṉai tāḻntiraḷ vīḻmpaṭai eṉa akkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉṟu ittokai+ coṟkaḷ kāṭṭalum oṉṟu. uraiyāciriyarum irumoḻikkaṇ+ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟku avvīṟukaṭōṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ piṉṉar vēṇṭāmai uṇarka. iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyātaliṉ īṇṭu vaittār.

iṉi neṭiṟkīḻēyaṉṟi+ palaveḻuttum^ toṭarntu niṉṟataṉ piṉṉum īroṟṟu varutal koḷka. avai vēntarkku aṉṉāykku eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavām. (15)

TE49n

avaṟṟuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram^ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.

iḵtu eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttal kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram --- rakāramum ḻakāramum, kuṟṟoṟṟākā --- kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā; neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām eṉṟavāṟu.

kīḻeṉṉum urupu tokuttu+ kūṟiṉār. ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇamiṉṟu.

utāraṇam: kār vīḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy vantaṉa. karu maḻu eṉa+ kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy vantaṉa. ivaṟṟai vilakkavē, yakaram poy eṉavum noy eṉavum iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāy varutal peṟṟām. pukar pukaḻ pulavar eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉavōveṉiṉ, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttiṉai+ colvaṉavaṟṟiṟkē īṇṭu ārāycciyātalāl avai vēṇṭiyavāṟē varumeṉṟu uṇarka. aṉṟiyum^ kuṟṟoṟṟeṉṟē cūttirañceytaliṉ+ kuṟiliṇai yoṟṟiṉai+ kāṭṭi+ kaṭāvalākāmai uṇarka. itu varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi uyirmeyyōṭu taṉimey mayaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ+ cila voṟṟiṟku varaiyaṟai īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu. (16)

TE50n

kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyu maḷaviṉ+ kōṭaliṉ+
ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻuttu iyala.

iḵtu `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkkiya nūṉmarapiṉ+ cūttirattiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭaiyāy ataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟukiṉṟatu; eṉṉai? uyirum uyirmeyyum aḷapiṟantu icaikkuṅkāl+ kuṟilō neṭilō icaippateṉa māṇākkarkku nikaḻvatōr aiyam aṟuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum --- eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiyataṉmaiyum neṭiyataṉmaiyum, aḷaviṟkōṭaliṉ --- māttiraiyeṉṉum uṟuppiṉai+ cevi karuviyāka aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭku+ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻiyellām --- ammāttirai tammuḷ+ ṭoṭarntu niṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻuttiyala --- neṭṭeḻuttu māttirai mikku naṭakkumpaṭiyāka+ toṭarnta collām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `varuvarkolvayaṅkiḻāay' ((kali//11)) eṉavum, `kaṭiyavēkaṉaṅkuḻāay' (kali11) eṉavum^ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām neṭṭeḻuttiṉai māttirai mikuttaṟku+ kūṭiyavāṟu uṇarka. ēṉai+ ceyyaṭkaḷaiyum ivvāṟē kāṇka.

eṉavē, māttirai aḷakkuṅkāl neṭṭeḻuttē māttirai peṟṟu mikku niṟkum eṉṟamaiyāṉ, etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi+ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki+ kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār, neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭu māttiraiyiṉ ikantuvarumeṉpatu aṟivittaṟku.

aḷapeṉṟu māttiraiyai+ kūṟātu aḷaveṉa+ cūttiram^ ceytamaiyāṉ aḷavu toḻiṉmēṉiṉṟatu. atu ceyyuḷiyaluḷ `māttiraiyaḷavum' ((ceyyuḷiyal//2)) eṉpataṉāṉum uṇarka. iyalaveṉṟataṉai+ ceyaveṉeccamākki+ paṭuttalōcaiyāṉ+ kūṟuka.

iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. `kuraṅṅkuḷai+ polinta koycuvaṟ puravi' ((akam//4)) eṉṟa kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇattiṟku uriya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām iṭaiyiṉiṉṟa oṟṟeḻuttai māttirai mikuttaṟku+ kūṭiniṉṟavāṟu uṇarka. eṉavē, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷōṭum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷōṭum^ kūṭi avaṟṟaiyē ōcaimikuttu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē oṟṟicai nīṭalumeṉṟa oṟṟicai nīḷuṅkāl+ kuṟṟeḻuttāy nīḷumeṉṟār. iṉi uraiyāciriyar pukar pukaḻ eṉa+ kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ vanta toṭarmoḻikaḷellām^ tār tāḻ eṉṟāṟpōla ōcaiyottu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ taṉmaiyavām eṉṟārāleṉiṉ, pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai neṭṭeḻutteṉṟē evviṭattum āḷāmaiyāṉum neṭṭeḻuttāka+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattāl orupayaṉ koḷḷāmaiyāṉum^ ceyyaḷiyaluḷ ivaṟṟai+ kuṟiliṇai oṟṟaṭutta niraiyacaiyākavum^ tār tāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai neṭṭeḻuttu oṟṟaṭutta nēracaiyākavum^ kōṭalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmai uṇarka. (17)

TE51n

ceyyu ḷiṟuti+ pōli moḻivayi
ṉakāra makāra mīroṟ ṟākum.

itu ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyuṭpōli moḻi iṟutivayiṉ --- ceyyuṭkaṇ+ pōlumeṉṉum^ colliṉ iṟutikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īroṟṟākum --- ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu uṭaṉilaiyāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `annūlai munnūlā+ koḷvāṉum pōṉm' `citaiyum^ kalattai+ payiṉāṉ+ ^tirutti+ --- ticai y-aṟi mīkāṉ um pōṉm' ((pari//10-55)) eṉa varum. pōlum eṉṉum^ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟu īṟṟumicaiyukaram meyyoḻittu+ keṭṭu lakāram^ tirintu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṟutiyil muṟṟu iṭaiyiṉ+ peyareccamākiya uvamavurupu. īṇṭu muṟṟeṉpār iṟutimoḻi eṉṟār. (18)

TE52n

ṉakārai muṉṉar makāram^ kuṟukum.

iḵtu araiyaḷapu kuṟukumeṉṟa makārattiṟku+ kuṟukum iṭam ituveṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakārai muṉṉar makāram^ kuṟukum --- muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta makaram^ taṉ araimāttiraiyiṉ+ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pōṉm eṉa muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉām. ṉakāraiyeṉa iṭaiccol īṟutirintu niṉṟatu.

iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ ṇakāravoṟṟiṉ muṉṉum makāram^ kuṟukutal koḷka. `maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇm' eṉa varum. (19)

TE53n

moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum^ terintuvē ṟicaippiṉu
meḻuttiya ṟiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi eṇṇappaṭṭu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ eṇṇappaṭātu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: terintu --- oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷtaru nilaimaiyai ārāyntu, moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum --- collāka+ cērttu+ colliṉum, vēṟu icaippiṉum --- ceyyuḷiyaluḷ oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷ tarumēṉum māttirai kuṟaintu niṟkum nilaimaiyai nōkki eḻutteṇṇappaṭāveṉṟu āṇṭaikku vēṟāka+ kūṟiṉum, eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avviraṇṭiṭattum araimāttirai peṟṟu niṟkum oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum muṟkūṟiya eḻuttām^ taṉmai tiriyāveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉāṉ oṟṟum āytamum^ kuṟṟukaramum eḻuttāki niṉṟu poruḷ tantum, eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāveṉpatu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. terintu vēṟicaittal kuṟṟukarattiṟku iṉṟātaliṉ ēṟpuḻikkōṭalāṉ oṟṟiṟkum āytattiṟkum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: al il uṇ eṇ ol eṉavum, kal vil muḷ cel col eṉavum, āl īr ūr ēr ōr eṉavum, kāl cīr cūl tēṉ kōṉ eṉavum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya kuṟṟeḻuttaiyum neṭṭeḻuttaiyum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aṭuttuniṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭam kaṭām uṭaiyāṉ tiruvārūr akattiyaṉār eṉa īreḻuttaiyum mūveḻuttaiyum nāleḻuttaiyum aiyeḻuttaiyum iṟutiyilum iṭaiyilum oṟṟaṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka. eḵku teḷku kokku kuraṅku eṉpaṉavum eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāta kuṟṟukaram aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. `uyiri leḻuttu meṇṇappaṭāa --- uyirttiṟa miyakka miṉmai yāṉa' ((ceyyuḷiyal//44)) eṉpatu eḻuttu eṇṇappeṟāmaikku viti.

iṉi iccūttirattiṟku eḻuttukkaḷai+ collākki+ kūṟiṉum piṟitāka+ kūṟiṉum māttirai tiriyāteṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, akaram eṉpuḻiyum a eṉpuḻiyum ālam eṉpuḻiyum ā eṉpuḻiyum kakaram eṉpuḻiyum^ ka eṉpuḻiyum^ kālam eṉpuḻiyum^ kā eṉpuḻiyum ōcai ottu niṟkumeṉṟāl, atu muṉṉar+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāṉ+ peṟappaṭutaliṉ+ payaṉil kūṟṟāmeṉka. (20)

TE54n

akara ikara maikāra mākum.

itu cilaveḻuttukkaḷ kūṭi+ cilaveḻuttukkaḷpōla icaikkumeṉa eḻuttuppōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ikaram aikāram ākum --- akaramum ikaramum^ kūṭṭi+ colla aikārampōla icaikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.

utāraṇam: aivaṉam aivaṉam eṉavarum. ākumeṉṟataṉāl iḵtu ilakkaṇamaṉṟāyiṟṟu. (21)

TE55n

akara ukara maukāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ukaram aukāram ākum --- akaramum ukaramum kūṭṭiccolla aukārampōla icaikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu. pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.

utāraṇam: auvai auvai eṉavarum. (22)

TE56n

akarattu impar yakara+ puḷḷiyu
maiye ṉeṭuñciṉai meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akarattimpar yakarappuḷḷiyum --- akarattiṉpiṉ ikaramēyaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷi vantālum, aiyeṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivupeṟa+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aivaṉam ayvaṉam eṉavarum. meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ akarattiṉpiṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟi vakarappuḷḷiyum aukārampōla varumeṉṟu koḷka. auvai avvai eṉavarum. (23)

TE57n

ōraḷa pāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
tērum^ kālai moḻivayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu atikārattāṉ aikārattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum etiratu pōṟṟaleṉpataṉātal+ ceyyuḷiyalainōkki māttirai+ curukkam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: moḻivayiṉāṉa --- oru colliṭattē niṉṟa aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ, tēruṅkālai --- ārāyumiṭattu, ōraḷapākum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē --- orumāttiraiyāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummaiyāṉ iraṇṭumāttirai peṟutalē valiyuṭaittāyiṟṟu. iṭaṉumeṉṟatu orucolliṉ mutaliṭaikaṭaiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum^ kuṟukum, atu ceyyuṭkaṇ ōcai iṭarppaṭṭolikkumiṭattu+ kuṟukumeṉṟataṟku, uraiyiṟkōṭalāl aikāram mutaliṭaikaṭaiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum^ kuṟukum, aukāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukumeṉakkoḷka.

utāraṇam: aippaci kaippai iṭaiyaṉ kuvaḷai eṉa varum. `aṭaippaiyāykōṟā' eṉavum, `puṉaiyiḷaṅkoṅkaiyāy varum' eṉavum piṟavāṟum varuvaṉa ceyyuḷiyaluḷ+ kāṇka. auvai kauvai eṉavarum. aukāram `kauvai nīrvēlikūṟṟu' ((veṇpā//23)) eṉa+ toṭainōkki+ kuṟukiṉavāṟum^ kāṇka. tēruṅkālai yeṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ kuṟukum. kai pai eṉavarum. (24)

TE58n

ikara yakara miṟuti viravum.

ituvum pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ikarayakaram iṟutiviravum --- ikaramum yakaramum orumoḻiyiṉ iṟutikkaṇ ōcai viravivarum, avvikāram^ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

nāy nāi eṉavarum. (25)

TE59n

paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum.

itu mēl eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇartti ammoḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu ivaiyeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉṉīruyirum --- paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum, moḻimutal ākum --- moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṭai āṭai ilai īyam uḷai ūrti eḻu ēṇi aivaṉam oḷi ōṭam auviyam eṉavarum. (26)

TE60n

uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.

iḵtu uyirmey moḻikku mutalām eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmeyyallaṉa moḻimutal ākā --- uyirōṭukūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, uyirōṭukūṭiya meykaḷē moḻikku mutalāvaṉa eṉṟavāṟām. īṇṭu uyirmeyyeṉṟatu vēṟṟumainayaṅkarutiṟṟu. oṟṟumainayam^ karutiṉ mēlaiccūttirattu uyirōṭuṅkūṭi āmeṉṟal payaṉiṉṟām. (27)

TE61n

ka ta na pa ma ~eṉum ā ~aintu* eḻuttu* um*
ellā ~uyiroṭu* um^ cellumār mutal ē.

itu mēl+ potuvakaiyāṉ eytuvitta irunūṟṟorupattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷai+ ciṟappuvakaiyāṉ varaiyaṟuttu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: katanapama eṉum āvainteḻuttum --- katanapama eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avvaintu taṉimeyyum, ellā uyiroṭum^ cellumār mutalē --- paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟku+ cellum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalai kār kiḷi kīri kuṭi kūṭu keṇṭai kēḻal kaitai koṇṭal kōṭai kauvai eṉavum, tantai tāy titti tīmai tuṇi tūṇi teṟṟi tēṉ taiyal toṇṭai tōṭu tauvai eṉavum, nantu nārai nilam nīlam nukam nūl neytal nēmi naivaḷam nocci nōkkam nauvi eṉavum, paṭai pāl piṭi pīṭu pukaḻ pūmi peṭai pēṭai paital poṉ pōtu pauvam eṉavum, maṭi mālai miṭaṟu mīḷi mukam mūppu melivu mēṉi maiyal moḻi mōttai mauval eṉavum varum. (28)

TE62n

cakara+ kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai auveṉu mūṉṟalam^ kaṭaiyē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cakara+ kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē --- cakaramākiya taṉimeyyum muṟkūṟiyavaipōla ellā uyirōṭum^ kūṭi moḻikku mutalām, a ai au eṉum mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē --- akara aikāraaukārameṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu uyirum allāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāntu ciṟṟil cīṟṟam curai cūral cekku cēval col cōṟu eṉavarum. caṭṭi cakaṭam camaḻppu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa `kaṭicollillai' ((eccaviyal//56)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. caiyam cauriyam eṉpavaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (29)

TE63n

uū oō veṉṉu nāṉkuyir
va eṉ ṉeḻuttoṭu varuta lillai.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uū oō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir --- u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutalillai --- va eṉṟu collappaṭum^ taṉimeyyeḻuttōṭu kūṭi moḻikkumutalāy varutalillai eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, oḻintaṉa moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: vaḷai vāḷi viḷari vīṭu veḷḷi vēṭkai vaiyam vauvutal eṉavarum (30)

TE64n

ā e, o eṉu mūvuyir ñakārattu uriya.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ā e o eṉum mūvuyir --- ā e o eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu uyirum, ñakārattu uriya --- ñakāra oṟṟoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalātaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉaiya uriyavalla eṉpatām.

utāraṇam: ñālam ñeṇṭu ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.

`ñamalitantamaṉavuccūluṭumpu' ((perumpāṇ//132)) eṉpatu ticaiccol. ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku. (31)

TE65n

āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu --- ākārattōṭu kūṭiyallatu yakaravoṟṟu moḻikku mutalākātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉai yāṭu yāmam eṉavarum.

yavaṉar yutti yūpam yōkam yauvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (32)

TE66n

mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum.

itu moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalāvum --- moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum, ēṉavum --- moḻikku mutalā meṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum, tampeyar mutalum --- tattampeyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

mutalāvum ēṉavum eṉṟa ummaikaḷ tokkuniṉṟaṉa.

utāraṇam: ṅakaramum ṭakaramum ṇakaramum rakaramum lakaramum ḻakaramum ḷakaramum ṟakaramum ṉakaramum eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta oṉpatum mutalāmāṟu, ṅakkaḷaintār ṭapperitu ṇannaṉṟu eṉavarum. ivvāṟē ēṉaiyavaṟṟaiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi ēṉaveṉṟataṉāṉ kakkaḷaintār tapperitu akkuṟitu āneṭitu eṉa moḻikkumutalāmavaṟṟaiyum^ tampeyar kūṟutaṟku mutalāmāṟu oṭṭikkoḷka. varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭu moḻikku mutalākātu niṉṟa meykkum ivvitikoḷka. avai cakarattu mūṉṟum vakarattu nāṉkum ñakarattu oṉpatum yakarattu+ patiṉoṉṟumām. (33)

TE67n

kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅki
ṉoṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷai moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟi muṟaiyē kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ --- kuṟṟiyalukaramāṉatu muṉṉilaimuṟai+ peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum --- taṉimeyyāy niṉṟa nakarattumēṉiṉṟa nakarattōṭukūṭi moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

nuntai eṉavarum. itaṉāṉē muṟaippeyar iṭamum nakaram paṟṟukkōṭumāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyppiṉṉar niṉṟatēṉum oṟṟumai nayattāṉ moḻikku mutaleṉṟār. itu ceyyuḷiyalainōkki+ kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. (34)

TE68n

muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
tappeyar maruṅki ṉilaiyiya lāṉa.

itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: appeyarmaruṅkiṉ nilaiyiyalāṉa --- ammuṟaippeyariṭattē niṟṟalilakkaṇamāṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram, muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭāatu --- itaḻkuvittu+ kūṟumvaḻi varum muṟṟukarattōṭu avviṭattu+ kuṟṟukaram poruḷ vēṟupaṭumāṟupōla īṇṭu+ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu nillātu eṉṟavāṟu.

kātu kaṭṭu kattu marukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāy+ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭuniṉṟāṟpōla tantaiyeṉṟu itaḻkuvittu muṟṟakkūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻkuviyāmal+ kuṟaiyakkūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē tantavāṟu kāṇka. nuntāyeṉpatōveṉiṉ aḵtu itaḻkuvittē kūṟavēṇṭutaliṉ+ kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉṟeṉṟu koḷka. itaṉāṉē moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu+ toṇṇūṟṟunāṉkeṉṟu uṇarka. (35)

TE69n

uyirau veñciya viṟuti yākum.

iḵtu uyirmoḻikku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyir au eñciya iṟutiyākum --- uyirkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintaṉavellām moḻikku īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, aukāravuyir īṟākātāyiṟṟu. iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.

ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉa ivaitāmē īṟāyiṉa. āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉa+ kuṟilaintum aḷapeṭaikkaṇ īṟāyiṉa. kā tī pū cē kai kō eṉavum, viḷa kiḷi maḻu eṉavum varum. ekara okaram mēlē vilakkupa. `aḷapeṭai mikūu mikara viṟupeyar' ((col//127)) eṉpārātaliṉ, aḷapeṭaippiṉ vanta kuṟṟeḻuttum^ koḷvar āciriyareṉṟu uṇarka. neṭṭeḻuttēḻum mutaṉ moḻiyāmeṉṉum^ tuṇaiyē muṉṉuṇarttutaliṉ īṇṭu avai īṟāmeṉṟum uṇarttiṉār. (36)

TE70n

kavavō ṭiyaiyi ṉauvu mākum.

iḵtu īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻiyāmeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: auvum --- muṉ īṟākāteṉṟa aukāramum, kavavōṭu iyaiyiṉ ākum --- kakaravakarattōṭu iyainta vaḻi īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kau vau eṉavarum. eṉavē, oḻinta uyirellām^ tāmē niṉṟum patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷōṭum^ kūṭi niṉṟum īṟātal itaṉāṉ+ peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē aukāram ēṉai meykkaṇ vārāteṉa vilakkutalum peṟṟām. uyir ṅakarattōṭu kūṭi moḻikku īṟāmeṉpatu itaṉāl eytiṟṟēṉum atu moḻikku īṟākāmai tantu puṇarnturaittalāṉ uṇarka. itu varaiyaṟai kūṟiṟṟu. (37)

TE71n

e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.

iḵtu ekaram^ tāṉēniṉṟa vaḻiyaṉṟi meyyōṭu kūṭiṉāl īṟākāteṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: e eṉa varum uyir mey īṟākātu --- e eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uyir tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi yāṇṭum meykaḷōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu eṉṟavāṟu. (38)

TE72n

ovvu maṟṟē navvalam^ kaṭaiyē.

itu vilakkum varaiyaṟaiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ovvum aṟṟu --- okaramum muṉcoṉṉa ekarampōla+ tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meykaḷōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu, navvalaṅkaṭaiyē --- nakaravoṟṟoṭu allāta iṭattil eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: no koṟṟā `noalaiyaṉiṉṉāṭṭainī' eṉavarum. (39)

TE73n

ē ō eṉumuyir ñakārattu illai.

itu cilauyir cila uṭalōṭēṟi muṭiyāteṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēō eṉum uyir ñakārattillai --- ēō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭuyirum^ tāmēniṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭu niṉṟum īṟātalaṉṟi ñakārattōṭu īṟātalillai eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉaiyuyirkaḷ ñakārattōṭu īṟāmeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: uriña uriñā uriñi uriñī uriñu uriñū ivai eccamum viṉaippeyarum paṟṟivarum. aññai maññai ivai peyar. ēṉaiyaintum vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. uriñō eṉpatu `kaṭicollillai' eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. (40)

TE74n

u ū kāra navavoṭu navilā.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: u ūkāram --- ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ tāmē niṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭuniṉṟum payilvataṉṟi, navavoṭu navilā --- nakara voṟṟōṭum vakaravoṟṟōṭum payilā eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉaiyuyirkaḷ nakaravakaraṅkaḷōṭu varumāyiṉa.

utāraṇam: nakaram poruna eṉa viṉaippeyarākiyum, nā nī nē eṉa+ peyarākiyum, nai no nō eṉa viyaṅkōḷākiyum varum. porunai eṉṟum^ kāṭṭupa. vakaram uva vē eṉa viyaṅkōḷāyum, uvā cevvi vī vai eṉa+ peyarāyum varum. oruva oruvā oruvi oruvī auvai eṉṟum^ kāṭṭupa. īṇṭu vilakkāta ēṉai yuyirkaḷōṭu vanta nakaravakaraṅkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kōṭum. ivvitiyāl iṉi navilāveṉṟataṉāṉē vakara vukaram katavu turavu kuvavu puṇarvu nukarvu novvu kavvu eṉa+ payiṉṟu varutalum^ koḷka. (41)

TE75n

uccakāram iru moḻikku urittē.

itu cakāra ukāram palacoṟku īṟāy vārātu irucoṟku īṟāmeṉṟu varaiyaṟai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uccakāram --- ukārattōṭu kūṭiya cakāram, irumoḻikku urittu --- iraṇṭu moḻikkē īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. urittēyeṉṉum ēkāram moḻikkēyeṉa+ kūṭṭuka.

utāraṇam: ucu; iḵtu uḷuviṉpeyar. mucu; itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ orucāti. pacu eṉpatōveṉiṉ, aḵtu āriya+ citaivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukaram ēṟiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉai uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāmarapiṟṟu. uca ucā vici cē kaccai cō eṉa+ peyarāyum, tuñcu eñcā eñci mucī mucū eṉa eccamāyum varum. accō eṉa viyappāyum varum. iṉṉum ivai vaḻakkiṉkaṇ+ palavāmāṟum uṇarka. (42)

TE76n

uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
viruvayi ṉilaiyum poruṭṭā kummē.

iḵtu orucolvaraiyaṟaiyum aḵtu ōcaivēṟṟumaiyāl iruporuḷ tarumeṉavum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa --- ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram orumoḻikkallatu paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar. iruvayiṉilaiyum poruṭṭākummē --- atu tāṉ taṉviṉai piṟaviṉai yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilaipeṟum poruṇmaittām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tapu eṉavarum. itu paṭuttukkūṟa nī cāveṉa+ taṉviṉaiyām. eṭuttukkūṟa nī oṉṟaṉai+ cāvappaṇṇeṉa+ piṟaviṉaiyām. uppu kappu eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram oṉṟeṉavē ēṉaiyuyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiya pakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāy+ palaporuḷ tarumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. maṟantapa tuppā eṉa eccamāyum, nampi cempū pē petumpai eṉa+ peyarāyum, pō eṉa ēvalāyum varum. ivaṟṟai+ piṟacoṟkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. ēṉai īkārapakaram iṭakkarāy vaḻaṅkum. (43)

TE77n

eñciya vellā meñcuta lilavē.

itu muṉṉar moḻikku īṟāmeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ eñci niṉṟaṉa moḻikku īṟāmāṟum moḻikku īṟākāveṉṟavai tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku īṟāmāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eñciyavum eñcutalila --- `kavavōṭiyaiyiṉ' ((eḻu//70)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ patiṉōruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meykkaṇṇum vantu moḻikku īṟāmeṉṟa potuvitiyiṟ, piṉṉai vicēṭittu+ kūṟiyavaṟṟai oḻintaṉavum moḻikku īṟātaṟku oḻivila, ellām eñcutalila --- moḻikku īṟākāteṉṟa uyirmeykaḷum^ tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻivila eṉṟavāṟu.

ellāmeṉṟatu colliṉeccam^ colliyāṅku*uṇarttal eṉṉum utti. ummai virikka. īṇṭu eñciyaveṉṟatu muṉṉar utāraṇam^ kāṭṭiya ñakaramum nakaramum vakaramum cakaramum pakaramum orumoḻikkum īṟākāta ṅakaramum oḻinta paṉṉiraṇṭu meykkaṇṇum ekaramum okaramum aukāramum oḻinta oṉpatuyirum ēṟi moḻikku īṟāy varuvaṉavaṟṟaiyeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: varuka pukā vīṅki pukī ceku pukū īṅkē maṅkai eṅkō eṉavum, kaṭṭa kaṭā maṭi maṭī maṭu paṭū paṭai eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, maṇṇa eṇṇā kaṇṇi uṇī kaṇu kaṇṇū paṇṇai eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, ata putā pati vati atu kaitū tantai antō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, kama neṉmā ammi mī cemmu koṇmū yāmai kāttumvammō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, ceya kāyā koyyū aiyai aiyō eṉavum, itaṟku ikara īkāra ukara ēkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, vā tārā pari kurī karu veru nārai eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, cila palā vali valī valu kollū vallē kalai eṉavum, itaṟku ōkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, toḻa viḻā nāḻi vaḻī maḻu eḻū tāḻai eṉavam, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, uḷa uḷḷā veḷḷi kuḷī uḷu eḷḷū kaḷai eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, kaṟṟa kaṟṟā uṟi uṟī maṟu uṟū kaṟṟai eṟṟō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, naṉa kaṉā vaṉṉi tuṉī muṉṉu tuṉṉū eṉṉē aṉṉai aṉṉō eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ peyarāyum viṉaiyāyum varuvaṉa uṇarka. ivaṟṟuḷ+ kakaraṉakaraṅkaḷ vilakkāta oṉpatum vantaṉa. āka īṟu nūṟṟunāṟpattumūṉṟum utāraṇamillāta patiṉeṭṭum āka nūṟṟaṟupattoṉṟu. ṅakaram moḻikku īṟākāteṉpatu perumpāṉmaiyātaliṉ+ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṉi ṅakaramum aukāramum ēṟāta mey patiṉaintum ekaramum okaramum ēkāramum ōkāramum ukaramum ūkāramum ēṟāta meykaḷum^ tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku īṟāmāṟu, ṅapperitu cauaḻakitu ñautītu eṉavarum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. kekkuṟaintatu kottītu ñevvaḻakitu ñottītu nunnaṉṟu nūpperitu vucciṟitu vūpperitu eṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyam ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. iṉṉum ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉē kannaṉṟu ānaṉṟu eṉa moḻikku īṟu* āvaṉa v-um^ tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi āmeṉṟu koḷka. (44)

TE78n

ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉu
mappati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.

itu muṉṉar uyir īṟāmāṟu uṇartti+ puḷḷikaḷuḷ īṟāvaṉa ivaiyeṉkiṉṟatu.

TE79n

ucca kāramoṭu nakāram^ civaṇum

TE80n

uppa kāramoṭu ñakāraiyu maṟṟē
yapporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇai yāṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uppakāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟē --- ukārattōṭu kūṭiya pakarattōṭu ñakaramum ottu orumoḻikku īṟām, ivaṇaiyāṉa apporuḷ iraṭṭātu --- ivviṭattu ñakārattiṉ kaṇṇāṉa apporuḷ pakarampōla iruporuṭpaṭātu eṉṟavāṟu.

TE81n

TE82n

makara+ toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta

āytam vikāram.

utāraṇam: ekiṉ cekiṉ viḻaṉ payiṉ kuyiṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ kaṭāṉ vayāṉ eṉavarum. ekiṉ ekiṉam eṉṟāṟpōla vēṟōr peyarāy+ tirivaṉavum^ cantiyāṉ+ tirivaṉavumāy īṟṟuḷ+ tiripuṭaiyaṉa kaḷaintu oṉpatum varum. mēṟkaṇṭu koḷka. nilam nilaṉ pilam pilaṉ kalam kalaṉ valam valaṉ ulam ulaṉ kulam kulaṉ kaṭam kaṭaṉ polam polaṉ pulam pulaṉ nalam nalaṉ kuḷam kuḷaṉ vaḷam vaḷaṉ eṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa tammuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa. vaṭṭam kuṭṭam ōṭam pāṭam ivaipōlvaṉa mayaṅkātaṉa. varaiyaṟai ṉakarattiṉ mēl+ cellum. mayaṅkāveṉavē mayakkamum peṟṟām. ((moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((3. piṟappiyal))

TE83n

unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi+
talaiyiṉu miṭaṟṟiṉu neñciṉu nilaii+
pallu mitaḻu nāvu mūkkum
aṇṇamu muḷappaṭa veṇmuṟai nilaiyā
yellā veḻuttum^ collum^ kālai
piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
tiṟappaṭa+ teriyum^ kāṭci yāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu piṟappu uṇarttutaliṉ+ piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu eḻuttum^ taṉimeyyum moḻiyiṉaṉṟi uṇarttalākāmaiyiṉ avai piṟakkum moḻiyai moḻimarapiṭai uṇartti+ piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉar vaiyātu itaṉai moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉar vaittār.

iccūttiram eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu potuppiṟavi ittuṇai nilaikkaḷattu niṉṟu pulappaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkam^ colluṅkālai --- tamiḻeḻuttu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravai yām^ kūṟumiṭattu, untimutalā+ tōṉṟi muntu vaḷi --- koppūḻaṭiyāka+ tōṉṟi muntukiṉṟa utāṉaṉeṉṉum^ kāṟṟu, talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaii --- talaiyiṉkaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nilaipeṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ uṟuppuṟṟu --- pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamu meṉṟa aintuṭaṉē akkāṟṟu, niṉṟa talaiyum miṭaṟum neñcum^ kūṭa eṭṭākiya muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya taṉmaiyōṭu kūṭiya uṟuppukkaḷōṭu oṉṟuṟṟu, amaiya --- iṅṅaṉam amaitalāṉē, vēṟuvēṟu iyala --- avveḻuttukkaḷatu tōṟṟaravu vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalaiyuṭaiya, kāṭciyāṉa nāṭi neṟippaṭa --- ataṉai aṟivāṉ ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉampaṭa, tiṟappaṭa+ teriyum --- appiṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷellām^ kūṟupaṭa viḷaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

colluṅkālai vaḷi nilaipeṟṟu uṟuppukkaḷuṟṟu iṅṅaṉam amaitalāṉē avai vaḻaṅkutalaiyuṭaiya; avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkam avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉam^ tiṟappaṭa+ teriyumeṉa+ kūṭṭi uraittu+ koḷka. iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē muyaṟciyum muyaluṅkaruttāvum uṇmaipeṟṟām. (1)

TE84n

av vaḻi
paṉṉīr uyir um^ tam^ nilai tiriyā
miṭaṟṟu+ piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum.

iḵtu uyireḻuttiṟku+ potuppiṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉṉīruyirum tannilai tiriyā --- paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum^ tattam māttirai tiriyāvāy, avvaḻippiṟanta --- avvuntiyiṭattu+ piṟanta, miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyiṉ icaikkum --- miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇ nilaipeṟṟa kāṟṟāṉ olikkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaram um^ kuṟṟiyalukaram um^ tannilai tiriyumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. avveḻuttukkaḷai+ kūṟi uṇarka. (2)

TE85n

avaṟṟuḷ
a ā āy iraṇṭu* aṅkāntu* iyalum.

iḵtu avvuyirkaḷuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ ciṟappu+ piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu --- akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya avviraṇṭum, aṅkāntu iyalum --- aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉ+ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

muyaṟci uyirkkiḻavaṉkaṇṇatu. a ā eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (3)

TE86n

i ī e ē aiyeṉa vicaikku
mappā laintu mavaṟṟē raṉṉa
vavaitā
maṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa luṭaiya.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: i ī e ē ai eṉa icaikkum appālaintum --- i ī e ē ai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum akkūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa --- akara ākāraṅkaḷpōla aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉ+ piṟakkum, avaitām aṇpal mutal nā viḷimpu uṟal uṭaiya --- avaitām aṅṅaṉam piṟakkumāyiṉum aṇpallum aṭināviḷimpum uṟa+ piṟakkum vēṟupāṭuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

aṇpal viṉaittokai. eṉavē, nāviḷimpu aṇukutaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa palleṉṟu ataṟkōr peyarāyiṟṟu. i ī e ē ai eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (4)

TE87n

u ū o ō au eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (5)

TE88n

tattam^ tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.

itu muṟkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum potuvitikūṟi+ ciṅkanōkkāka+ kiṭantatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tattam^ tiripē ciṟiya eṉpa --- uyirkaḷum meykaḷum ovvoru tāṉaṅkaḷuḷ+ piṟappaṉavaṟṟai+ kūṭṭi+ kūṟiṉēmāyiṉum nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyumiṭattu+ tammuṭaiya tammuṭaiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ ciṟiyavāka uṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

avai eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital vilaṅkal eṉṟavāṟṟāṉum^ talaivaḷi neñcuvaḷi miṭaṟṟuvaḷi mūkkuvaḷi eṉṟavāṟṟāṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum vēṟupaṭumāṟu nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaiyōr kūṟi uṇarka. ai vilaṅkaluṭaiyatu. valliṉam^ talaivaḷiyuṭaiyatu, melliṉam mūkkuvaḷiyuṭaiyatu. iṭaiyiṉam miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyuṭaiyatu. ēṉaiyavum^ kūṟi+ kaṇṭu uṇarka. (6)

TE89n

kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā vaṇṇam.

itu meykaḷuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟappu+ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kakāra ṅakāram mutal nā aṇṇam --- kakāramum ṅakāramum mutal nāvum mutal aṇṇamum uṟappiṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

uyirmeyyāka+ cūttirattu+ kūṟiṉum^ taṉimeyyāka+ kūṟi+ kāṇka. mutalai iraṇṭiṟkum^ kūṭṭuka. ka ṅa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. 97)

aṇṇa naṇṇiya paṉmutaṉ maruṅki

TE101n

cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉa+
tērntuveḷi+ paṭutta vēṉai mūṉṟum^

yotta kāṭciyiṉ+ ṟammiyal piyalum.
