kāṭciyeṉṟatu neñciṉai. kēṇmiyā nāku nuntai eṉavum eḵku eṉavum varum.

āytattiṟku+ cārpiṭam^ `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//38)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. iṉi āytam^ talaivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakkumeṉpārum uḷar. moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ aḷapeṭaiyum uyirmeyyum^ tammai ākkiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (19)

TE102n

ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭa+ kiḷantu
colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ+
piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārattu
akatteḻu vaḷiyicai yariṟapa nāṭi
yaḷapiṉ+ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittē
yaḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟattu icaikku
meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaṭam piṟappiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāveḻuttum^ kiḷantu veḷippaṭa --- āciriyaṉ ellāveḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkumāṟu muntunūṟkaṇṇē kūṟi veḷippaṭukkaiyiṉālē, colliya paḷḷi piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi --- yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiya eṇvakai nilattum piṟakkiṉṟa piṟappōṭē avveḻuttukkaḷai+ kūṟumiṭattu, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ uṟaḻccivārattiṉ aḷapu kōṭal --- yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟu aṉṟi untiyil tōṉṟum^ kāṟṟiṉatu tiritarum^ kūṟṟiṉ kaṇṇē māttiraikūṭṭikkōṭalum, akattu eḻu vaḷiyicai ariltapanāṭi+ kōṭal --- mūlātārattil eḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉōcaiyai+ kuṟṟamaṟa nāṭikkōṭalum, antaṇar maṟaittē --- pārppāratu vētattu uḷatē; annilaimai āṇṭu uṇarka, aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu --- aṅṅaṉam kōṭalai īṇṭu+ kūṟalākāmaiyiṉ innūṟkaṭkūṟātē, eḻuntu puṟattu icaikkum --- untiyiṟṟōṉṟi+ puṟattē pulappaṭṭu olikkum, meyteri vaḷiyicai aḷavu nuvaṉ ṟiciṉē --- poruṭeriyum^ kāṟṟiṉatu tuṇiviṟkē yāṉ māttirai kūṟiṉēṉ; avaṟṟiṉatu māttiraiyai uṇarka eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉai iraṇṭu cūttiramākkiyum uraippa.

itu piṟaṉkōṭkūṟaleṉṉum uttikku iṉam. eṉṉai? untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟiṉai+ kūṟupaṭutti māttiraikūṭṭi+ kōṭalum mūlātāram mutalāka+ kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟalum vētattiṟku uḷateṉṟu ivvāciriyar kūṟi ammatampaṟṟi avar koḷvatōr payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟaliṉ. untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟu muṉṉar+ talaikkaṭceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilē vantu piṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟalai uṟaḻccivārattu eṉṟār, akatteḻuvaḷi yeṉavē mūlātārameṉpatu peṟṟām.

iṉ cāriyaiyai attuccāriyaiyōṭu kūṭṭuka. ēkāram^ tēṟṟam.

meyterivaḷiyeṉavē poruṭeriyā muṟkum vīḷaiyum muyaṟciyāṉāmeṉiṉum poruṭeriyāmaiyiṉ avai kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, colla+ piṟantu coṟku uṟuppām ōcaiyai ivar eḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭuvareṉa uṇarka.

`nilaiyum vaḷiyum muyaṟciyu mūṉṟu
miyala naṭappa teḻutteṉa+ paṭumē.'
eṉṟārākaliṉ. (20)

((piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((4. puṇariyal))

TE103n

mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻutti
ṉiraṇṭutalai yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵ
taṟunāṉ kīṟṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalu
mellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala.

eṉpatu cūttiram. moḻimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya moḻikaḷai+ potuvakaiyāṉ+ puṇarkkum muṟaimai uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ+ puṇariyaleṉṟu ivvōttiṟku+ peyarāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu muṟaimaiyeṉṟatu mēṟceykai yōttukkaḷuḷ+ puṇartaṟku uriyavāka īṇṭu+ kūṟiya karuvikaḷai.

iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttum ittuṇai yeṉṟalum, ellā moḻikkum īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirumallatu illaiyeṉṟu varaiyaṟuttalum, īṟum mutalumāka eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu uḷavōveṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟumē aṅṅaṉam īṟum mutalumāy niṟpateṉṟu aiyamaṟuttalum nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutal iraṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa irupaḵtu īṟu aṟunāṉkākum mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttiṉoṭu --- moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iraṇṭai muṭiyilēyiṭṭa irupaḵtum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttu irupattunāṉkumākiṉṟa mūṉṟai muṭiyilē yiṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttukkaḷōṭē, neṟiniṉṟu iyalum ellā moḻikkum --- vaḻakku neṟikkaṇiṉṟu naṭakkum mūvakai moḻikkum, meyyē uyireṉṟu āyīriyala iṟutiyum mutalum --- meyyum uyirumeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷē īṟum mutalum āvaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.

irupattiraṇṭu mutalāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutal+ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. irupattunāṉku īṟāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. meyyai muṟkūṟiṉār nālvakai+ puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻā veṉṟaṟku.

utāraṇam: maram eṉa meymmutalum meyyīṟum, ilai eṉa uyir mutalum uyirīṟum, āl eṉa uyirmutalum meyyīṟum, viḷa eṉa meymmutalum uyirīṟumām. moḻiyākkam iyalpum vikāramumeṉa iraṇṭām uyir tāmē niṉṟu mutalum īṟumātal iyalpu. avai meyyōṭu kūṭiniṉṟu aṅṅaṉamātal vikāram. (1)

TE104n

avaṟṟuḷ
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.

itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāl taṉimey mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉai vilakkaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya meyyum uyirumeṉṟa iraṇṭiṉuḷ, meyyīṟu ellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal --- meymoḻikku īṟāyavai yellām puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē moḻikku mutalāyiṉavai yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu uyirēṟi niṟku meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum īṟṟu mey puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟataṉāṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi tammēl vantāl avai uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. ivviti muṟkūṟiyataṉṟōveṉiṉ āṇṭu+ taṉimey patiṉeṭṭum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟum avaitām uyirēṟuṅkāl+ puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkumeṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār; īṇṭu meymutal meyyīṟeṉa+ poruḷuraikka vēṇṭiyamaiyiṉ moḻimutaṉ meykaḷum puḷḷipeṟumōveṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyam akaṟṟa+ kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. maram eṉa+ puḷḷi peṟṟu niṉṟatu ariteṉa vantuḻi maramariteṉṟu ēṟi muṭintavāṟu kāṇka. (2)

TE105n

kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa.

itu muṉṉar+ puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalāteṉṟu meykku eytuvikkiṉṟa karuviyai etiratu pōṟṟi uyirkkum eytuvikkiṉṟa karuvi+ cūttiram.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa --- īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭai+ cēṟal puḷḷi peṟāmaiyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam uyirēṟuṅkāl+ kuṟṟukaram^ keṭṭuppōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. nākariteṉpuḻi muṉṉar+ kuṟṟukaravōcaiyum piṉṉar uyirōcaiyumpeṟṟu avviraṇṭum^ kūṭiniṉṟatallatu apporu ḷuṇarttalākāmaiyiṉ, iḵtu uyirōṭuṅkūṭi niṟkumeṉṟār. (3)

TE106n

uyirmey yīṟu muyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.

itu `meyyēyuyireṉṟāyīriyala' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟa uyirkkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; uyirmeyyeṉpatōr īṟu uṇṭēṉum atu puṇarkkappaṭātu, atuvum uyirāyē aṭaṅkumeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmeyyīṟum --- uyirmey moḻiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉkaṇ niṉṟatum, uyirīṟṟiyaṟṟē --- uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummaiyāṉ iṭainiṉṟa uyirmeyyum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. ummai eccavummai īṟṟiṉum iṭaiyiṉum niṉṟaṉa uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē mutal niṉṟaṉa meyyuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉṟār. itaṉāṉē mēl viḷa eṉṟāl+ pōlum uyirmeykaḷellām akaravīṟeṉṟu puṇarkkumāṟu uṇarka. varaku itaṉai mēl uyirttoṭarmoḻiyeṉpa. muṉṉar `meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu ((eḻu//18)) eṉṟatu ōreḻuttiṟkeṉṟu uṇarka. ittuṇaiyum moḻimarapiṉ oḻipu kūṟiṟṟu. (4)

TE107n

uyiriṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
muyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyu
meyyiṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ
ṟivveṉa vaṟiya+ kiḷakkum^ kālai
niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
ṟāyī riyala puṇarnilai+ cuṭṭē.

itu mēṟkūṟum puṇarccikaḷellām irumoḻippuṇarcci yallatu illaiyeṉpatūum aḵtu eḻuttuvakaiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum --- uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟum^ colliṉmuṉ uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaruvaḻiyum --- uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟum^ colliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum --- meytaṉakku īṟāka iṟuñcolliṉ muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaruvaḻiyum --- mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñcolliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻivaru iṭamum, eṉṟu puṇarnilai+ cuṭṭu --- eṉṟu collappaṭṭa oṉṟiṉōṭoṉṟu kūṭum nilaimaiyākiya karuttiṉkaṇ, ivveṉa aṟiya+ kiḷakkuṅkālai --- avaṟṟai ittuṇaiyeṉa varaiyaṟaiyai ellārum aṟiya yām^ kūṟuṅkālattu, niṟuttacollē kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟu āyīriyala --- muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa collum ataṉai muṭittalaikkuṟittu varuñcollum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, nāṉkuvakaiyāṉum^ kūṭuṅkāl irumoḻiyallatu puṇarcciyiṉṟu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: āvuṇṭu āvalitu ālilai ālvīḻntatu eṉa muṟaiyē kāṇka. viḷaviṉai+ kuṟaittāṉ eṉṟavaḻi+ cāriyaiyum urupum nilaimoḻiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu nōkki ataṉai niṟutta colleṉṟum muṭikkum^ collai+ kuṟittu varukiḷavi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāṉē nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum^ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉar `meyyēyuyir' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟatu orumoḻikku, itu irumoḻikkeṉṟu uṇarka. (5)

TE108n

avaṟṟu* uḷ
niṟutta colliṉ īṟu* āku* eḻuttoṭu
kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyaiya+
peyaroṭu peyarai+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
peyaroṭu toḻilai+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
toḻiloṭu peyarai+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
toḻiloṭu toḻilai+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
mūṉṟu* ē tiripu* iṭaṉ oṉṟu* ē y-iyalpu* eṉa
v-āṅku* a+ nāṉku* ē moḻi puṇar iyalpu* ē.

itu muṉṉar eḻuttuvakaiyāṉ nāṉku puṇarcci eytiya iruvakai+ collum^ colvakaiyāṉum nāṉkākumeṉpatūum aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatu collum^ collum aṉṟu eḻuttum eḻuttumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- nilaimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻuttu iyaiya --- muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttōṭē ataṉai muṭikkakkaruti varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttu+ porunta, peyaroṭu peyarai+ puṇarkkuṅkālum --- peyarccollōṭu peyarccollai+ kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilai+ puṇarkkuṅkālum --- toḻiṟcollōṭu toḻiṟcollai+ kūṭṭum iṭattum, mūṉṟē tiripu iṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa āṅku annāṉkē --- tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉṟu muntunūliṉ+ kūṟiya annāṉku ilakkaṇamumē, moḻi puṇar iyalpu --- īṇṭu moḻikaḷ tammuḷ+ kūṭum ilakkaṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttaṉkai, cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ, vantāṉcāttaṉ, vantāṉpōyiṉāṉ eṉa muṟaiyē kāṇka. ivai nāṉku iṉattōṭum^ kūṭa+ patiṉāṟām. iṭaiyum uriyum^ tāmāka nillāmaiyiṉ+ peyarviṉaiyē kūṟiṉār. iṭaiccollum uriccollum puṇarkkum^ ceykaippaṭṭuḻi+ puṇarppu+ ciṟupāṉmai. peyarppeyarum oṭṭuppeyarumeṉa iraṇṭuvakaippaṭum peyar. terinilaiviṉaiyum^ kuṟippuviṉaiyumeṉa iraṇṭuvakaippaṭum^ toḻil. nilaimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉar+ piṟantu keṭṭuppōka varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu+ piṉ piṟantu keṭṭamaiyāṉ muṟaiyē piṟantu keṭuvaṉa oruṅku niṉṟu puṇarumā ṟiṉmaiyiṉ+ puṇarcciyeṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷai+ kūṟukiṉṟōrum^ kēṭkiṉṟōrum avvōcaiyai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭāmai uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇē uṇarvarātaliṉ avvōcai kēṭiṉṟi uḷḷattiṉkaṇ nilaipeṟṟu+ puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē piṉṉar+ kaṇkūṭāka+ puṇarkkiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyāmeṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi muyaṟkōṭu uṇṭeṉṟāl atu kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yaṉmaiyiṉ+ puṇarkkappaṭātu. itutāṉ iṉṟeṉṟāl+ puṇarkkappaṭumeṉṟu uṇarka. (6)

TE109n

avaitām
meypiṟi tātaṉ mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ
ṟivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaitām^ tiriyumāṟu --- muṉṉar+ tiripeṉṟu kūṟiya avaitām^ tirintu puṇarum neṟiyai, mey piṟitātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu ivveṉamoḻipa --- mey vēṟupaṭutal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu kūṟappaṭum immūṉṟu kūṟṟaiyuṭaiyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

immūṉṟum allātatu iyalpāmeṉṟu uṇarka. ivai vikaṟpikka+ patiṉāṟu utāraṇamām. maṭkuṭam malaittalai maravēr ivai peyarōṭu peyarpuṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. maṇmalai eṉpatu iyalpu. coṟkēṭṭāṉ palākkuṟaittāṉ maranaṭṭāṉ ivai peyarōṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṉāṟcāttaṉ koṭāpporuḷ ōṭunākam ivai toḻiloṭu peyarpuṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉcāttaṉ iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṟkoḷḷum pāṭappōyiṉāṉ cāñāṉṟāṉ ivai toḻiloṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ koṇṭāṉ iḵtu iyalpu. mūṉṟu tiripeṉṉātu iṭaṉeṉṟataṉāl oru puṇarccikkaṇ mūṉṟum oruṅkēyum varappeṟumeṉṟuuṇarka. makattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ. iḵtu aṅṅaṉam vantavāṟu makara īṟṟu `nāṭpeyar+ kiḷavi' ((eḻu//331)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. iraṇṭu varuvaṉavum^ kāṇka. (7)

TE110n

niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi y-um
aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇar-nilaikku* uriya.

itu nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttum avvirumoḻiyum aṭaiyaṭuttum puṇarumeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: niṟuttacollum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum --- nilaimoḻiyāka niṟuttiṉacollum ataṉai+ kuṟittu varum^ collum, aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya --- tāmē puṇarātu orōvōrcol aṭaiyaṭuttuvariṉum iraṇṭum aṭaiyaṭuttuvariṉum puṇarnilaimaikku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

aṭaiyāvaṉa, ummaittokaiyum irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtokaiyumām.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu āyirattorupaḵtu patiṉāyirattirupaḵtu eṉa varum. ivvaṭaikaḷ orucollēyām. vēṟṟumai+ tokaiyum uvamai+ tokaiyum muṭiya+ paṇputtokaiyum viṉaittokaiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmaiyiṉ orucollēyām. aṉmoḻi+ tokaiyum^ taṉakku vēṟōr muṭipiṉmaiyiṉ orucollēyām. ittokai+ coṟkaḷellām aṭaiyāyvaruṅkālattu oru collāy varumeṉṟu uṇarka. uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum orucollēyām. (8)

TE111n

maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyu
muriyavai yuḷavē puṇarnilai+ cuṭṭē.

itu marūuccoṟkaḷum puṇarccipeṟumeṉpatūum niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy+ poruḷiyaipillaṉavum puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟkumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maru moḻiyum --- iruvakaiyāki maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉmoḻiyum --- cevikkiṉitāka+ coṟṟiraḷiṭattu niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟpōlaniṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu piṉṉar+ ceṉṟu oṭṭi+ poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum, puṇarnilai+ cuṭṭu uriyavai uḷa --- puṇarum nilaimai+ karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

moḻiyumeṉpataṉai maruveṉpataṉōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. iṉṟokutiyeṉṟār, pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻa+ poruḷoṭṭāmal+ cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷai+ cērttaliṉ.

utāraṇam: muṉṟil mīkaṇ ivai ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya maru. ilakkaṇam allā maru `vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum' ((eḻu//481)) eṉpuḻi+ kāṭṭutum. iṉi,

`irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṉ+
paralava laṭaiya viralai teṟippa.' ((akam//4))

eṉpuḻi maruppiṉiralaiyeṉṟu oṭṭi iraṇṭāvataṉ tokaiyāy+ poruṭantu `puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉittu iyalā' ((eḻu//128)) eṉṟu uyirēṟi muṭintu mayaṅki niṉṟatu; āyiṉ, maruppiṟparaleṉṟu mey piṟitāy oṭṭi niṉṟavāṟeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ maruppiṉaiyuṭaiya paraleṉa vēṟṟumai+ tokaipporuḷ uṇarttāmaiyiṉ aḵtu acceyyuṭku iṉṉōcai nikaḻttaṟku+ pakarattiṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintuniṉṟa tuṇaiyēyāy+ puṇarcci+ payaṉiṉṟi niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcciyeytiṉāṟpōla māṭṭilakkaṇattiṉkaṇṇum moḻimāṟṟiṉkaṇṇum niṟṟal coṟku iyalpeṉṟaṟku aṉṟē āciriyar iṉṟokuti yeṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. `karuṅkā lōmai+ kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉai' eṉpuḻi ōmaicciṉaiyeṉṟu oṭṭi ōmaiyiṉatu ciṉaiyeṉa+ poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu iṉṉōcai tarutaṟku+ kakaravoṟṟumikku+ kāṇpiṉeṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟatu. `teyva mālvarai+ tirumuṉi yaruḷāl' eṉpuḻi+ teyvavarai yeṉṟu oṭṭi+ teyvattaṉmaiyuṭaiya varaiyeṉa+ poruḷtarukiṉṟatu iṉṉōcai tarutaṟku māleṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāl+ pōla+ kuṟaintu niṉṟatu. mūṉṟu tiripum vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi eccattiṉkaṇṇum,

`eyiṟupaṭaiyāka veyiṟkataviṭā
poṉṉōṭai+ pukaraṇinutaṟ
ṟuṉṉaruntiṟal+ kamaḻkaṭāttu+
kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa kaviḻmaṇimaruṅkiṉ+
peruṅkaiyāṉai yirumpiṭarttalaiyiruntu
maruntu il kūṟṟattu arum^ toḻil cāyā' ((puṟam//3))

eṉa māṭṭāy oṭṭiniṉṟatu kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa eṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu iṉṉōcai peṟutaṟku. piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ ivvāṟum piṟavāṟum puṇarcciyilvaḻi+ puṇarccipeṟṟāṟpōla niṟpaḷa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ituvē ōttāka+ koḷka. (9)

TE112n

vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarnilai moḻiyum
vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi+ puṇarmoḻi nilaiyu
meḻuttē cāriyai yāyiru paṇpi
ṉoḻukkal valiya puṇarum^ kālai.

itu mūvakai+ tiripiṉuḷ mikku+ puṇarum puṇarcci iruvakaiya eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viḷaṅkōṭu iḵtu eḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. makaviṉ kai itu cāriyai peṟṟatu. iṉi alvaḻikkaṇ viḷakkuṟitu iḵtu eḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. paṉaiyiṉkuṟai itu cāriyai peṟṟatu. itaṟku+ paṉai kuṟaintateṉpatu poruḷām. iḵtu aḷavu+ peyar. oḻukkalvaliya veṉṟataṉāṉ ikkūṟiya iraṇṭum eḻuttum^ cāriyaiyum uṭaṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka. avaṟṟukkōṭeṉpatu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. kalattukkuṟai yeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. itaṟku+ kalaṅkuṟaintateṉpatu poruḷām. iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ ivviraṇṭum uṭaṉpeṟutaliṉṟu. alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu vēṟṟumaiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ. eḻuttuppēṟu yāppuṭaimaiyāṉum eḻuttiṉāṉ+ cāriyaiyātalāṉum eḻuttu muṟkūṟiṉār. vēṟṟumai mēlai+ cūttirattē kūṟukiṉṟār.

alvaḻiyāvaṉa avvurupukaḷ tokkum virintum nillātu puṇarvaṉa. avai eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai āṟupayaṉilaiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumai taṉporuḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟuppeyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, peyareccamum viṉaiyeccamum peyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, uvamattokaiyum ummaittokaiyum irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtokaiyum iṭaiccollum uriccollum peyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, paṇputtokaiyum viṉaittokaiyum virintuniṉṟavaḻi+ puṇarnta puṇarcciyumeṉa uṇarka. (10)

TE113n

aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉu
mavvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumai yurupē.

itu mēl vēṟṟumaiyeṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyarum muṟaiyum^ tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaiyurupu --- muṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumai+ coṟkaḷai, ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum avvāṟeṉpa --- ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum avvāṟu urupumeṉṟu colvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

mēl+ collatikārattu eḻuvāyaiyum viḷiyaiyum^ kūṭṭi vēṟṟumai eṭṭēṉpārāleṉiṉ ai mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyāṟum^ tokkum virintum perumpāṉmaiyum pulappaṭṭu niṉṟu peyarpporuḷai+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavāka vēṟupāṭuceytu puṇarcciyeytuvikkumeṉṟaṟku īṇṭu āṟeṉṟār. āṇṭu eḻuvāyum viḷiyum^ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum^ tammai vēṟupaṭuttu+ poruḷmāttiram uṇarttiniṉṟum viḷiyāy etirmukamākkiniṉṟum iṅṅaṉam^ ciṟupāṉmaiyāy+ pulappaṭanillā vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyavēṉum avaiyum oruvāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉaveṉṟaṟku āṇṭu eṭṭeṉṟāreṉa uṇarka.

TE114n

valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai yurupiṉ+
kolvaḻi yoṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.

itu nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟku --- valleḻuttu aṭiyāyniṉṟa nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭaimikutal vēṇṭum --- poruntiyavaḻi valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭaikkaṇ mikku+ puṇartalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

varaiyātu oṟṟeṉavē valloṟṟum melloṟṟum peṟṟām.

utāraṇam: maṇikku maṇikkaṇ tīkku tīkkaṇ maṉaikka maṉaikkaṇ eṉavum, vēykku vēykkaṇ ūrkku ūrkkaṇ pūḻkku pūḻkkaṇ eṉavum, uyirīṟu mūṉṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟu mūṉṟiṉum perumpāṉmai valloṟṟu mikkuvarum. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ nuṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa melloṟṟu mikkatu. ivaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻi makarakkēṭu urupiyaliṉ+ kūṟupa. āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟukkēṭu kūṟutaṟku oṟṟiṉṟu.

iṉi nāṉkaṉurupiṟku melloṟṟu mikāteṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi olvaḻiyeṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāmurupiṉkaṇ nampikaṇ eṉa ikara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum naṅkaikaṇ eṉa aikāra īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum^ tāykaṇ eṉa yakaraīṟṟiṉkaṇṇum aracarkaṇ eṉa rakaraīṟṟiṉkaṇṇum oṟṟu mikāmai koḷka.

iṉi mey piṟitātalai muṉṉē kūṟātu mikutalai muṟkūṟiya ataṉāṉē poṟku poṟkaṇ vēṟku vēṟkaṇ vāṭku vāṭkaṇ eṉattirintu muṭivaṉavuṅkoḷka. itaṉāṉē avaṉkaṇ avaḷkaṇ eṉa uyartiṇaippeyarkkaṇ ēḻaṉurupu iyalpāy varutaluṅkoḷka. ivaṟṟiṟku+ kuṉṟiya puṇarccivarumēṉum^ koḷka.

koṟṟikku koṟṟikaṇ kōtaikku kōtaikaṇ eṉa viravuppeyarkkum itaṉāṉē koḷka. (12)

TE115n

āṟa ṉurupi ṉakara+ kiḷavi
yīṟā kakaramuṉai+ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu āṟāvataṟku+ tokaimarapai nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟaṉurupiṉ akarakkiḷavi --- atuveṉṉum āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇiṉṟa akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟāku akaramuṉai+ keṭutal vēṇṭum --- neṭumutal kuṟukumoḻikaṭku `īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum' ((eḻu//161)) eṉa vitittataṉāl uḷatākiya akarattiṉ muṉṉarttāṉ keṭutalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

tamatu namatu ematu numatu taṉatu eṉatu niṉatu eṉa varum. itu nilaimoḻikku ōr akaram peṟumeṉa vitiyātu urupu akaram ēṟi muṭiyumeṉa vitittāl varum kuṟṟam uṇṭōveṉiṉ, `niṉavakūṟuvaleṉavakēṇ mati' ((puṟam//35)) eṉṟāṟpōla āṟāvataṟku uriya akara urupiṉ muṉṉarum ōr akara eḻuttuppēṟu nilaimoḻikkaṇ varutal uḷatāka+ karutiṉārātaliṉ, āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum potuvāka nilaimoḻikkaṇ akarappēṟu vitittu, atuveṉṉum orumaiyurupu vantāl āṇṭuppeṟṟu niṉṟa akarattiṉmuṉṉar atuveṉpataṉkaṇ akaraṅkeṭukaveṉṟu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ ataṟku+ kuṟṟam uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarka. (13)

TE116n

vēṟṟumai vaḻiya peyarpuṇar nilaiyē.

itu vēṟṟumai peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaipeyarvaḻiya --- vēṟṟumaikaḷ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉavām, puṇarnilai --- avaṟṟōṭu puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttaṉai cāttaṉoṭu cāttaṟku cāttaṉiṉ cāttaṉatu cāttaṉkaṇ eṉavarum. maṟṟu itu `kūṟiya muṟaiyiṉ' ((co//26)) eṉṉum vēṟṟumaiyōttiṉ+ cūttirattāṉ+ peṟutumeṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyarai+ puṇarttal mutaliya nālvakai+ puṇarcciyiṉaiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkutaliṉ+ ṟoḻiṟpiṉṉum urupu varumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakkutaṟku īṇṭukkūṟiṉāreṉka. āyiṉ ivvilakkutal viṉaiyiyaṉmutaṟcūttirattāṉ+ peṟutumeṉiṉ, atu mutaṉilaiyai+ kūṟiṟṟeṉpatu āṇṭu uṇarka. (14)

TE117n

uyartiṇai+ peyarē aḵṟiṇai+ peyareṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilai+ cuṭṭē.

itu muṟkūṟiya peyarkaṭku+ peyarum muṟaiyum^ tokaiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭunilaippeyar --- poruḷai oruvar karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa nilaimaiyaiyuṭaiya peyarkaḷai, uyartiṇai+ peyarē aḵṟiṇaippeyareṉṟu āyiraṇṭeṉpa --- uyartiṇai+ poruḷai oruvaṉ karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa peyarum aḵṟiṇai+ poruḷai oruvaṉ karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa peyarum eṉṉum avviraṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

peyariyaluḷ avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉpatu mutalāka uyartiṇaippeyarum atu itu utu eṉpatu mutalāka aḵṟiṇaippeyarum āmāṟu avaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇam^ kūṟukiṉṟār īṇṭu+ kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal māttiraiyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi+ koṟṟaṉ koṟṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum viravuppeyarum^ koṟṟaṉkuṟiyaṉ koṟṟikuṟiyaḷ koṟṟaṉ kuḷampu koṟṟi kuṟitu eṉa+ piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ ṟiṇaiteritaliṉ irutiṇai+ peyariṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. koṟṟaṉcevi koṟṟicevi eṉpaṉavum piṉṉar varukiṉṟa viṉaikaḷāṉ+ tiṇaiviḷaṅka aṭaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. iṉi `aḵṟiṇai viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē' ((eḻu//155)) eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟāṇṭum ōtutalpaṟṟi nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ viravuppeyar kōṭalum oṉṟu.

TE118n

avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ+ cāriyai varumē.

itu cāriyai varumiṭam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ --- a+ collappaṭṭa iruvakai+ peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattē, cāriyaivarum --- cāriyaiccoṟkaḷ varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭūuviṉkai makaṭūviṉkai palavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa+ puṇariyaṉilaiyiṭai+ poruṇilaikku utavi vantaṉa. cāriyai yeṉṟataṉ poruḷ vēṟāki niṉṟa irumoḻiyum^ tammiṉ+ cārtaṟporuṭṭu iyaintu niṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu. (16)

TE119n

avaitām
iṉṉē vaṟṟē yattē yammē
yoṉṉē yāṉē yakkē yikkē
yaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yuḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉa veṉpa cāriyai moḻiyē.

iḵtu accāriyaikaṭku+ peyarum muṟaiyum^ tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaitām --- muṉṉar+ cāriyai yeṉappaṭṭa avaitām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉṉeṉkiḷavi uḷappaṭa aṉṉa eṉpa --- iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉṉeṉṉum^ collōṭukūṭa oṉpatākiya attaṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉavum, piṟavum^ cāriyaimoḻi eṉpa --- avaiyoḻintaṉavum^ cāriyai+ collā meṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

piṟavāvaṉa tam nam num um ñāṉṟu keḻu ē ai eṉpaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñāṉṟu oḻintaṉa eṭuttōtuvar āciriyar. `eṭuttakaṟaliṉ kulaiyalaṅkāntaḷ' itu viṉaittokai; cāriyaiyaṉṟu. iṉ cāriyai vaḻakkuppayiṟciyum palakāl eṭuttōtappaṭutalum potuvakaiyāṉ ōtiyavaḻi+ tāṉēcēṟalumākiya ciṟappunōkki muṉvaittār. vaṟṟum attum iṉpōla mutal tiriyumākalāṉum^ ceykaiyoppumaiyāṉum ataṉpiṉ vaittār. am īṟu tiriyumēṉum vaḻakkuppayiṟciyiṉṟi nāṉkāmurupiṉkaṇ tiritaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vaittār. āṉ poruṭpuṇarccikkum urupupuṇarccikkum varumeṉṟu ataṉpiṉ vaittār. akku īṟutiriyumēṉum urupupuṇarccikkaṇ vārāmaiyiṉ ataṉpiṉ vaittār. ikku mutal tiriyumēṉum^ ciṟupāṉmaipaṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vaittār. aṉ iṉpōla+ ciṟattaliṉ+ piṉvaittār.

āṟaṉurupiṟkum āṉcāriyaikkum iṉṉurupiṟkum iṉcāriyaikkum vēṟṟumai yāteṉiṉ; avai cāriyaiyāṉa iṭattu yātāṉum ōr urupēṟṟu muṭiyum; urupāyiṉa iṭattu vēṟōr urupiṉai ēlāveṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi makattukkai eṉpuḻi+ takaravoṟṟum^ takaravukaramum varumeṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉeṉṟāl attu eṉavē vēṇṭutaliṉ āṇṭum attuniṉṟē keṭṭateṉṟu kōṭum. akku ikku eṉpaṉavum pirittukkūṭṭa+ kiṭakkum. tāḻakkōleṉa akku+ peṟṟuniṟṟalāṉum āṭikku eṉpuḻi+ kukaram nāṉkaṉurupākāmaiyāṉum ivai cāriyaiyāmāṟu uṇarka. (17)

TE120n

avaṟṟuḷ
iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉar+ keṭuta lurittu mākum.

itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya cāriyaikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram --- iṉcāriyaiyatu ikaram, āviṉ iṟuti muṉṉar --- ā eṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉar, keṭutal urittumākum --- keṭṭumuṭiyavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

urittumākumeṉṟataṉāṉ+ keṭātu muṭiyavum peṟumeṉṟavāṟu. iḵtu oppakkūṟaleṉṉum utti.

utāraṇam: āṉai āviṉai āṉoṭu āviṉoṭu āṟku āviṟku āṉiṉ āviṉiṉ āṉatu āviṉatu āṉkaṇ āviṉkaṇ eṉavarum.

iṉi muṉṉareṉṟataṉāṉē māviṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. māṉai māviṉai māṉoṭu māviṉoṭu māṟku māviṟku eṉa oṭṭuka. ākāra īṟeṉṉātu āviṉiṟutiyeṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyiṉ mā ilēciṉāṉ+ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

iṉi āṉkōṭu āviṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟacāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻiyum^ koḷka. (18)

TE121n

aḷavāku moḻimuta ṉilaiiya vuyirmicai
ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilaittē.

iḵtu avviṉcāriyai īṟutiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavākum moḻi --- aḷavuppeyarāy+ piṉṉiṟkum moḻikku, mutal nilaiiya uyirmicai ṉaḵkāṉ --- muṉṉarniṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟuniṉṟa kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl vanta iṉcāriyaiyatu ṉakaram, ṟaḵkāṉākiya nilaittu --- ṟakaramāy+ tiriyum nilaimaiyaiyuṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku. ivaṟṟai+ patteṉa niṟutti `niṟaiyumaḷavum' ((eḻu//436)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāl iṉcāriyai koṭuttuk `kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē' ((eḻu//433)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kuṟṟukaram meyyōṭum^ keṭuttu vēṇṭum^ ceykaiceytu `muṟṟaviṉvarūum' ((eḻu//433)) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭittu muṭikka.

nilaiiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. patiṟṟeḻuttu patiṟṟaṭukku oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu patiṟṟoṉṟu patiṟṟiraṇṭu patiṟṟoṉpatu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. accūttirattiṉ+ `kuṟaiyātākum' ((eḻu//436)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ poruṭpeyarkkum eṇṇuppeyarkkum iṉkoṭukka. (19)

TE122n

vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa+ cuṭṭumuta laimmu
ṉaḵkā ṉiṟṟa lākiya paṇpē.

itu vaṟṟumutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutalaimmuṉ --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuccoṉmuṉ vaṟṟu varuṅkālai, vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa aḵkāṉ niṟṟalākiya paṇpu --- avvaṟṟu+ cāriyaiyiṉatu vakaramākiya oṟṟukkeṭa āṇṭu ēṟiya akaram niṟṟal ataṟku uḷatākiya kuṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaiyaṟṟai ivaiyaṟṟai uvaiyaṟṟai eṉavarum. iṉṉum ivaṟṟai, avai ivai uvai eṉa niṟuttic `cuṭṭumutalākiyavaiyeṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//177)) eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṟṟum urupum^ koṭuttu vēṇṭum^ ceykai ceyka. ivvāṟē ellā urupiṟkum oṭṭuka. avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻiyum^ koḷka.

ākiyapaṇpu eṉṟataṉāṉē evaṉeṉpatu paṭuttalōcaiyāṉ+ peyarāyavaḻi evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟum urupuṅkoṭuttu vaṟṟumicaiyoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅkeṭuttu akaravuyir muṉṉar vaṟṟiṉ vakaram^ keṭumeṉa+ keṭuttu evaṟṟai evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka. (20)

TE123n

ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku.

iḵtu iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉṉeṉṉum ṉakara īṟu nāṉkum^ tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉaḵkāṉ nāṉkaṉurupiṟku ṟaḵkāṉ --- ṉakāra īṟṟu nāṉku cāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkāmurupiṟṟu ṟakāramāy+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṟṟu kōoṟku orupāṟku ataṟku eṉavarum.

itaṉai `aḷavāku moḻimutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉ piṉ vaiyātu īṇṭu vaittatu ṉakara īṟukaḷellām uṭaṉ tiriyumeṉṟaṟku. āṇṭuvaippiṉ iṉcāriyaiyē tiriyumeṉpatu paṭum. `oṉṟu mutalāka+ pattūrntu varūu --- mellāveṇṇum' ((eḻu//199)) eṉpataṉāṉ orupāṟku eṉpataṉai muṭikka. (21)

TE124n

āṉi ṉakaramu mataṉō raṟṟē
nāṇmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal+ ṟoḻiṟkē.

iḵtu āṉiṉ īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal+ toḻiṟku --- nāṭpeyarmuṉṉar varum valleḻuttai mutalāka uṭaiya toḻiṟcoṟku iṭaiyē varum, āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟu --- āṉcāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉ cāriyaipōla ṟakaramāy+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paraṇiyāṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum. `nāḷ% muṉ+ ^tōṉṟum^ toḻil* ^nilai+ kiḷavikku' ((eḻu//247)) eṉṟataṉāṉ āṉcāriyai koṭuttu+ ceykai ceyka. iṉi ummaiyai iṟantatutaḻīya tākki nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉōṭu okkumeṉa+ poruḷuraittu+ paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ vaḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakaramātal koḷka.

iṉi, ñāpakattāl toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram^ tiriyumeṉavē peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram^ tiritalum^ tiriyāmaiyum^ koḷka. kuṟumpiṟkoṟṟaṉ paṟampiṟpāri eṉa+ tirintu vantaṉa. kurukiṉkāl eruttiṉpuṟam eṉa+ tiriyātu vantaṉa. (22)

TE125n

atti ṉakara makaramuṉai yillai.

iḵtu attu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: attiṉ akaram --- attuccāriyaiyiṉ akaram, akaramuṉai illai --- akara īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉar illaiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

`attavaṇ variṉum varainilai yiṉṟē' ((eḻu//219)) eṉpataṉāṉ makappeyar attuppeṟṟu niṉṟatu makattukkaiyeṉa akaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. viḷavattu+ kaṇṇeṉpuḻi+ keṭātu niṟṟal `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉuḷ `teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē' eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟupa. (23)

TE126n

ikki ṉikara mikaramuṉai yaṟṟē.

iḵtu ikku mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ikkiṉ ikaram --- ikku+ cāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram, ikaramuṉai aṟṟu --- ikaraīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉar muṟkūṟiya attuppōla+ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

`tiṅkaṇmuṉvariṉ' ((eḻu//248)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟa ikku āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ikaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṭapporuṭṭu. (24)

TE127n

aiyiṉ muṉṉaru mavviya ṉilaiyum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal nilaiyum --- ikkiṉ ikaram ikara īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉaraṉṟi aikāra īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉarum mēṟkūṟiya keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

`tiṅkaḷ um* nāḷ um* muntu kiḷantaṉṉa' ((eḻu//286)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ cittiraikku+ koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻippeṟṟa ikku aikārattiṉ muṉṉar+ keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. (25)

TE128n

eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
yakki ṉiṟutimeym micaiyoṭum^ keṭumē
kuṟṟiya lukara muṟṟa+ tōṉṟātu.

iḵtu akku mutal oḻiya ēṉaiya keṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eppeyar muṉṉarum --- evvakaippaṭṭa peyarccoṉmuṉṉarum, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi --- valleḻuttu varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, akkiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟattōṉṟātu --- iṭai niṉṟa akkuccāriyaiyiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiya+ tōṉṟātu, meymmicaiyoṭuṅkeṭum --- akkuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟu+ taṉakku mēlniṉṟa valloṟṟōṭuṅkeṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

`oṟṟunilai tiriyā takkoṭu varūum' ((eḻu//418)) eṉṟataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa kuṉṟakkūkai maṉṟappeṇṇai eṉpaṉavum, `vēṟṟumaiyāyi ṉēṉai yiraṇṭum' ((eḻu//329)) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam eṉpaṉavum, `tamiḻeṉkiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//385)) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa tamiḻakkūttu eṉpatuvum akku īṟukeṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam varutaliṉ eppeyareṉṟār. muṟṟaveṉpataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivviti koḷka. tamiḻanūl tamiḻayāḻ tamiḻavaraiyar eṉavarum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tamakkēṟṟa iyaipu valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭittu+ koḷka. aṉṟi+ kēṭōtiya kakaravoṟṟu niṟkumeṉiṉ, cakaram^ takaram pakaram vantavaṟṟiṟku+ kakaravoṟṟākāmai uṇarka. (26)

TE129n

ammi ṉiṟuti kacata+ kālai+
taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum.

iḵtu am īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ammiṉ iṟuti --- ammuccāriyaiyiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu, kacatakkālai --- kacatakkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum --- taṉvaṭivu tirintu ṅañanakkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl eṉavarum.

itu `puḷimarakkiḷavikku' ((eḻu//244)) eṉpataṉāṉ ammuppeṟṟatu. kacatakkālai+ tiriyumeṉavē pakarattiṉkaṇ tiripiṉṟāyiṟṟu. meytirinteṉṉātu taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṉ ammiṉ makaramēyaṉṟi+ tam nam num um eṉṉum^ cāriyai makaramum^ tirital koḷka. ellār taṅkaiyum ellār naṅkaiyum ellīr nuṅkaiyum vāṉavari villuntiṅkaḷum eṉavarum. tuṟaikēḻūraṉ vaḷaṅkēḻūraṉ eṉa+ keḻu v --- eṉṉum^ cāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyum^ ceyyuṇmuṭipeṉṟu koḷka. (27)

TE130n

meṉmaiyu miṭaimaiyum varūum kālai
yiṉmai vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ammīṟu iyalpukaṇattu muṉṉar+ keṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meṉmaiyam iṭaimaiyum varūuṅkālai --- meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇamum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar --- ammuccāriyai iṟutimakaramiṉṟi muṭitalai vēṇṭumeṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyañeri nuṉi muri yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavarum. uraiyiṟkōṭaleṉpataṉāṉ+ puḷiyavilaiyeṉa uyirvaruvaḻi īṟukeṭutalum puḷiyilaiyeṉa ammu muḻuvatum^ keṭutalum^ koḷka. puḷiyavilai yeṉṟatu `oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku' ((eḻu//132)) aṉṟu. meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum^ tammuḷokkumēṉum ammu muḻuvatum^ keṭṭuvarutaliṉ uyirai eṭuttōtārāyiṉār. puḷiṅkāy eṉpatu marumuṭipu. (28)

TE131n

iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ
kiṉṉeṉ cāriyai yiṉmai vēṇṭum.

iḵtu iṉcāriyai aintāmurupiṉkaṇ muḻuvatum^ keṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku --- iṉṉeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟku, iṉṉeṉcāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum --- iṉṉeṉṉum^ cāriyai tāṉ iṉṟi muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉ palāviṉ kaṭuviṉ taḻuviṉ cēviṉ vauviṉ eṉavarum. ivaṟṟiṟku vīḻpaḻameṉavum nīṅkiṉāṉeṉavum^ koṭuttu muṭivuṇarka. ūriṉīṅkiṉāṉ eṉa ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉi `avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉikaram' ((eḻu//120)) eṉṟataṉpiṉ itaṉai vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ iṉcāriyai keṭātu vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum niṟṟalkoḷka. pāmpiṉiṉ+ kaṭitutēḷ `kaṟpiṉiṉvaḻāa naṟpalavutavi' `akaṭucērpu porunti yaḷaviṉiṉ+ ṟiriyātu' eṉavarum. iṉi iṉmaiyum vēṇṭumeṉṉum ummai tokkuniṉṟatākki ataṉāṉ ivai kōṭalum oṉṟu. (29)

TE132n

peyarum^ toḻilum pirintu* oruṅku* icaippa
vēṟṟumai yurupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum^
tōṟṟam vēṇṭā+ tokutikkaṇṇum
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi+
coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
tiṭainiṉ ṟiyalum^ cāriyai yiyaṟkai
yuṭaimaiyu miṉmaiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum.

itu muṟkūṟiya cāriyaikaḷellām puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷē varumeṉpatūum ammoḻitām ivaiyeṉpatūum avaivārāta moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉpatūum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peyarum^ toḻilum --- peyarccollum^ toḻiṟcollum, pirintu icaippa oruṅku icaippa --- peyarum^ toḻilumāy+ pirinticaippa+ peyarum peyarumāykkūṭiyicaippa, vēṟṟumaiyurupu nilaipeṟuvaḻiyum --- vēṟṟumai ceyyum urupukaḷ tokātu nilaipeṟṟa iṭattum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭā+ tokutikkaṇṇum --- avvēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ tōṟṟutal vēṇṭātu tokka iṭattum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi --- tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkōṭē porunti, coṟcitarmaruṅkiṉ --- cāriyaipeṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷai+ pirittukkāṇumiṭattu, cāriyai iyaṟkai vaḻivantu viḷaṅkātu iṭainiṉṟa iyalum --- accāriyaiyiṉatu taṉmai accoṟkaḷiṉ piṉṉē vantu viḷaṅkātu naṭuvē niṉṟu naṭakkum, uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyam oṭuvayiṉ okkum --- accāriyai uṇṭātalum illaiyātalum oṭuvurupiṉiṭattu ottuvarum eṉṟavāṟu.

oṭuviṟkokkum eṉavē ēṉaiya ovvāvāyiṉa.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉaikkuṟaittāṉ, kūḻiṟku+ kuṟṟēval ceyyum ivai pirinticaittu urupunilai peṟṟaṉa. `aṉṉeṉcāriyai' ((eḻu//194)) eṉpataṉaik `kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟuti' ((eḻu//195)) eṉpataṉai+ cēravaittataṉāl iṉ cāriyai varutal koḷka. ivviraṇṭurupum^ cāriyainiṟpa+ perumpāṉmaiyum^ tokāveṉṟu uṇarka. viḷaviṉaikkuṟaittavaṉ, kaṭicūttirattiṟku+ poṉ ivai oruṅkicaippa urupu nilaipeṟṟaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukki, vāṉattuvaḻukki eṉa+ cāriyaipeṟṟu+ pirinticaittu aintāmurupu nilaipeṟṟum nilaipeṟātum vantatu. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal ivai `melleḻuttuṟaḻum' ((eḻu//112)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu `vaḻakkattāṉa' eṉpataṉāṉ attukkoṭuttu makaraṅkeṭukka oruṅkicaittaṉa. viḷaviṉatukōṭu, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa oruṅkicaittu+ cāriyai peṟṟavaḻi āṟaṉurupu tokātum^ tokkum niṉṟatu. itaṟku+ pirinticaittaliṉṟu.marattukkaṭkaṭṭiṉāṉ, marattukkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa+ pirinticaitta vaḻiyum, marattukkaṭkuraṅku, marattukkuraṅku eṉa oruṅkicaitta vaḻiyum^ cāriyai niṉṟavaḻi ēḻaṉurupu tokātum^ tokkam niṉṟatu. `kiḷaippeyarellām' ((eḻu//307)) eṉṟataṉuḷ+ `koḷa' eṉṟataṉāṉ ṇakāram ṭakāramāyiṟṟu. `nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattōṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//228)) eṉa vititta attu nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟavaḻi+ peṟātāyiṟṟu, aḵtu `oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiyavaḻakku' aṉmaiyiṉ. nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttukkoṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa urupu tokkuḻi iruvaḻiyum peṟṟaṉa. ellār tammaiyum eṉa+ cāriyai īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ iṭainiṟṟal perumpāṉmaiyeṉṟaṟku iyalumeṉṟār. pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntal eṉa uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottatu. iṉi iyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāṉ oṭu urupiṉkaṇ+ peṟṟum peṟāmaiyum varutalaṉṟi+ peṟṟēvarutalum^ koḷka. palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum. (30)

TE133n

attē vaṟṟē yāyiru moḻimē
loṟṟumey keṭuta ṟeṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
yavaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu attu vaṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻiyatu oṟṟukkēṭum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ oṟṟuppēṟumākiya ceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl oṟṟu --- attum vaṟṟumākiya avviraṇṭu cāriyaimēl niṉṟa oṟṟu, meykeṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu --- taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttumikamē --- avviru cāriyai muṉṉam varum valleḻuttu mikka muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalattukkuṟai, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavarum. `attiṭai varūum^ kalameṉ ṉaḷavē' ((eḻu//168)) `cuṭṭu mutal vakara maiyu meyyum' ((eḻu//183)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ attum vaṟṟam peṟṟuvarum makara vakara īṟukaṭku īṟṟu valleḻuttuviti iṉmaiyiṉ avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumeṉṟu cāriyai valleḻuttu vitittār. valleḻuttu iṉṟi+ tirintu muṭivaṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum lakāramum ḷakāramumām. makara īṟṟiṟku attum vakara īṟṟiṟku vaṟṟum varumeṉpatu accūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ peṟṟām. vaṟṟē yattē yeṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ+ puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉ muṉṉar attiṉmicai yoṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: viṇṇattukkoṭkum, veyilattucceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattu+ koṇṭāṉ eṉavarum. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaram akaramuṉṉarēyaṉṟi+ piṟa uyirmuṉṉarum^ keṭum orōviṭatteṉṟu koḷka. aṇṇāttēri, tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉa ākārattiṉ muṉṉarum varum attiṉ akaram^ keṭṭatu. ivaṟṟai akara īṟākkiyum muṭippa. iṉi+ teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē eṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaram^ teṟṟeṉa+ keṭātu niṟkum iṭamum^ koḷka. atavattukkaṇ, viḷavattukkaṇ eṉavarum. (31)

TE134n

kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nēra+ tōṉṟu meḻuttiṉ cāriyai.

itu moḻiccāriyaiyai viṭṭu eḻuttukkaṭku varuñcāriyaikaḷatu peyarum muṟaiyum^ tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu civaṇi --- kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu porunti, eḻuttiṉ cāriyai nērattōṉṟum --- eḻuttiṉkaṇ varum^ cāriyaiyātaṟku ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭampaṭattōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

kāramum^ karamum eṭuttu+ colliyavaḻi iṉiticaittalāṉum, vaḻakku+ payiṟciyuṭaimaiyāṉum, vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum uriyavātalāṉum^ cēra+ kūṟiṉār. kāṉ attaṉmai yiṉmaiyiṉāṉ+ piṉ vaittār. nērattōṉṟumeṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avai āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam eṉka. ivai citainta vaḻakkēṉum^ kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa. (32)

TE135n

avaṟṟuḷ
karamum^ kāṉu neṭṭeḻuttu ilavē.

iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ+ cilacāriyai cilaveḻuttōṭu vārāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ, karamum^ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttila --- karamum^ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku varutaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, neṭṭeḻuttiṟku+ kāram varumāyiṟṟu. ākāram īkāram eṉa oṭṭuka. aikāram aukārameṉa+ cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ varumāṟu kāṇka. (33)

TE136n

varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟum^ kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭaiya.

iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; eṉṉai? neṭṭeḻuttiṟku+ cilacāriyai vilakkiṉāṟpōla+ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum vilakkaṟpāṭu uṇṭōveṉa aiyam nikaḻtaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟum --- varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟucāriyaiyum, kuṟṟeḻuttuṭaiya --- kuṟṟeḻuttuppeṟṟu varutalaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

akāram akaram maḵkāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. `vakāramicaiyum' `akara ikaram' `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' eṉavum piṟavum^ cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ+ kāṇka. iḵkāṉ oḵkāṉ, eṉpaṉa peruvaḻakkaṉṟu. varaṉmuṟaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytam peṟutal koḷka. itu `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāyta+ puḷḷi' ((eḻu//38)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟātāyiṟṟu, moḻiyāy nillāmaiyiṉ. (34)

TE137n

aikāra aukāram^ kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu `avaṟṟuḷ+ karamum^ kāṉum' eṉpataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aikāra aukāram^ kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum --- neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

aikāṉ aukāṉ eṉavarum. ummai iṟantatutaḻīiyiṟṟu. kārattai+ karututaliṉ. (35)

TE138n

puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉittu iyalātu
meyyoṭum^ civaṇu mavviyal keṭuttē.

itu puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantakālattu+ puṇarum muṟaimai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu --- puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉarvanta uyir mutaṉmoḻiyiṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum^ civaṇum --- appuḷḷiyōṭum kūṭum, avviyal keṭuttu --- tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa avviyalpiṉai+ keṭuttu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, nīrōṭu kūṭiya pālpōla niṉṟateṉṟu oṟṟumai kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu itaṉāṉē uyirmeyyeṉa+ peyarpeṟṟatu.

utāraṇam: pālaritu pālāḻi ālilai poruḷīṭṭam vāṉulaku vāṉūṭu vēleṟintāṉ vēlēṟṟāṉ poruḷaiyam poruḷoṉṟu nāṇōṭiṟṟu collauviyam eṉa varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ iyalpallāta puḷḷi muṉṉar uyirvantālum ivviti koḷka. ataṉai ataṉoṭu nāṭuri eṉa varum. ivaṟṟaic `cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu' ((eḻu//176)) `urivarukālai nāḻikkiḷavi' ((eḻu//240)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka. puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉumeṉa ummaiyai māṟi eccavummaiyākki+ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉarum eṉa avviti koḷka. eṉavē, `kuṟṟiyalukaramumaṟṟu' ((eḻu//105)) eṉṟataṉōṭum poruntiṟṟām. nākaritu varakaritu eṉa varum. (36)

TE139n

meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ+ ṟaṉṉuru vākum.

iḵtu uyirmey puṇarccikkaṇ uyirnīṅkiyavaḻi+ paṭuvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mey uyir nīṅkiṉ --- mey taṉṉōṭu kūṭiniṉṟa uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu+ pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, taṉ uruvākum --- tāṉ muṉṉar+ peṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

āl ilai ataṉ ai eṉa varum.

uyir eṉṉa vaṭiviṟṟeṉṟu āciriyar kūṟāmaiyiṉ uyirkkaṇ ārāycciyiṉṟu.

iṉi ekara okaraṅkaḷai+ puḷḷiyāṉ vēṟṟumai ceytaliṉ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭivuṭaiya veṉṟu kōṭalumām. puṇarcciyuḷ uyirmeyyiṉai+ pirippārātaliṉ itu kūṟākkāl+ kuṉṟakkūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (37)

TE140n

ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē
yuṭampaṭu meyyi ṉuruvukoḷal varaiyār.

iḵtu uyirīṟum uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻvatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāmoḻikkum --- nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāy+ puṇarum evvakai moḻikkum, uyir varuvaḻi --- uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal varaiyāruṭampaṭumeyyiṉatu vaṭivai uyirīṟu kōṭalai nīkkār koḷvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

avai yakaramum vakaramumeṉpatu mutaṉūl paṟṟikkōṭum;

`uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāra

muyirmutaṉ moḻivarūum^ kālai yāṉa'

eṉavum,

`iṟutiyu mutalu muyirnilai variṉē

yuṟumeṉa moḻipa vuṭampaṭu meyyē'

eṉavum^ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey koḷḷum. ēkāram, yakāramum, vakāramum^ koḷḷum. allaṉavellām vakara uṭampaṭumeyyē koḷḷumeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: kiḷiyaḻakitu, kurīiyōppuvāḷ, varaiyāmakaḷir eṉavum; viḷavaḻakitu, palāvaḻakitu, kaṭuvaḻakitu, pūvaḻakitu, kōvaḻakitu, kauvaṭaintatu eṉavum oṭṭuka. `ēe yivaḷorutti pēṭiyō veṉṟār' ēvāṭal kāṇka eṉa ēkārattiṟku iraṇṭum vantaṉa.

oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ vikārappaṭṭa moḻikkaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka. maravaṭi āyirutiṇai eṉa varum. varaiyāreṉṟataṉāṉ uṭampaṭumeykōṭal orutalaiyaṉṟu. kiḷiaritu mūṅkāillai eṉavum varum. oṉṟeṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāl `viṇvattukkoṭkum' eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmai puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum varum. celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpaṉa viṉaittokaiyeṉa maṟukka. (38)

TE141n

eḻuttō raṉṉa poruṭeri puṇarcci
yicaiyiṉ+ ṟirita ṉilaiiya paṇpē.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavāmeṉa eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuttōraṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci --- eḻuttu orutaṉmaittāṉa poruḷviḷaṅka niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, icaiyiṉ+ ṟirital nilaiiya paṇpu --- eṭuttal paṭuttal nalitaleṉkiṉṟa ōcaivēṟṟumaiyāṉ+ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilaipeṟṟa kuṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, cemparutti, kuṟumparampu, nākaṉṟēvaṉpōttu, tāmarakkaṇiyār, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa ivai icaiyiṉ+ ṟirintaṉa. (39)

TE142n

avaitām
muṉṉa+ poruḷa puṇarcci vāyi
ṉiṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttukkaṭa ṉilavē.

itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaitām --- pala poruṭku+ potuveṉṟa puṇarmoḻikaḷ tām, muṉṉapporuḷa --- kuṟippāṉ uṇarum poruṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, puṇarccivāyiṉ iṉṉa veṉṉum eḻuttu+ kaṭaṉ ila --- puṇarcciyiṭattu ittaṉmaiya veṉṉum eḻuttumuṟaimaiyai uṭaiyavalla eṉṟavāṟu.

cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟuḻi+ poṉṉārāycci yuḷavaḻi+ poṉṉeṉavum^ cempārāycci yuḷavaḻi+ cempeṉavum^ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. icaiyiṉ+ ṟiritaleṉṟatu oliyeḻuttiṟkeṉavum eḻuttukkaṭaṉila veṉṟatu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavum^ koḷka. (40)

((puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((5. tokaimarapu))

TE143n

kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl+ ṟōṉṟu
melleḻuttu iyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
yaṉṉa marapiṉ moḻi vayiṉāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum mēlai akattōttiṉuḷ muṭikkumvaḻi īṟukaṭōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟai īṇṭu orōvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ ṟokuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ivvōttu+ tokaimara peṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl mūvakaimoḻiyum nālvakaiyāṉ+ puṇarvuḻi, mūṉṟutiripum ōriyalpum eyti vēṟṟumai alvaḻiyeṉa irupakutiyavāki eḻuttum^ cāriyaiyum mikku+ puṇarumāṟu ituveṉṟu uṇartti avaitām virinta cūttirapporuḷavaṉṟiyum^ tokku+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, ivvōttu+ puṇariyalōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. i+ talaiccūttiram uyirmayaṅkiyalaiyum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalaiyum nōkkiyatōr varumoḻi+ karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟṟōṉṟum iyaṟkai melleḻuttu --- uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum muṉṉar niṟpa+ kacatapakkaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya moḻikaḷ vantāl avaṟṟiṟku mēlē tōṉṟiniṟkum iyalpākiya melleḻuttukkaḷ, colliya muṟaiyāṉ ṅañanama eṉṉum oṟṟākum --- neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ+ poruntakkūṟiya muṟaiyāṉē kacatapakkaḷukku ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷ niraṉiṟaivakaiyāṉām, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉāṉa --- attaṉmaittākiya muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. itu `marappeyarkkiḷavi' ((eḻu//314)) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. maram^ kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ ṭiriyumāṟu `alvaḻiyellām' ((eḻu//314)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟutumēṉum īṇṭu+ tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāl nilaimoḻikkaṭṭōṉṟiniṉṟa oṟṟu+ tirital koḷka. aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyaṉmaiyiṉ viḷakkuṟumai viḷakkuṟaittāṉ eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu+ peṟāvāyiṉa; ivai ēḻāvatum iraṇṭāvatum^ tiritaliṉ. iṅṅaṉam eḻuttu+ peṟuvaṉavum^ tirivaṉavumellām varumoḻiyēpaṟṟi varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (1)

TE144n

ñanama yavaveṉu mutalāku moḻiyu
muyirmuta lākiya moḻiyu muḷappaṭa
vaṉṟi yaṉaittu mellā vaḻiyu
niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpā kummē.

itu muṟkūṟiya nālvakai+ puṇarcciyuḷ iyalpu puṇaruṅkāl ikkūṟiya patiṉēḻeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum vēṟṟumaiyilum alvaḻiyilum varumoḻi iyalpāy muṭika veṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñanamayava eṉum mutalāku moḻiyum --- ñanamayava eṉṟu collappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷ mutalāyniṟkum^ coṟkaḷum, uyirmutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa --- uyireḻuttu mutalāyniṉṟa coṟkaḷum^ tammiṟkūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉaittum --- appatiṉēḻākiya varumoḻikaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum --- vēṟṟumaiyum alvaḻiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, niṉṟacoṉmuṉ --- irupattunāṉku īṟṟavāyniṉṟa peyarccoṉmuṉṉar, iyalpākum --- tiripiṉṟi iyalpu puṇarcciyāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

uyirīṟṟiṉkaṇ ekara okaram oḻintaṉa koḷka.

utāraṇam: viḷa palā kiḷi kurī kaṭu pū cē kai cō kau eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntaiyatu eṉa meymmutaṉmoḻi varuvittu, poruḷ tarutaṟku ēṟpaṉa aṟintu kūṭṭuka. cō eṉpatu araṇ. ataṟku cō ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉa+ koḷka. kauveṉpataṟku+ kauñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu eṉka. iṉi ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl aḻakitu āyiṟṟu illai īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṟiṟṟu eḻuntatu ēyntatu aitu oṉṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyattatu eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ cōvukku iṭintatu īṇṭaiyatu uḷḷatu ūṟiṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟaiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ viḷa mutaliyavaṟṟai niṟutti ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai aḻaku ākkam iḷamai īṭṭam uyarvu ūṟṟam eḻucci ēṟṟam aiyam oḻivu ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka. ēlātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōla ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka.

iṉi+ puḷḷiyīṟṟu ūkāramum ṉakāramum mēṟkūṟupa. ēṉaiya īṇṭu+ kūṟutum.

utāraṇam: uriñ verin eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñekiḻcci nīṭṭippu aṭaivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. maram vēy vēr yāḻ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntaiyatu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ makara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ keṭutal `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. nilaimoḻi+ tiripu īṇṭu+ koḷḷāmai uṇarka. yakara īṟu yakarattiṉmuṉṉar iraṇṭiṭattum^ keṭutal īṇṭu ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. vēl tev kōḷ eṉa niṟutti ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka. ṇakāra lakāra ḷakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar nakaram varumoḻiyāka vantuḻi annakaram^ tiritaliṉ attirinta utāraṇaṅkaḷ īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka. ivaṟṟuḷ tirintu varuvaṉavuḷa; avai eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ēṉaiyōttukkaḷuḷ muṭikkiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ uyirkkaṇamāyiṉ oṟṟiraṭṭiyum uṭampaṭu meypeṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyum^ karuvittiripukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā ivviyalpiṉkaṇṇeṉṟu uṇarka. varakuñāṉṟatu varakuñāṟci eṉa+ kuṟṟukarattiṉkaṇṇum ivvāṟē koḷka. irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkum alvaḻikkum akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟai oru cūttirattāṉ+ ṟokuttu muṭittār. mēlum ivvāṟē kūṟupa. ivviyalpu varumoḻi nōkki+ kūṟiyateṉṟu uṇarka. ivviyalpupuṇarcci meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu. uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāmaiyiṉ mey muṟkūṟiṉār. (2)

TE145n

avaṟṟuḷ
melleḻuttu iyaṟkai yuṟaḻiṉum varaiyār
colliya toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku ammuṭipu vilakki+ piṟitu viti eytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉuḷ, melleḻuttiyaṟkai uṟaḻiṉum varaiyār --- melleḻuttu iyalpiyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa --- collappaṭṭa toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

ummai etirmaṟai. eṉavē, uṟaḻāmai valiyuṭaittāyiṟṟu. katirñeri katirññeri nuṉi muri eṉavum, itaḻñeri itaḻññeri nuṉi muri eṉavum varum. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuḷḷum^ cila uṟaḻccipeṟṟu muṭital koḷka. pūñeri pūññeri nuṉi muri kāyñeri kāyññeri nuṉi muri eṉavarum. colliyaveṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuḷ+ cila mikkumuṭital koḷka. kaiññerittār nīṭṭiṉār maṟittār eṉavarum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē īreḻuttorumoḻikkaṇ meyññāṉam nūlmaṟantār eṉa varum. ivaṟṟai nalintu kūṟa+ piṟattaliṉ iyalpeṉpārum uḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mikātaṉa. (3)

TE146n

ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvu ñāvum
viṉaiyō raṉaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu yakara ñakara mutaṉmoḻivanta iṭattu nikaḻvatōr taṉmai kūṟukiṉṟatu. ituvum puṇariyaloḻipāy+ karuvippāṟpaṭum.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum --- ṇakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta yāvum ñāvum mutalākiya viṉaiccoṟkaḷ, viṉaiyōraṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- oruviṉaivanta taṉmaiyai okkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē' `maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē' eṉavum `poṉyātta tārppuravi parikkumē' `poṉñātta tārppuravi parikkumē' eṉavum varum.

viṉaikkaṇṇeṉavē maṇyāmai maṇñāmai eṉa+ peyarkkaṇ vārāvāyiṉa. ñāmuṟkūṟātu yāmuṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ñācceṉṟavaḻi yāccellātu yācceṉṟavaḻi ñāccellumeṉṟu koḷka. maṇñāṉṟatu eṉṟavaḻi maṇyāṉṟatu eṉṟu vārāmai uṇarka. (4)

TE147n

moḻimuta lāku mellā veḻuttum
varuvaḻi niṉṟa vāyiru puḷḷiyum
vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi+ tiripiṭa ṉilavē.

itu ṇakāra īṟum ṉakāra īṟum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: moḻimutalākum ellāveḻuttum varuvaḻi --- moḻikku mutalā meṉappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum --- muṉṉarkkūṟi niṉṟa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum, vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi+ tiripiṭaṉ ilavē --- vēṟṟumai yallāta iṭattu+ tiriyumiṭam ila eṉṟavāṟu.

maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti+ kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntaiyatu aṭaintatu āyiṟṟu illai īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṭṭiṟṟu evviṭattatu ēṟiṟṟu aitu oḻukiṟṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auvaiyatu eṉa oṭṭuka. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ṇakārattiṟku+ ciṟupāṉmai tiripum uṇṭeṉṟu koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum; itaṟku+ cāṇākiya kōleṉka. ivai `niṉṟacoṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟa vaḻi aṭaṅkāvāyiṉa; atu varumoḻipaṟṟi+ tiriyāmai kūṟiyatākaliṉ. itu nilaimoḻipaṟṟi+ tiriyāmai kūṟiyatu. (5)

TE148n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻi
mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai yāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiripu eyti niṉṟavaṟṟai īṇṭu vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻi+ tiriyāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉāṉa --- alvaḻikkaṇ aṅṅaṉam^ tiriyātu niṉṟa avvoṟṟukkaḷ, vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarcciyiṭattum, valleḻuttalvaḻi mēṟkūṟiyaṟkai --- valleḻuttallāta iṭattu mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tiriyumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai nuntaiyatu aḻaku ākkam iṉmai eṉa ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka. ituvum^ ceykai+ cūttiram. mēl nāṉku cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟiyaṉa valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tiriyumāṟu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (6)

TE149n

laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
tanaveṉa variṉ+ ṟaṉavā kummē.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatōr varumoḻi+ karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷimuṉṉar --- lakāra ṇakārameṉṟu collavarukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tana eṉa variṉ --- takāramum nakāramum mutaleṉṟu collumpaṭiyāka+ cila coṟkaḷ variṉ, ṟaṉavākum --- niraṉiṟaiyāṉē avai ṟakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷāka+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu kaṉṉaṉṟu poṉṟītu poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilaimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (7)

TE150n

ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉa+ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ --- ṇakāra ḷakāra meṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar atikārattāl takāra nakāraṅkaḷ varumeṉiṉ, ṭaṇaveṉa+ tōṉṟum --- avai niraṉiṟaiyāṉē ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷāy+ tirintu tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṭītu maṇṇaṉṟu muḵṭītu muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilaimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (8)

TE151n

uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilai+ kiḷaviyum
puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilai+ kiḷaviyu
miyalpā kunavu muṟaḻā kunavumeṉ
ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.

itu muṉṉilaiviṉaiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilai+ kiḷaviyum --- uyirīṟāyvanta muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkaḷum, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilai+ kiḷaviyum --- puḷḷiyīṟāy vanta muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkaḷum, valleḻuttuvariṉ --- valleḻuttu mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, iyalpākunavum uṟaḻākunavumeṉṟu āyīriyala --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavumeṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṟikoṟṟā koṇākoṟṟā uṇkoṟṟā tiṉkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivai iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā naṭakkoṟṟā īrkoṟṟā īrkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci. īṟeṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyiṉ viṉaiccollē koḷka. ivai muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻi luṇarttuvaṉavum, avaṉai+ toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavu meṉa iruvakaiya. i ai āy mutaliyaṉa toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa. naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum^ toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa. nilkoṟṟā niṟkoṟṟā eṉa+ tirintuṟaḻntaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavu meṉṉum potuvakaiyāṉ muṭikka. iyalpu muṟaḻvu meṉṟiraṇṭiyalpiṉa eṉṉātu ākunavu meṉṟataṉāṉ tukkoṟṟā nokkoṟṟā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā eṉa ōreḻuttu orumoḻi muṉṉilaiviṉaiccol mikkē muṭitalkoḷka. (9)

TE152n

auveṉa varūu muyiriṟu collum
ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyum^
kuṟṟiya lukarattu iṟutiyu muḷappaṭa
muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā muṉṉilai moḻikkē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ+ cila ākātaṉavaṟṟai varaintu uṇarttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum --- auveṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟu+ collum, ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷiyiṟutiyum --- ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum --- kuṟṟiyalukarattai iṟutiyilēyuṭaiya collum, muṉṉilaimoḻikku uḷappaṭa muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā --- muṉṉar muṉṉilai moḻikku+ porunta+ kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻcciyumākiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ nilaimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭital koḷka.

utāraṇam: kauvukoṟṟā kauvukkoṟṟā, vauvukoṟṟā vauvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā uriñukkoṟṟā, porutukoṟṟā porutukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā tevvukkoṟṟā, kūṭṭukoṟṟā kūṭṭukkoṟṟā eṉa varum. (10)

TE153n

uyirī ṟākiya vuyartiṇai+ peyarum
puḷḷi yiṟuti yuyartiṇai+ peyaru
mellā vaḻiyu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu uyartiṇaippeyar vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭaikkaṇam uyirkkaṇa meṉṉum nāṉku kaṇattiṉum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaippeyarum --- uyirīṟāy vanta uyartiṇai+ peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇai+ peyarum --- puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya uyartiṇai+ peyarkaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum --- nāṉku kaṇattu alvaḻiyam vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷai `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭippārum uḷar. atu poruntātu, ivvāciriyar uyartiṇai+ peyarum viravuppeyarum eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ.

utāraṇam: nampi avaṉ eṉavum, naṅkai avaḷ eṉavum niṟutti, kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ eṉavum, yāvaṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum, yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭaintāṉ āyiṉāṉ auviyattāṉ eṉavum, aḻakiyaḷ āṭiṉāḷ auviyattāḷ eṉavum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum, yāppu vaṉmai eṉavum, aḻaku auviyam eṉavum ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. oruvēṉ eṉa+ taṉmai+ peyarkkaṇṇum^ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ eṉavum, kai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka. nī muṉṉilai viravu+ peyar ātaliṉ īṇṭaikku* ākā.

iṉi, uyirīṟu puḷḷiyiṟuti eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē uyartiṇaippeyar tirintu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. kapilaparaṇar, iṟaivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuvamāṇikkar eṉa ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. ācīvakappaḷḷi nikkantakkōṭṭam eṉa ivai avvīṟu keṭṭu oṟṟu mikku muṭintaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam kumarakkōṭṭam, piramakōṭṭam piramakkōṭṭam eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntaṉa. vaṇṇārappeṇṭir itu mikku muṭintatu. palcaṅkattār palcāṉṟōr pallaracar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa rakaravīṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa akaramum^ keṭṭu+ piṟaceykaikaḷum peṟṟumuṭintaṉa.

iṉi, ellāvaḻiyu meṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇai viṉaiccol iyalpāyum^ tirintum muṭivaṉa ellāṅkoḷka. uṇku uṇṭu varutu cēṟu uṇpal uṇṭēṉ uṇpēṉ eṉṉum^ taṉmai viṉaikaḷai+ koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭuka. uṇṭīr cāṉṟīr pārppīr eṉa muṉṉilaikkaṇṇum uṇpa uṇṭār cāṉṟār pārppār eṉa+ paṭarkkaikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka. ivai iyalpu. uṇṭaṉem^ cāṉṟēm uṇṭēnām eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa tirintu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa. (11)

TE154n

avaṟṟuḷ
ikara viṟupeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭaittē.

iḵtu uyartiṇaippeyaruḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātateyvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ ikara iṟupeyar --- muṟkūṟiya uyartiṇaippeyarkaḷuḷ ikara īṟṟuppeyar, tiripiṭaṉuṭaittu --- iruvaḻiyum^ tirintu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṭṭippū kāvitippū nampippēṟu eṉa ivvuyartiṇai+ peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ mikku muṭintaṉa. eṭṭi kāviti eṉpaṉa tēyavaḻakkākiya ciṟappuppeyar. eṭṭimaram aṉṟu. aḵtu `eṭṭikkumaraṉiruntōṉṟaṉṉai' eṉpataṉāṉ uṇarka. ivai eṭṭiyatupū eṭṭikkuppū eṉa viriyum. iṉi nampikkollaṉ nampiccāṉṟāṉ nampittuṇai nampippiḷḷai eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉeṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ uyartiṇaippeyar mikku muṭintaṉa. iṭaṉuṭaitteṉṟataṉāṉ ikaraīṟallātaṉavum īṟu tiriyātu niṉṟu valleḻuttuppeṟutal koḷka. naṅkaippeṇ naṅkaiccāṉi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ ciṟupāṉmai aikāra īṟu mikkaṉa. ivvīṟṟaḵṟiṇaippeyar mikkumuṭital uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷ+ kūṟupa. (12)

TE155n

aḵṟiṇai viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē.

itu viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḵṟiṇaiviravuppeyar --- uyartiṇaippeyarōṭu aḵṟiṇai viraviya viravuppeyar, iyalpu māruḷa --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ummaiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

uyartiṇaippeyarōṭu aḵṟiṇaiceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟateṉṉai? collatikārattu `irutiṇai+ coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimaiyiṉ' ((col//172)) eṉṟu cūttiram^ ceyvārāleṉiṉ, atuvum poruntumāṟu kūṟutum. cāttaṉ cātti, muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉavarum viravuppeyarkkaṇ uyartiṇaikku urittāka ōtiya āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiniṉṟa īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇai yāṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiṟṟeṉṟal vēṇṭum; eṉṉai? aḵṟiṇaikku orumaippālum paṉmaippālum uṇarttum īṟaṉṟi āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ uḷavāka āciriyar ōtāmaiyiṉ; aṅṅaṉam uyartiṇai irupālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ niṉṟē aḵṟiṇaiyāṇpālaiyum peṇpālaiyum uṇarttutaliṉ aḵṟiṇai uyartiṇaiyōṭu ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ uṇmai+ taṉmai+ tōṟṟam^ kūṟuvāṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟē viḷimarapiṉkaṇ+ `kiḷantaviṟuti yaḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar' ((col//150)) eṉpuḻiyum āciriyar uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viraviya viravuppeyareṉa āṇṭum uṇmai+ taṉmai+ tōṟṟam^ kūṟuvar. māṇākkaṉ iṉitu uṇartaṟku ivvāṟu viravuppeyariṉatu uṇmaittaṉmai+ tōṟṟam iraṇṭu atikārattuṅkūṟi, avviravuppeyar vaḻakkiṉkaṇ irutiṇaipporuḷum uṇartti irutiṇai+ collāy niṟṟaṟkum otta urimaiyavāmeṉa+ pulappaṭa niṟkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi, avai alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy niṟkumāṟu: --- cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉ cātti koṟṟi eṉa niṟutti+ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāvaṉ valiyaṉ ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭaintāṉ auvittāṉ aṭaintāḷ auvittāḷ eṉavam nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭi uṇarka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai aḻaku auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ṉakāram niṟpa+ takāra nakāram vantuḻi+ tiriyum utāraṇam īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka. iṉi+ cāttaṉkuṟitu cāttikuṟitu eṉa aḵṟiṇai muṭipēṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. ivaṟṟoṭu viṉaiccol talaippeyya ivai irutiṇaikkum uriyavām. āṇṭu nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivataṉai īṇṭu+ tokuttār. iḵtu uyartiṇaikkum. ummaiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa ṉakāra īṟṟuḷ+ kāṭṭutum. (13)

TE156n

puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāṉ+
ṟammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ
meymmai yākalu muṟaḻa+ tōṉṟalu
mammuṟai yiraṇṭu muriyavai yuḷavē
vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṉ+ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.

itu mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcol --- mūṉṟāvataṟku uriya viṉaimutaṟporuḷāṉuḷavākiya toḻiṟcol, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉum uyiriṟukiḷavi muṉṉum variṉ --- puḷḷiyīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum uyirīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum varumāyiṉ, meymmaiyākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalum ammuṟaiyiraṇṭum uriyavai uḷa --- avaṟṟuḷ iyalpākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalumākiya ammuṟai yiraṇṭum peṟutaṟku uriya uḷavātalāl, vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṉ+ colliya muṟaiyāṉ vēṇṭum valleḻuttumikuti --- uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumaippuṇarccikku+ colliya muṟaiyāṉ virumpum valleḻuttu mikutiyai, pōṟṟal --- īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

meymmai paṭṭāṅkātaliṉ iyalpām.

utāraṇam: nāy puli eṉa niṟutti+ kōṭpaṭṭāṉ cārappaṭṭāṉ tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa varuvittu iyalpāyavāṟu kāṇka. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai uṟaḻntaṉa. ivai nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟai+ tokuttār.

puḷḷiyiṟuti uyiriṟukiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ekarappēṟum uṟaḻccikku+ koṭukka. ammuṟaiyiraṇṭu muriyavai yuḷavē eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilaimoḻiyoṟṟu+ tiritalum^ koḷka. ivvīṟukaḷ nāykkāl tērkkāl kiḷikkāl eṉa āṇṭu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikumāṟu kāṇka. (14)

mel=eḻuttu miku-vaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟal um
val=eḻuttu miku-vaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟal um

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum --- `marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//217)) eṉṟataṉāṉ viḷaṅkuṟaittāṉeṉa melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu viḷakkuṟaittāṉeṉa valleḻuttu tōṉṟutalum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum --- `makaraviṟuti'(eḻu//310) eṉpataṉāṉ marakkuṟaittāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu maraṅkuṟaittāṉ eṉa melleḻuttu+ tōṉṟutalum, iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum --- `tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkaiyākum' ((eḻu//358)) eṉṟavaḻi+ tāykolai eṉa iyalpāy varumiṭattu+ tāykkolai eṉa mikuti tōṉṟalum, uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum --- `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum'(eḻu//226) eṉavum^ `kuṟṟeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉavum `ēyeṉiṟutikku' ((eḻu//277)) eṉavum^ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ uyirmikku varumiṭattu+ palākkuṟaittāṉ kaḻukkoṇarntāṉ ēkkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa uyirkeṭa varutalum, cāriyai uḷvaḻi+ cāriyai keṭutalum `vaṇṭum peṇṭum' (eḻu//420 eṉpataṉāṉ+ cāriyaippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉa+ cāriyai keṭṭu niṟṟalum, cāriyai uḷvaḻi+ taṉṉurupu nilaiyalum --- `vaṇṭum peṇṭum' eṉpataṉāṉ+ cāriyaippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭiṉai+ koṇarntāṉ eṉa+ taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum (itaṟku valleḻuttuppēṟu īṟṟuvakaiyāṉ+ koḷka), cāriyai iyaṟkai uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟalum --- `puḷimarakkiḷavikku' ((eḻu//244))eṉavum `paṉaiyumaraiyum' ((eḻu//283)) eṉavam `pūlvēleṉṟā' ((eḻu//375)) eṉavum peṟṟa cāriyai peṟātu iyalpāyniṉṟu puḷikuṟaittāṉ puḷikkuṟaittāṉ paṉaitaṭintāṉ paṉaittaṭintāṉ pūlkuṟaittāṉ pūṟkuṟaittāṉ eṉa mikkum^ tirintum uṟaḻcciyāka+ tōṉṟalum, uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum --- `uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇai+ peyarum' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iyalpāy varumeṉṟavai nampiyai+ koṇarntāṉ naṅkaiyai+ koṇarntāṉ eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭaṉurupu tokātē niṟṟalum (oḻiyātu eṉṟataṉāṉ makaṉ+ peṟṟāṉ makaḷ+ peṟṟāṉ eṉavum `āṭūuvaṟicol' `maḻavarōṭṭiya' `avarkkaṇ ṭemmuḷ' eṉavum oḻintum varumeṉṟu koḷka), aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyarkku avviyal nilaiyalum --- uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viravam peyarkku+ koṟṟaṉai+ koṇarntāṉeṉa urupu tokātē niṟṟalum, (avviyal nilaiyalum eṉṟataṉāṉē makappeṟṟēṉeṉa viravu+ peyarkkaṇṇum^ tokutal koḷka urupiyaluḷ+ `tēruṅkālai' ((eḻu//202)) eṉṟa ilēcāṉ itaṟkam muṉṉaiyataṟkum valleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka), meypiṟitākiṭattu iyaṟkaiyātalum --- puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ṇakāra ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttiyaiyiṉ meypiṟitāmeṉṟa iṭattu meypiṟitākātu maṇkoṇarntāṉ poṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉa iyaṟkaiyāy varutalum, aṉṉa piṟavum --- avaipōlvaṉa piṟavum, (avai `ekkaṇṭu peyarum^ kālai yāḻaniṉ kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi' eṉavam `nappuṇarvillā nayaṉillōr naṭpu' eṉavum varuvaḻi ekkaṇṭu nappuṇarvu eṉṉum^ toṭakkaṅkuṟukum uyartiṇai+ peyarkaḷ melleḻuttu+ peṟutaṟku uriyaṉa valleḻuttu+ peṟutal koḷka. iṉṉum^ tiṉaipiḷantāṉ mayirkuṟaittāṉ taṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṟuttāṉ pukaḻntāṉ eṉa varum) taṉ iyalmaruṅkiṉ --- taṉṉaiyē nōkki+ tiripu naṭakkumiṭattu, meypeṟa+ kiḷantu poruḷvaraintu icaikkum --- poruḷpeṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ēṉai vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭipiṉai+ tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumai+ tiripeṉa moḻipa --- iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē cāriyaiyuḷvaḻi+ taṉṉarupu nilaiyātu ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉavum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. `maṟaṅkaṭinta varuṅkaṟpiṉ' eṉavum `āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉ' eṉavum piṟāṇṭum perumpāṉmaiyum varumeṉṟu koḷka. maikoṇarntāṉ maikkoṇarntāṉ vilkōḷ viṟkōḷ eṉa varum. iṉi, ivvāṟu tiriyātu akattōttiṉ+ kūṟiya potumuṭipē tamakku muṭipāka varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. avai kaṭukkuṟaittāṉ ceppukkoṇarntāṉ eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa. `tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ' ((eḻu//156)) eṉṟa atikārattāṉ viṉaivantuḻiyē iṅṅaṉam perumpāṉmai tirivateṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṟkum viṉaiyōṭu muṭivuḻi+ tirital koḷka. atu `varaipāy varuṭai' `pulampukkaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟkāḷai' ((puṟam//258)) eṉṟāṟpōla varum. TE158n

vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi iai eṉṉu

vavaitā
miyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavu

iḵtu ikara īṟṟuppeyarkkum aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi --- vēṟṟumaiyallā iṭattu, iai eṉṉum īṟṟuppeyarkkiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya --- i ai eṉṉum īṟṟaiyuṭaiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakaiyākiya muṭipunilaiyaiyuṭaiya, avaitām --- ammuṭipukaṭām, iyalpākunavum --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum, valleḻuttu mikunavum --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum --- uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, eṉmaṉār pulavar --- eṉa ivaiyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paruttikuṟitu nāraikuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa ivai iyalpu. mācittiṅkaḷ cittiraittiṅkaḷ alikkoṟṟaṉ pulaikkoṟṟaṉ kāvikkaṇ kuvaḷaikkaṇ eṉa ivai mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu kiḷikkuṟitu tiṉaikuṟitu tiṉaikkuṟitu eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.

peyarkkiḷavi mūvakainilaiya veṉavē peyarallāta iraṇṭīṟṟu viṉaiccollum iṭaiccollum uriccollum iyalpum mikutiyumākiya iruvakai nilaiyavām. ollaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāra īṟṟu viṉaiccol mikuti. `iṉi yaṇi' ((eḻu//236)) yeṉpataṉkaṇ ikara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam eṭuttōtupa. ivaṟṟiṟku iyalpuvantuḻi+ kāṇka. `ceṉmati pāka' iḵtu ikara īṟṟu iṭaiccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻi+ kāṇka. `tillai+ collē' iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccoṉ mikuti. iyalpu vantuḻi+ kāṇka. `kaṭikā' iḵtu ikara īṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻi+ kāṇka. paṇaittōḷ iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu uriccoṉmikuti. iyalpu vantuḻi+ kāṇka.
