TE159n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya vikara viṟutiyu
mekaramutal viṉāvi ṉikara viṟutiyum^
cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya vaiye ṉiṟutiyum
yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉaiye ṉiṟutiyum
valleḻuttu mikunavu muṟaḻā kunavum^
colliyaṉ maruṅki ṉuḷaveṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu ēḻāmvēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟa ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: colliyal maruṅkiṉ --- ikara aikāraṅkaṭku muṉṉar+ kūṟiya mūvakai yilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ iyalpai nīkki, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, ekara viṉāviṉ mutal ikara iṟutiyum --- ekaramākiya viṉāviṉai mutalākavuṭaiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa avvaikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- yāveṉ viṉāviṉai mutaṟkaṇuṭaiya avvaikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum --- uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, uḷaveṉa moḻipa --- uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ utōḷikkoṇṭāṉ etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, āṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ īṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ ūṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ yāṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivai mikkaṉa. atōḷi avviṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻikoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ evvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ eṉa uṟaḻntaṉa. cuṭṭucciṉai nīṇṭataṟkum yāviṉāviṟkum varum aikāra īṟṟukku utāraṇam akkālattu āyiṭaikoṇṭāṉ āyiṭaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum vaḻaṅkiṟṟuppōlum. iṉi, āṅkavai koṇṭāṉ āṅkavaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. avai tiripuṭaiyaṉavām. colliyal eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟa aikāra īṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. aṉṟaikkūttar paṇṭaiccāṉṟō reṉavum orutiṅkaḷaikkuḻavi orunāḷaikkuḻavi eṉavum varum. (17)

TE160n

neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutalum^
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru viraṭṭalu
maṟiya+ tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟu meykeṭutalum --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu keṭutalum. kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉ uru iraṭṭalum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu iraṭṭittalum, aṟiyattōṉṟiya neṟiyiyal eṉpa --- aṟiyumpaṭi vanta aṭippāṭṭiyaleṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvaṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum makāramum lakāramum ḷakāramum eṉa aivakaiyām.

utāraṇam: kōṇimirntatu tāṉallaṉ nānallar vēṉaṉṟu tōṇaṉṟu eṉa nakaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭṭatu. kōṟītu vēṟītu eṉa+ takaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ lakāravoṟṟu+ keṭṭatu. ēṉaiya vantuḻi+ kāṇka. ivaṟṟai `laṉaveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//146)) `ṇaṉaveṉpuḷḷimuṉ' ((eḻu//150)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭittu+ keṭumāṟu kāṇka. oṟṟiraṭṭuvaṉa ñakāra ṅakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. kaṇṇaḻakitu poṉṉakal tammāṭai collaḻakitu eḷḷaḻakitu neyyakal tevvalaṉ eṉa+ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru iraṭṭiṉa.

mēlai+ cūttirattu nāṉkaṉurupu piṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻikkaṇṇateṉṟu uṇarka. kuṟiyatu piṟkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl tammai emmai niṉṉai eṉa neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum^ kuṟiyataṉmuṉṉaroṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, viraṉaṉṟu kuṟaṇimirntatu eṉa+ kuṟiliṇaiyiṉmuṉṉar vanta oṟṟu+ keṭutalum, varāṟītu naṉṟu eṉa+ kuṟaṉeṭiṟkaṇiṉṟa oṟṟu+ keṭutalum, atukoṟōḻi eṉavum^ kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉavum^ toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuniṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum iṭaiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭuniṟṟaluṅ, kāṟṟītu eṉavum viraṟṟītu eṉavum oṟṟuniṟṟalum^ koḷka. iṉi aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ tēṉṟītu kāṇṭītu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa keṭāmainiṟṟaluṅ, keṭutalum^ takaranakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻiyeṉpatūum^ koḷka. neṟiyiyaleṉṟataṉāṉ+ cuṭṭiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvaṭai avvāṭai eṉa iṭaivakaravoṟṟu ilvaḻiyum iraṭṭutal koḷka. (18)

TE161n

āṟa ṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉum^
kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai
yīṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyu
neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.

iḵtu urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ āṉa --- neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttu+ kuṟukimuṭiyum aṟuvakaippaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉarvanta, āṟaṉurupiṉum nāṉkaṉurupiṉum --- āṟām vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum nāṉkām vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum, kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭalillai --- muṉṉar nilaimoḻikku iraṭṭumeṉṟa kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭivarutalillai, īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum --- nilaimoḻiyīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkaḷ akarampeṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyalil `nīyeṉṉorupeyar' ((eḻu//176)) eṉavum^ `tām nāmeṉṉum' ((eḻu//188)) eṉavun, `tāṉ yāṉeṉṉum' ((eḻu//192)) eṉavum^ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ+ kuṟuki oṟṟiraṭṭi+ tammai nammai emmai taṉṉai niṉṉai eṉṉai eṉa varuvaṉa itaṉāṉē tamatu namatu ematu taṉatu niṉatu eṉatu eṉavum, tamakku namakku emakku taṉakku niṉakku eṉakku eṉavum oṟṟiraṭṭātu akarampeṟṟuvantaṉa. nāṉkāvataṟku oṟṟumikutal `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. āṟaṉurupākiya akaram ēṟimuṭiyāmaikku+ kāraṇam `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpuḻi+ kūṟiṉām. oṟṟiraṭṭāmaiyum akarappēṟum iraṇṭaṟkum ottavitiyeṉṟu uṇarka. kūṟiyaveṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukāta tam nam num eṉa varum^ cāriyaikaṭkum ivviru vitiyum^ koḷka. ellārtamakkum ellānamakkum ellīr numakkum ellārtamatum ellānamatum ellīrnumatum eṉa varum. (19)

TE162n

numme ṉiṟutiyu mannilai tiriyātu.

itu neṭumutalkuṟukāta nummeṉkiṉṟatum avviti peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟutiyum --- neṭumutalkuṟukā nummeṉṉum makaravīṟum, annilai tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāmaiyum īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyalum eytum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: numatu numakku eṉa varum. (20)

TE163n

ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
yakaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟkai.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻicceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟuti --- ukarappēṟṟōṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟkai --- yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu avvukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

av= īṟukaḷ āvaṉa puḷḷi-mayaṅkiyal uḷ ukaram peṟum eṉṟu vitikkum pala īṟukaḷ um eṉṟu koḷka. uriñ yāṉā aṉantā ātā ikalā īntā uḻuntā ūrā eyiṉā ēṟā aiyā oḻukkā ōtā auviyā eṉavum, uriñ yātu aḻaku eṉa v-um oṭṭuka. ēṉai+ puḷḷikaḷōṭum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu oṭṭuka.

`ñakāraiyoṟṟiya' ((eḻu//296)) eṉpataṉāṉum `ñanamava viyaiyiṉum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉpataṉāṉum yakaramum uyirum vantāl ukarampeṟātu iyalpāmeṉpatu peṟutaliṉ īṇṭu vilakkal vēṇṭāveṉiṉ, eṭuttōttil vaḻiyatē uyttuṇarcciyeṉṟu koḷka.

itu mutalāka alvaḻi kūṟukiṉṟār. (21)

TE164n

uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
yaḷavu niṟaiyu meṇṇum^ cuṭṭi
yuḷaveṉa+ paṭṭa vellā+ collum^
tattam^ kiḷavi tammaka+ paṭṭa
muttai varūum^ kālam^ tōṉṟi
ṉotta teṉpa vēyeṉ cāriyai.

iḵtu aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇumākiya peyarkaḷ tammiṉ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki --- uyirum puḷḷiyum^ tamakku īṟāy, aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇum^ cuṭṭi uḷaveṉappaṭṭa ellāccollum --- aḷavaiyum niṟaiyaiyum eṇṇaiyum^ karuti varuvaṉavuḷaveṉṟu āciriyar kūṟappaṭṭa ellāccoṟkaḷum, tattam^ kiḷavi tammakappaṭṭa --- tattamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāy+ tammiṉ+ kuṟainta coṟkaḷ, muttai varūum^ kālantōṉṟiṉ --- tammuṉṉē varuṅkālam^ tōṉṟumāyiṉ, ēyeṉ cāriyai ottatu eṉpa --- tām ēyeṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalai+ poruntiṟṟeṉpār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

muntai muttaiyeṉa vikāram.

nāḻiyēyāḻākku uḻakkēyāḻākku kalaṉēpatakku eṉa aḷavuppeyar ēkārampeṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟaintaṉa vantaṉa. toṭiyēkaḵcu kaḻañkēkuṉṟi koḷḷēyaiyavi eṉa niṟaippeyar ēkāram peṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟaintaṉa vantaṉa. oṉṟēkāl kālēkāṇi kāṇiyēmuntirikai eṉa eṇṇuppeyar ēkārampeṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟaintaṉa vantaṉa. atikārampaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē kuṟuṇināṉāḻi ainnāḻiyuḻakku eṉa ēkāramiṉṟi varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. (22)

TE165n

arai y-eṉa varūum pāl varai kiḷavikku+
puraivatu aṉṟu* āl+ cāriyai y-iyaṟkai.

itaṉ poruḷ: araiyeṉavarūum pālvaraikiḷavikku --- ammūvakaiccoṉ muṉṉar varum araiyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟai uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku, cāriyai yiyaṟkai puraivataṉṟu --- ēyeṉcāriyai peṟuntaṉmai poruntuvataṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

āl acai.

utāraṇam: uḻakkarai ceviṭṭarai mūvuḻakkarai eṉavum, kaḵcarai kaḻañcarai toṭiyarai koḷḷarai eṉavum, oṉṟarai pattarai eṉavum ivai ēyeṉcāriyai peṟāvāy vantaṉa. puraivataṉṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kalavaraiyeṉpataṉai oṟṟukkeṭuttu+ ceykaiceytu muṭikka. itaṉāṉē ceviṭṭaraiyeṉpuḻi ṭakaravoṟṟu mikutalum^ koḷka. `oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku' ((eḻu//132)) aṉmaiyiṉ+ cāriyai peṟāvāyiṉaveṉṟālōveṉiṉ avai peṟṟum peṟātum varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ kūṟiyatākalāṉum itu `tammakappaṭṭa' ((eḻu//164)) eṉa varaintōtiṉamaiyāṉum vilakkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. (23)

TE166n

kuṟaiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kālai
niṟaiya+ tōṉṟum vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu, ēyeṉcāriyai vilakki vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟaiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvarukālai --- kuṟaiyeṉṉuñcol aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattiṟku, vēṟṟumaiyiyaṟkai --- vēṟṟumaippuṇarcci muṭipiṟku urittāka+ kūṟuntaṉmai, niṟaiyattōṉṟum --- nirampattōṉṟam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: urikkuṟai kalakkuṟai eṉavum, toṭikkuṟai koṭkuṟai eṉavum, kāṇikkuṟai kāṟkuṟai eṉavum varum. uriyanellum^ kuṟainellu meṉka.

vēṟṟumaiyiyaṟkaiyeṉavē ivai vēṟṟumaiyaṉṟāyiṉa. eṉavē urikkuṟaiyeṉpataṟku uriyum uḻakkumeṉa+ poruḷāyiṟṟu. ēṉaiyavum aṉṉa.

muṉvarukālai yeṉṟataṉāṉē kalappayaṟu kalappāku eṉṟāṟpōla+ poruṭpeyarōṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ivvēṟṟumai muṭipu eytuvikka. pākeṉṟatu pākkiṉai. niṟaiyaveṉṟataṉāṉē urikkūṟu toṭikkūṟu kāṇikkūṟu eṉa+ kūṟeṉṟaṟkum immuṭipu eytuvikka. (24)

TE167n

kuṟṟiyal ukarakku iṉṉē cāriyai.

itu vēṟṟumaimuṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukarakku+ cāriyai --- kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku+ kuṟaiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum vaḻi varuñcāriyai, iṉṉē --- iṉcāriyaiyēyām eṉṟavāṟu.

kuṟṟiyalukarakku itaṟku attu vitittu muṭikka. kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṉṉē yeṉpatum pāṭam.

uḻakkiṉkuṟai āḻākkiṉkuṟai eṉavum, kaḻañciṉkuṟai kaḵciṉkuṟai eṉavam, oṉṟiṉkuṟai pattiṉkuṟai eṉavum varum. itaṟku uḻakkum^ kuṟaiyumeṉpatu poruḷ. itu vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇāyiṉ uḻakkiṟkuṟaiyeṉa niṟkum. (25)

TE168n

attiṭai varūum^ kalame ṉaḷavē.

ituvum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, vēṟṟumaiviti vilakki attu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kalameṉ aḷavē --- kalameṉṉum aḷavuppeyar kuṟaiyōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, attu iṭaivarūum --- attu+ cāriyai iṭaivantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

kalattukkuṟai. itaṉai `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. itaṟku+ kalamum^ kuṟaiyumeṉpatu poruḷ. cāriyai muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē muṉ iṉcāriyai peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (26)

TE169n

paṉai y-eṉ aḷavu*um^ kāl eṉ* ^niṟai y-um*
niṉaiyum^ kālai y-iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

ituvum atu, vēṟṟumai viti vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉaiyeṉ aḷavum^ kāveṉ niṟaiyum --- paṉaiyeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarum^ kāveṉṉum niṟaippeyarum^ kuṟaiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉaiyuṅkālai iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- ārāyuṅkālattu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

paṉaiyiṉkuṟai kāviṉkuṟai eṉavarum. ivaiyum ummaittokai. niṉaiyuṅkālai yeṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikku uriya vitiyeyti valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum^ ciṟupāṉmai koḷka. paṉaikkuṟai kākkuṟai eṉavarum.

ittuṇaiyum alvaḻimuṭipu. ivaṟṟai `vēṟṟumai alvaḻi iai' ((eḻu//158)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟātu vēṟōtiṉār, ivai aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇumātaliṉ. (27)

TE170n

aḷaviṟku niṟaiyiṟku moḻimuta lāki
yuḷaveṉa+ paṭṭa voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
yavaitām^
kacatapa veṉṟā namava veṉṟā
vakara vukaramō ṭavaiyeṉa moḻipa.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aḷaviṟkum niṟaikkum moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iṉaittu eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷaviṟkum niṟaiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷaveṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttē --- aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum moḻikku mutalāyuḷḷaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttukkaḷ, avaitām^ kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā akaravukaramoṭu avaiyeṉa moḻipa --- avveḻuttukkaḷ tām^ kacatapakkaḷum namavakkaḷum akara ukaramākiya avaiyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku ivai aḷavu. kaḻañcu cīrakam toṭi palam niṟai mā varai antai ivai niṟai. niṟaikku ukaramutal+ peyar vantuḻi+ kāṇka. iṉi uḷaveṉappaṭṭa eṉṟataṉāṉē uḷaveṉappaṭātaṉavum uḷa. avai immi ōraṭai iṭā eṉa varaiyaṟai kūṟātaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tēya vaḻakkāy oruñār oru tuvali eṉpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṅṅaṉam varaiyaṟai kūṟiṉār, akattōttiṉuḷ muṭipukūṟiyavaḻi atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēl+ cellātu oḻintakaṇattiṉum^ cellumeṉṟaṟku. eṇṇuppeyar varaiyaṟaiyiṉmaiyiṉ+ kūṟārāyiṉār. (28)

TE171n

īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉivaiyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉa+
kūṟiya kiḷavi+ pallā ṟellā
meyttalai+ paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
yottavai yuriya puṇarmoḻi nilaiyē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭai; eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātaṉavaṟṟiṟku ituvē ōttākaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ --- uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyōṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivaṟṟu iyalpu ivaiyeṉa+ kūṟiya kiḷavi+ pallāṟellām --- immoḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu ivaiyeṉakkūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉatu avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātuniṉṟa palavakaimuṭipukaḷellām, meyttalaippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭucivaṇi --- uṇmaiyai+ talaippaṭṭa vaḻakkōṭu kūṭi, puṇarmoḻinilai ottavai uriya --- puṇarum moḻikaḷiṉ nilaimaikkaṇ+ poruntiṉavai uriyavām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: naṭañeḷḷā eṉa uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilaikkiḷavi meṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpāy muṭintatu. maṇṇukoṟṟā maṇṇukkoṟṟā maṉṉukoṟṟā maṉṉukkoṟṟā uḷḷukoṟṟā uḷḷukkoṟṟā kollukoṟṟā kollukkoṟṟā eṉpaṉa puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilaikkiḷavi ukarampeṟṟum uṟaḻntum muṭintaṉa. uriñuñeḷḷā iḵtu `auveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//152)) eṉpataṉ oḻipu. patakkanāṉāḻi patakkamunnāḻi eṉa ivai ēyeṉcāriyai peṟātu akkuppeṟṟu ataṉ iṟuti meymmicaiyoṭum^ keṭṭu+ puṇarntaṉa. vāṭṭāṉai tōṟṟaṇṭai eṉpaṉa takaram vantuḻi+ tirintu neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭātu niṉṟaṉa. cīrakarai eṉpataṉai+ cīrakam arai yeṉa niṟutti+ kakaravoṟṟiṉ mēlēṟiṉa akarattaiyum makaravoṟṟaiyum^ keṭuttu araiyeṉpataṉ akarattai yēṟṟi muṭikka. kalavarai yeṉpataṉai+ kalarai eṉa muṭikka. akara makaram^ keṭuttu nākaṇai yeṉa+ piṟavum varuvaṉa vellām iccūttirattāṉ muṭikka. (29)

TE172n

palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
peyariṭai vakaram^ keṭutalu mēṉai
yoṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭai
yoṉṟiya vakaram varutalu miraṇṭu
maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ+ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.

itu marūuccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum peyariṭai vakaram^ keṭutalum --- palarai yaṟiyum avareṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvareṉṉum peyar iṭaiyil vakaraṅkeṭutalum, ēṉai oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉā iṭaiyoṉṟiya vakaram varutalum --- oḻinta oṉṟaṉai aṟiyum atuveṉṉum^ colliṉmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāteṉṉum viṉāccol iṭaiyilē uyirōṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum --- ākiya avaiyiraṇṭum, maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ maṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum --- marumuṭipiṉ pakutiyiṭattu mikavum payiṉṟu tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaryār eṉavum atu yāvatu eṉavum varum. avar yāvareṉpatu vakaraṅkeṭṭu avaryāreṉa niṉṟavaḻi `yāa reṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi' ((col//210)) eṉṟu viṉaiyiyaluḷ+ kūṟum viṉaikkuṟippu+ collām piṟaveṉiṉ, ākātu; avvakaraṅkeṭṭālum īṇṭu yāvareṉṉum peyarttaṉmaiyāyē niṟṟaliṉ. atu peṟṟavāṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, īṇṭu+ palaraṟi coṉmuṉ vanta yāvareṉpataṉ vakaram^ keṭumeṉavē, ēṉai avaṉ avaḷ eṉṉum irupāṉ muṉṉum yāvareṉpatu vārāteṉṟum atu tirintu maruvāy niṟkumeṉṟum^ kūṟutalāṉum `yāvareṉṉum peyariṭai' eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē avaṉ yāvar avaḷyāvar eṉṟāl+ pālvaḻuvāmeṉpatu peṟṟām. itaṉai `yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉu --- māvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭu ((col//192)) eṉa+ peyarāka ōtiyavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. aṉṟiyum yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi muppāṟkum uritteṉṟu yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉai+ kuṟippiṉai avaṉyār avaḷyār avaryār eṉa muppāṟkum oppa urimai kūṟutalāṉum atu vēṟeṉpatu peṟṟām. atu viṉaiyiyaluḷ ōtiṉamaiyāṉuṉam `viṉāviṟ kēṟṟal' ((col//66)) eṉa+ payaṉilaiyāka ōtiṉamaiyāṉum vēṟāyiṟṟu.

iṉi `yāryārkkaṇṭēyuvappar' eṉa+ palaraṟicoṉ muṉṉaraṉṟi iyalpāka vanta yāreṉpatu yāṇṭu aṭaṅkumeṉiṉ atuvum yārai yārai+ kaṇṭeṉa urupu viritaliṉ yāvarai eṉṉum vakaraṅkeṭṭa peyarēyām. aṅṅaṉam nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyāy niṟṟal payiṉṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. itaṉāṉē `yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum' eṉa ēṉai oṉṟaṟicollum nilaimoḻiyāy niṟṟal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē yāravar yāvatatu eṉa ivviru collum nilaimoḻiyāy varutal koḷka. (30)

((tokaimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((6. urupiyal))

TE173n

aā uū ēau veṉṉu
mappā lāṟa ṉilaimoḻi muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyai.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

urupukaḷōṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ ivvōttu urupiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟṟokuttu+ puṇarttataṉai īṇṭu virittu+ puṇarkkiṉṟārākaliṉ, ivvōttu+ tokaimarapōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram akara mutaliya īṟṟāṉ varum āṟu īṟṟuccoṟkaḷ niṉṟa iṉ peṟṟu urupiṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. urupiṉ poruḷ paṭavarum puṇarcci mēṟkūṟupa.

itaṉ poruḷ: a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilaimoḻi muṉṉar --- a ā u ū ē au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu āṟiṉaiyum īṟākavuṭaiya nilaimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa, vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku iṉṉē cāriyai --- vēṟṟumai yurupukaṭku iṭaiyē varum^ cāriyai iṉcāriyaiyē eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉai viḷaviṉoṭu viḷaviṟku viḷaviṉatu viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum, palāviṉai palāviṉoṭu palāviṟku palāviṉatu palāviṉkaṇ eṉavum, kaṭuviṉai kaṭuviṉoṭu kaṭuviṟku kaṭuviṉatu kaṭuviṉkaṇ eṉavam, taḻūuviṉai taḻūuviṉoṭu taḻūuviṟku taḻūuviṉatu taḻūuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, cēviṉai cēviṉoṭu cēviṟku cēviṉatu cēviṉkaṇ eṉavum, vauviṉai vauviṉoṭu vauviṟku vauviṉatu vauviṉkaṇ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟē ceykaiyaṟintu oṭṭuka.

`iṉ= eṉa varūum vēṟṟumai y-urupiṟku*
iṉ= eṉ cāriyai y-iṉmai vēṇṭum.' ((eḻu//131))

eṉavē, ēṉaiya iṉpeṟumeṉṟaliṉ, `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeṉṟatu vilakkiṟṟām. (1)

TE174n

pallavai nutaliya vakara viṟupeyar
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇa lecca miṉṟē.

iḵtu iṉcāriyai vilakki vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pallavai nutaliya peyariṟu akaram --- paṉmaipporuḷai+ karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccamiṉṟu --- vaṟṟu+ cāriyaiyōṭu poruntutalai oḻitalillai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pallavaṟṟai pallavaṟṟoṭu palavaṟṟai palavaṟṟoṭu cillavaṟṟai cillavaṟṟoṭu cilavaṟṟai cilavaṟṟoṭu uḷḷavaṟṟai uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu illavaṟṟai illavaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.

eccamiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāṉē mēl iṉ peṟṟaṉa piṟa cāriyaiyum peṟutal koḷka. nilāttai tulāttai makattai eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pallavai nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟāmurupu vaṟṟuppeṟṟē muṭital koḷka. (2)

TE175n

yāveṉ viṉāvu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāveṉ viṉāvum --- yāveṉṟu collappaṭum ākāra īṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟuppēṟṟiṉ+ ṟiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

yāvaṟṟai yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉavarum. (3)

TE176n

cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu civaṇi
yoṭṭiya meyyoḻittu ukaram^ keṭumē.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara īṟṟuccol aṉ cāriyaiyōṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaram^ keṭum --- tāṉ poruntiya meyyai niṟutti ukaram^ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

ataṉai ataṉoṭu itaṉai itaṉoṭu utaṉai utaṉoṭu eṉa varum. atiṉai atiṉoṭu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa maru muṭippuḻi muṭintaṉa. oṭṭiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ cuṭṭumutal ukaramaṉṟi+ piṟa ukaramum uyirvaruvaḻiyiṉ+ keṭuvaṉa koḷka. avai katavu kaḷavu kaṉavu eṉa niṟutti aḻakitu illai eṉa varuvittu ukaram^ keṭuttu muṭikka. ivaṟṟai vakara īṟākki ukaram peṟṟaṉaveṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟuvarum vakara īṟukaḷai oḻittu āciriyar `vakara+ kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu' ((eḻu//81)) eṉṟāṟpōla varaintōtal kuṉṟakkūṟalām ātaliṉ, avai ukara īṟeṉṟu koḷka. avai celavu varavu taravu uṇavu kaṉavu eṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum `puṉkaṇ ṇuṭaittāl+ puṇarvu' `pāṭaṟiyātāṉai yiravu' `kaṇṇāra+ kāṇakkatavu' eṉa+ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟuvarumāṟu uṇarka. (4)

TE177n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya vaiye ṉiṟuti
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalu murittē.

iḵtu aikāra īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiyeṉiṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu --- vaṟṟuccāriyaiyōṭu porunti niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu. ummaiyāṉ vaṟṟoṭu cila urupiṉkaṇ iṉcāriyai peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉṟavāṟu.

avaiyaṟṟai avaiyaṟṟoṭu ivaiyaṟṟai ivaiyaṟṟoṭu uvaiyaṟṟai uvaiyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iṅṅaṉam aikāram niṟka vaṟṟu vantuḻi `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' ((eḻu//122)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.

iṉi, ummaiyāṉ avaiyaṟṟiṟku avaiyaṟṟiṉkaṇa, eṉa nāṉkāvatum ēḻāvatum vaṟṟum iṉṉum peṟṟu vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ+ pallavai nutaliya akara īṟṟiṟkum ivviraṇṭu urupiṉkaṇ vaṟṟum iṉṉum^ koṭuttu+ palavaṟṟiṟku palavaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa muṭikka. itu mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum okkum. (5)

TE178n

yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉaiye ṉiṟutiyu
māyiya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulava
rāvayiṉ vakara maiyoṭum^ keṭumē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāviṉaiyuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuccollum, āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya cuṭṭumutal aikārampōla vaṟṟuppeṟum avviyalpil tiriyāteṉṟu colluvār āciriyar, āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭum^ keṭum --- avvīṟṟiṭattu vakaram aikārattōṭu kūṭakkeṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

yāvaṟṟai yāvaṟṟōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. vakaram vaṟṟumicaiyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuvataṉai+ kēṭu ōtiya mikaiyāṉē piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟu+ peṟutal koḷka. kariyavaṟṟai kariyavaṟṟoṭu neṭiyavaṟṟai neṭiyavaṟṟoṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟai kuṟiyavaṟṟoṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivai karumai neṭumai kuṟumai eṉṉum paṇpuppeyaraṉṟi+ kariyavai neṭiyavai kuṟiyavai eṉa+ paṇpukoḷpeyarāy niṟṟaliṉ vakara aikāram^ keṭuttu vaṟṟuccāriyai koṭuttu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai `aimpā laṟiyum paṇpu tokumoḻi' aṉmai uṇarka. (6)

TE179n

nīye ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟuku
māvayi ṉakara moṟṟā kummē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nī eṉ orupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum --- nīyeṉṉum orupeyar taṉmēl niṉṟa neṭitākiya īkāram^ kuṟuki ikaramām, āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum --- avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: niṉṉai niṉṉoṭu niṉakku eṉa+ ceykaiyaṟintu oṭṭuka. niṉakku eṉpataṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉunāṉkaṉurupiṉum' ((eḻu//161)) `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//164)) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka. niṉateṉpataṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. niṉṉeṉpatu vēṟorupeyarō eṉa+ karututalai vilakkutaṟku orupeyareṉṟār. peyarkkuṟuppākumeṉṉātu mutalkuṟukumeṉṟatu appeyariṉ eḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu kuṟukka meṉṟaṟku. neṭumutaleṉavē nakaraṅkuṟukutalai vilakkiṟṟu. uyirmey yoṟṟumai paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu. `uṭaimaiyu miṉmaiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum' ((eḻu//132)) eṉṟataṉai nōkki oṭuviṭattu+ cāriyaipeṟṟēvanta atikārattai māṟṟutaṟku+ cāriyaippēṟṟiṭai eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiṉār. (7)

TE180n

ōkāra v-iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyai.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōkāra iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyai --- ōkāra īṟṟiṟku iṭaivarum^ cāriyai oṉcāriyai eṉṟavāṟu.

kōoṉai kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṉēyeṉṟa ēkārattāṉ+ perumpāṉmaiyāka varum^ cāriyai oṉṉē, ciṟupāṉmai iṉ cāriyai varumeṉṟu koḷka. `oṉṟākaniṉṟa kōviṉaiyaṭarkkavanta' eṉavum, kōviṉai kōviṉoṭu cōviṉai cōviṉoṭu ōviṉai ōviṉoṭu eṉavumvarum.

ōveṉpatu matakunīr tāṅkum palakai. (8)

TE181n

aā v-eṉṉu marappeyar+ kiḷavikku
attoṭum^ civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupē.

iḵtu akara ākāra īṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aā eṉṉum marappeyar+ kiḷavikku --- aāveṉṟu collappaṭum marattai uṇarttukiṉṟa peyarākiya colliṟku, ēḻaṉurupu attoṭum^ civaṇum --- ēḻāmurupu iṉṉōṭaṉṟi attōṭum poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷavattukkaṇ palāvattukkaṇ eṉavarum.

`valleḻuttumutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ valleḻuttu+ koṭuttut `teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ `attiṉakara makaramuṉai' ((eḻu//125))+ keṭāmai+ ceykaiceytu muṭikka. (9)

TE182n

ña na v-eṉ puḷḷikku iṉ= ē cāriyai.

itu puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ñakāra īṟum nakāra īṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñana eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyai --- ñanaveṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭkuvarum^ cāriyai iṉcāriyai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñiṉai uriñiṉoṭu poruniṉai poruniṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (10)

TE183n

cuṭṭumutal vakara maiyu meyyum^
keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.

itu nāṉkumoḻikku īṟām vakara īṟṟuḷ+ cuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal vakaram --- av iv uv eṉṉum^ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya vakara īṟṟu+ col, aiyum meyyum^ keṭṭa iṟutiyiyal tiripiṉṟu --- aikāramum aikārattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyum^ keṭṭu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭinta yāvai ((eḻu//178)) eṉṉum aikāra īṟṟu+ colliyalpil tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaṟṟai avaṟṟoṭu, ivaṟṟai ivaṟṟoṭu, uvaṟṟai uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (11)

TE184n

ēṉai vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu eytātatu* eytuvittatu; peyarkku* ē y-aṉṟi uriccol vakarattiṟku muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- oḻinta uriccol vakaram iṉ cāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

tevviṉai tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu uriccol= āyiṉum paṭuttal ōcaiyāṉ+ peyar āyiṟṟu. (12)

TE185n

maḵkāṉ puḷḷi muṉ attu* ē cāriyai.

itu makara īṟu puṇarum āṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷi muṉ attu* ē cāriyai --- makaram ākiya puḷḷi y-īṟṟu+ col% muṉ varuñ cāriyai attu+ cāriyai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: marattai marattoṭu nukattai nukattoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) `attiṉakaram' ((eḻu//125)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka. (13)

TE186n

iṉṉiṭai varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

itu makara īṟṟiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉiṭai varūum moḻiyumāruḷa --- makara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷuḷ attēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyai iṭaiyē vantu muṭiyum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

mār acai.

utāraṇam: urumiṉai urumiṉoṭu, tirumiṉai tirumiṉoṭu eṉa voṭṭuka. (14)

TE187n

numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟkai yākum.

itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟkaiyākum --- nummeṉṉum makara īṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nummai, nummoṭu, numakku, nummiṉ, numatu, nuṅkaṇ eṉa varum.

numakku numatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉu nāṉkaṉurupiṉum' ((eḻu//121)) `nummeṉiṟutiyumannilai'(eḻu//162) `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka.

nuṅkaṇeṉpataṟku mēlaiccūttirattu `mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ makaravoṟṟu+ keṭuttu `valleḻuttu mutaliya' eṉpataṉāṉ melloṟṟu+ koṭukka. iyaṟkaiyeṉṟār cāriyai peṟāmai karuti. (15)

TE188n

tānā meṉṉu makara viṟutiyum
yāme ṉiṟutiyu mataṉō raṉṉa
āe ākum yāme ṉiṟuti
yāvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭu
mēṉai yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum.

itu makara īṟṟil muṟkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉōraṉṉa --- tām nāmeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum makara īṟum yāmeṉṉum makara īṟum nummeṉṉum makaraīṟupōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitalaiyuṭaiya, yāmeṉ iṟuti ā e ākum --- yāmeṉṉum makara īṟṟuccollil ākāram ekaramām, āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum --- avviṭattu niṉṟa yakaramākiya mey keṭutalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, ēṉai iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum --- oḻinta tām nāmeṉṉum iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutalkuṟuki+ tamnam eṉa niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tammai tammoṭu nammai nammoṭu emmai emmoṭu eṉa āṟu urupōṭum oṭṭuka. āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum^ karuvi yaṟintu muṭikka.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavayiṉmeyyum^ keṭukka. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa ēḻaṉurupiṉkaṇ makaraṅkeṭuttu `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttu+ koṭukka. (16)

TE189n

ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟa+ tōṉṟu
mummai nilaiyu miṟuti yāṉa.

itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāmeṉṉum iṟutimuṉṉar vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟattōṉṟum --- ellāmeṉṉum makara īṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉar attum iṉṉum aṉṟi vaṟṟeṉṉum^ cāriyai muṭiya+ tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ummai nilaiyum iṟutiyāṉa --- āṇṭu ummeṉṉum^ cāriyai iṟutikkaṇ nilaipeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

makaram vaṟṟiṉmicai yoṟṟeṉa+ keṭukka.

ellāvaṟṟaiyum ellāvaṟṟiṉum ellāvaṟṟukkaṇṇum eṉa varum. muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉaimuṟṟukarattiṟkum ummiṉ ukaraṅkeṭuttukkoḷka. ellāvaṟṟoṭum ellāvaṟṟukkum ellāvaṟṟatum eṉa varum. muṟṟukaramātaliṉ ēṟi muṭiyā. (17)

TE190n

makara īṟṟiṉai mēl vaṟṟiṉmicai yoṟṟeṉakkeṭutta atikārattāṉ+ keṭukka. ellānammaiyam ellānammiṉum ellānaṅkaṇum eṉa ukarampeṟṟum ellānammoṭum ellānamakkum ellānamatum eṉa ukaraṅkeṭṭum makaram niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku ellāraiyum ellāroṭum eṉpatu poruḷāka oṭṭuka. itaṟka nammuvakuttatē vēṟupāṭu. īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niṟṟal ((eḻu//161)) nāṉkāvataṟkum āṟāvataṟkum^ koḷka. TE191n

ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkai iṟuti um
ellīrum eṉṉum* muṉṉilai ~iṟuti ~um
oṟṟum ukaram um^ keṭum eṉa moḻipa
niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakara+ puḷḷi

tam= iṭaivarūum paṭarkkai mēṉa
num= iṭaivarūum* muṉṉilai moḻikku* ē.

itu makara īṟṟu uyartiṉaippeyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkaiyiṟutiyum --- ellārumeṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇai+ paṭarkkai+ peyarum, ellīrumeṉṉum muṉṉilaiyiṟutiyum --- ellīrumeṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇai muṉṉilaippeyarum, oṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭumeṉa moḻipa --- makaravoṟṟum ataṉmuṉṉiṉṟa ukaramum^ keṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟa colluvar pulavar, rakara+ puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- av vukaram ēṟiniṉṟa rakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, iṟutiyāṉa ummai nilaiyum --- avvirumoḻiyi ṉiṟutikkaṇṇum ummeṉṉum^ cāriyai nilaipeṟum, paṭarkkai mēṉa tam iṭaivarūum --- paṭarkkai+ colliṭattu+ tammuccāriyai iṭaivarum. muṉṉilai moḻikku num iṭaivarūum --- muṉṉilaiccoṟku nummuccāriyai iṭaivarum eṉṟavāṟu.

muṉṉar `mey' ((eḻu//188)) eṉṟa ilēcāṟkoṇṭa makarakkēṭu ivaṟṟiṟkum mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka.

paṭarkkaippeyar muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē rakara īṟṟu+ paṭarkkaippeyarum muṉṉilaippeyarum makara īṟṟu+ taṉmai+ peyarum tam num nam eṉṉum^ cāriyai iṭaiyē peṟṟu iṟuti ummuccāriyaiyum peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyār tammaiyum cāṉṟārtammaiyum eṉavum, kariyīrnummaiyum cāṉṟīrnummaiyum eṉavum, kariyēnammaiyum iruvēnammaiyum eṉavum ellāvurupoṭum^ ceykai yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉātataṉāṉ ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟkellām mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukaram^ keṭutal koḷka. `niṟṟalvēṇṭum rakarappuḷḷi' eṉṟataṉāṉē tammuppeṟāmai varumavaiyum^ koḷka. `ellārkku mellā nikaḻpavai' eṉa varum. (19)

aḻattai aḻaṉiṉai, puḻattai puḻaṉiṉai eṉa+ ceykaiyaṟintu ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. ṉakarattai attiṉmicai oṟṟu* eṉṟu keṭuttu `attiṉakaram' ((eḻu//125)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. tōṉṟaleṉṟataṉāṉ evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟu+ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceykai ceytu evaṟṟai evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka. ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. evaṟṟai eṉpuḻi nilaimoḻivakaram itaṉāṉ+ keṭukka. iṉi ottateṉṟataṉāl ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti attum iṉṉum koṭuttu+ ceykai ceytu ekiṉattai ekiṉiṉai eṉa oṭṭuka. attu iṉitu icaittaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār. TE199n

oṉṟumuta lāka+ pattūrntu varūu

iḵtu oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭu iṟutiyāka niṉṟa kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar ēḻiṉōṭum patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarntu oṉṟāyniṉṟa coṟkaḷ cāriyai peṟṟu+ tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

`niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam

vantiṭai nilaiyum' ((eḻu//437))

eṉpataṉāṉ āytam peṟṟatu.

utāraṇam: orupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉa+ kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaluḷ vitikkumāṟē niṟutti aḵteṉpataṉai+ keṭuttu+ pakaravoṟṟai niṟutti āṉcāriyai koṭuttu orupāṉai irupāṉai eṉa ellā eṇṇoṭum ellā urupiṉaiyum^ ceykai yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ummai etirmaṟaiyātaliṉ orupaḵtaṉai irupaḵtaṉai eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

colluṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pattūr kiḷaviyēyaṉṟi `oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai' ((eḻu//463)) `oṉpāṟkoṟṟiṭaimikumē' ((eḻu//475)) eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum pakarattuḷ akarampirittu aḵteṉpatukeṭuttu āṉkoṭukka. (27)

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarallāta kuṟṟukara īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

yātaṉai yātaṉoṭu eṉavum, ataṉai ataṉoṭu, itaṉai itaṉoṭu, utaṉai utaṉoṭu eṉavum varum. (28)

māvayi ṉiṟuti meyyoṭum^ keṭumē.

ituvum^ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉurupōṭu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: ticaippeyarmuṉṉar ēḻaṉurupiṟku --- ticaippeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta kaṇṇeṉṉum urupiṟku muṭipu kūṟuṅkāl, cāriyai+ kiḷavi iyaṟkaiyumākum --- muṉ kūṟiya iṉcāriyaiyākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum, āvayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭum^ keṭum --- aṅṅaṉam iyalpāyavaḻi+ ticaippeyariṟuti+ kuṟṟukaram^ taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭum^ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vaṭakkiṉkaṇ kiḻakkiṉkaṇ teṟkiṉkaṇ mēṟkiṉkaṇ eṉavum, vaṭakkaṇ kiḻakkaṇ teṟkaṇ mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum. iṉpeṟuvaḻi ukaram^ keṭāteṉṟu uṇarka.

āvayiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kīḻcār kīḻpuṭai, mēlcār mēlpuṭai, teṉcār teṉpuṭai, vaṭacār vaṭapuṭai eṉa+ cāriyaiyiṉṟi+ pala vikārappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē kīḻaikkuḷam mēlaikkuḷam, kīḻaiccēri mēlaiccēri eṉa aikāram peṟutalum^ koḷka. (29)

TE202n

puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum^
colliya valla vēṉaiya vellām^
tērum^ kālai yurupoṭu civaṇi+
cāriyai nilaiyum^ kaṭappā ṭilavē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttāyatōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: colliya alla puḷḷiyiṟutiyum uyiriṟukiḷaviyum --- muṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyīṟum uyirīṟum allāta puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ collum uyirīṟṟu+ collum, ēṉaiyavumellām --- muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷ tammuḷoḻintu niṉṟaṉavumellām, tēruṅkālai urupoṭu civaṇi+ cāriyainilaiyum^ kaṭappāṭu ila --- ārāyuṅkālattu urupukaḷōṭu porunti+ cāriyai niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟaimaiyai uṭaiyavalla, niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ēṉaiyavumeṉa ummai virikka. kūṟāta puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ aintu. avai ṇakara yakara rakara lakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām. maṇṇiṉai maṇṇai, vēyiṉai vēyai, nāriṉai nārai, kalliṉai kallai, muḷḷiṉai muḷḷai eṉavarum. uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu ikaram oṉṟumēyātaliṉ, ataṉai+ piṟkūṟiṉār. kiḷiyiṉai kiḷiyai eṉa varum.

iṉi+ tāṉ yāṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟiṉum ēḻeṉṉum ḻakara īṟṟiṉum oḻintaṉa poṉṉiṉai poṉṉai tāḻiṉai tāḻai eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa piṟavumām. iṉi, īkāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tīyiṉai tīyai īyiṉai īyai vīyiṉai vīyai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. aikāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tiṉaiyiṉai tiṉaiyai kaḻaiyiṉai kaḻaiyai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. ēṉai īṟukaḷiṉum varuvaṉa uṇarntu koḷka.

mēlē peyarīṟṟu+ ceykaiyellām^ tattam īṟṟiṉ kaṇ muṭippārātaliṉ avai īṇṭu+ kūṟalvēṇṭā.

iṉi+ tēruṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉē urupukaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka niṉṟu tamporuḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟu+ ceykaiyellām īṇṭu muṭittukkoḷka.

utāraṇam: nampiyai+ koṇarntāṉ maṇṇiṉai+ koṇarntāṉ koṟṟaṉaikkoṇarntāṉ eṉa mūvakai+ poruḷōṭum^ kūṭiniṉṟa urupiṟku oṟṟukkoṭukka. malaiyoṭu porutatu, mattikaiyāṉ+ puṭaittāṉ, cāttaṟku+ koṭuttāṉ, ūrkku+ ceṉṟāṉ, kākkaiyiṉ+ karitu, kākkaiyatu pali, maṭiyuṭpaḻukkāy, taṭāviṉuṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa urupu kāraṇamāka+ poruḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi iyalpāyum īṟutirintum oṟṟumikkum vantaṉa koḷka.

iṉi+ kaṇ kāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum urupāyum niṟkumātaliṉ avai urupāka+ koḷḷumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum^ ceykaikaḷellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. iḵtu urupiyalātaliṉ urupoṭu civaṇi eṉa vēṇṭā, am mikaiyāṉē urupupuṇarccikkaṇ+ ceṉṟa cāriyaikaḷellām īṟṟu+ poruṇmuṭipu uḷvaḻi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkum^ koḷka. viḷaviṉkōṭu kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa ellā īṟṟiṉum^ koḷka. nampiyai koṟṟaṉai eṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum^ cāriyaipeṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka. (30)

((urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((7. uyirmayaṅkiyal))

TE203n

akara viṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi+ kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ+
ṟatta motta voṟṟiṭai mikumē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum^ ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai+ kaṇaṅkaḷōṭum mayaṅki+ puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ ivvōttu uyirmayaṅkiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟpeyarōṭu urupu puṇarumāṟu kūṟi+ peyarvaruvaḻi urupu tokkuniṉṟa poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ urupiyalōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu.

iccūttiram akaraīṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar --- akaramākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi+ kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ --- vēṟṟumai yallāta viṭattu+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāy+ tōṉṟumāyiṉ, tattam otta oṟṟu iṭaimikum --- tattamakku+ poruntiya akkacatapakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷakkuṟitu nuṇakkuṟitu atakkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa oṭṭuka. ivai aḵṟiṇai iyal+ peyarākiya eḻuvāy viṉaikkuṟippu+ paṇpākiya payaṉilaiyōṭu muṭintaṉa.

ottaveṉṟamaiyātu tattamotta eṉṟataṉāṉ akara īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, akaram^ taṉṉai uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu mikku muṭiyum muṭipum^ koḷka. taṭakkai tavakkoṇṭāṉ vayakkaḷiṟu vayappuli kuḻakkaṉṟu eṉavum taṭañcevi kamañcūl eṉavum, akkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum varum. iṉi, iṭaiccol valloṟṟu+ peṟṟu varuvaṉa uḷavēl avaṟṟaiyum ivvilēciṉāṉ muṭittu+ koḷka. (1)

TE204n

viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvama+ kiḷaviyu
meṉave ṉeccamum^ cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyu
māṅka veṉṉu muraiyacai+ kiḷaviyu
ñāṅkar+ kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum iṭaiccollum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- viṉaiyai oḻipākavuṭaiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, uvamakkiḷaviyum --- uvamavurupāy niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, eṉaveṉ eccamum --- eṉaveṉṉam vāypāṭṭāl niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭākiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, āṅka eṉṉum uraiyacai+ kiḷaviyum --- āṅkaveṉṉum akara īṟṟu uraiyacai yiṭaiccollum, ñāṅkar+ kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum --- muṉṉarkkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: {uṇa tāva cāva} eṉa niṟuttik {koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉa valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. iv viṉaiyeccam oḻintaṉa ellām iṭaiccolleṉṟu uṇarka. {pulipōla+ koṉṟāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉavum, {koḷḷeṉa+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉavum, {akkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ} eṉavum, āṅka+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ `āṅkakkuyilu mayilum^ kāṭṭi+ kēcavaṉai viṭuttu+ pōkiyōṉē' eṉavum varum. uvamam viṉaiyeccaviṉai+ kuṟippēṉum oṉṟaṉōṭu poruvappaṭutal nōkki uvamaviyaliṉkaṇ āciriyar vēṟupaṭutti+ kūṟiṉār. eṉaveṉṉum eccamum irucollaiyum iyaivikkiṉṟa nilaimaiyāṉ iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ vēṟōtiṉār. āṅka eṉpatu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatallāmai `āṅkaveṉṉu muraiyacai' eṉṟataṉāṉum `āṅkavuraiyacai' eṉṉum iṭaiyiyal+ cūttirattāṉum uṇarka. ivai iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyum muṭipu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpuḻi+ kūṟiyatēyām. avai tāva pulipōla koḷḷeṉa āṅka eṉa niṟutti ñanamayava mutaliya moḻi ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu puṇartti iyalpāmāṟu oṭṭi+ koḷka. cuṭṭu mēl+ kūṟupa. (2)

TE205n

cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanama+ tōṉṟi
ṉoṭṭiya voṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.

itu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ meṉkaṇam iyalpākum eṉa muṟkūṟiyataṉai vilakki mikku muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ñanama+ tōṉṟiṉ --- akara+ cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaṇ mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭaimikutal vēṇṭum --- tattamakku+ poruntiṉa oṟṟu iṭaimikutalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

aññāṇ annūl ammaṇi eṉavarum.

oṭṭiyaveṉṟataṉāṉ aññeḷintatu annaṉṟu ammāṇṭatu eṉa akaram^ taṉṉaiyuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum mikutal koḷka. (3)

TE206n

yavamuṉ variṉē vakara moṟṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yava muṉvariṉ --- yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻi akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ, vakaram oṟṟum --- iṭaikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avyāḻ avvaḷai eṉa varum.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl akaram^ taṉṉaiyuṇara niṉṟa-vaḻi y-um vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḷaintatu eṉa varum. (4)

TE207n

uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu --- uyirkaḷ akaraccuṭṭiṉmuṉ variṉum muṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku varum iyalpiṟṟiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

a eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar aṭai eṉa varuvittu vakaram oṟṟi+ taṉṉuru iraṭṭi uyirēṟṟi avvaṭai avvāṭai avvilai avvīyam avvural avvūrti avveḻu avvēṇi avvaiyam avvoḻukkam avvōṭai avvauviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.

`neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//110)) eṉpataṉuḷ `neṟiyiyal' eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu nilaimoḻi+ toḻileṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ akaram^ taṉṉai yuṇaraniṉṟavaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḻakitu eṉavarum. tiriyāteṉṟataṉāṉ mēṟcuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka. (5)

TE208n

nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ ceyyuṭku āvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- akaraccuṭṭu nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `āy iru tiṇaiyiṉ icaikkumaṉa col= ē' ((col//1)) `āy iru pāl+ col' ((col//3)) eṉa varum. itu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟaliṉ vaṉkaṇam oḻintakaṇam ellāvaṟṟōṭum^ ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam vantavaḻi+ kāṇka. innīṭci irumoḻippuṇarccikkaṇ varutaliṉ `nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal' ākāmai uṇarka. (6)

TE209n

cāva veṉṉum^ ceya ~eṉ eccattu*
iṟuti vakaram^ keṭutal-um urittu* -ē

itu mēl viṉaiyeñcukiḷavi eṉṟa eccattiṟku eytātatu* eytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ:
cāva eṉṉuñ ceya ~eṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram
-- cāva ~eṉṟu collappaṭum^ ceya ~eṉ eccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakaramum,
keṭutalum urittu
-- keṭṭuniṟṟalum urittu eṉravāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōṭṭiṭaic cākkuttiṉāṉ eṉa varum cīṟiṉāṉ takarttāṉ puṭaittāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. keṭātatu muṉṉar muṭittām.

itaṉai `viṉaiyeñcukiḷavi' ((eḻu//204)) eṉṟataṉ piṉ vaiyātataṉāṉ iyalpukaṇattum innilaimoḻikkēṭu koḷka. cā ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāttāṉ vīḻttāṉ aṭaintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)

TE210n

aṉṉa veṉṉu muvama+ kiḷaviyu
maṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilai+ kiḷaviyum^
ceymmaṉa veṉṉum^ toḻiliṟu collu
mēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ+ kiḷaviyum^
ceyta veṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum^
ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
mamma veṉṉu muraipporuḷ+ kiḷaviyum
palavaṟ ṟiṟuti+ peyarkkoṭai yuḷappaṭa
vaṉṟi yaṉaittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ orucār peyarkkum viṉaikkum iṭaikkum muṉṉeytiyatu vilakkiyum eytātateytuvittum ilakkaṇam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṉṉa eṉṉum uvama+ kiḷaviyum --- aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvamavurupākiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilai+ kiḷaviyum --- aṇiyārai+ karutiṉa viḷiyākiya nilaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya akara īṟṟu uyartiṇai+ peyarccollum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum^ toḻiliṟucollum --- ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum^ toḻiṟcol+ poruḷ taruṅkāl um īṟṟāṉ iṟum^ collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ+ kiḷaviyum --- oruvarāṉ ēvaṟṟoḻiṉmai karuti+ kūṟappaṭṭa ēvaṟporuṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭittalai uṇarttum akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyyiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viṉaiyeccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, amma eṉṉum uraipporuḷ+ kiḷaviyum --- amma eṉṟu collappaṭum etirmukam-ākkiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccol= um, palavaṟṟu iṟutaṭi+ peyarkkoṭai uḷappaṭa --- paṉmai+ poruḷai uṇarttum akara īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷ aintiṉaiyum muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu kūṭṭikkoṭuttal uḷḷiṭṭu, aṉṟi aṉaittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- av= eṭṭu+ col= um iyalpu* āy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: poṉṉaṉṉa kutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa itu `viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi' ((eḻu//204)) eṉpataṉāṉ mikku muṭitalai vilakkiṟṟu. ūra kēḷ cel tā pō eṉa `uyirīṟākiyavuyartiṇai' eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ iyalpāymuṭivatu īṇṭu ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu akara īṟāy viḷiyēṟṟu muṭintamaiyiṉ eytātateytuvittatu. uṇmaṉakutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇṇumeṉaka virittum yāṉuṇmaṉa nīyuṇmaṉa avaṉuṇmaṉa eṉa niṟutti+ kūḻ cōṟu tēṉ pāl eṉa varuvittum muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟkum avvāṟē virittukkoḷka. iṅṅaṉam^ ceyyumeṉpataṉ poruṭṭākiya maṉave ṉiṟuticcol akkālam vaḻaṅkiyatātaliṉ āciriyar ataṉaiyum vēṟāka eṭuttōtiṉār. yāṉum niṉṉōṭuṭaṉvaruka avaṉcelka avaḷcelka avarcelka eṉa niṟutti+ kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ceṟuviṉkaṇ tāṉaikkaṇ pōriṉkaṇ eṉa varuvittu muṭikka. ivai ēvaṟporuṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭittaṉa. ēval kaṇṇiya veṉavē ēval kaṇṇātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avai: nīcelka atucelka avaicelka eṉa niṟutti muṟkūṟiya kāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟai varuvittu muṭikka. ivai ēval+ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭiyātaṉa. aḵṟiṇai ēval+ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭiyāmai viṉaiyiyaluḷ viyaṅkōḷ+ kaṇṇē poruḷiyalum^ ceyyuḷiyalumpaṟṟi+ kūṟutum. maṉavum viyaṅkōḷum eytātateytuvittatu. uṇṭakutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi ituvum atu. itaṟku uriya uṇṇātakutiraiyeṉṉum etirmaṟai y-um nalla kutirai y-eṉṉum^ kuṟippum^ koḷka. uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ itu muṉṉar viṉaiyeccam valleḻuttuppeṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eṇtiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. {amma koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā} eṉpatu iṭaiccol= ātaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu. {itukēḷāy koṟṟaṉē} eṉa etirmukam-ākkiyavāṟu kāṇka. pallakutirai palakutirai cillakutirai cilakutirai uḷḷakutirai illakutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa oṭṭuka. ikkālattu+ palla cilla eṉpaṉa vaḻaṅkā. ituvum viḷakkuṟitu eṉṟāṟpōla+ palakkutiraiyeṉa valleḻuttu eytiyataṉai vilakkiṟṟu. viḷinilai+ kiḷaviyākiya peyar muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē ceyyumeṉpataṉ maṟaiyākiya ceyyātaveṉpataṟkum ivviyalpu muṭipukoḷka. atu vārāta koṟṟaṉeṉa varum. ivviyalpu muṭipiṟku+ ceymmaṉa ciṟattaliṉ viyaṅkōṭkumuṉ vaittār. ēval kaṇṇiya eṉpataṉāṉ ēval kaṇṇātatum uḷateṉṟukūṟi `maṉṉiyaperumanī' ((puṟam//61)) eṉa utāraṇam^ kāṭṭukaveṉiṉ, atu poruntātu; kūṟukiṉṟāṉ avaṉ nilaipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟē karuti+ kūṟutaliṉ atuvum ēvalkaṇṇiṟṟēyām. ellāvaṟṟiṉum^ ciṟanta palavaṟṟiṟut muṟkūṟukaveṉiṉ, atu vaḻakkiṟkum^ ceyyuṭkum vēṟuvēṟu muṭipuṭaitteṉṟaṟkum^ ceyyuṇ muṭipu ivviyalpupōl+ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟaṟkum akara īṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟātu niṉṟa muṟṟuviṉaiyum viṉaikkuṟippum ivviyalpu muṭipu peṟumeṉṟaṟkum piṉ vaittār. uṇṭaṉakutirai itu muṟṟuviṉai. kariyaṉa kutirai itu muṟṟuviṉaikkuṟippu. iḵtu iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpuḻi+ poruntuvaṉavellām^ koḷka. (8)

TE211n

vāḻiya veṉṉum^ ceyaveṉ kiḷavi
yiṟuti yakaram^ keṭutalu murittē.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vāḻiya eṉṉum^ ceya eṉ kiḷavi --- {vāḻuṅkālam neṭuṅkālam ākuka} eṉṉum poruḷait tarum vāḻiyaveṉṟu collappaṭum^ ceyaveṉecca+ kiḷavi, iṟutiyakaram^ keṭutalum urittu --- iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟum^ keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

keṭutalumeṉavē, keṭātu muṭitalē perumpāṉmaiyeṉṟavāṟu.

vāḻikoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavarum. vāḻiya eṉpatē perumpāṉmai. vāḻiya yāṉ nī avaṉ avaḷ avar atu avai eṉa itu mūṉṟiṭattum^ cēṟaliṉ `uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilai+ kiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//151)) eṉpuḻi muṉṉilaiyiyalpām eṉṟataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. itu kuṟippuviyaṅkōḷ.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ iḵtu iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka. vāḻi ñeḷḷā eṉa varum. ivai vāḻttappaṭum poruḷ vāḻavēṇṭumeṉṉum^ karuttiṉaṉāka+ kūṟutaliṉ ēvalkaṇṇiṟṟēyām. allākkāl `vāḻttiyal vakaiyē nāṟpāṟku murittē' ((ceyyuḷiyal//109)) eṉpataṟkum vāḻttiyalāka+ cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷukkum payaṉiṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (9)

TE212n

uraipporuḷ+ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum varaiyār.

iḵtu ammaveṉpataṟku eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uraipporuḷ+ kiḷavi --- etirmukamākkum poruḷaiyuṭaiya ammaveṉṉum^ col, nīṭṭamum varaiyār --- akaramāki niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭumuṭitalaiyum nīkkār eṉṟavāṟu.

ammā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum. ummaiyāl nīḷāmaiyē perumpāṉmaiyām. varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ nīṭci iyalpu kaṇattum^ koḷka. ammā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā ātā eṉa oṭṭuka. (10)

TE213n

palavaṟṟu* iṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiya palavaṟṟiṟutikkaṇ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa --- palavaṟṟai uṇarttum aivakaiccolliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyumoḻikaḷum^ cilauḷa, ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi āṉa --- yāṇṭuḷaveṉiṉ+ ceyyuḷātalai+ karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum^ ceyyuṇ muṭipuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

uṭaitteṉṉātu uḷaveṉṟa paṉmaiyāṉ varumoḻikkaṇ+ cila eṉpatu vantu nīṭumeṉṟu koḷka. ceyyuḷāṉa eṉṉātu ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa eṉṟataṉāṟ, pala eṉpataṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭuḻi nilaimoḻi akarappēṟum varumoḻi ñakaramākiya melleḻuttuppēṟum, varumoḻiyiṟuti nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttuppēṟum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `palāam^ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar'; itaṉ coṉṉiṟu palacila eṉṉum^ cevveṇ. (11)

TE214n

toṭara liṟuti tammuṉ+ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟu* ākalu murittē.

itu palacila eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟi+ tiripum uṇṭeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toṭar al iṟuti --- toṭarmoḻiyallāta īreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya palacila eṉṉum akara īṟṟu+ col, tammuṉ tām variṉ --- tammuṉṉē tām varumāyiṉ, lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟu ākalum urittu --- tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟa lakaravoṟṟu ṟakaravoṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummaiyāṉ+ ṟiriyāmaiyum uritteṉṟār.

utāraṇam: paṟpalakoṇṭār ciṟcilavitti eṉavarum. akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭallāta kuṟṟeḻuttu ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākuvaṉa iṉmaiyiṉ toṭaraliṟuti yeṉavē īreḻuttorumoḻiyē uṇarttiṟṟu. taṉmuṉeṉṉātu tammuṉeṉṟa paṉmaiyāṉ+ palacila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭum^ taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa.

tammuṉvariṉeṉṉātu tāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ palaviṉmuṉ pala varutalum^ cilaviṉmuṉ cilavarutalum^ koḷka. lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟāmeṉa oṟṟiṟku+ tiripu kūṟi akaraṅkeṭutal kūṟiṟṟilareṉiṉ, atu vārātataṉāl vantatumuṭittaleṉṉum utti peṟavaittateṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉai ñāpakameṉpārum uḷar. aruttāpattiyāl tammuṉ tām variṉeṉavē tammuṉ piṟavariṉ lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟākātu akaram^ keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.

utāraṇam: palkaṭal cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, palyāṉai palvēḷvi eṉavum, cilkāṭu cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, cilyāṉai cilvēḷvi eṉavum varum. uritteṉṟatu akara īṟṇorumaipaṟṟi. (12)

TE215n

valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yuṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum.

itu muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷatōr muṭipu vēṟṟumai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu iyaṟkai --- muṟkūṟiya palacila veṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akara īṟṟu+ potuvitiyiṉ+ kūṟiya valleḻuttumikum iyalpu, uṟaḻattōṉṟum --- mikutalum mikāmaiyumāy uṟaḻntuvarattōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: palappala palapala cilaccila cilacila eṉavarum. īṇṭum^ tammuṉ tāmvarutal koḷka. iyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāṉ akaraṅkeṭa lakaram^ tirintum^ tiriyātum uṟaḻntum muṭitalum^ koḷka. paṟpala palpala ciṟcila cilcila eṉavarum. tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ akaraṅkeṭa lakaram melleḻuttum āytamumāka+ tirintu muṭitalum^ koḷka. paṉmīṉvēṭṭattu paṉmalar paḵṟāli paḵṟāḻicai ciṉṉūl ciḵṟāḻicai eṉavarum. itu muṉṉar+ tōṉṟumeṉṟu eṭuttōtiya ciṟappuvitiyāl akaraṅkeṭa niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟiṉ muṭipākaliṉ takaram varuvaḻi āytameṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu. (13)

TE216n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟi vaṉkaṇattōṭu vēṟṟumai tokkuniṉṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṟkūṟiya alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi+ tattam ottavoṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iruviḷa+ koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉa varum. iruviḷa+ kuṟumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa+ kuṇavēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum^ koḷka. iruviḷa veṉpatu ōlai; vēṇāṭṭakattu ōrūr; karuvūriṉakattu oru cēriyumeṉpa. iruviḷaviṉ+ koṟṟaṉ eṉa virikka. (14)

TE217n

marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟu marappeyarkku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum --- akara īṟṟu marappeyarāya col vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ataṅkōṭu viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

itu `kacatapa mutaliya' ((eḻu//143)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.

TE218n

makappeyar+ kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyai.

iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: makappeyar+ kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyai --- akara īṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyar+ colliṟku vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ varum^ cāriyai iṉ cāriyai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: makaviṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum. cāriyaippēṟu varaiyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ cellumeṉpatu āciriyarkku+ karuttākaliṉ makaviṉ ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa oṭṭuka.

mēl avaṇ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṉcāriyai peṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (16)

TE219n

attavaṇ variṉum varainilai yiṉṟē.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttum attum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: makattu+ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

avaṇ eṉṟataṉāl makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttu+ pēṟum makaviṉkai eṉa mēl iṉcāriyai peṟṟuḻi iyaipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvum, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka. nilaiyeṉṟataṉāl makam pālyāṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka. (17)

TE220n

palavaṟṟu* iṟuti yurupiya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttum vaṟṟum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palavaṟṟiṟuti --- palla pala cila uḷḷa illa eṉṉum palavaṟṟai yuṇarttum akara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupiyaṟkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu+ puṇarntāṟpōla urupiṉatu poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟuppeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

īṟṟu valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: pallavaṟṟukkōṭu palavaṟṟukkōṭu cilavaṟṟukkōṭu uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu illavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa oṭṭuka. urupu virintuḻi niṟkumāṟu pōlaṉṟi avvurupu tokku ataṉporuḷ niṉṟu puṇaruṅkāl vēṟupāṭu uṭaimaiyiṉ avvēṟupāṭukaḷ īṇṭu ōtiṉār ittuṇaiyu meṉṟu uṇarka. (18)

TE221n

ākāra viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu --- ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tattam otta oṟṟu iṭaimikum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mūṅkākkaṭitu tārākkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa oṭṭuka. (19)

TE222n

ceyyā veṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
mavviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu viṉaiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyā eṉṉam viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyyā veṉṉam viṉaiyeccamākiya collum ummaiyāṉ+ peyarecca maṟaiyākiya collum, avviyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam avviyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyā teṉṟu colluvār pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṇṇākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, uṇṇākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum. (20)

TE223n

ummai yeñciya virupeyar+ tokaimoḻi
meymmai yāka vakara mikumē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummaittokai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ummai eñciya irupeyar+ tokaimoḻi --- ummai tokkuniṉṟa irupeyarākiya tokaiccoṟkaḷ, meymmaiyāka akaram mikum --- meyyāka nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uvāappatiṉāṉku irāappakal eṉavarum. meymmaiyāka eṉpataṉāṉ valleḻuttu+ koṭukka. iḵtu eḻuvāyum payaṉilaiyumaṉṟi ummaittokaiyātaliṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ valleḻuttu+ peṟātāyiṟṟu.

ummaitokka eṉṉātu eñciya eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumaiyāṉ arāappāmpu eṉa+ paṇputtokaikkam irāakkoṭitu eṉa eḻuvāymuṭipiṟkum irāakkākkai eṉa+ peyareccamaṟaikkum akarappēṟu koḷka. varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāavaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum. iḵtu ummaittokai, arāakkuṭṭi eṉpatu paṇputtokaiyam vēṟṟumaittokaiyumām. uvāappaṭṭiṉi eṉpatu vēṟṟumaittokai. (21)

TE224n

āvu māvum viḷippeyar+ kiḷaviyum
yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟṟu* iṟutiyu
mēval kuṟitta vuraiyacai miyāvum^
taṉṟoḻi luraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō
ṭaṉṟi yaṉaittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytātateytuvittalum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvum --- āveṉṉum peyarum, māvum --- māveṉṉum peyarum, viḷippeyar+ kiḷaviyum --- viḷittalaiyuṭaiya peyarākiya uyartiṇaiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāvum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāppeyarum, palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum --- paṉmai+ poruḷai uṇarttum ākāra īṟṟu+ peyarecca maṟaiyākiya muṟṟu viṉaiccollum, ēval kuṟitta uraiyacai miyāvum --- muṉṉilai yēval viṉaiyai+ karutivarum etirmukamākkum^ colliṉaiccērnta miyā veṉṉum ākāraīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, taṉ toḻil uraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyoṭu --- taṉatu toḻiliṉai+ collum ākāra īṟṟu+ taṉmaiyākiya viṉā+ collōṭu kūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉaittum --- av veḻuvakaiyākiya collum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ākuṟitu mākuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu+ peyarākaliṉ mikku muṭivaṉa mikāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. ūrākēḷ cel tā pō eṉa iḵtu iyalpāmeṉṟa uyartiṇaippaṭarkkaippeyar tirintu muṉṉilaiyāy viḷiyēṟṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu. yā kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa ituvum peyarākaliṉ eytiya iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakkiyatām. uṇṇā kutiraikaḷ cennāykaḷ takarkaḷ paṉṟikaḷ eṉa iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, ceyyāveṉṉum^ cūttirattu ummaiyāṉ+ peṟṟa valleḻuttiṉai vilakkaliṉ. kēṇmiyā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, uṇkā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum ivviṭaiccoṟkaḷ muṭiyāmaiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatumām. uṇkā eṉpatu yāṉuṇpēṉō eṉṉumporuṭṭu. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ+ āyiṉ `ñanamayava'(eḻu//144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭipeytum. (22)

TE225n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟu vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- ākāra īṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi vēṟṟumaipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttuvantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tārā mūṅkā vaṅkā eṉa niṟutti+ kāl cevi talai puṟam eṉa varuvittu valleḻuttukkoṭuttu oṭṭuka. (23)

TE226n

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉaru mōreḻuttu moḻikku
maṟiya+ tōṉṟu makara+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu avvīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum --- ōreḻuttu orumoḻiyākiya ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyattōṉṟum --- niṟumoḻikkaṇ akaramākiya eḻuttu viḷaṅka+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: palāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kāakkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum varum. ōreḻuttorumoḻi akaram peṟutal ciṟupāṉmai yeṉṟaṟku ataṉai+ piṟkūṟiṉār. itu nilaimoḻi+ ceykaiyātaliṉ+ palāavilai palāanār eṉa iyalpu kaṇattum^ koḷka. aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ avvakaram īriṭattum poruntiṉavaḻi+ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē aṇṇāattēri tiṭṭāattukkuḷam eṉa attu+ koṭuttum, uvāattu ñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum ñāṉṟum^ koṭuttum, uvāattāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum āṉum^ koṭuttum iṭāviṉuḷ+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉum ēḻaṉurupum^ koṭuttum^ ceykaiceytu muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mūṅkāviṉkāl mūṅkāviṉṟalai eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttukkēṭum^ koḷka. (24)

TE227n

irā v-eṉ kiḷavikku akaram illai.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuppeyarkku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: irāveṉ kiḷavikku --- irāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu+ colliṟku, akaram illai --- muṟkūṟiya akaram peṟutaliṉṟi valleḻuttuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: irākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. irāakkākkai irāakkūttu eṉa+ peyareccamaṟaipporuḷ tārātu irāviṭattu+ kākkai irāviṭattu+ kūttu eṉa vēṟṟumai karutiyavaḻi irākkākkai irākkūttu eṉa akaram peṟāteṉṟa uṇarka. (25)

TE228n

nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu akaram vilakki atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nilāveṉkiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum --- nilā veṉṉum^ col attuccāriyaiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nilāattukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. nilaimoḻittoḻil nilaimoḻi+ toḻilai vilakkumātaliṉ attuvakuppa akaram vīḻntatu. itaṟku ēḻaṉurupu virikka. nilāakkatir eṉpatu `vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum' ((eḻu//225)) eṉpataṉāṉ īṟṟu valleḻuttuppeṟṟatu. nilāamuṟṟameṉpatu `oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku' aṉmaiyiṉ attu+ peṟātāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ nilāattu ñāṉṟāṉ eṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ+ um ēṟpaṉakoḷka. (26)

TE229n

yāmara+ kiḷaviyum piṭāvum^ taḷāvu
māmu+ peyaru melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāmara+ kiḷaviyum --- yāveṉṉum marattai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, piṭāvum --- piṭāveṉṉum^ collum, taḷāvum --- taḷāveṉṉum^ collum, ām muppeyarum melleḻuttu mikum --- ākiya mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttumikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāaṅkōṭu piṭāaṅkōṭu taḷāaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. varumoḻi+ toḻilākiya melleḻuttu vakuppavē valleḻuttu vilakkiṟṟām. itaṟku vilakkāmaiyiṉ akaram peṟṟatu. (27)

TE230n

valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.

iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttatu, akarattōṭu melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāakkōṭu piṭāakkōṭu taḷāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. māṉamillai eṉṟataṉāl immūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāviṉkōṭu piṭāviṉkōṭu taḷāviṉkōṭu eṉavarum. cāriyai peṟavē akaram vīḻntatu.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē yāattukkōṭu piṭāattukkōṭu taḷāattukkōṭu eṉa attuppeṟutalum^ koḷka, akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ.

yāmarakkiḷavi yeṉpataṉaik `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//226)) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyātavataṉāṉ irāviṉ+ koṇṭāṉ nilāviṉ+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (28)

TE231n

māmara+ kiḷaviyu māvu māvu
māmu+ peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
vakaram valleḻuttu avaiyava ṇilaiyā
ṉakara moṟṟu māvu māvum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki eytātateytuvittatu. immūṉṟum valleḻuttu+ peṟā eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. māmarattukku akaramum ṅañanama oṟṟum ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟku ṉakara oṟṟum eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māmarakkiḷaviyum āvum māvum ām muppeyarum avaṟṟōraṉṉa --- māmaramākiya collum āveṉṉum^ collum māveṉṉum^ collumākiya mūṉṟupeyarum yāmaram mutaliyamūṉṟōṭum orutaṉmaiyavāy melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭiyam, āvum māvum akaram avaṇnilaiyā ṉakaram oṟṟum --- avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum puṇarcciyiṭattu akaram nilaipeṟāvāy ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum, eṉavē aruttāpattiyāṉ māmarattiṟku akaram nilaipeṟṟu ṅañanama oṟṟum peṟumāyiṟṟu; avai valleḻuttu avaṇ nilaiyā --- am mūṉṟu peyarum muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ puṇarcciyiṭattu nilaipeṟāvāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

avaṇilaiyā eṉṟataṉai iraṇṭiṭattum^ kūṭṭuka.

utāraṇam: māaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū āṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

āvum māvum avaṟṟōraṉṉa eṉṟu ñāpakamāka+ kūṟiyavataṉāl māṅkōṭeṉa akara miṉṟiyum varum.

iṉi, avaṇ eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kāyāṅkōṭu nuṇāṅkōṭu āṇāṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟavum melleḻuttu+ peṟutalum, aṅkākkoṇṭāṉ iṅkākkoṇṭāṉ uṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivaṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumaiyiṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum, āviṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmai iṉ peṟutalum, peṟṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka.

māṭṭēṟṟāṉ mūṉṟupeyarum valleḻuttu+ peṟātu melleḻuttu+ peṟṟavāṟum māmaram akaram peṟṟavāṟum iccūttirattiṉkaṇṇaḻivāṉ uṇarka. (29)

TE232n

āṉ oṟṟu* akaramoṭu nilai y-iṭaṉ uṭaittu* ē.

iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ āṉ eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṉ oṟṟu --- ā v-eṉṉum^ coṉ muṉṉar+ peṟṟu niṉṟa ṉakara v-oṟṟu, akaramoṭu* um nilai y-iṭaṉ uṭaittu --- akarattōṭu kūṭi-niṟkum iṭaṉ um uṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

iṭaṉ uṭaittu* eṉṟavataṉāl vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇattu i+ muṭipu* eṉa+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi' `āṉamaṇi kaṟaṅkum^ kāṉattāṅkaṇ' eṉa varum. akaramoṭum eṉṟa ummaiyāl akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmai. āṉey teḷittu āṉmaṇi āṉvāl eṉa varum. (30)

TE233n

āṉmuṉ varūu mīkāra pakaram^
tāṉmika+ tōṉṟi+ kuṟukalu murittē.

iḵtu āṉeṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaram --- āṉeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattōṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mika+ tōṉṟi --- appakaramākiya tāṉ mikku niṟpa nilaimoḻi ṉakarattiṟku+ kēṭu tōṉṟi, kuṟukalum urittu --- īkāram ikaramāka+ kuṟuki niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āppi eṉa varum.

ummai etirmaṟai yākalāṉ āṉpī eṉṟumām. (31)

TE234n

kuṟiyata ṉiṟuti+ ciṉaikeṭa vukara
maṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyataṉ iṟuti+ ciṉaikeṭa --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu iraṇṭu māttiraiyil orumāttiraikeṭṭu aḵtu akaramāy niṟpa, ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- āṇṭu ukaram pulappaṭa varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:
`iṟavu+ puṟattu aṉṉa piṇar+paṭu taṭavu mutal+
cuṟavu+ kōṭṭu* aṉṉa muḷ= ilai+ tāḻai' ((naṟ//19))

`puṟavuppuṟattu aṉṉa puṉkālu kāay ((kuṟu//274))

eṉavarum. ukaram vakuppavē nilaimoḻi akaram^ keṭṭatu. atikāra valleḻuttu vilakkāmaiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu. iṉi nilaimoḻi+ toḻil varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattiṟkum ivviti eytiṟṟākaliṉ, āṇṭu varum ukaram pulappaṭa vārāmaiyum uṇarka. kaṟavuyarkoṭi aravuyarkoṭi muḻavuṟaḻtōḷ eṉa ivai kuṟiyataṉiṟuticciṉai keṭṭu varumoḻi uyir mutaṉ moḻiyāy varutaliṉ vakara uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟu ukaram peṟātu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka; mūṉṟāvatumām. (32)

TE235n

ikara viṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟuppeyarkku alvaḻimuṭipu tokaimarapiṉ+ kūṟi īṇṭu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: ikara iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar --- ikaraīṟṟu+ peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṉ+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum --- vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakku+ poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kiḷikkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam eṉavarum. puli nari eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉavum avai.

iṉi+ kiḷikuṟumai kiḷikkuṟumai eṉa+ kuṇampaṟṟi vanta uṟaḻcci muṭipu, mēl `valleḻuttu mikiṉum' ((eḻu//246)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu `olvaḻiyaṟital' eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. (33)

TE236n

iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉum^ kālaiyu miṭaṉum
viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum^ cuṭṭu maṉṉa.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu, ivvīṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkum viṉaiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉi aṇi eṉṉum^ kālaiyum iṭaṉum --- iṉiyeṉṟum aṇiyeṉṟum^ collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccollum, viṉaiyeñcukiḷaviyum --- ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya collum, cuṭṭum --- ivvīṟṟu+ cuṭṭākiya iṭaiccollum, aṉṉa --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟē valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iṉikkoṇṭāṉ aṇikkoṇṭāṉ tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, ikkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum. ivviṭaiccoṉ mūṉṟum ippoḻutu koṇṭāṉ aṇiya iṭattē koṇṭāṉ ivviṭattukkoṟṟaṉ eṉa urupiṉporuḷpaṭavanta vēṟṟumaiyātaliṉ vēṟōtimuṭittār. (34)

TE237n

iṉṟi y-eṉṉum viṉai y-eñcu* iṟuti
niṉṟa v-ikaram ukaram ātal*
^toṉṟiyal% maruṅkiṉ+ ceyyuḷ uḷ urittu* ē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu viṉaiyeccattuḷ oṉṟaṟku+ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal --- iṉṟiyeṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiyecca+ kuṟippiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramāka+ tirintu muṭital, toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ+ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟaiyuṭaiya ceyyuḷuḷ urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `uppiṉṟu puṟkai yuṇkamā koṟkaiyōṉē'.

niṉṟa eṉṟataṉāl viṉaiyeccattiṟkumuṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.

toṉṟiyaṉmaruṅkiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ aṉṟi yeṉpatūum^ ceyyuḷil immuṭipu eytutal koḷka. `iṭaṉaṉṟu tuṟattal valli yōrē' `vāḷaṉṟu piṭiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar' `nāḷaṉṟupōki' ((puṟam//124)) eṉa varum. muṟṟiyalikaram^ tirintu kuṟṟiyalukaramāy niṉṟatu. (35)

TE238n

cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟkai muṟkiḷantu* aṟṟu* ē.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭuppeyar iyalpukaṇattōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉporuḷ: cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟkai --- ikara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉ iyalpu, muṉ kiḷantaṟṟu --- muṉ akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṟku+ kūṟiya taṉmaittām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉṟatu `cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ñanamattōṉṟi' ((eḻu//205)) eṉpatu mutaliya nāṉku cūttirattāṉum^ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai. avai meṉkaṇattu melleḻuttu mikutalum iṭaikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum vakaram peṟutalum^ ceyyuṭkaṇ vakaraṅkeṭṭu+ cuṭṭu nīṭalumām.

i+ ñāṇ nūl maṇi eṉa v-um, iv yāḻ iv vaṭṭu eṉa v-um, iv= aṭai iv= āṭai iv= ilai iv= īyam iv= ural iv= ūrti iv= eḻu iv= ēṇi iv= aiyam iv= oṭu iv= ōkkam iv= auviyam eṉa v-um ī vayiṉāṉa eṉa v-um varum.

`ī kāṇ ^tōṉṟum em^ ciṟu nal= ūr ē'

eṉṟatu* um,

`kaḷvaṉ ō vallaṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉ kāl+cilampu
koḷḷum vilai+ poruṭṭāl+ koṉṟār ē y-ītu* oṉṟu' ((cilap. ūr cūḻ vari//7))

eṉṟatu* um itu v-eṉṉum^ cuṭṭu mutal ukara īṟu* ātaliṉ atu ceyyuḷ akattu+ puṟanaṭaiyāṉ muṭiyum eṉa uṇarka. (36)

TE239n

patakku muṉ variṉ ē tūṇi+ kiḷavi
mutal+ kiḷantu* eṭutta vēṟṟumai y-iyaṟṟu* ē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷuḷ aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ tokaimarapiṉuḷ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyai vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tūṇi+ kiḷavimuṉ patakku variṉ --- tūṇiyākiya aḷavuppeyariṉ muṉṉar+ patakku eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumai iyaṟṟu --- muṉpu vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tūṇippatakku eṉavarum. iḵtu ummaittokai.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ aṭaiyoṭu vantuḻiyum iv viti koḷka. irutūṇippatakku muttūṇippatakku eṉa oṭṭuka. kiḷanteṭutta veṉṟataṉāl tūṇikkoḷ cāmai tōrai pāḷitam eṉa+ poruṭpeyar muṉ vantuḻiyum, irutūṇikkoḷ eṉa atutāṉ aṭaiyaṭuttuḻiyum^ tūṇittūṇi toṭittoṭi kāṇikkāṇi pūṇippūṇi eṉa+ taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantuḻiyum viti koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantuḻiyam atu tāṉ, aṭaiyaṭuttu vantuḻiyum ikkuccāriyai peṟutalum^ koḷka. tūṇikkuttūṇi irutūṇikkuttūṇi eṉavarum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ paṇputtokaiyum uḷa. (37)

TE240n

urivaru kālai nāḻi+ kiḷavi
iṟuti ikara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē
ṭakara moṟṟu māvayiṉāṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: urivarukālai --- nāḻimuṉṉar uri varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nāḻikkiḷavi --- annāḻi yeṉṉum^ col, iṟuti ikaram meyyoṭum^ keṭum --- taṉ iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ikaram^ tāṉēṟiya meyyoṭum^ keṭum, āvayiṉ āṉa ṭakaram oṟṟum --- avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāyvarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṭuri eṉavarum. itaṉāṉ ēkāramum vilakkuṇṭatu.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ irunāṭuri munnāṭuri eṉavum oṭṭuka.

iṟutiyikarameṉa muṉṉum ōr ikaram uḷḷatu pōla+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ īṇṭai nilaimoḻiyam varumoḻiyum nilaimoḻiyāy niṉṟu peyarōṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅkoḷka. nāḻikkāyam urikkāyam cukku tōrai pāḷitam eṉa varum. (38)

TE241n

paṉi y-eṉa varūum^ kāla vēṟṟumaikku
attu um iṉ= um^ cāriyai y-ākum.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyai peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyeṉa varūum^ kāla vēṟṟumaikku --- paṉiyeṉṟa collavarukiṉṟa nōyaṉṟi+ kālattai uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumai muṭipuṭaiya peyarkku, attum iṉṉum^ cāriyai ākum --- attum iṉṉum^ cāriyaiyāka varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉiyattukkoṇṭāṉ paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.

vēṟṟumai yeṉṟataṉāṉ iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (39)

TE242n

vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūta+ kiḷaviyu
mavviya ṉilaiyal cevvi teṉpa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūta+ kiḷaviyum --- vaḷiyeṉṟucolla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, avviyalnilaiyal cevviteṉpa --- muṉṉaikkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum avviyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vaḷiyattukkoṇṭāṉ vaḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

cevviteṉṟataṉāṉ iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (40)

TE243n

utimara+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu marappeyaril oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: utimara+ kiḷavi --- utittaleṉṉum^ toḻilaṉṟi utiyeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟacol, melleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: utiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

ammuccāriyai vitikkiṉṟa puḷimarattiṉai itaṉpiṉ vaittamaiyāṉ utiyaṅkōṭu eṉa itaṟkum ammuppeṟutal koḷka. iḵtu ikkālattu otiyeṉama maruviṟṟu. (41)

TE244n

puḷimara+ kiḷavikku ammē cāriyai.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷimarakkiḷavikku ammē cāriyai --- cuvaiyaṉṟi+ puḷiyeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku ammeṉṉum^ cāriyai varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

cāriyai+ pēṟṟiṭai muṉṉar+ cūttirattu eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiya vataṉāl ammuppeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (42)

TE245n

ēṉai+ puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai+ puḷippeyar melleḻuttumikum --- ammarappeyaraṉṟi+ cuvaippuḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiṅkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum. pāḷitam pāṟcōṟu. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. (43)

TE246n

valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai
yolvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamillai --- cuvaippuḷi melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamillai, olvaḻiyaṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa --- poruntum iṭam aṟika vaḻakkiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷikkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉavarum.

olvaḻi eṉṟataṉāṉ+ puḷiccāṟu pōla ēṉaiya vaḻakkuppayiṟci illeṉṟu koḷka.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ iv vīṟṟukkaṇ eṭuttōttum ilēcumiṉṟi varuvaṉa vellāvaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkumāṟu ceykaiyaṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. avai iṉṉiṉi+ koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇi+ koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa aṭaiyaṭuttaliṉ `iṉiyaṇi' ((eḻu//236)) eṉṟavaḻi muṭiyāvāy valleḻuttu+ peṟṟaṉa. kappitantai ceṉṉitantai eṉpaṉa `aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉuḷ iyalpeytātu īṇṭu varumoḻi+ takara akaram^ keṭṭu+ kappintai ceṉṉintai eṉa muṭintatu.

kūtāḷi kaṇaviri eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ammu+ koṭuttu ikaraṅkeṭuttu+ kūtāḷaṅkōṭu kaṇaviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. `kūtaḷa naṟumpū' eṉa+ kuṟaintum varum. iṉi ivai makaraīṟāyum vaḻaṅkum. atu `veṇkūtāḷattu+ taṇpūṅkōtaiyar' eṉa attuppeṟṟu makaraṅkeṭṭum^ `kaṇaviramālai yiṭūukkaḻintaṉṉa' eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭum^ kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ peṟṟum niṟkum.

cuṭṭi eṉa niṟutti iṭi akal eṉa+ tantu ṭakarattil ikaraṅkeṭuttu+ kaṭṭiṭi kaṭṭakal eṉa muṭikka. paruttikku+ ceṉṟāṉeṉa īṟṟu valleḻuttum ikkum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. tuḷiyattu+ koṇṭāṉ tuḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. puḷiṅkāy vēṭkaittaṉṟu eṉavum puḷimpaḻam eṉavum ammuppeṟātu melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭitalum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu+ keṭuttu+ kiḷiyiṉkāl puḷiyiṉkōṭu utiyiṉkōṭu eṉa muṭikka. (44)

TE247n

nāṇmuṉ+ ṟōṉṟum^ toḻiṉilai+ kiḷavikku
āṉ iṭai varutal aiyam iṉṟē.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttu vilakki āṉcāriyai vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟum^ toḻiṉilai+ kiḷavikku --- ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum^ toḻil+ coṟku, āṉ iṭaivarutal aiyamiṉṟu --- āṉ cāriyai iṭaivantu muṭital aiyamiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paraṇiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ cōtiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. aiyamiṉṟeṉṟataṉāl iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. itaṟku+ kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka. (45)

TE248n

tiṅkaṇ muṉvari ṉikkē cāriyai.

iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyai --- tiṅkaḷai uṇaraniṉṟa ikara īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ toḻiṉilai+ kiḷavivariṉ varuṅcāriyai ikku+ cāriyaiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa iyaipuvalleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. itaṟkum^ kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka. (46)

TE249n

īkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu --- īkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īkkaṭitu tīkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (47)

TE250n

nīyeṉ peyaru miṭakkar+ peyarum
mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvarai kiḷaviyu
māvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytāta teytuvittalum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarppeyarum --- nī yeṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarppeyarākiya īkāra īṟṟuppeyarum, mīyeṉamarīiya iṭam varai kiḷaviyum --- mīyeṉṟu colla maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa ōriṭattai varaintu uṇarttum collum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum --- puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nīkuṟiyai ciṟiyai tīyai periyai eṉavum, pīkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum ivaiyiṟṟukku+ potuvāṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkuṇṭaṉa. mīkaṇ cevi talai puṟam. iḵtu ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya maruvātaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

nīyeṉpatu aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyaruḷ aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ mēl niṉkaiyeṉa+ tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. mīkaṇ eṉpatu mēliṭattu+ kaṇṇeṉa vēṟṟumaiyeṉiṉum iyalpupaṟṟi uṭaṉ kūṟiṉār. (48)

TE251n

iṭamvarai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūu
muṭaṉilai moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.

itu valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu* ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭam varai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum --- iṭattai varaintu uṇarttum mīyeṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉar iyalpāy muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum, uṭaṉilai moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa --- tammil ōcai yiyaintu niṟṟalai yuṭaiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mīkkōḷ mīppal eṉa varum.

uṭaṉilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ mīṅkuḻi mīntōl eṉa melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. (49)

TE252n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īkkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam, tīkkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa varum. (50)

TE253n

nīye ṉorupeya rurupiya ṉilaiyu
māvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, valleḻuttu vilakki ṉakaravoṟṟē peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīyeṉ orupeyar urupiyal nilaiyum --- nīyeṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi urupu puṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakaram oṟṟi niṉṟāṟpōla īṇṭu+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum --- avvāṟu muṭipuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu mikā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: niṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

iḵtu īkāra īṟu ikara īṟāy ikara īṟu ṉakara īṟāy niṉṟuḻiyum `nīyeṉorupeyar' eṉṟaliṉ+ ṟirintataṉ ṟiripatuvēyāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkaiyākumeṉavē nilaimoḻittoḻil atikāra valleḻuttai vilakkātāyiṟṟu. (51)

TE254n

ukara viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu --- ukara īṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭukkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum (52)

TE255n

cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉaru mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟṟu ākum --- ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉum valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ukkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavarum. (53)

TE256n

ēṉavai variṉē mēṉilai yiyala.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉavai variṉ --- ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉ muṉ valleḻuttallāta meṉkaṇam mutaliya mūṉṟum variṉ, mēlnilai iyala --- akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu muṭintāṟpōla ñanama+ tōṉṟiṉ oṟṟu mikkum yavvariṉum uyirvariṉum vakaram oṟṟiyum^ ceyyuḷil nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, uvyāḻ uvvaṭṭu eṉavum, uvvaṭai uvvāṭai eṉavum, ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum. (54)

TE257n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yiyalpā kummē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara īṟṟuppeyar, iyalpākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atukuṟitu itukuṟitu utukuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

muṟkūṟiyavai cuṭṭumāttirai, ivai cuṭṭuppeyarāka uṇarka. (55)

TE258n

aṉṟuvaru kālai yāvā kutalum
aivaru kālai meyvaraintu keṭutalum^
ceyyuṇ maruṅki ṉuritteṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu+ cuṭṭumutal+ peyarkku ōr ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṉṟu varukālai āvākutalum --- atikārattāl niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉar aṉṟeṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippuccol varuṅkālattu a+ takaravoṟṟiṉ mēl ēṟiniṉṟa ukaram ākāramāy+ tirintu muṭitalum, ai varukālai meyvaraintu keṭutalum --- ataṉmuṉṉar aiyeṉṉum^ cāriyai varuṅkālattu a+ takaravoṟṟu niṟka ataṉmēl ēṟiya ukaram^ keṭutalum, ceyyuṇmaruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa --- ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atāaṉṟamma itāaṉṟamma utāaṉṟamma `atāaṉṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē' eṉavum, ataimaṟṟamma itaimaṟṟamma utaimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.

moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ muṭiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭitta leṉpataṉāl ataṉṟu itaṉṟu utaṉṟa eṉa ukaraṅkeṭṭu+ takaravoṟṟu niṟṟal koḷka. (56)

TE259n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- ukara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭukkāy cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kaṭukkaṭumai eṉavum varum. (57)

TE260n

eruvum^ ceruvu mammoṭu civaṇi+
tiripiṭa ṉuṭaiya teriyum^ kālai
yammiṉ makaram^ ceruvayiṉ+ keṭumē
tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻuttu iyaṟkai.

iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyai vitiyum oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyai vitiyum^ cāriyai peṟātavaḻi valleḻuttu melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eruvum^ ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi --- eruveṉṉum^ collum ceruveṉṉum^ collum ammu+ cāriyaiyōṭu porunti, tiripu iṭaṉuṭaiya teriyuṅkālai --- atikāra valleḻuttu+ peṟāmal tiriyum iṭaṉuṭaiya ārāyuṅkālattu, ammiṉ makaram^ ceruvayiṉ+ keṭum --- āṇṭu ammuccāriyaiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉ makaram^ ceruveṉṉum^ colliṭattu+ keṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu mikūum --- āṇṭu+ ceruviṉkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum, iyaṟkai+ tam oṟṟu mikūum --- ammuppeṟātavaḻi iraṇṭiṟkum^ tamakku iṉamākiya valloṟṟum melloṟṟum mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eruveṉa niṟutti+ kuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa+ tantu ammukkoṭuttu `ammiṉiṟutikacatakkālai' ((eḻu//129)) eṉpataṉāl eruvaṅkuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi yeṉa muṭikka. ceruveṉa niṟutti+ kaḷam cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉa+ tantu iṭai ammukkoṭuttu makaram^ keṭuttu valleḻuttukkoṭuttu+ ceruvakkaḷam cēṉai tāṉai paṟaiyeṉa muṭikka. iṉi ammu+ peṟātavaḻi erukkuḻi eruṅkuḻi eṉa valleḻuttum melleḻuttum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. iṉi+ ceruviṟku ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāṉ+ cerukkaḷameṉa valleḻuttē koṭuttu muṭikka.

teriyuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ eruviṉ kuṟumai ceruviṉ kaṭumai eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻtalum eruvañāṟci ceruvañāṟci eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ ammuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. makaram `meṉmaiyu miṭaimaiyum'(eḻu//130) eṉpataṉāṉ+ keṭukka.

tammoṟṟumikūum eṉa uṭampoṭu puṇarttu+ cūttiram^ ceytaliṉ ukaram nīṭavarutalum^ koḷka. varūum tarūum paṭūum eṉa varum. (58)

TE261n

ḻakara vukara nīṭiṭa ṉuṭaittē
yukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram peṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭaittu --- ukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷuḷ ḻakarattōṭu kūṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu, āvayiṉ āṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻūukkatavu ciṟai tāṉai paṭai eṉa varum.

nīṭiṭaṉuṭaittu eṉṟataṉāṉ nīḷātum ukaram peṟātum varumāyiṟṟu. kuḻuttōṟṟam eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉ+ paḻukkāyeṉa alvaḻikkaṇṇum ivvitiyaṉṟi varutal koḷka.

āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ+ perumpāṉmai ceyyuṭkaṇ nīṇṭu ukarampeṟṟu

`eḻūuttāṅkiya katavumalaittavar

kuḻūukkaḷiṟṟu+ kuṟumpuṭaittaliṉ' ((puṟam//97))

eṉavum,

`paḻūuppallaṉṉa paruvukirppāvaṭi' ((kuṟun//180))

eṉavum varutal koḷka. (59)

TE262n

oṭumara+ kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṭumarakkiḷavi --- oṭuveṉṉam marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa col, utimara iyaṟṟu --- utiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṭuṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. marameṉṟār oṭuveṉṉum nōyai nīkkutaṟku.

muṉṉar utamarattiṉpiṉṉar ammu+ peṟukiṉṟa puḷimaram vaitta iyaipāṉ itaṟku ammuppēṟum^ koḷka. oṭuvaṅkōṭu eṉa varum. (60)

TE263n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉilaiyu
moṟṟiṭai mikāa valleḻuttu iyaṟkai.

itu cuṭṭuppeyarkku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyai vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum urupupuṇarcciyiṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpilē niṉṟu aṉcāriyai peṟṟu ukaram^ keṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu iyaṟkai oṟṟu iṭaimikāa --- valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ataṉkōṭu itaṉkōṭu utaṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. oṟṟiṭaimikā eṉavē cāriyai vakuppa valleḻuttu vīḻāveṉpatu peṟṟām.

valleḻuttiyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāṉ ukara īṟṟuḷ eruvum^ ceruvum oḻittu ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟai oṭuviṉkuṟai eḻuviṉpuṟam koḻuviṉ kūrmai eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē utukkāṇ eṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum^ koḷka. (61)

TE264n

ūkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē ūkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḻūukkaṭitu koṇmūkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (62)

TE265n

viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikku muṉṉilai moḻikku
niṉaiyum^ kālai yavvakai varaiyār.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccattiṟku mikku muṭiyumeṉṟaliṉ, eytātateytuvittatūum muṉṉilai viṉaikku iyalpum uṟaḻpum māṟṟataliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyatūum nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikkum --- ūkāra īṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya coṟkum, muṉṉilai moḻikkum --- muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkum, niṉaiyuṅkālai avvakai varaiyār --- ārāyuṅkālattu avvalleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum^ kūṟṟiṉai nīkkār eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṇṇūkkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, kaitūkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum varum.
