niṉaiyuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇai+ peyarkkum alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. āṭūukkuṟiyaṉ makaṭūukkuṟiyaḷ eṉavarum. uyartiṇaippeyar eṭuttōti muṭippārātaliṉ ammuṭipu peṟāmaiyiṉ īṟṟu+ potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyātu ilēcāṉ muṭittām. (63)

TE266n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkuṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḻūukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai, koṇmūkkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟaivu eṉavarum. (64)

TE267n

kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum ōr eḻuttu moḻikku
niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukara+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ vanta ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum --- ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra īṟṟumoḻikkum, ukarakkiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṭūukkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum, tūukkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum varum.

niṟṟal eṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇai+ peyarkkum valleḻuttum ukaramum^ koṭukka. āṭūukkai makaṭūukkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum. ivai tokai marapiṉuḷ iyalpātal eytiyavaṟṟai īṇṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum muṭittār, īṟṟu+ potu oppumai kaṇṭu. (65)

TE268n

pūve ṉorupeya rāyiyal piṉṟē
yāvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.

iḵtu ūkāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum iyaipu valleḻuttum vilakki+ perumpāṉmai melleḻuttum^ ciṟupāṉmai valleḻuttum peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pūveṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu --- pūveṉṉum ūkāra īṟṟaiyuṭaiya oru peyar mēṟkūṟiya ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum avviyalpiṉmaiyai uṭaittu, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu --- avviṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūṅkoṭi cōlai tāmam pantu eṉavum, pūkkoṭi ceykai tāmam pantu eṉavum varum.

pūveṉpatu voliveṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippai uṇarttātu niṟṟaṟku orupeya reṉṟār. (66)

TE269n

ūve ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu. ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ṉakaram vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ūveṉ orupeyar --- ūveṉa+ tacaiyai uṇarttiniṉṟa ōreḻuttorumoḻi, āvoṭu civaṇum --- ākāra īṟṟil āveṉṉum^ col valleḻuttu+ peṟātu ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭintāṟpōla ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ūveṉa niṟutti ṉakara oṟṟu+ koṭuttu ūṉkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa muṭikka. ūveṉpatu tacaiyai uṇarttiniṉṟa vaḻakku āciriyar nūlceyta kālattu vaḻakku. aṉṟi+ tēyavaḻakkēṉum uṇarka. (67)

TE270n

akkeṉ cāriyai peṟutalu murittē
takkavaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akkeṉ cāriyai peṟutalum urittu --- atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ūveṉṉum peyar muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akkeṉṉum^ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu, vaḻakkattāṉa takkavaḻi aṟital --- ammuṭipu vaḻakkiṭattu+ takka iṭam aṟika eṉṟavāṟu.

takkavaḻiyaṟital eṉṟataṉāṉ+ cāriyaipeṟṟuḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāl vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttu+ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ūṉakkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavarum.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ ūkāra īṟṟu+ colliṟku urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu+ keṭukka. koṇmūviṉkuḻām uṭūuviṉṟalai ūviṉkuṟai eṉa varum.

TE271n

āṭūu makaṭūu vāyiru peyarkku
miṉṉiṭai variṉu māṉa millai.

itu `kuṟṟeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉpataṉuḷ niṟṟaleṉṟa ilēcāṉ eytiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi+ cāriyaiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti uṇarttiyatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum --- āṭūu makaṭūuvākiya uyartiṇaippeyar iraṇṭiṟkum, iṉ iṭaivariṉum māṉam illai --- muṉṉeytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi iṉcāriyai iṭaiyē variṉum^ kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭūuviṉkai makaṭūuviṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum.

māṉamillai eṉṟataṉāṉ iṉ peṟṟuḻi mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (69)

TE272n

ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā
muṉṉilai moḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar
tēṟṟamum^ ciṟappu malvaḻi yāṉa.

iḵtu ekara okaram īṟām iṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṟṟamum^ ciṟappum alvaḻi āṉa --- teḷivuporuḷum^ ciṟappupporuḷum allāta vēṟṟumai+ poruṇmaiyiṭattu aḷapeṭuttu+ kūṟutaliṉ uḷavākiya, ekara okaram peyarkku īṟākā --- ekara okaraṅkaḷ peyarkku īṟāy vārā, viṉaikku īṟāy varum, muṉṉilai moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avaitām^ taṉmaiyiṉ um paṭarkkaiyiṉ um vārā, muṉṉilaiccolliṭattaṉavāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, teḷivu poruḷiṉum^ ciṟappu+ poruḷiṉum vantu peyarkku īṟām iṭaiccollākiya ekara okaram mūṉṟiṭattiṟkum uriyavāmeṉṟu poruḷāyiṟṟu. eṉa iṅṅaṉam aruttāpattiyāṉ+ koṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam mēlai+ cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa.

utāraṇam: ēekkoṟṟā ōokkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum.

ivai eṉakku oru karumampaṇi eṉavum, iṅṅaṉam^ ceykiṉṟataṉai oḻiyeṉavum muṉṉilaiyēval+ poruṭṭāy vantaṉa. itaṟṟu valleḻuttu+ peṟumāṟu mēlē kūṟupa. (70)

TE273n

tēṟṟa vekaramum^ ciṟappi ṉovvu
mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai valleḻuttu mikā.

iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṟṟa ekaramum^ ciṟappiṉ ovvum mēṟkūṟu iyaṟkai --- muṉṉar aruttāpattiyāṉ+ peyarkkaṇ varumeṉṟa tēṟṟapporuṇmaiyil ekaramum^ ciṟappu+ poruṇmaiyil okaramum mūṉṟiṭattum varumeṉṟa ilakkaṇattaṉavām, valleḻuttu mikā --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyā, eṉavē muṉṉilaikkaṇ varumeṉṟa ekara okaraṅkaḷ valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉēekoṇṭēṉ niyēekoṇṭāy avaṉēekoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, yāṉōokoṭiyaṉ nīyōokoṭiyai avaṉōokoṭiyaṉ eṉavum peyarkkaṇ īṟāy iyalpāy vantavāṟu kāṇka. itu muṉṉar eytiya ilakkaṇam ikavāmal+ kāttār. muṉ niṉṟa cūttirattiṉ muṉṉilaikkum valleḻuttu mikuttu eytāta teytuvittār. iccūttirattiṟku aḷapeṭuttal `teḷiviṉēyum' ((col//261)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ koḷka. eṉavē, muṭivupeṟṟuḻi iṅṅaṉam iṭaiccollum eṭuttōti+ puṇarppareṉpatūum peṟṟām. (71)

TE274n

ēkāra viṟuti yūkāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ēkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēkāra iṟuti --- ēkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ, ūkāra iyaṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēkkaṭitu cēkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (72)

TE275n

māṟukō ḷeccamum viṉāvu meṇṇum^
kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu iṭaiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāy+ puṇarkaveṉa eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māṟukōḷ eccamum --- māṟukōṭalaiyuṭaiya eccapporuṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, viṉāvum --- viṉāpporuṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, eṇṇum --- eṇṇupporuṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉēkoṇṭēṉ ceṉṟēṉ tantēṉ pōyiṉēṉ eṉpuḻi yāṉkoṇṭilēṉeṉa māṟukoṇṭa oḻipupaṭa niṉṟatu. nīyēkoṇṭāy ceṉṟāy tantāy pōyiṉāy eṉavum, nilaṉē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē eṉavum varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pirinilai ēkāramum īṟṟacai ēkāramum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaruḷ ivaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, `kaḻiyē, ciṟukuraṉeytaloṭu pāṭōvātē --- kaṭalē, pāṭeḻuntolikkum' eṉavum varum. (73)

TE276n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- ēkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēkkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum vēkkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum varum.

vēkkuṭam vētalaiyuṭaiya kuṭameṉa viriyum. (74)

TE277n

ē y-eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varumē.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ekaram vitittaliṉ eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum --- avvēṟṟumaikkaṇ ēyeṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēekkoṭṭil cālai tuḷai puḻai eṉa varum.

varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum varumeṉa+ koḷka. ēeñekiḻcci nērmai eṉa varum. uraiyiṉ+ kōṭalāl ekāram ēṟpuḻi+ koḷka. (75)

TE278n

cēveṉ marappeya roṭumara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cē eṉ marappeyar --- peṟṟamaṉṟi+ cēveṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa peyar, oṭumara iyaṟṟu --- oṭumarampōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cēṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. (76)

TE279n

peṟṟa māyiṉ muṟṟaviṉ vēṇṭum.

iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peṟṟam āyiṉ --- muṟkūṟiya cēveṉpatu peṟṟattiṉai uṇarttiya poḻutāyiṉ, muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum --- muṭiya iṉ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyavēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cēviṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē muṟkūṟiya cēveṉṉum marappeyarkkum ēveṉpataṟkum urupiṟku eytiya cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻtal koḷka. cēviṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, ēviṉ kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum.

cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum iṉ peṟutal koḷka. cēviṉalam maṇivāl cēviṉimil cēviṉaṭai cēviṉōṭṭam eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ peṟātu varutalum^ koḷka. ceyyuṭkaṇ `teṉṟaṟku vīṇaikku+ cēmaṇikku+ kōkilattiṉ+ --- kaṉṟiṟku' eṉavarum. (77)

TE280n

aikāra viṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. tokai marapiṉuḷ `vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi iaiyeṉṉum' ((eḻu//158)) eṉpataṉkaṇ alvaḻi muṭittār.

itaṉ poruḷ: aikāra iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar --- aikāraīṟṟu+ peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṉ+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum --- vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakkupporuntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉaikkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum yāṉaiyai+ koṇarntāṉeṉa valleḻuttu mikutal koḷka. (78)

TE281n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉilaiyum.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu vantu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuppeyar, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupupuṇarcciyiṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpupōla+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu ivaiyaṟṟukkōṭu uvaiyaṟṟukkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum. itaṉai `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' ((eḻu//122)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. (79)

TE282n

vicai mara+ kiḷavi y-um* ñemai y-um* namai y-um
ā mu+ peyar um^ cē maram* v-iyala.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vicaimarakkiḷaviyum --- vicaittaṟṟoḻilaṉṟi vicaiyeṉṉum marattai uṇaraniṉṟacollum, ñemaiyum --- ñemai yeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, namaiyum --- namai yeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, ā muppeyarum --- ākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala --- valleḻuttu mikātu cēmarampōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vicaiṅkōṭu ñemaiṅkōṭu namaiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. ivai kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu eṉṟu uṇarka. (80)

TE283n

paṉaiyu maraiyu māvirai+ kiḷaviyu
niṉaiyum^ kālai yammoṭu civaṇu
maiye ṉiṟuti yaraivaraintu keṭumē
meyyava ṇoḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉaiyum araiyum āvirai+ kiḷaviyum --- paṉaiyeṉṉum peyarum araiyeṉṉum peyarum āviraiyeṉṉum peyarum, nilaiyuṅkālai ammoṭu civaṇum --- ārāyum^ kālattu valleḻuttu mikātu ammuccāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum, aiyeṉ iṟuti arai varaintu keṭum --- avviṭattu aiyeṉṉum īṟu araiyeṉṉum^ collai nīkki ēṉai iraṇṭiṟkum^ keṭum, mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meykeṭātu accolliṭattē niṟka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉai āvirai eṉaniṟutti ammu varuvittu aikāram^ keṭuttu oṟṟiṉ mēlē akaramēṟṟi+ paṉaṅkāy āviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. araiyeṉa niṟutti ammukkoṭuttu aikāraṅkeṭātu araiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. valleḻuttukkēṭu mēlē `kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kūṟutum.

niṉaiyuṅkālai yeṉṟataṉāl tūtuṇai vaḻutuṇai tillai ōlai tāḻai eṉa niṟutti ammukkoṭuttu aikāram^ keṭuttu+ tūtuṇaṅkāy vaḻutuṇaṅkāy tillaṅkāy ōlampōḻ tāḻaṅkāy eṉa muṭikka. (81)

TE284n

paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉa raṭṭuvaru kālai
nilaiyiṉ ṟāku maiye ṉuyirē
yākāram varuta lāvayiṉāṉa.

itu nilaimoḻicceykai nōkki eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu varukālai --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi+ paṉaiyeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar aṭṭeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nilaiyiṉṟu ākum aiyeṉ uyir, āvayiṉ āṉa ākāram varutal --- avviṭattu ākāram vantu ammeym mēlēṟi muṭika eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum. itaṟku mūṉṟāvatum āṟāvatum viriyum.

āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāl ōrānayam viccāvāti eṉṉum vēṟṟumai muṭipum^ kēṭṭāmūlam pāṟāṅkal eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipum^ koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻikaḷai maṟuttalum oṉṟu. (82)

TE285n

koṭimuṉ variṉē yaiyava ṇiṟpa+
kaṭinilai yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.

itu mēl aikāram^ keṭuttu ammuppeṟuka eṉṟār, īṇṭu atu keṭātuniṟka eṉṟaliṉ eṇtiyatu ikantu paṭāmal+ kāttatu. ammu vilakki valleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatumām.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ koṭi variṉ --- paṉaiyeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar+ koṭiyeṉṉum^ colvariṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa --- kēṭu ōtiya aikāram āṇṭu+ keṭātu niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉaikkoṭi eṉa varum. itaṟku iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum viriyum.

kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāṉ aikāra īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷellām eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ammuccāriyaiyum piṟacāriyaiyum peṟṟuḻi atikāra valleḻuttu+ keṭuttu+ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. paṉaiyiṉkuṟai araiyiṉkōṭu āviraiyiṉkōṭu vicaiyiṉkōṭu ñemaiyiṉkōṭu namaiyiṉkōṭu eṉavum, tūtuṇaiyiṉkāy vaḻutuṇaiyiṉkāy uḻaiyiṉkōṭu vaḻaiyiṉkōṭu eṉavum varum.

paṉaittiraḷ paṉaintiraḷ eṉaṉum uṟaḻccimuṭipu tokaimarapiṉuḷ+ puṟanaṭaiyāṉ+ koḷka; alvaḻiyu mātaliṉ. aṉṟi īṇṭu avaṇeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷvārum uḷar. (83)

TE286n

tiṅkaḷ um* nāḷ um* muntu kiḷantu* aṉṉa.

iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku+ cāriyaiyum valleḻuttu vilakki āṉcāriyaiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuvitiyum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvitiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiṅkaḷum nāḷum --- aikāra īṟṟu+ tiṅkaḷai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum nāḷai uṇara niṉṟa peyarum, muntu kiḷantaṉṉa --- ikara īṟṟu+ tiṅkaḷum nāḷumpōla ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cittiraikku+ koṇṭāṉ kēṭṭaiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. cittirai nāḷāyiṉ āṉcāriyai koṭukka. valleḻuttukkēṭu muṉṉark `kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

tiṅkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl uḻaiṅkōṭu amaiṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ koṭuttum^ kalaiṅkōṭu kalaikkōṭu eṉa uṟaḻcci eytuvittum^ kariyavaṟṟukkōṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟukkōṭu neṭiyavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa aikāra īṟṟu+ paṇpuppeyarkku vaṟṟukkoṭuttu aikāram^ keṭuttu vaṟṟumicai yoṟṟeṉṟu oṟṟukkeṭuttum avaiyattukkoṇṭāṉ avaiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉuṅkoṭuttum paṉaiyiṉ māṇpu kēṭṭaiyiṉāṭṭiṉāṉeṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉcāriyai koṭuttum muṭikka. aikāra īṟu iṉ cāriyai peṟutal tokaimarapiṉuḷ+ kūṟāmaiyiṉ īṇṭukkoṇṭām. (84)

TE287n

maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiya ṉilaiyum.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuvitiyum iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvitiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi --- maḻaiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaḷiyiyal nilaiyum --- vaḷiyeṉṉum^ col attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭinta iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: maḻaiyattukkoṇṭāṉ maḻaiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

īṇṭu iṉpeṟṟuḻi valleḻuttukkēṭu `kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum^ koḷka. maḻaiyattuñāṉṟāṉ maḻaiyiṉkaṇñāṉṟāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māṭṭiṉāṉ vantāṉ aṭaintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. (85)

TE288n

ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai yeṉṉu
maiye ṉiṟuti yavāmuṉ variṉē
meyyoṭum^ keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
ṭakāra ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.

itu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai eṉṉum aiyeṉ iṟuti --- ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṭkaiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuccol, avā muṉ variṉ --- avāveṉṉum^ coṟku muṉṉar variṉ, meyyoṭum^ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvaikāram^ tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭum^ kūṭa+ keṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum --- avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāra oṟṟu ṇakāra oṟṟāy+ tirital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `vēṇavānaliya veyyavuyirā' eṉa varum.

vēṭkaiyāvatu poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu apporuḷkaḷai+ peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu mēṉmēl nikaḻum ācai. eṉavē, vēṭkaiyāluṇṭākiya avāveṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu virintatu. itaṉai vēṭkaiyum avāvumeṉa alvaḻiyeṉpārum uḷar. iṅṅaṉam^ kūṟuvār pāṟaṅkal eṉpataṉai ammukkoṭuttu īṇṭu muṭippar. (86)

TE289n

ōkāra viṟuti yēkāra viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ peyarccol ēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōkkaṭitu cōkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (87)

TE290n

māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu maiyamum^
kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu iṭaiccoṉmuṭipu kūṟaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māṟukoḷ eccamum --- māṟupāṭṭiṉai+ koṇṭa eccapporuṇmaiyai oḻipākavuṭaiya ōkāramum, viṉāvum --- viṉāpporuṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya ōkāramum, aiyamum --- aiyapporuṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉō koṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattō patiṉoṉṟō puṟṟō putalō eṉavum varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ `yāṉō tēṟē ṉavarpoy vaḻaṅkalarē' ((kuṟu//21)) eṉa+ pirinilaiyum naṉṟō tītō kaṇṭatu eṉa+ terinilaiyum ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ ciṟappum^ `kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷai māveṉkō' eṉa eṇṇunilaiyum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. itaṉāṉē īṟṟacai varumēṉum uṇarka. (88)

TE291n

oḻintata ṉilaiyu moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oḻintataṉ nilaiyum moḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu --- oḻiyicai ōkārattiṉatu nilaiyum muṟkūṟiya ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: koḷalōkoṇṭāṉ celalōceṉṟāṉ taralōtantāṉ pōtalōpōyiṉāṉ eṉa ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāṉ orucol tōṉṟa+ poruḷ tantu niṟkum. (89)

TE292n

vēṟṟumaikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōr aṟṟu* ē
y-okaram varutal ā vayiṉ āṉa.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum av vēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉa okaram varutal --- avviṭattu okaram varuka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōokkaṭumai kōokkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa varum. (90)

TE293n

illoṭu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. eṉṉai? muṉṉar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi okaram peṟukaveṉa varaintu kūṟātum nilaimoḻittoḻil varaiyātum^ kūṟaliṉ nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ cēṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ kōveṉṉum moḻiyiṉai illeṉṉum varumoḻiyōṭu colliṉ okaram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōvil eṉa varum. kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇai+ peyaraṉṟōveṉiṉ, kōvantateṉṟu aḵṟiṇaiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇaippāṟpaṭṭateṉka. (91)

TE294n

urupiya ṉilaiyu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
yāvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyai vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: urupiyal nilaiyum moḻiyumāruḷa --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ cilaporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ urupupuṇarcciyatu iyalpilēniṉṟu oṉcāriyai peṟṟumuṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum --- avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōoṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttai vilakkāmai itaṉāṉum peṟṟām. (92)

TE295n

aukāra viṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉa
ralvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum
valleḻuttu mikutal varainilai yiṉṟē
yavviru vīṟṟu mukaram varutal
cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.

iḵtu aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aukāra iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar --- aukāram iṟutiyākiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻikkaṇṇam vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum, valleḻuttu mikutal varainilaiyiṉṟu --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu, avviru īṟṟum ukaram varutal cevviteṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr --- avvirukūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaramvantu muṭital cevviteṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kauvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum.

cevviteṉṟataṉāṉ meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattum ukarampeṟutal koḷka. kauvuñemirntatu ñemircci eṉavum, vauvu valitu valimai eṉavum varum.

nilaimoḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kauviṉkaṭumai eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē aikāramum ikaramum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ urupu tokaiyāyuḻi iyalpātal koḷka. (93)

((uyirmayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((8. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal))

TE296n

ñakārai yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉa
rallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum
valleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē
yukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē uyirīṟu puṇarttu+ puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum^ ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai+ kaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ ivvōttu+ puḷḷimayaṅkiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.

iccūttiram ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻiyum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñakārai oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar --- ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṉṟākaniṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉmuṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻiyaiccollumiṭattum vēṟṟumaiyai+ collumiṭattum, valleḻuttu kiḷappiṉ avveḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvalleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉ āṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, uriñukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum. (1)

TE297n

ñanamava viyaiyiṉu mukara nilaiyum.

iḵtu avvīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattōṭum muṭiyumeṉa eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñanamava iyaiyiṉum ukaram nilaiyum --- aññakara īṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ñanamava mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉum nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñuñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum.

yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokaimarapiṉuḷ `ukaramoṭu puṇarum' ((eḻu//163)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. (2)

TE298n

nakara viṟutiyu mataṉō raṟṟē.

itu nakara īṟu muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟi eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nakara iṟutiyum --- nakara īṟṟuppeyarum muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi, ataṉōraṟṟu --- aññakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku ukarampeṟṟum ñanamava vantuḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: porunukkaṭitu verinukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. muṭipu oppumainōkki nakara īṟu īṇṭu+ puṇarttār. īṇṭu vēṟṟumai yoḻittatu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. (3)

TE299n

vēṟṟumaikku u+ keṭa vakara nilaiyum.

itu nilaimoḻi ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaikku --- annakara īṟu vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikku, ukkeṭa akaram nilaiyum --- mēleytiya ukaraṅkeṭa akarattōṭu nilaipeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: porunakkaṭumai verinakkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉa varum. akaranilaiyumeṉṉātu ukaraṅkeṭa eṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvum^ ciṟupāṉmai ukarappēṟum^ koḷka. veriniṉkuṟai poruniṉkuṟai uriñiṉkuṟai eṉavum, `uyaval yāṉai verinu+ ceṉṟaṉaṉ' ((akam//65)) eṉavum varum. yāṉaiyiṉatu mutukiṉmēl+ ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉa virikka.

porun eṉpatu oru cātippeyarum porunutaleṉṉum viṉaippeyarumām. (4)

TE300n

verine ṉiṟuti muḻutum^ keṭuvaḻi
varumiṭa ṉuṭaittē melleḻuttu iyaṟkai.

iḵtu annakara īṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: verin eṉ iṟuti --- verineṉṟu collappaṭum nakara īṟṟumoḻi, muḻutum^ keṭuvaḻi --- taṉ īṟṟu nakaram muṉpeṟṟa ukarattōṭu eñcāmai+ keṭṭa iṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟkai varum iṭaṉ uṭaittu --- melleḻuttuppeṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: veriṅkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa varum. melleḻuttu varumoḻi nōkki vantatu. `veyilveri niṟutta payilitaḻ+ pacuṅkuṭai' eṉpatil nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa. (5)

TE301n

āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.

iḵtu ataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉ --- avverin eṉṉum^ col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟa iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu --- melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: verikkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa varum. (6)

TE302n

ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ
ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumai+ poruṭkē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ṇakāra īṟu vēṟṟumai+ poruṭkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇakāra iṟuti --- ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar, valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ, ṭakāramākum vēṟṟumaipporuṭku --- ṭakāramāka+ tirintu muṭiyum vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṭkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavarum. maṇkai puṇkai eṉpaṉa iraṇṭāvataṉ tiripiṉ muṭintaṉa.

kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉpaṉa mēl muṭittum. (7)

TE303n

āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇai yiyaṟkai.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṇum peṇṇum --- āṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum peṇṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum, aḵṟiṇai iyaṟkai --- tokaimarapiṉuḷ `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇaippeyar muṭinta iyalpupōla+ tāmum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṇkai peṇkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum.

itu tokaimarapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉuḷ muṭinta iyalpaṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivai āṇṭu muṭintaṉapōla+ tattam marapiṉ viṉaiyāṉ+ pālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa aṉṟi irutiṇaikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇai y-āy muṭitaliṉ, aḵṟiṇai+ peyaratu iyalpōṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittāreṉka. āṇkaṭitu peṇkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻimuṭipu `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉpataṉkaṇ varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ muṭikka. (8)

TE304n

āṇmara+ kiḷavi yaraimara viyaṟṟē.

itu tiripuvilakki+ cāriyai vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṇmara+ kiḷavi --- āṇpālai uṇarttātu āṇeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa col, arai mara iyaṟṟu --- araiyeṉṉum maram ammuppeṟṟa iyalpiṟṟāy+ tāṉum ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṇaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka. āṇanār ilai eṉa varum.

viravuppeya raṉṟeṉṟaṟku marameṉṟār. (9)

TE305n

viṇṇeṉa varūum^ kāya+ peyarvayi
ṉuṇmaiyu murittē yatteṉ cāriyai
ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ+ ṟoḻilvaru kālai.

itu ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakki+ cāriyai vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṇ eṉa varūum^ kāyappeyarvayiṉ --- viṇṇeṉṟu collavarukiṟa ākāyattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyai mikutalum urittu --- atteṉṉum^ cāriyai uṇṭātalum urittu illaiyātalum urittu, ceyyuḷ maruṅkil toḻilvarukālai --- ceyyuḷiṭattu+ toḻiṟcol varuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `viṇṇattukkoṭkum vaṇṇattamarar' `viṇṇattukkoṭkum viraicelalūrtiyōy' eṉavum, `viṇkuttu nīḷvarai veṟpa kaḷaiyavō' ((nālaṭi//226)) eṉavum varum. viṇṇeṉṉum kuṟippiṉai nīkkutaṟku+ kāyameṉṟār.

viṇvattukkoṭkum eṉa uṭampaṭumey puṇarntu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. atikāra valleḻuttaṉmaiyiṉ+ cāriyai valleḻuttu+ koṭukka. (10)

TE306n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyaratu iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṇukkaṭitu paṇṇukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāl toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum iṉcāriyai peṟṟum puṇarvaṉa koḷka. veṇṇukkarai `tāḻpeyal kaṉaikural kaṭuppa+ paṇṇuppeyarttu' eṇṇuppāṟu veṇṇiṉ karai eṉa varum. (11)

TE307n

kiḷaippeya rellām^ koḷattiri pilavē.

iḵtu ivaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷaippeyarellām --- ṇakāra īṟṟuḷ ōriṉattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām, koḷattiripu ila --- tiripuṭaiyaveṉṟu karutumpaṭiyāka+ tiritaliṉṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: umaṇ eṉa niṟatti+ kuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa+ tantu muṭikka.

iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa cāriyaipeṟṟu muṭivaṉavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. maṇṇappattam eṇṇanōlai eṉavum, kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉavum koḷka.

koḷa eṉṟataṉāl ēḻām vēṟṟumai+ poruṇmai uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷ tirintu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. aṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ uṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ īṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, avaṭkoṇṭāṉ ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ uvaṭkoṇṭāṉ evaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka. (12)

TE308n

vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yeṇṇe ṉuṇavuppeyar
vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumai muṭipupōla+ tirintu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumai alvaḻi --- vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattu, eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar --- varaiyaṟaipporuṇmai uṇarttātu eṇṇeṉṟu collappaṭum uṇaviṉai uṇarttumpeyar, vēṟṟumai iyaṟkai nilaiyalum urittu --- vēṟṟumaiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ummaiyāl tokaimarapiṉuḷ `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpu perumpāṉmaiyāyiṟṟu.

aḵtu eṇkaṭitu eṉa varum. (13)

TE309n

muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ toḻiṟpeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ toḻiṟpeyarkku eytiya ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumai muṭipum eytuvittatu.

itaṉporuḷ: muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar --- māṟupāṭu uṇarttum muraṇeṉṉum^ toḻiṟpeyar, mutaliyal nilaiyum --- tokaimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum īṇṭu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muraṇkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, muraṭkaṭumai cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, muraṇñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum. itaṉait `toḻiṟpeyarellām' ((eḻu//306)) eṉṟataṉpiṉ vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ muraṇkaṭumai muraṭkaṭumai, araṇkaṭumai iraṭkaṭumai eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyum^ koḷka. (14)

TE310n

makara viṟuti vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ+
ṟuvara+ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.

itu muṟaiyāṉē makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: makara iṟuti vēṟṟumai āyiṉ --- makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvara+ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum --- annilaimoḻi makaram muṟṟakkeṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: marakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. muṇṭakakkōtai eṉavum varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyartiṇaippeyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. marañāṇ maranūl ivaṟṟiṟku nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. maramaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai āṭai eṉa oṭṭuka. nuṅkai eṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, taṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum varum. īṇṭu makarakkēṭē koḷka; muṭipu mēṟkūṟupa ((eḻu//320)). (15)

TE311n

akara ākāram varūum^ kālai
yīṟṟumicai yakara nīṭalu murittē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ākāram varūum^ kālai --- akara mutaṉmoḻiyum ākāra mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, īṟṟumicai akaram nīṭalum uritatu --- makara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu nīṭāmaiyum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maram kuḷam eṉa niṟutti makaraṅkeṭuttu aṭi āmpal eṉa+ tantu rakara ḷakaraṅkaḷil niṉṟa akaram ākāramākki marāaṭi kuḷāampal eṉa muṭikka.

mēl+ `celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa' ((eḻu//312)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kuḷāampal eṉpuḻi ākārattai akaramākkuka.

ummaiyāṉ maravaṭi kuḷavāmpal eṉa nīṭāmaiyuṅkoḷka.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu+ piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka. kōṇākōṇam kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉavarum. ivaṟṟiṟku uḷḷeṉṉum urupu virikka.

kōṇākōṇattiṟku valleḻuttukkēṭu mēlai+ cūttirattu ilēcāṉ+ koḷka. (16)

TE312n

melleḻuttu uṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa.

itu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikutalōṭu melleḻuttum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa --- makara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, vaḻakkattu āṉa celvaḻi aṟital --- vaḻakkattiṉkaṇṇum vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kuḷaṅkarai kuḷakkarai cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ kuḷaṅkarai kuḷakkaraipōla allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyāy vaḻaṅkā veṉṟaṟku+ celvaḻi yaṟitaleṉṟār.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ kuḷattukkoṇṭāṉ īḻattucceṉṟāṉ kuṭattuvāy pilattuvāy eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu attu+ peṟṟaṉa. ivai `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟukkeṭātāyiṟṟu, aḵtu alvaḻikku+ kūṟutaliṉ. maḻakaḷiṟu eṉpatu `maḻavum^ kuḻavu miḷamai+ poruḷa' eṉṟa uriccol. atu makara īṟaṉṟu. caṇpakaṅkōṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkiṭattu+ cellātu.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē makaraṅkeṭātu niṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. `pulampukkaṉavē' ((puṟam//258)) `kalampeṟu kaṇṇuḷa rokkal+ ṟalaiva' ((malaipaṭu//50)) eṉa varum. (17)

TE313n

illa marappeyar vicaimara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: illa marappeyar --- pukku uṟaiyum illaṉṟi illameṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa col, vicaimara iyaṟṟu --- vicaiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: illaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

mēlai+ cūttirattu `vaḻakkattāṉa' ((eḻu//312)) eṉṟataṉāṉ makarakkēṭu koḷka. (18)

TE314n

alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttu ākum.

itu makaram alvaḻikkaṇ tirika eṉa muṟkūṟāmaiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām melleḻuttākum --- makara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyttirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maraṅkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. maram peritu eṉpuḻi+ tiripiṉṟeṉpatu āṇaikūṟaleṉṉum utti.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāl alvaḻikkaṇ makara īṟu piṟavāṟṟāṉ muṭivaṉavellām muṭikka. vaṭṭattaṭukku, caturappalakai, āytappuḷḷi, vēḻakkarumpu; kalakkoḷ cukku tōrai payaṟu; nīlakkaṇ eṉṉum paṇpuppeyarkaḷ makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭintaṉa. āytavulakkai akaramutala ivai iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu muṭintaṉa. ellāruṅkuṟiyar nāṅkuṟiyēm ivai uyartiṇaippeyar makaram^ tirintu melleḻuttāy muṭintaṉa. colluṅkoṟṟaṉ uṇṇuñcāttaṉ kavaḷamāntumalaikeḻunāṭaṉ porumāraṉ tāvupari paṟakkunārai ōṭunākam āṭupōr varukālam kollumyāṉai pāṭumpāṇaṉ eṉa ivai makarantirintum^ keṭṭum nilaipeṟṟum vanta peyareccam.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum makaram^ keṭutalum^ keṭāmaiyum^ koḷka. marañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, maram yātu valitu aṭaintatu eṉavum varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pavaḷavāyeṉa uvamattum nila nīreṉa eṇṇiṭattum^ kēṭu koḷka. (19)

TE315n

akameṉ kiḷavikku+ kaimuṉ variṉē
mutaṉilai yoḻiya muṉṉavai keṭutalum
varainilai yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu makara īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ immoḻi immuṭipu eytuka eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akam eṉ kiḷavikku+ kai muṉ variṉ --- akameṉṉum^ colliṟku+ kaiyeṉṉum^ col muṉṉē varumāyiṉ, mutaṉilai oḻiya muṉṉavai keṭutalum --- muṉṉiṉṟa akaram^ keṭātu niṟpa ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa kakaramum makaravoṟṟum^ keṭṭu muṭitalum^ keṭātuniṉṟu muṭitalum, varainilai iṉṟē yāciriyarkka --- nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku, āvayiṉ āṉa melleḻuttu mikutal --- avai keṭṭa vaḻi melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṅkai eṉavarum. akaṅkai eṉa+ keṭātu muṭintavaḻi `alvaḻiyellām' ((eḻu//314)) eṉṟataṉāṉ makaram^ tirintu muṭital koḷka. itu paṇputtokai. atikārattāṉum poruṇōkkāṉum vēṟṟumai+ tokaiyaṉmai uṇarka. (20)

TE316n

ilameṉ kiḷavikku+ paṭuvaru kālai
nilaiyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.

iḵtu ilameṉpatu muṟṟuviṉai+ collākātu kuṟippākiya uriccollāy niṟkuṅkāl alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ilam eṉ kiḷavikku --- illāmaiyeṉṉum kuṟippākiya uriccoṟku, paṭuvarukālai --- uṇṭātaleṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ceyyuḷāṉa nilaiyalum urittu --- ceyyuḷiṭattu makarakkēṭum^ tiripumiṉṟi niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ummaiyāṉ+ piṟacol varuṅkālattu+ kēṭum^ tiripum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉa+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟakai niṟaiya' itaṟku illāmai uṇṭākiṉṟa pulavareṉa+ poruḷ kūṟuka. `ilampāṭu nāṇuttarum' eṉkiṉṟatōveṉiṉ, illāmai uṇṭātal nāṇuttarumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka. itaṉai neṟpāṭu periteṉṟāṟpōla+ koḷka. itu poruḷilameṉa muṟṟuviṉai+ collāmāṟum uṇarka. ilaniṉṟateṉa+ keṭṭavāṟum, ilaṅkeṭaviyantāṉ ilañciṟitāka ilantīteṉṟu eṉa+ kacatakkaḷ varumvaḻi+ tirintavāṟum^ kāṇka.

`ellā' ((eḻu//314)) meṉṟataṉāṉ ilamvaruvatupōlum ilam yāriṭattu eṉa vakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.

itaṉai ilattāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉa vēṟṟumaiyeṉṟārāl uraiyāciriyareṉiṉ, paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉpatu peyareccamātaliṉ+ paṟṟaveṉṉum^ toḻil tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa mutaṉilaiccollai+ cūttirattu āciriyar eṭuttōtiṟṟilarātalāṉum paṭuveṉpatu tāṉum pulavareṉṉum peyarōṭu muṭiyaṅkāl iraṇṭukālamum kāṭṭum īṟukaḷ tokka mutaṉilai+ collāy niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉai eṭuttōtiṉārātalāṉum āciriyarkku aṅṅaṉam kūṟutal karuttaṉmai uṇarka.

aṉṟiyum paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉpuḻi iraṇṭu mutaṉilai kūṭi oṉṟāy niṉṟu paṟṟutalai+ ceyyappaṭṭa eṉa+ poruḷ tārāmaiyāṉum alvaḻi yatikāramātalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmai uṇarka. (24)

TE317n

attoṭu civaṇu māyirattu iṟuti
yotta veṇṇu muṉvaru kālai.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ eṇṇuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ tokaimarapiṉuḷ `uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki' ((eḻu//164)) eṉpataṉāṉ eytiya ēyeṉcāriyai vilakki attu vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyirattu iṟuti --- āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyariṉ makaram, otta eṇ muṉvarukālaitaṉakku akappaṭum moḻiyāy+ poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, attoṭu civaṇum --- tokaimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya ēyeṉ cāriyai oḻittu attuccāriyaiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyirattoṉṟu āyirattoṉpatu eṉa oṉṟumutal oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. makarattai attiṉmicaiyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu `attiṉakara makaramuṉaiyillai' ((eḻu//125)) eṉṟu muṭikka. āyirattu orupatu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṭṭuka.

nilaimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ āyirattukkuṟai kūṟu mutal eṉpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē āyirappatteṉpuḻi makaraṅkeṭuttu valloṟṟu mikuttu muṭikka. (22)

TE318n

aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉu mataṉō raṟṟē.

iḵtu `aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya' ((eḻu//110)) eṉṟamaiyiṉ avveṇṇu+ peyarai aṭaiyaṭuttu muṭikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum --- avvāyira meṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭaiyaṭutta moḻiyōṭu variṉum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- muṟkūṟiyataṉōṭu orutaṉmaittāy attu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu iraṇṭu irupatiṉāyirattoṉṟu āṟāyirattoṉṟu nūṟāyirattoṉṟu munnūṟāyirattoṉṟu ainnūṟāyirattoṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. muṉṉar ilēciṉāṉ muṭintavaṟṟaiyam aṭaiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟai puṟam kūṟu mutal eṉavum, nūṟāyirappattu eṉavum varum. (23)

TE319n

aḷavu niṟaiyu vēṟṟumai yiyala.

iḵtu avveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyum --- atikārattāl āyiram^ tāṉē niṉṟuḻiyum aṭaiyaṭuttu niṉṟuḻiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl, vēṟṟumai iyala --- makara īṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttumikkum iyalpukaṇam vantuḻit `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṉum ilēcāṉ eytiya makaram^ keṭṭum puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyiram patiṉāyiram nūṟāyiram eṉa niṟutti+ kalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭil akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḵcu kaḻañcu toṭi tulām palam eṉavum^ tantu oṭṭuka.

vēṟṟumaiyiyala eṉavē tām vēṟṟumaiyallavāyiṉa. (24)

TE320n

paṭarkkai+ peyaru muṉṉilai+ peyarum^
toṭakkam^ kuṟukum peyarnilai+ kiḷaviyum
vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉurupiya ṉilaiyu
melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu uyartiṇaippeyarum viravuppeyarum urupiyaluḷ muṭintavāṟē īṇṭu+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭikaveṉa eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṭarkkai+ peyarum muṉṉilaippeyarum --- ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkaippeyarum ellīrumeṉṉum muṉṉilaippeyarum, toṭakkam^ kuṟukum peyarnilai+ kiḷaviyum --- kiḷaittoṭarcci+ poruḷavāy neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum^ tām nām yāmeṉṉum peyarākiya nilaimaiyaiyuṭaiya collum, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ urupiyal nilaiyum --- vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ urupupuṇarccikku+ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, āvayiṉ āṉa melleḻuttu mikutal --- mēl neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ellāru meṉpataṉai makaraoṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭuttu rakarappuḷḷiyai niṟutti+ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉa+ tantu iṭaiyilē tammuccāriyaiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyaiyum^ koṭuttu ellārtaṅkaiyum ceviyum talaiyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. itaṟku `ammiṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//129)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟacāriyaikkaṇ makara oṟṟu+ tirintu `ṅañanavākum' eṉa+ ceykaiceytu muṭikka. ellīru meṉpataṟku iṭaiyilē nummuccāriyaiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyaiyum^ koṭuttu muṟkūṟiya ceykaikaḷellām^ ceytu ellīrnuṅkaiyum ceviyum talaiyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. tām nām eṉpaṉavaṟṟai `ēṉai yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum' ((eḻu//188)) eṉa+ kuṟukki `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉpataṉāṉ makaram^ keṭuttu+ taṅkai naṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa muṭikka. yām eṉpataṉai ākārattai ekaramākki yakara oṟṟai+ keṭuttu `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāl makaraṅkeṭuttu eṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa muṭikka. toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku iccūttirattāṉ melleḻuttu mikukka.

urupiyal nilaiyum eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaiyātal peṟāniṟkavum piṉṉum vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉeṉṟa mikaiyāṉē paṭarkkaippeyarum muṉṉilaippeyarum iyalpukaṇattu ñakāramum ṅakāramum vantuḻi+ tammuccāriyaiyum nummuccāriyaiyum peṟutalum makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟutalum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. ellārtam^ ñāṇum ellīrnum^ ñāṇum nūlum eṉa varum.

iṉi+ toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum avviraṇṭu ilēcāṉum makaraṅkeṭutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. taññāṇ naññāṇ eññāṇ nūl eṉa varum.

iṉṉum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉē ellārtam ellīrnum eṉa niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar ēṉai maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉpaṉa vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭāmaiyum ummuppeṟutalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ tammaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa makaram^ keṭāmaiyum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tamakāṇam namakāṇam emakāṇam numakāṇam eṉa urupīṟṟu+ ceykaikaḷum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē num eṉpataṟku makarattai melloṟṟākki nuṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varutalum, nuññāṇ eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, numvali eṉa makaram^ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ellārkaiyum ellīrkaiyum eṉa+ tammum nummum peṟātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. (25)

TE321n

allatu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum --- ammūṉṟu peyarum alvaḻiyai+ collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

īṇṭu iyaṟkaiyeṉṟatu cāriyai peṟāmai nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital `alvaḻiyellām' eḻu//314) eṉpataṉuḷ ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ellārum^ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, ellīrum^ kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavum, tām^ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉavum, nāṅkuṟiyam ciṟiyam tīyam periyam eṉavum, yāṅkuṟiyēm ciṟiyēm tīyēm periyēm eṉavum varum.

iṉi ellārum vantār yāttār aṭaintār, ellīrum vantīr yāttīr aṭaintīr eṉavum, tāmvantār yāttār aṭaintār eṉavum, nām varutum yāttum aṭaitum, yāmvaruvēm yāppēm aṭaivēm eṉavum ēṉaikkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar makaram^ tiriyātu niṟṟalum `uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaippeyarum'(eḻu//153) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. (26)

TE322n

allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum
ellām eṉum peyar urupiyal nilaiyum
vēṟṟumai y-alvaḻi+ cāriyai nilaiyātu.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum urupiyalōṭu māṭṭeṟintu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻikkaṇ+ colliṉum vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ+ colliṉum, ellāmeṉum peyar urupiyal nilaiyum --- ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi+ cāriyai nilaiyātu --- appeyar vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarcciyallāta iṭattu vaṟṟu+ cāriyai nillātāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyaṉilaiyum eṉṟa māṭṭēṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummuppeṟṟu niṟṟalum poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uṇarttiṟṟu.

ummuccāriyai oṉṟumē peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa alvaḻiyiṉaiyum vaṟṟum ummumpeṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa vēṟṟumaiyōṭu uṭaṉōti atuvum vaṟṟuppeṟumāṟupōla māṭṭeṟinta mikaiyāṉē vaṉkaṇattu alvaḻikkaṇ nilaimoḻi makarakkēṭum varumoḻi valleḻuttuppēṟum meṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟum peṟātum varutalum ēṉai+ kaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟum makaraṅkeṭātu ummuppeṟātum varutalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ellākkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavum, ellāñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ellāñāṉṟaṉa nīṇṭaṉa māṇṭaṉa eṉavum, ellāyāḻum vaṭṭum aṭaiyum eṉavum, ellāmvāṭiṉa āṭiṉa eṉavum varum. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum ceviyum talaiyum puṟamum eṉa ivai vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku makaram vaṟṟiṉmicaiyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukka. iṉi meṉkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟuñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ēṉaikkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟiyāppum valiyum aṭaiyum eṉavum varum. ēṉaikkaṇamum vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. (27)

TE323n

melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.

iḵtu orucār valleḻuttai vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mel=eḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai --- avvellāmeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṉ+ kūṟiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, valleḻuttumikutalē perumpāṉmaiyāyiṟṟu. muṟkūṟiya ceykaimēlē itu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ makarakkēṭum ummuppēṟum^ koḷka.

ellāṅkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavarum.

māṉamillai eṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇaikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttumikku iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum^ koḷka. ellākkollarum cāṉṟārum taccarum pārppārum kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉavum ellā ñāṉṟārum nāykarum maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum aracarum eṉavum varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē uyartiṇaikkaṇ ellām^ kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikku ummuppeṟutalum ellāṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, kuṟiyīr kuṟiyam eṉavum ummu+ peṟātu varutalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iṭaikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum makaraṅkeṭātu ummiṉṟi varutalum^ koḷka. ellāmvantēm aṭaintēm eṉavarum. (28)

TE324n

uyartiṇai yāyi ṉurupiya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu ellāmeṉpataṟku uyartiṇai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyartiṇaiyāyiṉ urupiyal nilaiyum --- ellāmeṉpatu uyartiṇaiyāy niṟkumāyiṉ urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iṭaikkaṇ nammum iṟutikkaṇ ummumpeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyaluḷ `ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar --- vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai' ((eḻu//389)) vakuttataṉāṉ vaṟṟiṉ micaiyoṟṟeṉṟu makaraṅkeṭutta atikārattāṉ `uyartiṇai yāyi ṉammiṭai varum' ((eḻu//190)) eṉa nammiṉ muṉṉum makaraṅkeṭuttār, ataṉōṭu īṇṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ atu koṇṭu īṇṭum makaraṅkeṭukka. `ammiṉiṟuti'(eḻu//129) eṉpuḻit `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ nammuccāriyaiyiṉatu makarantirital koḷka.

ellānaṅkaiyum ceviyum talaiyum puṟamum eṉa oṭṭuka.

varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟaliṉ ellānaññāṟciyum nīṭciyum eṉa ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṭipu aṟintu oṭṭuka. (29)

TE325n

numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu makara īṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ orupeyar melleḻuttu mikum --- nummeṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viravuppeyar poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

nuṅkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum. `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉpataṉāṉ makaraṅkeṭukka.

oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ uṅkai eṉa varuvatūum^ koḷka. `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāṉ ñakaranakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭutalum orupeyar eṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu mikatalum^ koḷka. nuññāṇ nūl eṉavarum. iṉṉum orupeyar eṉṟataṉāṉ nummaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa makaraṅkeṭāmaiyum koḷka. (30)

TE326n

allataṉ maruṅkiṉ+ collum^ kālai
yukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayi ṉīvara
iyiṭai nilaii yīṟukeṭa rakara
niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
yappāṉ moḻivayi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.

itu nummeṉpataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allataṉ maruṅkiṉ+ colluṅkālai --- nummeṉpataṉai alvaḻikkaṇ+ kūṟumiṭattu, u keṭaniṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara --- nakarattuḷ ukaraṅkeṭṭu+ pōka oḻintu niṉṟa nakaravoṟṟiṭattē īkāram vantu niṟpa, i iṭainilai i īṟukeṭa --- ōr ikaram iṭaiyilē vantu nilaipeṟṟu makaramākiya īṟukeṭṭuppōka, puḷḷi rakaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- āṇṭu+ puḷḷi peṟṟu oru rakaram vantu niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, appāl moḻivayiṉ --- akkūṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya nilaimoḻiyiṭattu, iyaṟkaiyākum --- varuñcol iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nīyirkuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavarum.

colluṅkālai yeṉṟataṉāṉē nīyirñāṉṟīr nīṇṭīr māṇṭīr yāttīr vāṭiṉīr aṭaintīr eṉa ēṉaikkaṇattilum oṭṭuka. (31)

TE327n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

itu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkum alvaḻikkaṇ melleḻuttāy+ tirintum varumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakki ñakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar pōla niṟkumeṉa+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- makara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumaiyiṉum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cemmukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, cemmukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum. `tummu+ ceṟuppa' veṉpatum atu. ivai `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru viraṭṭal' ((eḻu//160)).

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ ukarampeṟātu nāṭṭaṅkaṭitu āṭṭaṅkaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāy alvaḻikkaṇ tiritalum nāṭṭakkaṭumai āṭṭakkaṭumai eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikutalum^ koḷka. (32)

TE328n

īmum^ kammu murumeṉ kiḷaviyu
māmu+ peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.

itu poruṭpeyaruḷ+ cila toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ottu muṭika eṉa eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īmum^ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum āmuppeyarum --- īmeṉṉum^ collum kammeṉṉum^ collum urumeṉṉum^ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa --- muṟkūṟiya toḻiṟpeyarōṭu orutaṉmaiyavāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

īm eṉpatu cuṭukāṭu; kam --- toḻil.

utāraṇam: īmukkaṭitu kammukkaṭitu urumukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, īmukkaṭumai kammukkaṭumai urumukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum oṭṭuka.

kiḷaviyeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum uyir varuvaḻi ukarampeṟātu īmaṭaivu īmaṭaintatu eṉa niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

taṉṉiṉamuṭitta leṉpataṉāl ammu tammu nammu eṉa+ cāriyaikkaṇṇum ukaram varutal koḷka. (33)

TE329n

vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉēṉai yiraṇṭum^
tōṟṟam vēṇṭu makkeṉ cāriyai.

itu mēl muṭipukūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vēṟōr muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ --- vēṟṟumaipporuṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ, ēṉai iraṇṭum tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyai --- iṟutiyil urumoḻinta iraṇṭum akkeṉṉum^ cāriyai tōṉṟi muṭitalaivēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, ñāṟci neruppu māṭci viṟaku eṉavum oṭṭuka.

akku vakuppavē nilaimoḻi+ toḻilākiya ukaraṅkeṭṭu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu vilakkappaṭāmaiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.

vaṉkaṇattiṟku muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟiyatu kuṇavēṟṟumaikkeṉṟum īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu poruḷ+ puṇarccikkeṉṟum^ koḷka. (34)

TE330n

vakāra micaiyu makāram^ kuṟukum.

itu muṉṉar `araiyaḷavu kuṟukal' ((eḻu//13)) eṉavum, `ṉakārai muṉṉar' ((eḻu//52)) eṉavum^ kūṟiya makaram irumoḻikkaṇṇum^ kuṟukumeṉa ataṉ īṟṟakattu eytātateytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vakāramicaiyum makāram^ kuṟukum --- makaram orumoḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi vakārattiṉmēlum^ kuṟukum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nilam valitu varumvaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa varum. (35)

TE331n

nāḷ+peyar+ kiḷavi mēl+kiḷantu* aṉṉa
attu* um āṉ micai varai-nilai y-iṉṟu* ē
y-oṟṟu mey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa --- makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar pōla āṉ cāriyaipeṟṟu muṭiyum, attum āṉmicaivarainilai iṉṟu --- attuccāriyai āṉcāriyai mēlum piṟacāriyai mēlum varutal nīkkunilaimaiyiṉṟu, oṟṟu meykeṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- āṇṭu nilaimoḻi makara oṟṟu+ keṭuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

ummaiyai āṉmicaiyumeṉa māṟuka.

utāraṇam: makattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ōṇattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉka. ēṉai nāṭkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. makara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu `atti ṉakara makaramuṉai yillai' ((eḻu//125)) eṉa akaram^ keṭuttu+ `kuṟṟiyalukaramumaṟṟeṉa' ((eḻu//105)) eṉa āṉēṟṟi `āṉi ṉakaramum' ((eḻu//124)) eṉṟataṉāṉ ṟakaramākki muṭikka. makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ñāṉṟeṉṉum^ cāriyaimēl attu vantatu.

varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ immuṭipu nāṉkukaṇattum^ koḷka. makattāṉ ñāṟṟiṉāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māyntāṉ vantāṉ aṭaintāṉ eṉa varum. (36)

TE332n

ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ
ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumai+ poruṭkē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ṉakara iṟuti vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṟakāramākum --- ṉakāra īṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyaiyiṉ ṟakāramākum, vēṟṟumai+ poruṭku --- vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: poṟkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉa varum. (37)

TE333n

maṉṉum^ ciṉṉu māṉu mīṉum
piṉṉu muṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
maṉṉa viyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu avvīṟṟu acainilai iṭaiccoṟkaḷum ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷum viṉaiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maṉṉum^ ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- maṉṉeṉṉum^ collum^ ciṉṉeṉṉum^ collum āṉeṉṉum^ collum īṉeṉṉum^ collum viṉaiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiyavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `atumaṉ+ koṇkaṉ ṟērē' `kāppum pūṇṭiciṉ+ kaṭaiyum pōkal' ((akam//7)) eṉavum, āṟkoṇṭāṉ īṟkoṇṭāṉ piṟkoṇṭāṉ muṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, variṟkoḷḷum cellum tarum pōm eṉavum varum.

peyarāntaṉmaiyavākiya āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ āṉkoṇṭāṉ īṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ tiriyāmaiyum^ koḷka.

piṉmuṉ eṉpaṉa peyarum urupum viṉaiyeccamumāy niṟṟaliṉ+ peyar īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār. ēṉaiya urupiyaluḷḷum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi yeṉpataṉkaṇṇum muṭiyum. appeyarai muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ+ piṉkoṇṭāṉ muṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ tiriyāmaiyum^ koḷka.

iyalaveṉṟataṉāl ūṉeṉṉum^ cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭāṉeṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. (38)

TE334n

cuṭṭumutal vayiṉu mekaramutal vayiṉu
mappaṇpu nilaiyu miyaṟkaiya veṉpa.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭaiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutal vayiṉum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya vayiṉeṉṉum^ collum, ekara mutal vayiṉum --- ekaramākiya mutal viṉāviṉaiyuṭaiya vayiṉeṉṉum^ collum, appaṇpunilaiyum iyaṟkaiya eṉpa --- mēl ṉakaram ṟakaramāmeṉṟa taṉmai nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpaiyuṭaiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ ivvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ uvvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ evvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.

iyaṟkaiya eṉṟataṉāl tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. (39)

TE335n

kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkai yākum.

itu ṉakāram^ tiriyātu iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkaiyākum --- kuyiṉeṉṉuñcol tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kuyiṉkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟaivu eṉavarum. kuyiṉeṉpatu mēkam. aḵtu aḵṟiṇai+ peyar.

tokaimarapiṉuḷ uyartiṇaippeyarum viravuppeyarum iyalpākaveṉṟār.

kuyiṉ viṉaiyumām.

iyaṟkai eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kāṉkōḻi nōṉkuṇam vāṉkarai eṉa varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. (40)

TE336n

ekiṉmara māyi ṉāṇmara viyaṟṟē.

itu tiripuvilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ekiṉ maramāyiṉ --- ekiṉeṉpatu puḷḷaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, āṇmara iyaṟṟu --- āṇmarattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ekiṉaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. (41)

TE337n

ēṉai yekiṉē yakaram varumē
valleḻuttu iyaṟkai mikutal vēṇṭum.

ituvum atu. tiripu vilakki akaram vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai ekiṉē akaram varum --- maramallāta ekiṉ nilaimoḻikkaṇ akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, valleḻuttiyaṟkai mikutal vēṇṭum --- āṇṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttiyalpu mikku muṭitalai vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ekiṉaṅkāl cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

mēlaiccūttirattōṭu itaṉai oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai yāppu aṭaivu eṉa varum.

mēlaiccūttirattōṭu itaṉai oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai yāppu aṭaivu eṉa varum.

iyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāṉ akarattōṭu melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka. ekiṉaṅkāl cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

iṉi+ ciṟupāṉmai ekiṉcēval ekiṉaccēval peṭai eṉpaṉa āṟaṉurupu virivuḻi īṇṭai ilēcāṉ muṭikka. paṇpu karutiyavaḻi ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka. (42)

TE338n

kiḷai+peyar ellām^ kiḷai+peyar iyala.

itu ṉakaram^ tiritalai vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷaippeyarellām --- ṉakara īṟṟu+ kiḷaippeyarellām, kiḷaippeyar iyala --- ṇakara īṟṟu+ kiḷaippeyarpōla+ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eyiṉkuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum. eyiṉ vantatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇaikkum eytutaliṉ tokai marapiṉuḷ muṭiyātāyiṟṟu, āṇṭu uyartiṇaikkē kūṟutaliṉ.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉē eyiṉakkaṉṉi piḷḷai eṉa akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, eyiṉavāḻvu eṉa valleḻuttu+ peṟāmaiyum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pārppaṉakkaṉṉi kumari cēri piḷḷai eṉa ākāraṅkuṟuki akkum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum pārppaṉavāḻkkai eṉa valleḻuttu+ koṭātum muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum veḷḷāḷaṉeṉa niṉṟataṉai aṉkeṭuttu+ pirittu ḷakāravoṟṟiṉai ṇakāravoṟṟākki veḷḷāṇkumari piḷḷai māntar vāḻkkai oḻukkam eṉa muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mutaleḻuttai nīṭṭi ḷakāra voṟṟiṉai+ keṭuttu vēḷāṇeṉa muṭikka.

itaṉāṉē poruna vāḻkkaiyum muṭikka.

vēṭṭuvakkumari yeṉpatu marūuvaḻakku. (43)

TE339n

mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvē.

ituvum atu, taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: mīṉeṉkiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu --- mīṉeṉṉum^ col tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mīṉkaṇ mīṟkaṇ, mīṉciṉai mīṟciṉai, mīṉṟalai mīṟṟalai, mīṉpuṟam mīṟpuṟam eṉa varum. (44)

TE340n

tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ
mēṉilai yottalum valleḻuttu mikutalu
māmuṟai yiraṇṭu murimaiyu muṭaittē
valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yillai.

ituvum atu. mēlataṉōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ --- tēṉeṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, mēl nilai ottalum --- mīṉeṉpataṟku+ kūṟiya tiripuṟaḻcci nilai ottu muṭitalum, valleḻuttu mikutalum --- varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya, āmuṟai iraṇṭum urimaiyum uṭaittu --- ammuṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭiṉaiyum urittātalaiyum uṭaittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟutiyillai --- valleḻuttu mikku varumiṭattu iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ṉakaram^ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

urimaiyumeṉṉum ummai `melleḻuttu mikiṉum' ((eḻu//341)) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟataṉai nōkkiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉkuṭam tēṟkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉa mēṉilai ottaṉa. tēkkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉa ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. (45)

TE341n

melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.

ituvum atu. uṟaḻcciyum valleḻuttum vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamillai --- muṟkūṟiya tēṉeṉkiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl avvalleḻuttu mikutalē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikiṉum^ kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: tēṅkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavarum. (46)

TE342n

melleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉiṟutiyo ṭuṟaḻum.

itu tokaimarapiṉuḷ `vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻuttalvaḻi' ((eḻu//148)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpai vilakki uṟaḻumeṉṟaliṉ eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ --- attēṉeṉkiḷavi melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ, iṟutiyoṭu uṟaḻum --- nilaimoḻiyiṟutiyiṉ ṉakara voṟṟu+ keṭutalum^ keṭāmaiyumākiya uṟaḻcciyāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉñeri tēñeri tēṉuṉi tēnuṉi tēṉmoḻi tēmoḻi eṉa varum.

mēl `āmuṟai' ((eḻu//340)) eṉṟataṉāl tēñeri tēññeri tēnuṉi tēnnuṉi tēmoḻi tēmmoḻi eṉa ṉakaram^ keṭṭu+ tattam melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum muṭintaṉavum^ koḷka.

iṉi mēl `māṉamillai' ((eḻu//341)) eṉṟataṉāṉ īṟu keṭṭu melleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koṇṭu tēñeri tēnuṉi tēmoḻi eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avai muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉka. (47)

TE343n

iṟāal+ ^tōṟṟam iyaṟkai y-ākum.

iḵtu a+ tēṉ eṉpataṟku uyir+kaṇattu oru moḻi muṭipu vēṟṟumai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṟāal+ ^tōṟṟam --- tēṉ eṉṉum^ col iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟkai y-ākum --- nilaimoḻiyiṉ ṉakaram^ keṭātu* ē niṉṟu iyalpu* āy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉ iṟāl eṉa varum. (48)

TE344n

oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.

ituvum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu --- attēṉeṉpatu iṟāloṭu puṇarumiṭattu+ piṟitumōr takarattōṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

`valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yillai' ((eḻu//340)) eṉṟataṉāṉ nilai moḻi īṟu keṭukka. takaram mikumeṉṉātu oṟṟumiku takarameṉṟataṉāṉ īroṟṟākkuka.

utāraṇam: tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.

mēlai+ cūttirattōṭu itaṉai yoṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ+ piṟavarumoḻikkaṇṇum immuṭipu koḷka. tēttaṭai tēttī eṉa varum.

tōṟṟameṉṟataṉāl tēṉaṭai tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpum^ koḷka. (49)

TE345n

miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum^ kaṉṉu
mannāl+ collum^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tirintum varukaeṉa eytuvitta muṭipai vilakki+ toḻil+ peyariyalpāmeṉa+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum^ kaṉṉum a+ nāṟcollum --- miṉṉeṉṉum^ collum piṉṉeṉṉum^ collum paṉṉeṉṉum^ collum^ kaṉṉeṉṉum^ collumākiya annāṉku collum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumaiyiṉum ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

`miṉṉuccey viḷakkattu+ piṉṉuppiṇi yaviḻnta' eṉavum, paṉṉukkaṭitu kaṉṉukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, miṉṉukkaṭumai piṉṉukkaṭumai paṉṉukkaṭumai kaṉṉukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum.

toḻiṟpeyarellām^ toḻiṟpeyariyala eṉṟu ōtātu kiḷantōtiṉār, ivai toḻiṉilaikkaṇṇaṉṟi vēṟu tam poruḷuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum immuṭipu eytumeṉṟaṟku.

miṉṉeṉpatu miṉṉutaṟṟoḻilum `miṉṉunimirntaṉṉa' eṉa miṉṉeṉappaṭuvatōr poruḷum uṇarttum. ēṉaiyavum aṉṉa. (50)

TE346n

vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉēṉai yekiṉoṭu
tōṟṟa mokkum^ kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.

itu nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaramvilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ ēṉai ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum --- vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ oḻinta maramallāta ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam ottu akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭiyum, kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi --- kaṉṉeṉṉum^ col eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṉṉakkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉa varum. kaṉṉakkaṭumai eṉa+ kuṇa vēṟṟumaiyum^ ciṟupāṉmai koḷka.

tōṟṟameṉṟataṉāl alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum melleḻuttum ēṉaikkaṇattu akaramum^ koḷka. kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavuṉṉañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. kaṉṉaṅkaṭumai eṉa+ kuṇavēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka.

`poṉṉakar varaippiṉ+ kaṉṉam^ tūkki' ((aiṅkuṟu//247)) eṉpatōveṉiṉ, atu makara īṟṟu+ poruṭpeyar. (51)

TE347n

iyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tantai muṟaivariṉ
mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa vakara nilaiyu
meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum avviyal+ peyarē.

iḵtu `aḵṟiṇai viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē' ((eḻu//155)) eṉṟataṟku īṇṭu+ tiripu kūṟaliṉ eṇtāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tantai muṟai variṉ --- iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar+ tantaiyeṉṉum muṟaippeyar varumoḻiyāy varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram nilaiyum --- attantai yeṉpataṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa takaravoṟṟukkeṭa ataṉ mēlēṟiniṉṟa akaram^ keṭātu niṟkum, avviyaṟpeyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum --- annilaimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar aṉṉeṉṉum^ collil akaram ēṟiniṉṟa meyyai oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttantai koṟṟantai eṉa varum.

mutaṟkaṇ meyyeṉṟataṉāl cāttaṉṟantai koṟṟaṉṟantai eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipum^ koḷka. (52)

TE348n

ātaṉum pūtaṉum^ kūṟiya viyalpoṭu
peyaroṟ ṟakaram^ tuvara+ keṭumē.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ātaṉum pūtaṉum --- muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tantaiyeṉṉum muṟaippeyaroṭu muṭiyuṅkāl, kūṟiya iyalpoṭu --- muṟkūṟiya nilaimoḻi aṉ keṭutalum varumoḻi+ takaravoṟṟu+ keṭutalumākiya ceykaikaḷuṭaṉē, peyaroṟṟu akaram^ tuvarakkeṭum --- nilaimoḻippeyaril aṉkeṭa niṉṟa takaravoṟṟum varumoḻiyil takaravoṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟakkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āntai pūntai eṉa varum.

iyalpeṉṟataṉāṉ+ peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṟṟalum^ koḷka. ātantai pūtantai eṉavarum.

iṉi+ tuvaraveṉṟataṉāṉ aḻāṉ puḻāṉ eṉa niṟutti+ tantai eṉa varuvittu nilaimoḻi ṉakaramum varumoḻi+ takaramum akaramum keṭuttu aḻāntai puḻāntai eṉa muṭikka. (53)

TE349n

ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi yiyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi --- avviyaṟpeyar paṇpu aṭuttu varumvaḻi, iyaṟkaiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya iruvakai+ ceykaiyum^ tavirntu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: peruñcāttaṉṟantai peruṅkoṟṟaṉṟantai eṉa varum. koṟṟam^ koṟṟaṉṟantai cāttam^ koṟṟaṉṟantai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa paṇpaṉṟi aṭai aṭuttaṉavātaliṉ+ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka. (57)

TE350n

appeyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭu vaḻiyu
niṟṟalu murittē yammeṉ cāriyai
makkaṇ muṟaitokūu maruṅki ṉāṉa.

itu mēlataṟku vēṟōr varumoḻikkaṇ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: appeyar makkaḷ āṉamuṟaitokūum maruṅkiṉum --- avviyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tantaiyaṉṟi makaṉākiya muṟaippeyar vantu tokumiṭattiṉum, meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭuvaḻi ammeṉ cāriyai niṟṟalum urittu --- avviyal+ peyaril tāṉēṟiya meyniṟka aṉkeṭṭu ammuccāriyai vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

āṉa eṉpataṉai makkaḷōṭum, ummaiyai maruṅkiṉōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. muṟaitokūu maruṅkiṉeṉṟatu iṉṉāṟku makaṉeṉṉum muṟaippeyarāy+ cērumiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉa nilaimoḻi aṉ keṭṭuḻi ammu vantatu. ivaṟṟiṟku atuveṉurupu viriyātu ataṉ uṭaimaipporuḷ virikka. itu muṟaippeyar.

iṉi ummaiyāṉ+ koṟṟaṅkuṭi cāttaṅkuṭi eṉa+ piṟa peyar tokkaṉavum^ koḷka.

meyyoḻittu eṉṟataṉāṉē koṟṟamaṅkalam cāttamaṅkalam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ ammiṉ makaraṅkeṭutalum vēṭṭamaṅkalam vēṭṭaṅkuṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ nilaimoḻiyoṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. (55)

TE351n

tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉu meṉṉu
māmuṟai yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.

itu mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉum eṉṉum āmuṟai iyaṟpeyar --- avviyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉumeṉṉum ammuṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tantaiyoṭum makkaḷ muṟaimaiyoṭum puṇarumvaḻi, tiripiṭaṉila --- muṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāṉṟantai pēṉṟantai kōṉṟantai eṉavum, tāṉkoṟṟaṉ pēṉkoṟṟaṉ kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum. pēṉ kōṉ eṉpaṉa muṟkālattu vaḻakku. ivai tokaimarapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇai viravu+ peyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpuḻi iyalpāyiṉavēṉum īṇṭu ivvīṟṟiṟku+ tiripu kūṟutaliṉ ataṉaiyum vilakki iyalpāmeṉpatūum^ kūṟiṉār. (56)

TE352n

tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeya rurupiya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, tokai marapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉāṉē iyalpāy niṉṟa tāṉ eṉpataṉaiyum `uyirīṟākiya' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉāl iyalpāy niṉṟa yāṉeṉpataṉaiyum avviyalpuvilakki urupiyalōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar --- tāṉeṉṉum viravuppeyarum yāṉeṉṉum uyartiṇaippeyarum, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupiyaliṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpilē nilaipeṟṟu+ tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟuki+ taṉṉeṉṟum yāṉeṉpatu ākāram ekaramāy yakaraṅkeṭṭu eṉṉeṉṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: taṉkai eṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum.

varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ taṉñāṇ eṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai āṭai eṉavarum. (57)

TE353n

vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi+ kuṟukalum^ tiritalum^
tōṟṟa millai yeṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumai alvaḻi --- muṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉeṉpaṉa vēṟṟumai+ puṇarcciyallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalum^ tiritalum^ tōṟṟamillai eṉmaṉār pulavar --- tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukutalum yāṉeṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalum^ tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāṉ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum, yāṉ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ ñāṉṟēṉ nīṇṭēṉ māṇṭēṉ valiyēṉ eṉavum varum.

tōṟṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟi ṉakaram^ tiritalum^ koḷka. taṟpukaḻ taṟpāṭi, eṟpukaḻ eṟpāṭi eṉa varum. (58)

TE354n

aḻaṉe ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.

itu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ṉakaram^ tiriyātu keṭuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḻaṉ eṉ iṟuti keṭa --- aḻaṉeṉṉuñcol taṉ īṟṟu ṉakaram^ keṭa, valleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:aḻakkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉa varum. aḻakkuṭameṉpatu piṇakkuṭattai. (59)

TE355n

muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉar+ tōṉṟu
milleṉ kiḷavimicai ṟakara moṟṟa
ṟolliyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.

itu `maruviṉṟokuti' ((eḻu//111)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa marūukkaḷil oṉṟaṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavimicai --- muṉṉeṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉē varum illeṉṉum^ colliṉmēlē, ṟakaram oṟṟal --- oru ṟakaravoṟṟu vantuniṉṟu muṭital, tolliyal maruṅkiṉ marīiyamarapu --- paḻaiyatākiya iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya vaḻakkiṭattu maruvivanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:muṉṟil eṉa varum. iṉmuṉ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu muṉṟileṉṟu talaitaṭumāṟutaliṉ marūuvāyiṟṟu. muṉṉeṉpataṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutal ilakkaṇamēṉum atuvaṉṟi+ taṉakku iṉamāyatōr ṟakaravoṟṟu+ peṟutaliṉ vēṟu muṭipāyiṟṟu. (60)

TE356n

poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟaiyiṉ
muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum lakāra makāram^
ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ+ ṟoṭariya lāṉa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuppeya roṉṟaṟku+ ceyyuḷmuṭipu kūṟutaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: poṉ eṉ kiḷavi ī,ṟa keṭa --- poṉṉeṉṉum^ col taṉṉīṟākiya ṉakaraṅkeṭāniṟka, muṉṉar lakāra makāram muṟaiyiṉ+ ṟōṉṟum --- ataṉ muṉṉar lakaramum makaravoṟṟum muṟaiyāṉē vantu niṟkum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkil toṭariyalāṉa --- aṅṅaṉam niṟpatu ceyyuḷiṭattu+ coṟkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarccippaṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟaiyiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ makaram oṟṟātal koḷka.

utāraṇam: `polampaṭa+ polinta koycuvaṟpuravi' eṉavarum.

toṭariyalāṉ eṉṟataṉāṉē vaṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum lakaram niṟka makaram valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttāka+ tirital koḷka. `polaṅkalañcumanta pūṇṭāṅkiḷamulai' ((akam//16)) `polañcuṭarāḻi pūṇṭatērē' `polantēr+ kuṭṭuvaṉ' eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē `polanaṟunteriyal' ((puṟam//26)) `polamalarāvirai' eṉṟāṟpōla makaraṅkeṭṭu+ piṟakaṇattu muṭitalum^ koḷka. (61)

TE357n

yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumai+ poruḷvayiṉ
valleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē.

itu muṟaiyāṉē yakara īṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumai+ poruḷvayiṉ --- yakara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ, av valleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāykkāl cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum. (62)

TE358n

tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkai yākum.

itu viravupporuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiya valleḻuttu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāyeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkaiyākum --- tāyeṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāykai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

mēlai+ cūttirattāṉ mikutiyum^ kūṟutaliṉ aḵṟiṇai viravu+ peyaruḷ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. (63)

TE359n

makaṉviṉai kiḷappiṉ mutaṉilai yiyaṟṟē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu, tāyeṉpatu aṭaiyaṭuttuḻi valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: makaṉ viṉaikiḷappiṉ --- tāyeṉṉum^ col taṉakku aṭaiyāy muṉvanta makaṉatu viṉaiyai+ piṉṉāka oruvaṉ kūṟumiṭattu, mutal nilai iyaṟṟu --- iv vīṟṟuḷ mutaṟkaṇ+ kūṟiya nilaimaiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: makaṉṟāykkalām ceru tuṟattal pakaittal eṉa varum. makaṉ tāyōṭu kalāytta kalām eṉa viriyum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkum urupu virikka. viṉai īṇṭu+ pakaimēṟṟu. (64)

TE360n

melleḻuttu uṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa --- yakara īṟṟuḷ atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttu mikku uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vēykkuṟai vēyṅkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa varum. (65)

TE361n

alvaḻi yellā miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu alvaḻikku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻi ellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- yakara īṟṟu alvaḻiyellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāykaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāl avvāykkoṇṭāṉ ivvāykkoṇṭāṉ uvvāykkoṇṭāṉ evvāykkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa vurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa muṭivaṉavum, tāykkoṇṭāṉ tūyppeytāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlum viṉaiyeccamum, poyccol meyccol eyppaṉṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum paṇputtokaiyum, vēykaṭitu vēykkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭivum^ koḷka. (66)

TE362n

rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē rakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: rakāra iṟuti --- rakāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, yakāra iyaṟṟu --- yakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tērkkāl celavu talai puṟam eṉa varum.

im māṭṭēṟṟiṉai yakara īṟṟu vēṟṟumai alvaḻiyeṉṉum iraṇṭaiyum^ karuti māṭṭeṟintāreṉpār alvaḻi muṭipum īṇṭu+ kāṭṭuvar. yām ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ+ kāṭṭutum.

itu ḻakara īṟṟiṟkum okkum.

māṭṭēṟṟāṉ uṟaḻcciyum^ koḷka. vērṅkuṟai vērkkuṟai eṉa varum. (67)

TE363n

ārum vetirum^ cārum pīru
melleḻuttu mikutaṉ meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟiṟku eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ārum vetirum^ cārum pīrum --- āreṉṉum^ collum vetireṉṉuñcollum^ cāreṉṉum^ collum pīreṉṉum^ collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum --- melleḻuttu mikkumuṭital meymmai peṟa+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ārṅkōṭu vetirṅkōṭu cārṅkōṭu pīrṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

pīr marameṉpār pīrṅkōṭeṉpar. `pīrvāy+ pirinta nīr niṟai muṟaiceytu' eṉṟāṟpōla+ cāṉṟōr palarum^ ceyyuḷ ceytavāṟu kāṇ.

meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ `āram^ kaṇṇi yaṭupōr+ cōḻar' ((akam//93)) eṉa ār ammuppeṟutalum `mārippīrattalar cilakoṇṭē ((kuṟu//98)) eṉa+ pīr attuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. itaṉai atikāra+ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭippārum uḷar.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē kūrṅkatirvēl īrṅkōtai eṉṟāl+ pōlavum^ kutirṅkōṭu vilarṅkōṭu ayirṅkōṭu tuvarṅkōṭu cilirṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōlavum melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tuvaraṅkōṭu eṉa ammuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. (68)

TE364n

cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻ vayiṉ valikkum.

iḵtu eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻ vayiṉ valikkum --- cār eṉpatu kāḻ eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum iṭattu valleḻuttu mikku+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cār+kāḻ eṉa varum. cāriṉatu vittu* eṉpatu* ē poruḷ. itaṉai vayiram eṉiṉ+ kiḷantōtuvār eṉṟu uṇarka. (69)

TE365n

pīr eṉ kiḷavi y-ammoṭu* um^ civaṇum.

iḵtu eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: pīr eṉ kiḷavi ammoṭu* um^ civaṇum --- pīr eṉṉum^ col melleḻuttu* ē y-aṉṟi ammu+ peṟṟu* um muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pīraṅkoṭi cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, `poṉpōl+ pīramoṭu pūtta putaṉmalar' ((neṭunalvāṭai//14)) eṉavum varum. ummai iṟantatutaḻīiyiṟṟu. (70)

TE366n

lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē

itu muṟaiyāṉē lakāra īṟṟai vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu --- lakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāya, lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṟkuṟai ciṟai talai puṟam neṟkatir cōṟu talai puṟam eṉa varum. (71)

TE367n

melleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉakāra mākum.

itu ṉakāramāmeṉṟaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṉakāramākum --- avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyaiyiṉ ṉakāramāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṉñeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum.

i+ cūttirattiṉai vēṟṟumai yiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttu+ kōṭaṟkaṇ+ ciṅkanōkkāka vaittamaiyāṉ alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka. kaṉñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.

TE368n

alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa --- iv vīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷellām^ tantiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalkuṟitu kaṟkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kalkuṟumai kaṟkuṟumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa+ kuṇampaṟṟi vanta vēṟṟumaikkum uṟaḻcci koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē viṉaiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum uruvu tirintaṉavum^ koḷka. vantāṉāṟkoṟṟaṉ poruvāṉāṟpōkāṉ eṉavum, attāṟkoṇṭāṉ ittāṟkoṇṭāṉ uttāṟkoṇṭāṉ ettāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum.

akkāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟavum muṭipu uḷḷaṉavellām itaṉāṉ muṭittukkoḷka. (73)

TE369n

takaram varuvaḻi yāyta nilaiyalum
pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulamai yōrē.

itu lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tiriyumeṉṟaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varuvaḻi āytam nilaiyalum --- takaram mutalākiya moḻi vantāl lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tirintuniṟṟalum, pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamaiyōr --- kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.

pukariṉṟeṉṟataṉāl `neṭiyataṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//370)) eṉpataṉuḷ vēṟītu vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipum^ koḷka. (74)

TE370n

neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpumā ruḷavē.

iḵtu `alvaḻiyellāmuṟaḻ' ((eḻu//168)) eṉṟataṉai vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumāruḷa --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉ īṟṟu lakāra īṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakārampōla+ tirintu uṟaḻtalēyaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pālkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. iyalpākātu tirintaṉa, vēlkaṭitu vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. (75)

TE371n

nellum^ cellum^ kollum^ collu
mallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumai yiyala.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ uṟaḻntumuṭika eṉṟataṉai vēṟṟumai muṭipeṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: nellum^ cellum^ kollum^ collum --- nelleṉṉum^ collum^ celleṉṉum^ collum^ kolleṉṉum^ collum^ colleṉṉum^ collumākiya i+ nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumai iyala --- alvaḻiyai+ collumiṭattum^ tām vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ummai ciṟappu.

utāraṇam: neṟkāyttatu ceṟkaṭitu koṟkaṭitu coṟkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (76)

TE372n

illeṉ kiḷavi y-iṉmai ceppiṉ
valleḻuttu mikutalu maiyiṭai varutalu
miyaṟkai yātalu mākāram varutalum^
koḷattaku marapi ṉākiṭa ṉuṭaittē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉaikkuṟippu+ colluḷ oṉṟaṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmai ceppiṉ --- illeṉṉuñcol iruppiṭamākiya illai uṇarttātu oru poruḷiṉatu illāmaiyai uṇarttum iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, ai iṭaivarutalum --- aikāram iṭaiyē varutalum, iyaṟkaiyātalum --- iraṇṭum vārātu iyalpāy muṭitalum, ākāram varutalum --- ākāramvantu muṭitalumākiya innāṉku muṭipum, koḷattaku marapiṉ --- coṟku muṭipāka+ koḷattakum muṟaiyāṉē, ākiṭa ṉuṭaittu --- taṉmuṭipām iṭaṉ uṭaittu eṉṟavāṟu.

koḷattaku marapiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi aikāram varutalum, aikāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmaiyum, ākāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: illeṉa niṟutti+ koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa+ tantu valleḻuttum aikāramum^ koṭuttu illai+ koṟṟaṉeṉa ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

iṉṉum avvāṟē niṟutti aikāramē koṭuttu illai koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa valleḻuttu mikātu muṭikka.

iṉṉum^ koḷattaku marapiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉai+ kaṇattiṉ muṉṉum aikāramē koṭuttu illaiñāṇ nūl maṇi vāṉam āaiṭa eṉa oṭṭuka.

iḵtu illeṉpatōr mutaṉilai niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu iṅṅaṉam puṇarntateṉpatu uṇartaṟku illeṉkiḷaviyeṉṟum iyaṟkaiyātalumeṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār. immuṭipu viṉaiyiyaluḷ viravuviṉaikkaṇ `iṉmai ceppal' eṉpuḻi `illai il' ((col//222)) eṉṟu uraikūṟiyavataṉāṉum, avaṉillai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa utāraṇamāka ellā āciriyarum^ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟāṉum uṇarka.

itaṉāṉē iṅṅaṉam puṇarttacollaṉṟi illaiyeṉa aikāra īṟāy niṟpatōr col iṉmaiyum āyiṉ, iṉmai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum ivvāṟē puṇarkkaveṉiṉ avai varumoḻiyiṉṟi oru collāy niṟṟaliṉ+ puṇarkkārāyiṉār.

iṉi iyalpu varumāṟu: --- eṇṇil kuṇam ceykai tuṭi poruḷ eṉavum, poyyil ñāṉam maiyil vāṇmukam eṉavum varum.

iṉi ākāram varumāṟu: --- illākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ poruḷ eṉa ākāram valleḻuttu+ peṟṟaṉa. piṉ+ kūṟiya iraṇṭum illeṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippu mutaṉilaiyaṭiyāka+ tōṉṟiya peyarecca maṟai tokkum virintum niṉṟaṉa.

iyalpumuṉ+ kūṟātataṉāl immuṭipiṟku vēṇṭum^ ceykai ceyka. tāviṉīṭci eṉṟāṟpōla vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭum^ ceykai ceytu muṭikka. (77)

TE373n

valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.

iḵtu iruvaḻiyum^ tirintum uṟaḻntum varumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: val eṉ kiḷavi --- valleṉṉum^ col alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakārattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum. (78)

TE374n

nāyum palakaiyum varūum^ kālai
yāvayi ṉukaram^ keṭutalu murittē
yukaram^ keṭuvaḻi yakara nilaiyum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: nāyum palakaiyum varūum^ kālai --- valleṉpataṉmuṉ nāyeṉṉum^ collum palakaiyeṉṉum^ collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āvayiṉ ukaram^ keṭutalum urittu --- avviṭattu ukaram^ keṭātu niṟṟalēyaṉṟi+ keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaram^ keṭuvaḻi akaram nilaiyum --- avvukaram^ keṭumiṭattu akaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vallanāy vallappalakai eṉa varum.

ummai etirmaṟaiyākalāṉ ukaram^ keṭātēniṉṟu vallunāy valluppalakai eṉa varutalum^ koḷka.

akaram nilaiyumeṉṉātu ukaram^ keṭumeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum ivvakarappēṟu koḷka. vallakkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavarum. (79)

TE375n

pūlvē leṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyo
ṭāmu+ peyarkku mammiṭai varumē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pūl vēl eṉṟā āleṉkiḷaviyoṭu āmuppeyarkkum --- pūleṉṉum^ collum vēleṉṉum^ collum āleṉṉum^ collumākiya ammūṉṟupeyarkkum, am iṭai varum --- vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyai iṭaivantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūlaṅkōṭu vēlaṅkōṭu ālaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka. pūlañeri vēlañeri ālañeri nīḻal viṟaku eṉavarum. eṉṟā eṉa eṇṇiṭaiyiṭṭamaiyāṉ+ pūlāṅkōṭu pūlāṅkaḻi eṉa ākāram pūlukku+ koḷka. (80)

TE376n

toḻil+ peyar ellām^ toḻil+ peyar iyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum^ tiripum uṟaḻcciyum vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- lakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻil+ peyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pullukkaṭitu kallukkaṭitu vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku+ pullutal kallutal vallutal eṉa+ poruḷuraikka.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ ṟoḻiṟpeyarviti eytātu piṟa viti eytuvaṉavum^ koḷka. kaṉṉalkaṭitu piṉṉalkaṭitu kaṉṉaṟkaṭumai piṉṉaṟkaṭumai eṉavum varum. itaṉāṉē meṉkaṇam vantuḻi+ piṉṉaṉñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉa oṭṭuka.

iṉi āṭal pāṭal kūṭal nīṭal mutaliyaṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tirintum muṭital itaṉāṉ+ koḷka. (81)

TE377n

veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻaiyiya ṉilaiyum.

itu tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: veyil eṉ kiḷavi mayaiyiyal nilaiyum --- veyileṉṉum^ col maḻaiyeṉṉum^ coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

maḻaiyeṉpataṉai `vaḷiyeṉa varūum' ((eḻu//242)) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum vaḷiyeṉpataṉai `paṉiyeṉa varūum' ((eḻu//241)) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum māṭṭeṟintavāṟu kāṇka.

utāraṇam: veyilattu+ koṇṭāṉ veyiliṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu attumicai yoṟṟu+ keṭātuniṉṟa iṭam. iḵtu `avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu' ((eḻu//133)) mikkatu atikāra valleḻuttiṉmaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka, cāriyai varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ. (82)

TE378n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta vurupiya ṉilaiyum.

itu muṟaiyāṉē vakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutalākiya vakara iṟuti --- vakara īṟṟu+ peyar nāṉkaṉuḷ+ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya vakara īṟṟuppeyar mūṉṟum, muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta urupiyal nilaiyum --- muṟpaṭakkūṟiya urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaṟṟukkōṭu ivaṟṟukkōṭu uvaṟṟukkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.

muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta eṉṟataṉāṉē vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. avaṟṟiṉkōṭu ivaṟṟiṉkōṭu uvaṟṟiṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ēṉai+ kaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. (83)

TE379n

vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.
