itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi āytamākum --- accuṭṭumutal vakaram vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattu āytamāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aḵkaṭiya iḵkaṭiya uḵkaṭiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa varum. ivvaḻakku ikkālattu aritu. (84)

TE380n

melleḻuttu iyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu ākum.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ avveḻuttākum --- avvakara īṟu meṉkaṇamvantu iyaiyumāyiṉ avvakaravoṟṟu avvammelleḻuttāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aññāṇ iññāṇ uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉa varum. (85)

TE381n

ēṉavai puṇari ṉiyalpeṉa moḻipa.

ituvum atu, av vīṟu ēṉai+ kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉavai puṇariṉ --- accuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟōṭu iṭaikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇarumāyiṉ, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- avvakaram^ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avyāḻ ivyāḻ uvyāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai āṭai eṉaoṭṭuka.

īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu nilaimoḻikkeṉṟum āṇṭu `niṉṟa coṉmuṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟatu varumoḻikkeṉṟum uṇarka. (86)

TE382n

ēṉai vakaram^ toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉaivakaram --- `vakarakkiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu'((eḻu//81)) eṉṟataṉuḷ oḻintuniṉṟa uriccollākiya vakaram iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu --- ñakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tevvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tevvukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.

uraiyiṟkōṭaleṉpataṉāl temmuṉai eṉa vakaravoṟṟu makaravoṟṟāka+ tirital koḷka. (87)

TE383n

ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē ḻakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu --- ḻakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumaikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūḻkkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam eṉa varum. (88)

TE384n

tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇari
ṉakkiṭai varuta lurittu mākum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu akku vakukattaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ --- tāḻeṉṉuñcol+ kōleṉṉum^ colloṭu puṇarumiṭattu, akku iṭaivarutalum urittākum --- valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyai iṭaiyē vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, akkuppeṟātu valleḻuttu mikutal valiyuṭaittāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: tāḻakkōl tāḻkkōl eṉa varum.

itu tāḻai+ tiṟakkum^ kōl eṉa viriyum (89)

TE385n

tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyu mataṉō raṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum --- tamiḻeṉṉum^ collum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyaiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ataṉōraṟṟē eṉṟataṉāl itaṟku+ tamiḻkkūtteṉa valleḻuttu mikutalē valiyuṭaittu.

utāraṇam: tamiḻakkūttu cēri tōṭṭam paḷḷi eṉa varum. tamiḻaiyuṭaiya kūttu eṉa virikka. tamiḻavaraiyar eṉṟāṟpōla valleḻuttuppeṟātu akku+ peṟṟaṉa, `uṇarakkūṟiya' ((eḻu//405)) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ+ koḷka. tamiḻanāṭu tamiḻnāṭu eṉa ēṉaikkaṇattu muṭipu `eppeyarmuṉṉarum' ((eḻu//128)) eṉpataṉuḷ `muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittām. (90)

TE386n

kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ+
pīreṉ kiḷaviyo ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttum ammum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ --- kumiḻeṉṉum^ col+ kumiḻttaleṉṉum^ toḻilaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyoṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum --- pīreṉṉum^ colloṭu ōriyalpiṟṟāy oruvaḻi melleḻuttum oruvaḻi ammum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kumiḻṅkōṭu kumiḻaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

ōriyaṟṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavaṟṟiṟkum immuṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu makiḻaṅkōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (91)

TE387n

pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvē.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttu+ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu --- pāḻeṉṉum^ collīṟu valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pāḻkkiṇaṟu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. itu pāḻuṭkiṇaṟu eṉa viriyum, pāḻtta kiṇaṟu eṉa viṉaittokai muṭiyāmaiyiṉ. (92)

TE388n

ēḻeṉ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyar ivvāṟu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi --- ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar iṟuti, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupu puṇarccikkaṇ+ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē nilaipeṟṟu aṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aḵtu `aṉṉeṉcāriyai yēḻaṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//194)) eṉpatām.

utāraṇam: ēḻaṉkāyam cukku tōrai payaṟu eṉa varum. iyaipu valleḻuttu ōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ vīḻkka. iḵtu ēḻaṉāṉ+ koṇṭa kāyam eṉa viriyum. (93)

TE389n

aḷavu niṟaiyu meṇṇum varuvaḻi
neṭumutal kuṟukalu mukaram varutalum^
kaṭinilai yiṉṟē yāciri yaṟka.

itu mēlataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyam eṇṇum varuvaḻi --- avvēḻeṉpataṉmuṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum^ kaṭinilaiyiṉṟē āciriyaṟka --- muṉṉiṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ māttirai kuṟukalum āṇṭu ukaram varutalum nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu āciriyaṟku eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻukalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, eḻukaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum, eḻumūṉṟu eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.

nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇattu+ poruṭpeyarkkum immuṭipu koḷka. eḻukaṭal cilai ticai piṟappu eṉa varum. (94)

TE390n

patteṉ kiḷavi yoṟṟiṭai keṭuvaḻi
niṟṟal vēṇṭu māyta+ puḷḷi.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, varumoḻi nōkki vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: pattu eṉ kiḷavi oṟṟiṭai keṭuvaḻi --- avvēḻaṉōṭu patteṉpatu puṇarumiṭattu appatteṉkiḷavi iṭaiyoṟṟu+ keṭuvaḻi, āytappuḷḷi niṟṟalvēṇṭum --- āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum. (95)

TE391n

āyiram varuvaḻi yukaram^ keṭumē.

itu neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram varuvaḻi --- ēḻeṉpataṉ muṉ āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, ukaraṅkeṭum --- neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻāyiram eṉa varum. (96)

TE392n

nūṟūrntu varūu māyira+ kiḷavikku+
kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.

iḵtu eytiyatu muḻuvatūum vilakkiṟṟu, ukaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉmēlē neṭumutal kuṟukātu eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu ūrntu varūum āyira+ kiḷavikku --- avvēḻeṉpatu nūṟeṉṉum^ coṉmēl varum āyirameṉṉum^ colliṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkamiṉṟu --- muṟkūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻnūṟāyiram eṉa varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutalkuṟuki ukarampeṟṟu eḻunūṟāyirameṉavum varum.

itaṉāṉē ēḻāyirameṉa mēl mutaṉilai kuṟukāmaiyum^ koḷka.

itaṉāṉē eḻuñāyiṟu eḻunāḷ eḻuvakai eṉa iyalpu kaṇattu neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutalum^ koḷka. (97)

TE393n

aiyam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
yalpeya reṇṇiṉu māyiya ṉilaiyum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti --- avvēḻaṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṟum ammeṉṟum palleṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷaiyuṭaiya, alpeyar eṇṇiṉum --- poruṭpeyarallāta eṇṇuppeyarākiya tāmarai veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉa vantālum, ā iyal nilaiyum --- neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu avviyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻtāmarai ēḻveḷḷam ēḻāmpal eṉa varum. (98)

TE394n

uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmuṉ variṉum --- avvēḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum eṇṇuppeyarumākiya uyirmutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ā iyal tiriyātu --- neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻakal ēḻuḻakku ēḻoṉṟu ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa varum. (99)

TE395n

kīḻeṉ kiḷavi yuṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumaikkaṇ uṟaḻcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum --- kīḻeṉṉum^ col uṟaḻcciyāy+ tōṉṟi muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukātu valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum varumeṉṟa iraṇṭum uṟaḻcciyāy varumeṉṟu koḷka. iyaipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: kīḻkkuḷam kīḻkuḷam cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum. (100)

TE396n

ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ḷakāra īṟṟuccol vēṟṟumaikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu --- ḷakāra īṟṟu+ peyar ṇakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṭakāramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṭkuṟai ciṟai talai puṟam eṉa varum. (101)

TE397n

melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.

itu mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṇakāramākum --- ḷakāra īṟu melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyaiyiṉ ṇakāramāy+ tirintumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṇñeri nuṉi maram eṉa varum.

itaṉai vēṟṟumaiyiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ+ ciṅkanōkkāka vaittaliṉ alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka. muṇñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum. (102)

TE398n

alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.

itu mēlataṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām --- ḷakāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām, uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa --- tiriyātum ṭakāramāy+ tirintum uṟaḻntu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muḷkaṭitu muṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kuṇa vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ivvuṟaḻcci koḷka. muḷkuṟumai muṭkuṟumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum, kōḷkaṭumai kōṭkaṭumai vāḷkaṭumai vāṭkaṭumai eṉavum oṭṭuka.

itaṉāṉē atōṭkoṇṭāṉ itōṭkoṇṭāṉ utōṭkoṇṭāṉ etōṭkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉeṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉavum^ koḷka. (103)

TE399n

āyta nilaiyalum varainilai yiṉṟē
takaram varūum^ kālai yāṉa.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu. takaram varuvaḻi uṟaḻcciyēyaṉṟi āytam āka+ tirintu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varūum^ kālaiyāṉa --- takara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āytam nilaiyalum varainilai yiṉṟu --- ḷakāram ṭakāramāka+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tirintu niṟṟalum nīkkum nilaimaiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muḵṭītu muṭṭītu eṉavarum (104)

TE400n

neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpā kunavum
vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi vēṟṟumai nilaiyalum
pōṟṟal vēṇṭu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpu ākunavum --- av vīṟṟu neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟaiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi vēṟṟumai nilaiyalum --- vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattu vēṟṟumaiyiṉ iyalpaiyuṭaiyaṉavāy+ tirintu muṭitalaiyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumāruḷa --- pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōḷkaṭitu vāḷkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, `puṭṭēmpa+ puyaṉmāṟi' ((paṭṭiṉappāmālai//4)) eṉavum varum.

pōṟṟalvēṇṭum eṉṟataṉāl utaḷaṅkāy cetiḷ pū tōl eṉa ammu+ peṟutalum^ koḷka. utaḷeṉpatu yāṭṭiṉai uṇarttuṅkāl muṟkūṟiya muṭipūkaḷ iruvaḻikkum ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka. utaṭkōṭu utaḷkaṭitu utaṇaṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. `mōttaiyum^ takaru mutaḷu mapparum' ((marapiyal//47)) eṉṟār marapiyalil. (105)

TE401n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarkku iruvaḻiyum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- ḷakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tuḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tuḷḷukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.

ellā meṉṟataṉāṉē iruvaḻiyum^ toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟātu tirintum tiriyātum muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. kōḷkaṭitu kōṭkaṭitu kōḷkaṭumai kōṭkaṭumai eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa piṟavum varum.

iṉi vāḷkaṭitu vāṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum vāḷkaṭumai vāṭkaṭumai eṉavum^ kāṭṭuka. vāḷ --- kollutal. (106)

TE402n

iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliya ṉilaiyum.

itu tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi --- iruḷeṉṉum col vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, veyiliyal nilaiyum --- veyileṉṉum coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉ iruḷiṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

cāriyai varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattum oṭṭuka. iruḷattuñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ iruḷiṉñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. (107)

TE403n

puḷḷum vaḷḷum^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

ituvum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, tiripum iyalpum vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarviti vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷum vaḷḷum --- puḷḷeṉṉum^ collum vaḷḷeṉṉum^ collum iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷḷukkaṭitu vaḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, puḷḷukkaṭumai vaḷḷukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.

itaṉait `toḻiṟpeyarellām' ((eḻu//401)) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyātataṉāl iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaittiripu eyti muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. puṭkaṭitu vaṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, puṭkaṭumai vaṭkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum, puṇñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, puṇñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum varum. puḷḷuvalitu puḷvalitu puḷḷu vaṉmai puḷvaṉmai eṉa vakarattiṉ muṉṉar ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum varutaliṉ `niṉṟacoṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyāmai uṇarka. itu vaḷḷiṟkum okkum. (108)

TE404n

makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu. `uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaippeyar' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉuḷ+ kūṟiya iyalpu vilakki+ tiripu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccol iṟuti --- makkaḷeṉṉum peyarccolliṟuti iyalpēyaṉṟi, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu --- takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

takkavaḻiyeṉṟār perumpāṉmai makkaḷ uṭampu uyir nīṅki+ kiṭantakālattiṉ aḵtu immuṭivupeṟum eṉṟaṟku.

utāraṇam: makkaṭkai cevi talai puṟam. `ikkiṭantatu makkaṭṭalai' eṉpataṉāṉ avvāṟātal koḷka. makkaḷ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉa+ tiriyātu niṉṟatu uyiruṇmai peṟṟu.

iṉi+ ciṟupāṉmai makkaṭpaṇpu makkaṭcuṭṭu eṉavum varum. (109)

TE405n

uṇara+ kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ+
kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavai kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṉkaṇ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttākakkoṇṭu cāriyai peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ cāriyaiyum, eḻuttu+ peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttum^ koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṇarakkūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ --- uṇara+ kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyalpiṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavai --- mēl muṭitta muṭipaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ+ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyavai tōṉṟiyavaḻi, kaṇṇiṉar koḷal --- avaṟṟaiyum^ karutikkoṇṭu ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṇappattam eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akku+ peṟṟatu. maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammu+ peṟṟatu. poṉṉappattam eṉa ṉakara īṟu akku+ peṟṟatu. poṉṉaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammu+ peṟṟatu. kāṉaṅkōḻi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammu+ peṟṟatu. maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi kāṉāṅkōḻi eṉpaṉa marūu. vēyiṉṟalai eṉa yakara īṟṟu urupiṟku+ ceṉṟacāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu+ keṭukka. nīr kuṟitu eṉa rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu. vērkuṟitu vērkkuṟitu itu rakara īṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci. vaṭacārkkūrai mēlcārkkūrai ivai valleḻuttu mikka marūu muṭipu. ampar+ koṇṭāṉ imparkkoṇṭāṉ umparkkoṇṭāṉ emparkkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa valloṟṟu+ peṟṟaṉa. takarkkuṭṭi pukarppōttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtokai karutiṟṟēl īṇṭu muṭikka. vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyum. viḻaleṉṉum lakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ṟakaramākātu ṉakaramāy muṭital koḷka. viḻaṉkāṭu ceṟu tāḷ puṟam eṉa varum. nallampāṟai ucilaṅkōṭu eliyālaṅkāy puṭōlaṅkāy eṉa av vīṟu ammuppeṟṟatu. kallāmpāṟai eṉpatu marūu. aḻalattu+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa avvīṟu attuppeṟṟatu. aḻukkaṟpōr puḻukkaṟcōṟu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻittiripu. yāḻkuṟitu eṉpatu ḻakara īṟṟu alvaḻi yiyalpu. vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci. tāḻampāvai eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi akku+ peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉkōṭu ceykai talai puṟam eṉa av vīṟṟu urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. muṉṉāḷaivāḻvu muṉṉāḷaipparicu orunāḷaikkuḻavi orutiṅkaḷaikkuḻavi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa ḷakāra īṟu aikāramum ataṉōṭu valleḻuttum peṟutal koḷka. piṟavum ivvōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vellām^ koṇarntu itaṉāṉ muṭikka. kuḷattiṉpuṟam marattiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka.

iṉik `kaṭicollillai' ((col//452)) eṉpataṉāṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vantu tirintu muṭiyum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa. avaṟṟai+ kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē eṉpataṉāṉ maṇṇukkuppōṉāṉ poṉṉukku viṟṟāṉ poruḷukkuppōṉāṉ nellukkuviṟṟāṉ koḷḷukkukkoṇṭāṉ patiṉēḻu eṉṟāṟpōla vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum, `viṇṇukkumēl' `maṇṇukkunāppaṇ'

`pallukku+ tōṟṟa paṉimullai paiṅkiḷikaḷ
collukku+ tōṟṟiṉṉam^ tōṉṟiṉavā --- ṉellukku
nūṟōoo nūṟeṉpā ṇuṭaṅkiṭaikkum vaṉmulaikku
māṟōmā laṉṟaḷanta maṇ.'

eṉṟāṟpōla+ ceyyuṭkaṇ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum muṭikka.

paṟku neṟku eṉpaṉa mutaliyavum^ koḷka. ivai urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vārātu urupiṉkaṇ vantaṉavēṉum īṇṭu+ kāṭṭiṉām, āṇṭu+ `puḷḷiyiṟutiyum' ((eḻu//202)) eṉṉum urupiyal+ cūttirattu ilēcu kōṭaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟeṉṟu karuti. iṉi accūttirattut `tēruṅkālai' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittalum oṉṟu. (110)

((puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((9. kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal))

TE406n

īreḻuttu orumoḻi yuyirttoṭa riṭaittoṭa
rāyta+ toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭa
rāyiru mūṉṟē yukaram^ kuṟukiṭaṉ.

eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu+ kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttu+ poruṭpeyarōṭum eṇṇuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum puṇarum muṟaimai uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itu `meyyēyuyireṉṟāyīriyala' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ uyiriṉatu vikāramāy niṉṟa kuṟṟukarattai iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarkkiṉṟamaiyiṉ mēlai ōttiṉōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. ittalaiccūttiram moḻimarapiṉakattu iruvakaittu eṉṟa kuṟṟukaram itaṉakattu iṉaittu moḻiyiṟuti varumeṉṟu avaṟṟiṟku+ peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. appeyarpeyar, ammuṟaimuṟai, attokaitokai `toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu' ((eḻu//36)) varumeṉṟu āṇṭu+ kūṟiyavataṉai īṇṭu aintuvakaippaṭutti ataṉōṭu `neṭṭeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//36)) eṉṟatu oṉṟēyātaliṉ ataṉaiyum^ kūṭṭi aṟuvakaitteṉṟār.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttu orumoḻi --- iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākiya orumoḻiyum, uyirttoṭar --- uyirmēlvarum meyyai+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, iṭaittoṭar --- iṭaiyoṟṟu mēlvarum meyyai+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyta+ toṭarmoḻi --- āytamākiya eḻuttu mēlvarum meyyai+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, vaṉṟoṭar --- valloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyai+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, meṉṟoṭar --- melloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyai+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyirumūṉṟē --- ākiya av vāṟucollumē, ukaram^ kuṟuku iṭaṉ --- kuṟṟiyalukaram^ kuṟukivarum iṭaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.

itaṉai ēḻeṉṟu koḷvārkku+ piṇṇākku cuṇṇāmpu āmaṇakku mutaliyaṉa muṭiyāmai uṇarka. (1)

TE407n

avaṟṟuḷ
īroṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi yiṭaittoṭa rākā.

iḵtu avvāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- avvāṟaṉuḷ, īroṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi --- iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ toṭarntu niṟkum^ colliṟku iṭaiyiṉa oṟṟu mutal niṉṟāl, iṭaittoṭarākā --- mēl iṭaiyiṉam^ toṭarntunillā, valliṉamum melliṉamum toṭarntu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ārkku, īrkku, noympu, moympu eṉa varum. (2)

TE408n

allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum
ellā v-iṟutiyum ukara nilaiyum.

iḵtu `iṭaippaṭiṟkuṟuku miṭaṉumāruṇṭē' ((eḻu//37)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ araimāttiraiyiṉum^ kuṟukumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakki `avviya ṉilaiyu mēṉai mūṉṟē' ((eḻu//12)) eṉṟa vitiyē peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum --- alvaḻiyai+ collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum --- vēṟṟumaippuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ellā iṟutiyum ukaram nilaiyum --- āṟu īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum ukaram^ taṉ araimāttiraiyai+ peṟṟuniṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

varumoḻiyāṉallatu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyum viḷaṅkāmaiyiṉ `allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum' eṉavē irumoḻi+ puṇarcciyeṉpatu peṟṟām. ivvirumoḻikkaṇ+ paḻaiya araimāttiraipeṟṟē niṟkumeṉṟār. aṉṟi irumoḻi+ puṇarccikkaṇ orumāttirai peṟumeṉṟārkku+ paṉmoḻi+ puṇarcciyākiya ceyyuḷilakkaṇam^ kuṟṟukarattāṉ nērpacai niraiyacai kōṭalum avaṟṟāṉ aṟupatu vañciccīr kōṭalum pattoṉpatiṉāyirattu irunūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟṟoru toṭaikōṭalum iṉṟāy muṟṟiyalukaramākavē koḷḷavēṇṭutaliṉ māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉṉum kuṟṟam^ taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu nākukaṭumai varakukaṭumai eṉa varum. ivai tam araimāttirai peṟṟaṉa. ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

iṉi itu `mālyāṟupōntu kālcurantupāyntu' eṉa+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum araimāttirai peṟṟatu eṉṉākkāl vañciccī riṉṟāmāṟu uṇarka. (3)

TE409n

valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi+
tollai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.

itu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ araimāttirai peṟum eṉṟataṉai vilakki `iṭaippaṭiṉ+ kuṟukumiṭaṉum' ((eḻu//37)) eṉṟataṉāṉ araimāttiraiyiṉum^ kuṟukumeṉṟu āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē varumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi --- valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukaram, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, tollai iyaṟkai nilaiyalum urittu --- `iṭaippaṭiṟkuṟukum' ((eḻu//37)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya araimāttiraiyiṉum^ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummai etirmaṟai.

utāraṇam: kokkukkaṭitu kokkukkaṭumai eṉa arai māttiraiyiṉ+ kuṟaintavāṟu kuraṅkukaṭiteṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttu+ cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.

muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu ukaraniṟaiyumeṉṟu pāṭamōti ataṟku ukaram araimāttiraiyiṉ+ ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷkūṟi iccūttirattiṟku+ paḻaiya araimāttirai peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar. (4)

TE410n

yakaram varuvaḻi yikaram kuṟuku
mukara+ kiḷavi tuvara+ tōṉṟātu.

itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvara+ tōṉṟātu --- yakaramutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi y-āy varumiṭattu nilaimoḻi+ kuṟṟukaraveḻuttu muṟṟattōṉṟātu, ikaram^ kuṟukum --- āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu araimāttirai peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum. `tuvara' eṉṟār, āṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaram^ keṭumeṉṟaṟku. (5)

TE411n

īreḻuttu moḻiyu muyirttoṭar moḻiyum
vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉoṟṟiṭai yiṉamika+
tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.

itu muṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ muṉṉarniṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum --- īreḻuttu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum uyirttoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ --- vēṟṟumai+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, iṉa oṟṟu iṭaimika --- iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭaiyilēmika, valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum --- valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṭṭukkāl cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, muyiṟṟukkāl ciṉai talai puṟam eṉavum varum. kayiṟṟu+ puṟam vayiṟṟuttī eṉpaṉavumām.

tōṟṟam eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉaikkaṇattum immuṭipu koḷka. yāṭṭu ñāṟci niṇam maṇi vāl ataḷ eṉavum, muyiṟṟu ñāṟci niṇam muṭṭai valimai aṭai āṭṭam eṉavum varum. (6)

TE412n

oṟṟiṭai yiṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
yattiṟattu illai valleḻuttu mikalē.

iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟu iṭai iṉam mikā moḻiyu māruḷa --- muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ iṉavoṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyāta moḻikaḷum uḷa, valleḻuttu mikal attiṟattillai --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital akkūṟṟuḷillai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākukāl cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, varaku katir ciṉai tāḷ patar eṉavum varum.

attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟku eytiya cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipuvalleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāṭṭiṉkāl muyiṟṟiṉkāl nākiṉkāl varakiṉkatir eṉavarum.

attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉaikkaṇattum oṟṟiṭai mikāmai koḷka. nākuñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)

TE413n

iṭaiyoṟṟu+ toṭaru māyta+ toṭaru
naṭaiyā yiyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu iṭainiṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭaiyoṟṟu+ toṭarum āyta+ toṭarum --- iṭaiyoṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, naṭai ā iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar --- naṭaipeṟa naṭakkumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya avviyalpu muṭipiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: teḷkukāl ciṟai talai puṟam eṉavum, eḵku kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum. (8)

TE414n

vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟiṭai mikumē
melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi melloṟ ṟellām
valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷaiyoṟ ṟākum.

itu piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum --- vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikkuṟṟukara īṟum, meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟiṭai mikum --- varumoḻiyāy vanta valleḻuttiṉatu oṟṟu iṭaiyilē mikkumuṭiyum, melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi melloṟṟellām --- avviraṇṭaṉuḷ melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa melloṟṟellām, iṟuti valloṟṟu --- iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa valloṟṟum, kiḷai oṟṟu ākum --- kiḷaiyākiya valloṟṟumāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

iṟuti valloṟṟu varutalāvatu kuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟu+ tāṉē muṉṉarvantu niṟṟalām. kiḷaivalloṟṟu varutalāvatu ṇakārattiṟku ṭakāramum ṉakārattiṟku ṟakāramum puṇarcciyum piṟappum nōkki+ kiḷaiyāmātaliṉ, avai muṉṉar vantu niṟṟalām.

utāraṇam: kokkukkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam, kuraṅku+ kāl cevi talai puṟam, kurakkukkāl cevi talai puṟam, eṭkukkuṭṭi cevi talai puṟam, eṟpukkāṭu curam talai puṟam eṉa varum. aṟputtaḷai eṉpatu aṉpiṉāṉ+ ceyta taḷaiyeṉa vēṟṟumaiyum aṉpākiya taḷaiyeṉa alvaḻiyumām.

vanta eṉṟataṉāṉ ivviraṇṭiṟkum urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kokkiṉkāl kuraṅkiṉkāl eṉavarum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ paṟampiṟpāri kuṟumpiṟcāṉṟār eṉa melloṟṟu+ tiriyāmaiyum^ koḷka.

oṟṟeṉṟa mikutiyāṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum kurakku ñāṟci niṇam mukam viral ukir eṉa melloṟṟu+ tirintu varumāṟu koḷka. cilappatikārameṉpatum atu.

vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ varutal `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. (9)

TE415n

marappeyar+ kiḷavikku ammē cāriyai.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, ammu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: marappeyarkkiḷavikku ammē cāriyai --- kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu marappeyarkku varuñcāriyai ammu+ cāriyai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēkkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

kamukaṅkāy teṅkaṅkāy cīḻkampul kampampul payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉṟāṟpōlum pulliṉaiyum marameṉa aṭakki māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉa+ taḻīikkoṇṭa citaiveṉpatām iccūttiram eṉṟu uṇarka. (10)

TE416n

melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻikku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyarum uḷa --- melloṟṟu valloṟṟāka+ tiriyātu melloṟṟāy muṭiyum marappeyarum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puṉkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kuruntaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum varum.

valiyāmarappeyarumuḷa eṉavē valikkum marappeyarum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka. vēppaṅkōṭu kaṭappaṅkāy īccaṅkulai eṉa varum. (11)

TE417n

īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟu+ toṭaru
mammiṭai varaṟku muriyavai yuḷavē
yammara poḻuku moḻivayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu īreḻuttu orumoḻikkum vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkum eytāta teytuvittatu, muṉṉar eytiyataṉai vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttumoḻiyum valloṟṟu+ toṭarum --- īreḻuttu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum, amiṭaivaraṟkum uriyavai uḷa --- muṉ muṭittuppōnta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi ammuccāriyai iṭaiyēvantu muṭitaṟku uriyaṉavum uḷa; yāṇṭeṉiṉ, ammarapu oḻukum moḻivayiṉāṉa --- avvilakkaṇam naṭakkum moḻiyiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēṟaṅkōḷ cūtampōr vaṭṭampōr puṟṟampaḻañcōṟu eṉa varum.

ummai etirmaṟaiyākaliṉ, ammuppeṟātaṉa nākukāl kokkukkāl eṉa muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉavēyām.

ammarapoḻukum eṉṟataṉāl aracakkaṉṉi muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum aracavāḻkkai muracavāḻkkai eṉa akkukkoṭuttum muṭikka.

iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattukkoṇṭāṉ viḷakkattukkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum mayilāppiṟkoṟṟaṉ paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttum^ kariyataṉkōṭu neṭiyataṉkōṭu eṉa aṉ koṭuttum muṭikka. (12)

TE418n

oṟṟunilai tiriyā takkoṭu varūu
makkiḷai moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟu nilaitiriyātu akkoṭuvarūum --- oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilaitiriyātu akkuccāriyaiyōṭum piṟa cāriyaiyōṭum varum, akkiḷai moḻiyumuḷa eṉa moḻipa --- akkiḷaiyāṉa coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṟku ummaiyai muṉṉar māṟuka.

utāraṇam: kuṉṟakkūkai maṉṟappeṇṇai eṉa varum. ummaiyāṉ+ koṅkattuḻavu vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.

nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu nilaitiriyā atikārattukkaṇ varum^ cāriyaikku iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. akkiḷai yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyai toṭarntu muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi cēri tōṭṭam piḷḷai eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭaimai virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟumām. pārppāṉākiya kuḻavi eṉṟāl īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka. pārppaṉamakaḷ pārppaṉa vaṉitai eṉpaṉavum pārppāṉ cāti uṇarttiṉa. (13)

TE419n

eṇṇuppeyar+ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉilaiyum.

itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu poruḷ+ peyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṇṇuppeyar+ kiḷavi --- eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ poruṭpeyarōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉṟaṉkāyam iraṇṭaṉkāyam cukku tōrai payaṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṟaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyameṉa viriyum. varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ koḷka. oṉṟaṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa varum. mēlai+ cūttirattu `nilai' ((eḻu//418)) eṉṟataṉāṉ iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (14)

TE420n

vaṇṭum peṇṭu miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟa muṭivu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- vaṇṭeṉṉum^ collum peṇṭeṉṉum^ collum iṉcāriyaiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vaṇṭiṉkāl peṇṭiṉkāl eṉa varum. itaṟku muṟkūṟiya ilēciṉāṉ valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (15)

TE421n

peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum varaiyār.

itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum varaiyār --- peṇṭeṉṉuñcoṟku iṉṉēyaṉṟi aṉcāriyai varutalaiyum nīkkār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: peṇṭaṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum. (16)

TE422n

yāte ṉiṟutiyum^ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
vāyta viṟutiyu murupiya ṉilaiyum.

iḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉṟaṟkum^ cuṭṭumutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yātu eṉ iṟutiyum^ cuṭṭumutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum --- yāteṉṉum īṟum^ cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupiyal nilaiyum --- urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟu+ cuṭṭumutaliṟuti āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yātaṉkōṭu ataṉkōṭu itaṉkōṭu utaṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉavarum. āytaṅkeṭāmuṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akarattai+ kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl ēṟṟuka, āytam^ keṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ. (17)

TE423n

muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu āyta+ puḷḷi
maṉṉal vēṇṭu malvaḻi yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ+ cuṭṭumutalukarattiṟku oruvaḻi alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu --- cuṭṭumutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, āytappuḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum --- āytavoṟṟu muṉpōla+ keṭātu nilaipeṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, alvaḻiyāṉa --- alvaḻikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉa niṟutti aṭai āṭai ilai īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.

maṉṉeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka. aḵtaṭaiva aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ēṉai ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka. (18)

TE424n

ēṉaimuṉ variṉē tāṉilai yiṉṟē.

itu mēlavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai muṉ variṉ --- muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇamallaṉa varumāyiṉ, tāṉ nilaiyiṉṟu --- avvāytam keṭṭumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atukaṭitu itukaṭitu utukaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉa oṭṭuka. (19)

TE425n

allatu kiḷappi ṉellā moḻiyum^
colliya paṇpi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.

iḵtu āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ --- alvaḻiyai+ collumiṭattu, ellā moḻiyum --- āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum --- mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya kuṇattaiyaṭaiya iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu teḷkukaṭitu eḵkukaṭitu kuraṅkukaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ēṉaikkaṇattukkaṇ `niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

ellāmoḻiyum eṉṟataṉāl viṉaiccollum viṉaikkuṟippuccollum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. kiṭantatu kutirai karitukutirai eṉa varum.

colliya eṉṟataṉāṉ irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtokai vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku valleḻuttuppeṟṟa muṭitalum iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku muṭitalum^ koḷka. karaṭṭukkāṇam kuruṭṭukkōḻi tiruṭṭuppulaiyaṉ kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi veḷiṟṟuppaṉai eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum, varaṭṭāṭu kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.

paṇpiṉeṉṟataṉāl melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. ōryāṭṭaiyāṉai aiyāṭṭaiyerutu eṉavum, aṟṟaikkūttar iṟṟaikkūttar eṉavum, maṉṟaittūtai maṉṟaippāṉai paṇṭaiccāṉṟār eṉavum varum. (20)

TE426n

valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu avvāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikum --- valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kokkukkaṭitu pākkukkaṭitu paṭṭukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (21)

TE427n

cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyu
māyiya ṟiriyā valleḻuttu iyaṟkai.

ituvum av āṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu+ ciṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum --- cuṭṭākiya ciṉaiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭar+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāmutalākiya meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻuttiyaṟkai ā iyal tiriyā --- valleḻuttu+ peṟṟa muṭiyum^ taṉmaiyākiya avviyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkukkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

iyaṟkaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam iyalpāyum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa koḷka. iruntu koṇṭāṉ āṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ tantu tīrntāṉ vantu pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, cettukkiṭantāṉ ceṟṟucceytāṉ uyttukkoṇṭāṉ naṭṭuppōṉāṉ eṉavum varum. (22)

TE428n

yāviṉā moḻiyē yiyalpu mākum.

itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu. valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpāmeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum --- avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉam viṉāvaiyuṭaiya col muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṅkukoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu eppaṭiyeṉṉum viṉāpporuḷai uṇarttiṟṟu. ummaiyāṉ mikkumuṭitalē valiyuṭaittu. ēkāram pirinilai. (23)

TE429n

annāṉ moḻiyum^ tannilai tiriyā.

itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻicceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: annāṉmoḻiyum --- cuṭṭumutaṉmūṉṟum yāmutaṉ moḻiyumākiya annāṉkumoḻiyum, tam nilai tiriyā --- tam melloṟṟāya taṉmai tirintu valloṟṟākātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṟkāṭṭiyavē. tannilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ melloṟṟu+ tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭivaṉa piṟavum^ koḷka. aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ uṅkukkoṇṭāṉ eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

iṉi muṉṉar yā moḻiyeṉṉātu viṉāveṉṟataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṉ iṭapporuṭṭākiya piṟavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. muntukoṇṭāṉ paṇṭukoṇṭāṉ iṉṟukoṇṭāṉ aṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉavarum. (24)

TE420n

uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi yuṇmai ceppiṉ
muntai yiṟuti meyyoṭum^ keṭutalu
mēṉilai yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalu
māmuṟai yiraṇṭu murimaiyu muṭaittē
valleḻuttu varūum^ kālai yāṉa.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ viṉaikkuṟippāy niṉṟatōr col+ paṇpai uṇarttuṅkāl vēṟumuṭivu peṟutal kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṇṭeṉkiḷavi uṇmaiceppiṉ --- uṇṭeṉṉuñcol viṉaikkuṟippaiyuṇarttātu oruporuḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇaiyum uṇṭāy niṟkiṉṟa taṉmaiyākiya paṇpai uṇarttiniṟkumāyiṉ, muntai iṟuti meyyoṭuṅkeṭutalum --- muṉṉarniṉṟa kuṟṟukarantāṉ ēṟiniṉṟa meyyoṭuṅkeṭutalum, mēṉilai oṟṟē ḷakāramātalum --- ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāra oṟṟātalumākiya, ā muṟai iraṇṭum urimaiyum uṭaittu --- ammuṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭu nilaiyum urittu. aḵtu urittaṉṟi muṉṉar niṉṟa nilaiyilē kēṭum^ tiripum iṉṟi niṟṟalum uṭaittu, valleḻuttu varūum^ kālaiyāṉa --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.

valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ vārāniṟpa valleḻuttu varūuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ avvirumuṭipum uḷa, paṇpai yuṇarttum pakaram varum moḻikkaṇṇēyeṉpatūum ēṉai+ kacatakkaḷilum iyalpukaṇattiṉum uṇṭeṉa niṉṟu viṉaikkuṟippāyum^ ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṟkumeṉpatūum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: uḷporuḷ uṇṭuporuḷ eṉa varum. itu poruṇmai cuṭṭātu uṇmaittaṉmai+ paṇpai īṇṭu uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi uṇṭukāṇam uṇṭucākkāṭu uṇṭutāmarai uṇṭuñāṉam nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai āṭai eṉa varuvaṉavellām^ kēṭum^ tiripumiṉṟi viṉaikkuṟippāyum ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu viṉaiyiyaluḷ viṉaikkuṟippu ōtum vaḻi uṇarka.

uḷporuḷeṉpatu paṇputtokai muṭipaṉṟōveṉiṉ, aḵtu ōcai oṟṟumaipaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu. iḵtu ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatupōlum. (25)

TE431n

iruticai puṇari ṉēyiṭai varumē.

itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu+ ticaippeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iruticai puṇariṉ --- iraṇṭu perunticaiyum^ tammiṉ+ puṇariṉ, ē iṭai varum --- ēyeṉṉum cāriyai iṭai niṉṟu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: teṟkēvaṭakku kiḻakkēmēṟku ivai ummaittokai. (26)

TE432n

tiripuvēṟu kiḷappi ṉoṟṟu mukaramum^
keṭutal vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulava
roṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
teṟkoṭu puṇarum kālai yāṉa.

itu perunticaikaḷōṭu kōṇatticaikaḷ puṇarttaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ --- apperum^ ticaikaḷōṭu kōṇatticaikaḷai vēṟāka+ puṇarkkumiṭattu, oṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa oṟṟum avvīṟṟu ukaramum^ keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅkālai --- atu teṟkeṉṉum^ ticaiyoṭu puṇaruṅkālattu, āṉa oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāramākum --- atticaikku+ poruntiniṉṟa ṟakāra oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu tirintu ṉakara oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

tirinteṉṟataṉāṉ vaṭakkeṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa kakara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka.

utāraṇam: vaṭakiḻakku vaṭamēṟku teṉkiḻakku teṉmēṟku eṉa varum.

vēṟeṉṟataṉāl ticaippeyarōṭu poruṭpeyarvariṉum immuṭipu koḷka. vaṭakaṭal vaṭacuram vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari teṉcuram teṉṉilaṅkai eṉa varum.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ uyir keṭṭum^ tirintum mey keṭṭum muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ticaippeyar muṉṉar+ poruṭpeyar vantuḻiyeṉṟu uṇarka. kiḻakku eṉpatu karai kūrai eṉpavaṟṟōṭu puṇarumiṭattu+ kīḻkarai kīḻkūrai eṉa nilaimoḻi yiṟuti ukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭṭu ataṉmēlniṉṟa kakara oṟṟum ḻakarattil akaramum^ keṭṭu mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭintaṉa. mēṟku, karai, kūrai, mīkarai mīkūrai eṉa nilaimoḻi īṟṟu ukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟum^ keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāki muṭintaṉa. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēṉmāṭu mēlpāl mēlaiccēri eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavum^ ceykaiyaṟintu muṭikka. (27)

TE433n

oṉṟumuta lāka veṭṭa ṉiṟuti
yellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṉ+
kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē
muṟṟa viṉvarūu miraṇṭalam^ kaṭaiyē.

niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaramum puṇarumāṟu uṇartti iṉi avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭikkiṉṟār; iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ+ patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarōṭu eṇṇuppeyarvantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇmutalāka eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, pattaṉ muṉvariṉ --- patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyariṉ muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭum --- appatteṉṉum^ colliṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram^ tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyōṭum^ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭai muṟṟa iṉvarūum --- āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉcāriyai iṭaivantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉoṉṟu patiṉmūṉṟu patiṉāṉku patiṉaintu patiṉēḻu patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.

nilaimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ+ piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar+ piṟapeyar vantuḻiyum iṉpeṟutal koḷka. oṉpatiṉkūṟu oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum. muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ+ patiṉāṉkeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṇ+ karuviceytu keṭuttu muṭikka. (28)

TE434n

patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa ṉakāra miraṭṭa
lotta teṉpa viraṇṭuvaru kālai.

itu mēl iṉpeṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟku+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal --- patteṉṉum^ colliṉ niṉṟa takara oṟṟu+ keṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukālai ottateṉpa --- iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ varuṅkālattiṉ+ poruntiṟṟeṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.

`kuṟṟiyalukara meyyoṭuṅkeṭum' ((eḻu//433)) eṉṟaviti itaṟkum mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka. (29)

TE435n

āyiram variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarvariṉ varum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyirameṉṉumeṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpil tiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.

ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu. (30)

TE436n

niṟaiyu maḷavum varūum^ kālaiyum^
kuṟaiyā tāku miṉṉeṉ cāriyai.

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: niṟaiyum aḷavum varūuṅkālaiyum --- muṟkūṟiya patteṉpataṉ muṉṉar niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkālattum, iṉṉeṉcāriyai kuṟaiyātākum --- avviṉṉeṉṉum^ cāriyai kuṟaiyātu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum, patiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.

kuṟaiyātāku meṉṟataṉāṉ+ poruṭpeyarum eṇṇu+ peyarum niṟaippeyarum vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceykai ceytu muṭikka. patiṟṟuvēli yāṇṭu aṭukku muḻam eṉavum, patiṉṟiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum. patiṟṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutal+ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.

iv vīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal `aḷavākumoḻimutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉuḷ `nilaiiya' eṉṟataṉāl muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaram varutalum valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum `oṉṟumutalāka' ((eḻu//433)) eṉpataṉuḷ `muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

TE437n

oṉṟumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam
vantiṭai nilaiyu miyaṟkaittu eṉpa
kūṟiya viyaṟkai kuṟṟiya lukara
māṟa ṉiṟuti yalvaḻi yāṉa.

iḵtu eṇṇu+ peyarōṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇu+ peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar --- oṉṟumutal oṉpatu īṟāka+ kūṟukiṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar variṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa --- varumoḻiyāka vantuniṉṟa patteṉṉum^ colliṉatu takara oṟṟu+ keṭa, āytam vantu iṭainilaiyum iyaṟkaittu eṉpa --- āytamāṉatu vantu iṭaiyē nilaipeṟum iyalpaiyuṭaitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa --- avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallātaviṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaram^ kūṟiya iyaṟkai --- kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya iyaṟkaiyāy meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuḷ+ kāṭṭutum.

vanteṉṟataṉāl āytamāka+ tiriyātu takara oṟṟukkeṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. (22)

TE438n

mutalī reṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakara māku
mukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻicceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalī reṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaram ākum --- avaṟṟiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉuṭai ṉakaraoṟṟum ṇakaraoṟṟum rakara oṟṟāka+ tirintuniṟkum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram varuka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupaḵtu eṉa varum. oṉṟeṉpataṉ īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu ṉakara oṟṟiṉai rakara oṟṟākki ukaramēṟṟi oruveṉa niṟutti niṉṟa patteṉpataṉ takara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu āytamākki+ paḵteṉa varuvittu orupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka. mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ kūṟum^ ciṟappuviti oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ituvē muṭipāka+ koḷka. (33)

TE439n

iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭu* eṉ eṇṇiṟku
naṭai maruṅku* iṉṟu* ē poruḷ vayiṉāṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iraṇṭe ṉeṇṇiṟku iṭainilai rakaram --- iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṟku iṭainiṉṟa rakaram, poruḷvayiṉāṉa --- ammoḻi poruḷpeṟum iṭattu, naṭaimaruṅkiṉṟu --- naṭakkum iṭamiṉṟi+ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: irupaḵtu eṉa varum. itaṟku rakaravuyirmey itaṉāṉ+ keṭuttu ēṉaiya kūṟiyavāṟē kūṭṭi muṭikka.

poruḷeṉavē eṇṇallā+ peyarum^ koḷka. irukaṭal iruviṉai irupiṟappu eṉa varum. (34)

TE440n

mūṉṟu māṟu neṭumutal kuṟukum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇum āṟeṉṉu meṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aṟu eṉa+ kuṟukki+ paḵtu eṉa varuvittu aṟupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka.

TE441n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟē pakāramākum --- mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu+ pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muppaḵtu eṉa varum. (36)

TE442n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉ oṟṟē ṟakāramākum --- nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum.(37)

TE443n

ainta ṉoṟṟē makāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē makāramākum --- ainteṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu makara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.

ēḻu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟaṉmai urupiyaluḷ+ kāṇka.(38)

TE444n

eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṭṭaṉoṟṟē ṇakāramākum --- eṭṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. (39)

TE445n

oṉpā ṉokaramicai+ takara moṟṟu
muntai yoṟṟē ṇakāra miraṭṭum
paḵteṉ kiḷavi yāytapaka raṅkeṭa
niṟṟal vēṇṭu mūkāra+ kiḷavi
yoṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ okaramicai+ takaram oṟṟum --- oṉpaḵtu eṉa niṟutti+ paḵtu eṉa varuvittu muṭikkuṅkāl, nilaimoḻiyākiya oṉpaḵteṉṉum eṇṇiṉatu okarattiṟku muṉṉāka oru takara oṟṟu+ tōṉṟi niṟkum, muntai oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum --- muṉ coṉṉa okarattiṟku muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, paḵteṉ kiḷavi āyta pakaraṅkeṭa --- varumoḻi y-ākiya paḵteṉṉum^ col taṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramum^ keṭṭuppōka, ūkārakkiḷavi niṟṟalvēṇṭum --- nilaimoḻiyil iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar ūkāramākiya eḻuttu vantu niṟṟalai āciriyaṉ virumpum, oṟṟiya takaram ṟakaramākum --- varumoḻiyākiya patteṉpataṉ īṟṟiṉmēlēṟiya ukaraṅkeṭātu pirintu niṟpa oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakara oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. itaṉai oṟṟāy vantuniṉṟa takara oṟṟiṉmēl nilaimoḻi okarattai ēṟṟi+ tovvākki ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭi ataṉmēl varumoḻikkaṇ+ pakaramum āytamuṅkeṭa vanta ūkāramēṟṟi+ toṇṇūvākki+ pakaravāytameṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ nilaimoḻikkaṇ+ pakaramum āytamum^ keṭuttu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭuttu varumoḻi iṟuti+ takara oṟṟu+ tirintu niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉmēlē niṉṟa ukaramēṟṟi+ toṇṇūṟeṉa muṭikka. (40)

TE446n

aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyu niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyum^
kiḷanta viyala tōṉṟum^ kālai.

itu mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉeṇkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyum^ tōṉṟuṅkālai --- muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷantaṟiyappaṭum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantu tōṉṟuṅkālattu, kiḷanta iyala --- āṟaṉ īṟu alvaḻi+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantu iṭainilai rakaraṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki nāṉkaṉoṟṟu vaṉkaṇattu ṟakaramāy eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakaramāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orukalam irukalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, orukaḻañcu irukaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa muṉṉar muṭittum. ivai mutalīreṇṇiṉ ceykai.

tōṉṟuṅkālai yeṉṟataṉāṉ ivveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātuniṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu muṭittukkoḷka. orumūṉṟu orunāṉku irumūṉṟu irunāṉku orukāl irukāl orumuntirikai irumuntirikai orumukkāl irumukkāl eṉpaṉa piṟavum^ koṇarntu oṭṭuka. iṉi+ piṟavit eytuvaṉa ōroṉṟu ōriraṇṭu ōraintu ōrāṟu ōrēḻu ōreṭṭu ōroṉpatu eṉavum, īroṉṟu īriraṇṭu īraintu īrāṟu īrēḻu īreṭṭu īroṉpatu eṉavum, mūvoṉṟu mūviraṇṭu mūvaintu mūvāṟu mūvēḻu mūveṭṭu mūvoṉpatu eṉavum, `mutalīreṇṇiṉmuṉuyir' ((eḻu//455)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ uyirkku eytiya piṟavitiyum `mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalumurittu' ((eḻu//457)) eṉṟa piṟavitiyum peṟṟu+ piṟaceykaikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa. nāloṉṟu nāliraṇṭu nālaintu nālāṟu nālēḻu nāleṭṭu nāloṉpatu eṉpaṉa `nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum' ((eḻu//453)) eṉṟa vitipeṟṟu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum ituvāṟēyaṉṟi aḷavum niṟaiyumaṉṟi varuvaṉavellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. (41)

TE447n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.

itu mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vantatokkum --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy vanta aḷavuppeyar niṟaippeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta valloṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mukkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, mukkaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

`nāṉkaṉoṟṟē ṟakāramākum' ((eḻu//442)) eṉṟa muṉṉai māṭṭēṟu niṟṟaliṉ nāṟkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, nāṟkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. (42)

TE448n

ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻuttu ākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē melleḻuttākum --- aintāvataṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttukku ēṟṟa melleḻuttāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aiṅkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, aiṅkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. ēkāram īṟṟacai. (43)

TE449n

kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūum^ kālai.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi varaiyaṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūum^ kālai --- mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu vantatoppatūum aintaṉoṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum avvaḷavuppeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya kacatapakkaḷ mutaṉmoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

atu muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉām. āṟu `neṭumutalkuṟukum' ((eḻu//440)) eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉē āṟu neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: aṟukalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, aṟukaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakka eṉpaṉa mēṟkāṭṭiṉām. ēḻu kuṟṟukara īṟaṉmaiyiṉ māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu. (44)

TE450n

namava veṉṉu mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi
yakaram variṉu meṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.

itu vēṇṭātu kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭikkiṉṟatu. `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ+ kūṟiyavaṟṟai+ kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṭṭaṉmuṉ --- eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉṉar, namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi akaram variṉum --- aḷavuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭaikkaṇattu oṉṟumākaya namava eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭu porunti uyirkkaṇattu akaram variṉum, ummaiyāṉ uyirkkaṇattu ukaram variṉum^ kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷ variṉum, iyalpu --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟē ṭakāram ṇakāramāy vēṟōr vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

namava veṉṉum mūṉṟum vantāṟpōla akaramvariṉu meṉpatu poruḷ.

utāraṇam: eṇṇāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum eṇṇakal eṇṇuḻakku eṉavum, eṇkalam cāṭi, tūtai pāṉai eṉavum varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ vaṉkaṇattu niṟaippeyarum^ koḷka. eṇkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉa varum.

ivvēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ eṇṇakaleṉa+ kuṟṟukara īṟāy+ kēṭum^ tirivumpeṟṟu uyirvarumoḻiyāṉa toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. (45)

TE451n

aintu mūṉṟu namavaru kālai
vanta tokku moṟṟiya ṉilaiyē.

ituvum mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvāmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintum mūṉṟum nama varukālai --- ainteṉṉu meṇṇum mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇum nakara mutaṉmoḻiyum makara mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, oṟṟiyalnilai --- nilaimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilaimai kūṟiṉ, vantatu okkum --- mēl+ kūṟiyavāṟē makaramum pakaramumākātu varumoḻimutal vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ainnāḻi aimmaṇṭai munnāḻi mummaṇṭai eṉa varum.

mūṉṟum aintum eṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl nāṉāḻi nāṉmaṇṭai eṉpuḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakaram ṟakaramākātu niṉṟavāṟē niṉṟu muṭitalum varumoḻi mutaṉiṉṟa nakaram ṉakaramāka+ tiriya nilaimoḻi nakaram^ keṭutalum^ koḷka. (46)

TE452n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakaram varuvaḻi+
tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvā kummē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻi --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvākum --- avvarumoḻiyāy+ tōṉṟiya vakarattiṉ vaṭivāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.

tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉē mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām. (47)

TE451n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟu --- nāṉkāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakāramākum --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl lakara oṟṟākattirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum. (48)

TE454n

ainta ṉoṟṟē muntaiyatu keṭumē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟu --- aintā meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu, muntaiyatu keṭum --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl muṉṉiṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.

muntaiyeṉṟataṉāl nakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu vakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu aivvaṭṭiyeṉa+ ciṟupāṉmai varum. (49)

TE455n

mūtalī reṇṇiṉmu ṉuyirvaru kālai+
tavaleṉa moḻipa vukara+ kiḷavi
mutaṉilai nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu māṭṭēṟṟāṉ eytiya ukarattiṟku+ kēṭukūṟi mutaṉīḷka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvit kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalīreṇṇiṉ muṉ uyir varukālai --- oru iru eṉa muṭintuniṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ukarakkiḷavi tavaleṉa moḻipa --- nilaimoḻiyukaramākiya eḻuttu+ keṭutalāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, āvayiṉāṉa mutaṉilai nīṭal --- avviraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōrakal īrakal ōruḻakku īruḻakku eṉa varum (50)

TE456n

mūṉṟu nāṉku mainteṉ kiḷaviyum^
tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkai yākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum --- mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇum nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇum ainteṉṉumeṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkaiyākum --- muṉṉar+ tōṉṟiniṉṟa vakaram varumoḻikku+ kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum, nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvakal muvvuḻakku eṉa itaṟku+ tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. nālakal nāluḻakku aiyakal aiyuḻakku eṉa varum.

tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutalnīṇṭa iṭattu nilaimoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟukkeṭuttukkoḷka. iyaṟkaiyeṉṟataṉāl toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. `mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē' mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramum^ koṇarntu muṭikka. (51)

TE457n

mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalu murittē
yuḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa.

itu muṉṉar+ kuṟukumeṉṟataṉai nīṇṭu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalum urittu --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa eḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, aḵtiyāṇṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉkiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa --- uḻakkeṉṉum^ coṉ muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mūvuḻakka eṉa varum.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉpataṉāṉ akaleṉ kiḷavikku mutaṉilai nīṭalum^ koḷka. mūvakal eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē nilaimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟu+ keṭukka.

mūḻakku mūḻākkeṉṉum marumuṭipu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka. (52)

TE458n

āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutaṉī ṭummē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟeṉkiḷavi --- āṟeṉṉumeṇṇu+ peyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal nīṭum --- muṉṉar+ kuṟukiniṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

atuveṉṉātu āṟeṉṟār, tirintataṉ ṟiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.

utāraṇam: āṟakal āṟuḻakku eṉa varum. (53)

TE459n

oṉpā ṉiṟuti yurupunilai tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭum^ cāriyai moḻiyē.

itu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉpeṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti urupunilai tiriyātu --- aḷavum niṟaiyam varuvaḻi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟukaram^ taṉvaṭivu nilaitiriyātu niṉṟu, cāriyai moḻi iṉ peṟalvēṇṭum --- cāriyai+ collākiya iṉ peṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉpatiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

cāriyaimoḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ iṉṟōṭu ukaramum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttu+ ceykaiceytu muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟukkalam cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

urupeṉpataṉāṉ oṉpatiṟaṟeṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē oṉpatiṉāḻiyeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarakkēṭum^ koḷka. `aḷavākumoḻimutal(eḻu//121) eṉpataṉāṉum `nilaiiya' eṉṉum ilēcāṉum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. (54)

TE460n

nūṟumuṉ variṉum^ kūṟiya viyalpē.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉpataṉai+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟuvariṉum --- nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu --- mēl+ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻi+ kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṇ+ keṭṭu muṭitalām.

utāraṇam: orunūṟu irunūṟu aṟunūṟu eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivai māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa. māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa. (55)

TE461n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.

itu māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē nakāramākum --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu nakaravoṟṟākum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: munnūṟu eṉa varum. (56)

TE462n

nāṉku maintu moṟṟumey tiriyā.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā --- nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇum ainteṉṉumeṇṇum tammoṟṟukkaḷ nilaitiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṉūṟu ainnūṟu eṉa varum.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ nāṉūṟeṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu `laṉaveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//149)) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakaravoṟṟākki ūkāramēṟṟi nilaimoḻi ṉakaram^ keṭuttu+ koḷka. (57)

TE463n

oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntu kiḷantu* aṟṟu* ē
muntai y-oṟṟu* ē ḷakāram iraṭṭum*
nūṟu* eṉ kiḷavi nakāram* mey-keṭa
ū v-ā v-ākum iyaṟkaittu eṉpa
v-āy iṭai varutal ikāra rakāram
īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntukiḷantaṟṟu --- oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram mēl+ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻi+ kūṟiyavāṟupōla oru takaram oṟṟi ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, muntai oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum --- avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū āvākum iyaṟkaitteṉpa --- varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉumeṇṇuppeyar nakaramākiya meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpaiyuṭaitteṉpar pulavar, āyiṭai ikāra rakāram varutal --- ammoḻiyiṭai ōr ikaramum rakāramum varuka, īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum --- īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉaiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉaiyum keṭuttu ōr makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

mey yeṉpataṉāṉ nilaimoḻikkaṇ+ pakaram^ keṭukka.

utāraṇam: toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.

itaṉai oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉmuṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉmēlē okarattaiyēṟṟi+ pakaram^ keṭuttu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttuniṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟiṉai iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu ūkāram ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭu meyyakāram varuvittu ṟakara ukaram^ keṭuttu makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka. (58)

TE464n

āyira+ kiḷavi varūum^ kālai
mutalī reṇṇi ṉukaram^ keṭumē.

iḵtu avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyirammuṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyirakkiḷavi varūum^ kālai --- āyirameṉṉum^ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉvaruṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum --- oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇ+ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaram^ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ukaram^ keṭumeṉavē ēṉaiyaṉa muṉṉar+ kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.

utāraṇam: orāyirai irāyiram eṉa varum. (59)

TE465n

mutaṉilai nīṭiṉu māṉa millai.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉilai nīṭiṉum māṉamillai --- ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamillai eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōrāyiram īrāyiram eṉa varum. (60)

TE466n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.

itu mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇ āyirattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvāyiram eṉa varum.

muṉṉiṉ+ cūttirattu nilai eṉṟataṉāṉ itaṉai mutaṉilai nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉa muṭikka. (61)

TE467n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.

itu nāṉkeṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum --- nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nālāyiram eṉa varum. (62)

TE468n

ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.

iḵtu ainteṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum --- ainteṉṉumeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aiyāyiram eṉa varum. (63)

TE469n

āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ+ kuṟṟiya lukara
mīṟumey yoḻiya+ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu āṟeṉṉu meṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram --- āṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟu keṭutal vēṇṭum --- atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭu+ puṇartalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.

muṉṉar `neṭumutal kuṟukum' ((eḻu//440)) eṉṟa vaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭu+ kēṭu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu peṟṟām, eṉṉai? kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟi muṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaram^ tirintu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipu kūṟiṉār. muṟṟiyalukaram īṟu meyyoḻiya+ keṭumeṉavē kuṟṟukaram^ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyumeṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṉ+ peṟutum. āṟāyiram eṉa varum.

maruṅkeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital koḷka. āṟākuvatē eṉa varum. (64)

TE470n

oṉpā ṉiṟuti yuruvunilai tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭum^ cāriyai marapē.

iḵtu oṉpateṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti --- oṉpateṉṉumeṇṇiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟukaram, uruvunilai tiriyātu --- taṉvaṭivunilai tirintu keṭātu, cāriyai marapu iṉ peṟalvēṇṭum --- cāriyaiyākiya marapiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉ peṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.

uruveṉṟum ciṉaiyeṉṟum^ cāriyai marapeṉṟum^ kūṟiya mikaiyāl āyiramallāta piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭpeyariṭattum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka. oṉpatiṟṟukkōṭi oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu oṉpatiṟṟuttaṭakkai oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉavarum.

iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē vēṟoru muṭipiṉmaiyiṉ+ kūṟātoḻinta eṇṇāyira meṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum īṇṭu+ kūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. `aḷavāku moḻi mutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉuḷ `nilaiiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. (65)

TE471n

nūṟā yiramuṉ varūum^ kālai
nūṟa ṉiyaṟkai mutaṉilai+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉumeṇ aṭaiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟāyiram muṉ varūum^ kālai --- nūṟāyirameṉṉum aṭaiyaṭuttamoḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉvarumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, mutaṉilai+ kiḷavi nūṟaṉ iyaṟkai --- oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉilai+ kiḷavi oṉṟu muṉ nūṟeṉṉumeṇṇōṭu muṭintāṟpōla vikārameyti muṭiyum. eṉavē vaḻinilaikkiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orunūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉaiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭu nūṟāyiram, munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉku nūṟāyiram, ainnūṟāyiram aintu nūṟāyiram, aṟunūṟāyiram āṟu nūṟāyiram, eṇṇūṟāyiram eṭṭu nūṟāyiram, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ivvikāra+ paṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram varuvittu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu meytiriyā vākki eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakāramākki ilēcukaḷāṉ+ koṇṭa ceykaikaḷil vēṇṭuvaṉavum^ koṇarntu muṭikka.

ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāṉ+ toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipiṉōṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka. muṉṉeṉpataṉāṉ iṉcāriyai peṟṟu oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram eṉṟumām. nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal kuṟukāmai koḷka. (66)

TE472n

nūṟeṉ kiḷavi yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ
kīṟuciṉai yoḻiya viṉavoṟṟu mikumē.

itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷai+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi --- nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya --- īṟākiya kuṟṟukaram^ taṉṉāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭum^ keṭātu niṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum --- acciṉaikku iṉamākiya ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu aḷavum^ ceykai yaṟintu oṭṭuka.

īṟuciṉai eṉṟu ōtiya mikaiyāṉ nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu piṟa eṇṇum piṟaporuṭpeyarum ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eyti muṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu nūṟṟukkōṭi nūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟu eṉavum, nūṟṟukkuṟai nūṟṟitaḻttāmarai nūṟṟukkāṇam nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉakoḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē irunūṟṟoṉṟu iraṇṭu nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭaiyaṭuttavaḻiyum^ koḷka. (67)

TE473n

avaiyūr pattiṉu mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu annūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭaiyaṭuttavaḻi+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avai ūrpattiṉum --- annūṟeṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷai ūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum --- īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟorupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu eḻupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉavarum. maṟṟu nūṟṟoṉpatu avai ūrappaṭṭu vantatu aṉmai uṇarka.

ākumeṉṟataṉāṉ orunuṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipum^ koḷka. (68)

TE474n

aḷavu* um* niṟai y-um āy iyal* tiriyātu
kuṟṟiyalukaram um val=eḻuttu iyaṟkai y-um*
muṉ+kiḷantaṉṉa v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyātu --- nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻuttiyaṟkaiyum --- avviṭattu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram^ keṭāmaiyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar --- `valloṟṟu+ toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//426)) eṉa vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikku+ kūṟiya taṉmaiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟukkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

tiriyāteṉṟataṉāl nūṟeṉpatu aṭaiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa varum. (69)

TE475n

oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭai mikumē
niṉṟa vāytam^ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭaiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷai+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi --- oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟāka+ patteṉṉam eṇ ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa āytam^ keṭutal vēṇṭum --- paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭaimikum --- āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupattoṉṟu irupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu irupattiraṇṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (70)

TE476n

āyiram variṉē yiṉṉeṉ cāriyai
yāvayi ṉoṟṟiṭai mikuta lillai.

iḵtu orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyirattai+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyai --- avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi āyirattōṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉ cāriyai peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭai mikutal illai --- avviṭattu+ takara oṟṟu iṭaivantu mikātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. ivai nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavum varum.

āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāl nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟpōla attu+ peṟutalum piṟavum^ koḷka. (71)

TE477n

aḷavu niṟaiyu māyiya ṟiriyā.

iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā --- orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭaimikātu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupatiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum, orupatiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu aṭaiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka.

tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ akaram ṟakaramāka+ tirintu iraṭṭutalum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟutalum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē orupatiṉāḻi eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaram^ tirintuḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭum^ koḷka.

aḷavu niṟaiyu mataṉō raṉṉa eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār muṉṉar+ cūttirattu `āvayiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉum ataṉ muṉṉarccūttirattu `niṉṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉum ivaṟṟai muṭippar. (72)

TE478n

mutaṉilai y-eṇṇiṉ muṉ val=eḻuttu variṉum*
ña na ma+ tōṉṟiṉum ya va vantu* iyaiyiṉum*
mutaṉilai y-iyaṟkai y-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyarai+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum --- ñanamakkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, yavavantu iyaiyiṉum --- yavakkaḷākiya iṭaiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, mutaṉilai iyaṟkai eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉeytiya muṭipu nilaimai eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē vaḻinilaiyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟukaṇamum mutaṉmoḻiyāy variṉ mutaṉilai muṭipāki vikāram eytiyam eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: orukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum, irukal rēṇṭukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivveṇkaḷiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantatu. irukal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṭainilai rakāram^ keṭukka. mukkal mūṉṟukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. itaṟku neṭumutal kuṟukki `mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vantatokkum' ((eḻu//447)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. muṉṉar eṇṇuppeyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum varuvaḻi+ kūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṉ+ poruṭpeyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu+ tirintu muṭikka. avai mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi vakara oṟṟātalumām. nāṟkal nāṉkukakal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai, nāṉñāṇ nāṉkuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai, aiññāṇ aintu ñāṇ nūl maṇi, aiyāḻ aintu yāḻ, aivaṭṭu aintu vaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai, eṇñāṇ eṭṭuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉa+ ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmaiyiṉ oḻikka. iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām muṭipu `nilai' yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka. (73)

TE479n

ataṉilai yuyirkkum yāvaru kālai
mutaṉilai yokara mōvā kummē
rakarattu ukaram^ tuvara+ keṭumē.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉilaikku --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum yāvum varukālai --- uyirmutaṉ moḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nilai --- ammutaṉilaiyiṉ taṉmai ivvāṟām, okaram ōvākum --- okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum, rakarattu ukaram^ tuvara+ keṭum --- rakarattu mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟa+ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

nāṉkāvataṉai mutaṉilaiyōṭu kūṭṭi ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummaiyai uyirōṭum yāvōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻinilaiyeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: ōraṭai ōrākam ōrilai ōrīṭṭam ōrulai ōrūcal ōreḻu ōrēṭu ōraiyam, ōroḻukku ōrōlai ōrauviyam eṉa varum. kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu mutaleṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōr yāḻ ōr yāṉai eṉa varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikarattai nīṭṭi rakarattuḷ ukarattai+ keṭuttu īracai īr yāṉai eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟu+ keṭuttu mūvacai mūyāṉai eṉavum muṭikka. ivai ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. (74)

TE480n

iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
makara vaḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.

iḵtu iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar --- iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ mutalāka oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ --- vaḻakkiṉ kaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavumutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya māveṉṉum^ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu --- avveṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭaiyeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

vaḻakkiyal vaḻaṅkiyaleṉ vikāram. makara aḷavu makara mutaṉmoḻiyākiya aḷavuppeyareṉa+ paṇputtokai. aḵtu `aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//419)) eṉpataṉuḷ oru maṇṭai eṉa muṭittatām.

utāraṇam: irumā mummā nāṉmā aimmā aṟumā eṇmā oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉarkkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vikārappaṭutti muṭikka. iṉi ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭuttātu iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉa muṭikka. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ `aḷavuniṟaiyum' ((eḻu//289)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ īṇṭu ēḻumā eṉa muṭikka. ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.

iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉeṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamaiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumāveṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illaiyāyiṟṟu.

vaḻaṅkiyaṉmā eṉṟār vilaṅkumāvai nīkkutaṟku. (75)

TE481n

laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
ṉummum^ keḻuvu muḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭai+ tōṉṟi+
ceyyuḷ+ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum
vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.

itu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintuniṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉ --- lakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟa varukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar ummum^ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa --- ummeṉṉum^ cāriyaiyum^ keḻuveṉṉum^ cāriyaiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭaittōṉṟi --- piṟacāriyaiyum appeṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉaiyuṭaiya moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum --- ceyyuyaṭcoṟkaḷai+ toṭarpu paṭutti+ kūṟumiṭattu+ poruḷpaṭa niṟkum, vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷvayiṉāṉa --- vēṟṟumaiyai+ kuṟitta poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `vāṉavari villum^ tiṅkaḷum pōlum' itaṟku ummeṉṉum^ cāriyaiyiṉ makarattai `ammiṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//129)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟacāriyaiyum^ tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilaimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉ mēl uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villuntiṅkaḷum pōlumeṉpataṟku villiṭaittiṅkaḷ pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittu+ poruḷuraikka. `kalkeḻukāṉavar nalkuṟumakaḷē' ((kuṟun//71)) itaṟku+ kallai+ keḻīiyiṉa eṉa uraikka. `mānitikkiḻavaṉumpōṉm' ((akam//66)) itaṟku+ kiḻavaṉai+ pōṉmeṉa uraikka. ivvummai ciṟappaṉṟu. `kāṉkeḻunāṭu' ((akam//98)) itaṟku+ kāṉaikkeḻīiya eṉa uraikka.

iṉi aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭai eṉṟataṉāṉ+ keḻuveṉṟatu piṟacolliṭattē `paṇaikeḻu peruntiṟal+ palvēṉ maṉṉar' `tuṟaikeḻumāntai' ((naṟṟiṇai//35)) eṉa iyalpāka varuvaṉavum `vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar' ((akam//17)) `payaṅkeḻumāmaḻai' ((puṟam//266)) eṉa nilaimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttu+ tiriya varuvaṉavum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iccāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyum^ koḷka. `pūkkēḻ toṭalai nuṭaṅka veḻuntu' ((akam//28)) `tuṟaikē ḻūraṉ koṭumai nāṇi' ((aiṅkuṟu//11)) ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟāvatum virikka. `ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi' ((akam//80)) eṉpuḻi+ keḻuveṉṉum uriccol `eḻuttu+ pirinticaittal' ((col//365)) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ+ pūkkēteṉpuḻi valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka.

iṉṉum^ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ+ piṟacāriyai peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittukkoḷka. (76)

TE482n

uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki+
kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉu micaiyiṉum^ tōṉṟi
neṟippaṭa vārā+ kuṟaiccol+ kiḷaviyu
muyartiṇai yaḵṟiṇai yāyiru maruṅki
ṉaimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum^
ceyyum^ ceyta veṉṉum^ kiḷaviyiṉ
mey=oruṅku* iyalum^ toḻiṟoku moḻiyum^
tammiyal kiḷappiṉ+ ṟammuṉ+ ṟāmvarūu
meṇṇiṉ ṟokuti yuḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉavai yellā maruviṉ pāttiya
puṇariya ṉilaiyiṭai yuṇara+ tōṉṟā.

iḵtu ivvatikārattāṉ+ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivaiyeṉa avaṟṟai eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki --- kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟpatōr collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉuntōṉṟi --- kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum icaiyiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟippaṭa vārā+ kuṟaiccol+ kiḷaviyum --- oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmai kuṟainta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai āyiru maruṅkiṉ --- uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷavākiya, aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum --- oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala eṉṉum aintupāliṉaiyum aṟitaṟku+ kāraṇamākiya paṇpukoḷpeyar tokum^ tokaiccollum, ceyyum^ ceyta eṉṉum^ kiḷaviyiṉ --- ceyyum^ ceyta eṉṉum peyarecca+ coṟkaḷiṉuṭaiya, mey oruṅku iyalum^ toḻil tokumoḻiyum --- kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum orucoṟkaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭaipeyarcci tokku niṟkum^ coṟkaḷum, tamiyal kiḷappiṉ --- tamatu taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu, tammuṉ tām varūum eṇṇiṉṟokuti uḷappaṭa --- niṟuttacollum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉar+ tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇuppeyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa, aṉṉa piṟavum ellām --- attaṉmaiyākiya piṟavumellām, maruviṉ pāttiya --- ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyai+ tam ilakkaṇamākavuṭaiya, puṇariyal nilai iṭai yuṇara+ tōṉṟā --- oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmai iṭam viḷaṅka+ tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṇ viṇṇaviṇaittatu viṇviṇaittatu ivai kuṟippuriccol; āṭai veḷḷa viḷarttatu veḷ viḷarttatu ivai paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu ollolittatu ivai icaiyuriccol. `ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlum^ tuṟaivaṉ' eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum. ivai uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakkalākāmaiyiṉ neṟippaṭavārā eṉṟār. viṇṇaviṇaittatu teṟippu+ tōṉṟa+ teṟittateṉṟum viṇviṇaittatu teṟippu+ teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṉ+ ṟaṉmai kuṟainta collāyiṟṟu. `viṉaiyēkuṟippē' ((col//260)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟiya eṉa eṉpataṉai ivaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭaiccollātaliṉ viṇṇeṉa viṇaittatu eṉa+ puṇarkkappaṭumāṟu uṇarka.

iṉi+ karumpārppāṉ karumpārppaṉi karumpārppār karuṅkutirai karuṅkutiraikaḷ eṉa varum ivaṟṟuḷ+ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi kariyarākiya pārppār kariyatākiya kutirai kariyaṉavākiya kutiraikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉaiyum uṇarttum paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuḷ+ karumai eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar tokkatēl+ karumaiyākiya pārppāṉeṉa virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyāmaiyiṉ+ paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.

veṟṟilai veṟṟuppili veṟṟaṭi veṟṟeṉattoṭuttal eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ veṟuvitākiya ilaiyeṉpatu pākkum^ kōṭṭu nūṟum^ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇartti īṟu tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyavāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu . veṟuvitākiya uppiliyeṉṟatu ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉaiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpālum^ tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉilaiyātaliṉ+ puṇarttalākāmai kūṟiṉār.

iṉi āṭaraṅku ceykuṉṟu puṇarpoḻutu arivāḷ kolyāṉai celcelavu eṉa nilam mutalākiya peyareccam^ tokka viṉaittokaikaḷai virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉa+ ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, āṭāniṉṟavaraṅku āṭumaraṅku eṉa+ ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭaipeyarcciyai+ tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum orucoṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ ataṉai oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakki+ puṇarkkalākāmaiyiṉ+ puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār. ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu `viṉaiyiṉṟokuti' ((col//415)) eṉṉum eccaviyal+ cūttirattuḷ+ kūṟutum. ivvum īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum oruṅkuṇarttutaṉ+ ciṟappunōkki+ ceyta eṉpataṉai āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.

iṉi+ pattu eṉa niṟutti+ patteṉa+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu pappatteṉavum paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē ōroṉṟōroṉṟāka+ koṭu eṉṟāl+ puṇarkkappaṭum.

iṉi aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉē uṇṭāṉeṉpuḻi uṇṇeṉṉum mutaṉilaiyum^ kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum iṭaṉum pālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarkkappaṭā, avai niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum aṉmaiyiṉ, kariyaṉeṉpuḻi+ karuveṉa niṟutti aṉṉeṉa+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭā, atu iṉṉaṉeṉṉum poruṭarutaliṉ. ēṉaiviṉaiccoṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittu+ puṇarkkalākāmai uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē koḷḷeṉakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi+ koḷḷeṉpataṉai eṉa eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmaiyum ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya viṉaippeyayarkaḷum piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmaiyum^ koḷka. ivvāciriyar puṇarkkappaṭāta iccoṟkaḷaiyum vaṭanūṟkaṇmuṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum piṉṉuḷḷōr muṭittal mutaṉūlōṭu māṟukoḷa+ kūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (77)

TE483n

kiḷanta valla ceyyuḷuḷ+ ṭirinavum
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
viḷampiya viyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟiṉ
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉa roḻukka
ṉaṉmati nāṭṭattu eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ivvatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟai yellām itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikāra+ puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum --- muṉṉar eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattu+ tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum --- nālvakai vaḻakkum naṭakkumiṭattu maruvutalōṭu tirintu muṭivaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟiṉ --- muṉṉar+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa muṟaimaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟai, naṉmati nāṭṭattu --- nalla aṟiviṉatu ārāycciyālē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ --- vaḻakku muṭintu naṭakkumiṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷai aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `taṭavuttirai' eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum^ `taṭavunilai' ((puṟam//140)) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akara īṟu attu+ peṟṟatu. kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ eṉa akara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar+ takaraṅkoṭukka.

`naṟavam^ kaṇṇi naṟpōr+ cempiyaṉ
kurava ṉīṭiya koṉṟaiyam^ kāṉal'

eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟuti+ ciṉaikeṭṭuḻi iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa.

`muḷavumā tolaicciya painniṇa+ piḷavai+
piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu viraii' ((malaipaṭu//176))

eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammuppeṟāta muṭivupeṟṟaṉa. `tiṇvaḷi vicitta muḻavo ṭākuḷi' ((malaipaṭu//3)) `cuṟaveṟimīṉ' `iravaḻaṅkuciṟuneṟi' ((akam//318)) ivai ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. kaḷḷiyam^ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkalai' ((akam//67)) eṉa ikaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟaṉa. īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉpeṟṟatu. `nalloḻukkam^ kākkum^ tiruvattavar' ((nālaṭi//57)) eṉa ukara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attu+ peṟṟatu, `eppeṟṟamāṉ piṇaipōla' ((cintā//2615)) eṉa ēkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ēkāram peṟātu vantatu. `kaittuṇṭām pōḻtē' ((nālaṭi//19)) `kaittillar nallar' eṉavum `puṉṉaiṅkāṉal' ((akam//80)) `mullaiyantoṭaiyal' eṉavum aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. `aṇṇaṉkōyil vaṇṇamē' ((cintā//nāmakaḷ//126)) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.

iṉi, `añceviniṟaiyavāliṉa' ((mullaip//89)) eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ kakaramum akaramum^ keṭṭaṉa. `maravam pāvai vayiṟāra+ parukiya' `māvanākam vaṇaṅki māṟcuṇam' eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammu+ peṟṟaṉa. `kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka' `poṉṉam^ tikiri muṉcamat turuṭṭi' `poṉṉam^ kuvaṭṭiṟ poli veyti' eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammu+ peṟṟaṉa. `vērpiṇi vetirattu+ kālporunālicai' eṉa rakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu.`nāvalantaṇpoḻil' `kāṉalamperuntuṟai' eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟaṉa. `neytalam^ ciṟupaṟai' iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟatu. `āyiṭai yirupērāṇmai ceytapūcal' ((kuṟun//43)) eṉpuḻi āyiṭaiyeṉpatu urupātaliṉ `nīṭavarutal' ((eḻu//208)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉai eṉa+ tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅkeṭṭu makara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭintatu. a eṉṉum^ cuṭṭu `aṉṟiyaṉaittum' eṉa+ tirintatu. `mutirkōṅkiṉ mukai' ((kuṟiñcikkali//20)) eṉavum^ `kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam' ((cintā//31)) eṉavum^ kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka. `toṇṭutalaiyiṭṭa pattukkuṟai' eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāy+ kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.

iṅṅaṉam^ ceyyuḷuḷ+ piṟavum^ tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ippuṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka. arumaruntāṉeṉpatu rakaravukaraṅkeṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉkeṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭināṭum atu. toṇṭaimānāṭu toṇṭaināṭeṉa īṟṟeḻuttu+ cilakeṭṭu muṭintatu, malaiyamāṉāṭu malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻuttoḻintaṉa palavum^ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvileṉpatu potiyileṉa ukaram^ tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ippuṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ amaittukkoḷka. (78)

((kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((eḻuttatikāram muṟṟiṟṟu))

((TOC))

nūliṉ marapu moḻimarapu nuṇpiṟappu
mēlai+ puṇarcci tokaimarapu --- pālā
murupiyaliṉ piṉṉuyir puḷḷi mayakkam^
teḷivariya kuṟṟukaram^ ceppu.

eḻuttati kārattu+ cūttiraṅka ḷellā
moḻukkiya oṉpatu ōttu uḷḷum --- vaḻukkiṉṟi
nāṉūṟ ṟirunāṟpāṉ mūṉṟeṉṟu nāvalarkaṇ
mēṉūṟṟu vaittār virittu.
